`CMakeLists.txt`:

```txt
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.20)
project(NoEscapeAC VERSION 7.0.0 LANGUAGES CXX)

set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)
set(CMAKE_CXX_EXTENSIONS OFF)

# Optimization & Compiler Flags
if(MSVC)
    add_compile_options(/W4 /arch:AVX2 /permissive- /utf-8 /EHsc /WX)
    add_compile_definitions(_CRT_SECURE_NO_WARNINGS)
else()
    add_compile_options(-O3 -Wall -Wextra -Werror -Wno-unused-parameter -mavx2 -msse2 -pthread)
endif()

# Strict Production Build Flag (strips environment overrides)
option(NOESCAPE_SECURE_BUILD "Enable strict production build disabling development environment overrides" ON)
if(NOESCAPE_SECURE_BUILD)
    add_compile_definitions(NOESCAPE_SECURE_BUILD)
endif()

# Core Include Directories
include_directories(
    ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/include
    ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}
)

# Core Library Sources
set(CORE_SOURCES
    src/core/logger.cpp
    src/core/thread_pool.cpp
    src/core/engine.cpp
    src/core/blackbox.cpp
    src/network/crypto_util.cpp
    src/network/panel_client.cpp
    src/security/anti_debug_linux.cpp
    src/security/anti_debug_windows.cpp
    src/security/integrity.cpp
    src/security/timing.cpp
    src/memory/pattern_scanner.cpp
    src/memory/memory_scanner.cpp
    src/memory/signature_database.cpp
    src/memory/unhooker.cpp
    src/memory/canary_monitor.cpp
    src/forensics/process_analyzer.cpp
    src/forensics/artifact_analyzer.cpp
    src/dma/dma_detector.cpp
    src/kinematics/aim_analyzer.cpp
    src/kinematics/phantom_monitor.cpp
    src/overlay/overlay_detector.cpp
    src/sdk/noescape_sdk.cpp
    src/sdk/auto_init.cpp
)

# Static Library Target (libnoescape.a / noescape_static.lib)
add_library(noescape_core STATIC ${CORE_SOURCES})
if(WIN32)
    set_target_properties(noescape_core PROPERTIES OUTPUT_NAME "noescape_static")
    target_link_libraries(noescape_core PUBLIC ws2_32 psapi advapi32 wintrust crypt32)
else()
    set_target_properties(noescape_core PROPERTIES OUTPUT_NAME "noescape")
    target_link_libraries(noescape_core PUBLIC pthread dl)
endif()

# Shared Library Target (libnoescape.so / noescape.dll)
add_library(noescape_shared SHARED ${CORE_SOURCES})
set_target_properties(noescape_shared PROPERTIES OUTPUT_NAME "noescape")
target_compile_definitions(noescape_shared PRIVATE NOESCAPE_EXPORTS NOESCAPE_BUILD_SHARED)
if(WIN32)
    target_link_libraries(noescape_shared PUBLIC ws2_32 psapi advapi32 wintrust crypt32)
else()
    target_link_libraries(noescape_shared PUBLIC pthread dl)
endif()

# Standalone Client Application
add_executable(noescape_app main.cpp)
target_link_libraries(noescape_app PRIVATE noescape_core)

# Interactive Subsystem Demo
add_executable(noescape_demo demo/demo_main.cpp)
target_link_libraries(noescape_demo PRIVATE noescape_core)

# Standalone Dummy Game Executable (Zero AC code, used for Scenario B / LD_PRELOAD verification)
add_executable(dummy_game tools/dummy_game.cpp)
if(NOT MSVC)
    target_link_libraries(dummy_game PRIVATE pthread)
endif()

# Vanguard Ban UI Helper executable
add_custom_target(noescape_ban_ui ALL
    COMMAND ${CMAKE_COMMAND} -E copy ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/tools/noescape_ban_ui.py ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/noescape_ban_ui
    COMMAND chmod +x ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/noescape_ban_ui
    COMMENT "Installing Vanguard Ban UI executable into build tree"
)

# Comprehensive Unit Test Suite
set(TEST_SOURCES
    tests/test_main.cpp
    tests/test_crypto.cpp
    tests/test_obfuscation.cpp
    tests/test_simd_scanner.cpp
    tests/test_kinematics.cpp
    tests/test_memory.cpp
    tests/test_forensics.cpp
    tests/test_dma.cpp
    tests/test_timing.cpp
    tests/test_thread_pool.cpp
    tests/test_engine.cpp
    tests/test_overlay.cpp
    tests/test_sdk.cpp
    tests/test_blackbox.cpp
    tests/test_audit_remediation.cpp
    tests/test_signatures.cpp
)

add_executable(noescape_tests ${TEST_SOURCES})
target_link_libraries(noescape_tests PRIVATE noescape_core)
target_compile_definitions(noescape_tests PRIVATE NOESCAPE_SOURCE_DIR="${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}")

enable_testing()
add_test(NAME UnifiedNoEscapeTests COMMAND noescape_tests)

```

`README.md`:

```md
# 🛡️ NoEscape Anti-Cheat Engine (v7.0 / Release 1.1)

[![GitHub Release](https://img.shields.io/badge/Release-NoEscapeAC%201.1-red?style=for-the-badge&logo=github)](https://github.com/CmdSuv/NoEscapeAC/releases/tag/v1.1)
[![Download Zip](https://img.shields.io/badge/Download-Source%20Code%20(.zip)-darkred?style=for-the-badge&logo=github)](https://github.com/CmdSuv/NoEscapeAC/archive/refs/tags/v1.1.zip)
[![License: Non-Commercial Share-Alike](https://img.shields.io/badge/License-Non--Commercial%20Share--Alike-blue?style=for-the-badge)](LICENSE)

**NoEscape Anti-Cheat (NoEscapeAC)** is a modern, lightweight, and unified user-mode (Ring-3) anti-cheat and runtime integrity framework engineered in **C++20** for Linux and Windows. Designed to safeguard competitive multiplayer games and custom game launchers without intrusive kernel drivers, NoEscapeAC delivers defense-in-depth security: AVX2 SIMD byte pattern scanning, unbacked memory forensics with JVM/JIT HotSpot awareness, automatic inline API unhooking, PCIe DMA anomaly detection, statistical mouse kinematics (silent-aim / instant-snap heuristics), ESP fake-window overlay interception, and a standalone crimson security ban enforcement UI.

---

### 📦 Latest Release: NoEscapeAC 1.1
> **[Direct Download: NoEscapeAC 1.1 Source Code (.zip)](https://github.com/CmdSuv/NoEscapeAC/archive/refs/tags/v1.1.zip)**  
> **Release Tag**: `v1.1` | **Full Release Notes**: [View on GitHub Releases](https://github.com/CmdSuv/NoEscapeAC/releases/tag/v1.1)
> 
> **Key Highlights in Release 1.1:**
> - **Unified C++20 Ring-3 Architecture**: Zero external dependencies beyond glibc / Win32.
> - **Standalone Crimson Ban Overlay**: Standalone GUI dialog with detailed English forensic report and immediate process kick (`tools/noescape_ban_ui.py`).
> - **JVM / JIT Runtime Guard**: Native HotSpot CodeCache awareness preventing false positives on Minecraft / Fabric / Java games.
> - **Full Verification Suite**: 86 automated unit tests + 5 Scenario B preload verification tests passing with 100% rate.

---

### ✨ Key Subsystems & Features

1. **Cross-Platform Architecture (Linux & Windows Ring-3)**:
   - Linux: `/proc` subsystem audit, `/proc/self/maps` page analysis, `dl_iterate_phdr` ELF parsing, and `mprotect` inline unhooking.
   - Windows: Win32/NTAPI direct PEB inspection, `NtQueryInformationProcess`, `Toolhelp32`, and `VirtualQueryEx`.
   - Compile-time XOR string obfuscation (`NOESCAPE_XOR`) and FNV-1a hashing (`NOESCAPE_HASH`).

2. **SIMD Pattern Scanner (AVX2 / SSE2 / Scalar)**:
   - High-throughput byte signature search utilizing AVX2 256-bit vector registers with scalar fallback (up to 35+ GB/s throughput).
   - Supports IDA-style wildcard patterns (`48 B8 ? ? ? ? ? ? ? ? FF E0`) and batch scanning in a single pass.

3. **Memory & Injection Analysis**:
   - **Unbacked Executable Memory Scanner**: Detects anonymous `RX`/`RWX` pages, reflective DLL / SO injection, deleted binary execution (`/proc/<pid>/exe (deleted)`), and memory-ghosted modules.
   - **Inline Hook Detector & Unhooker**: Identifies `0xE9` (JMP rel32), `0xEB` (JMP rel8), `0xFF 0x25` (JMP indirect), `MOV RAX; JMP RAX`, `PUSH/RET`, and `0xCC` (INT3) hooks on core system APIs and automatically restores clean instructions into memory.

4. **Overlay & ESP/Wallhack Detector (Rendering Hook & Fake Window Scanner)**:
   - **Fake Window Hijack Scanner**: Detects transparent/layered top-most fake windows (`WS_EX_LAYERED`, `WS_EX_TRANSPARENT`, `WS_EX_TOPMOST`) matching the game viewport bounding rect.
   - **Rendering Pipeline Integrity**: Inspects DirectX / Vulkan / OpenGL `Present`, `SwapBuffers`, and dynamic swapchain vtables to ensure function pointers reside strictly within loaded module address spaces and have not been redirected to unbacked trampolines or inline-hooked.
   - Linux overlay process detection across X11/Wayland display environments.

5. **Blackbox Crash/Violation Dumper**:
   - When a fatal security event occurs or threat score exceeds threshold, captures a compact, authenticated diagnostic snapshot:
     - Real-time timestamp & Hardware ID fingerprint.
     - Violating component & reason.
     - Stack backtrace return addresses (`backtrace()` on Linux / `CaptureStackBackTrace` on Windows).
     - Recent input kinematics circular trail buffer.
     - Sample of loaded executable modules.
   - Encrypted with AES/SHA-256 CTR stream cipher and authenticated with HMAC-SHA256 signature for tamper-evident offline diagnostics and backend telemetry.

6. **Industry-Standard C-API & Game Engine SDK (`noescape_sdk.h`)**:
   - Clean C-compatible ABI (`extern "C"`) enabling 2-line integration in Unity (P/Invoke), Unreal Engine, and custom C/C++ game engines:
     - `NoEscape_Init(const NoEscapeConfig* config)`
     - `NoEscape_Shutdown()`
     - `NoEscape_Tick()`
     - `NoEscape_PushMouseEvent(float delta_yaw, float delta_pitch, uint64_t timestamp_ms)`
     - `NoEscape_GetThreatScore()`
     - `NoEscape_SetViolationCallback(void (*cb)(int type, int score, const char* details))`
   - Exports both static (`libnoescape.a` / `noescape_static.lib`) and dynamic shared (`libnoescape.so` / `noescape.dll`) libraries.

7. **PCIe DMA Hardware & Bus Latency Detection**:
   - Micro-architectural cache eviction & memory bus contention latency jitter profiling (`rdtsc`, `clflushopt`, `mfence`) to detect external FPGA DMA cards (e.g., Screamer, CaptainDMA, Enigma X1).
   - Direct bus enumeration for known FTDI FT601 USB3 FIFO bridges and Xilinx Artix-7 PCIe VID/PID signatures.

8. **Input Kinematics & Aimbot Trajectory Analysis**:
   - Biomechanical mouse movement heuristics: computes angular velocity ($\omega$), acceleration ($\alpha$), and jerk ($j$).
   - **Instant Snap Detection**: Identifies 1-tick silent-aim flick shots ($\ge 20^\circ$ in $\le 35\text{ms}$) followed by immediate dead stops.
   - **Linearity & Smoothing Analysis**: Detects sub-millidegree robotic linear interpolation ($R^2 \approx 1.0$) and constant-velocity aimbot smoothing without natural physiological tremors.

---

### 🚀 Build & Verification

#### Prerequisites
- **Linux**: GCC 11+ or Clang 13+ with C++20 support, CMake 3.20+, `make`/`ninja`.
- **Windows**: MSVC v143+ (Visual Studio 2022) with C++20 standard `/std:c++20`.

#### Build Commands
```bash
# Configure and build
cmake -B build -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release
cmake --build build -j$(nproc)

# Run Comprehensive Unit Test Suite (48 test cases across all subsystems)
./build/noescape_tests

# Run Interactive Subsystem Demo
./build/noescape_demo

# Run Standalone Anti-Cheat Engine (Single Audit)
./build/noescape_app --once
```

### 📂 Directory Layout

```
NoEscapeAC/
├── include/
│   ├── noescape_sdk.h        # Clean C-ABI & C++ SDK layer
│   └── noescape/
│       ├── core/             # Types, Logger, ThreadPool, Engine, Blackbox, Obfuscation
│       ├── security/         # AntiDebug, Self-Integrity, TSC Timing
│       ├── memory/           # SIMD PatternScanner, MemoryScanner, Unhooker
│       ├── overlay/          # OverlayDetector (Window scanner & Swapchain validator)
│       ├── forensics/        # ProcessAnalyzer, ArtifactAnalyzer
│       ├── dma/              # DmaDetector (PCIe Leech & Latency Jitter)
│       ├── kinematics/       # AimAnalyzer (Snap, Linearity, Smoothing)
│       ├── network/          # CryptoUtil (SHA-256 / HMAC), PanelClient
│       └── sdk/              # SDK forwarding header
├── src/                      # Subsystem implementations (Linux + Windows)
│   ├── core/
│   ├── security/
│   ├── memory/
│   ├── overlay/
│   ├── forensics/
│   ├── dma/
│   ├── kinematics/
│   ├── network/
│   └── sdk/
├── demo/                     # Interactive demonstration program
├── tests/                    # 48 Comprehensive unit tests
└── CMakeLists.txt            # Exports libnoescape.a, libnoescape.so, and executables
```

`demo/demo_main.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "noescape/core/engine.hpp"
#include "noescape/core/logger.hpp"
#include "noescape/memory/pattern_scanner.hpp"
#include "noescape/network/crypto_util.hpp"
#include "noescape/overlay/overlay_detector.hpp"
#include "noescape/core/blackbox.hpp"
#include "noescape_sdk.h"
#include <iostream>
#include <chrono>
#include <thread>
#include <cstring>

using namespace noescape;

int main() {
    std::cout << "\033[1;36m"
              << "============================================================\n"
              << "      NoEscape Anti-Cheat Engine v7.0 - Interactive Demo     \n"
              << "============================================================\033[0m\n\n";

    core::EngineConfig config;
    config.enforceBanOnViolation = false; // Demo mode: observe all alerts without terminating

    core::AntiCheatEngine engine(config);
    engine.Initialize();

    std::cout << "\n\033[1;33m[DEMO 1] Hardware ID & Tamper-Resistant Signature Verification\033[0m\n";
    std::string hwid = network::CryptoUtil::GetMachineHWID();
    std::cout << "  -> Hardware ID: " << hwid << "\n";
    std::string sig = network::CryptoUtil::HmacSha256("DemoSecretKey", hwid + ":TIMESTAMP:100");
    std::cout << "  -> HMAC-SHA256 Signature: " << sig << "\n";

    std::cout << "\n\033[1;33m[DEMO 2] High-Speed SIMD Pattern Scanner Performance\033[0m\n";
    std::vector<uint8_t> largeBuffer(4 * 1024 * 1024, 0x90); // 4MB buffer
    size_t cheatOffset = 3 * 1024 * 1024 + 42;
    // Inject signature
    largeBuffer[cheatOffset] = 0x48;
    largeBuffer[cheatOffset + 1] = 0xB8;
    largeBuffer[cheatOffset + 10] = 0xFF;
    largeBuffer[cheatOffset + 11] = 0xE0;

    memory::Pattern testPat("48 B8 ? ? ? ? ? ? ? ? FF E0", "Demo_X64_Shellcode");

    auto t0 = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto match = memory::PatternScanner::Scan(largeBuffer.data(), largeBuffer.size(), testPat);
    auto t1 = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

    double scanMs = std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(t1 - t0).count();
    std::cout << "  -> Scanned 4MB in " << scanMs << " ms ("
              << (4.0 / (scanMs / 1000.0)) << " MB/s throughput)\n";
    if (match) {
        std::cout << "  -> \033[32mMatched pattern '" << testPat.GetName() 
                  << "' at offset " << *match << "\033[0m\n";
    }

    std::cout << "\n\033[1;33m[DEMO 3] Live System Audit Cycle\033[0m\n";
    auto events = engine.AuditCycle();
    std::cout << "  -> Audit cycle triggered " << events.size() << " security events.\n";
    std::cout << "  -> Cumulative Threat Score: " << engine.GetThreatScore() << "\n";

    std::cout << "\n\033[1;33m[DEMO 4] Real-Time Kinematics Aimbot Detection Simulation\033[0m\n";
    std::cout << "  -> Simulating human aim trajectory...\n";
    uint64_t nowMs = 10000;
    for (int i = 0; i < 10; ++i) {
        engine.PushInputSample(i * 0.4f, i * 0.7f, nowMs);
        nowMs += 16;
    }
    std::cout << "  -> Human aim validated: Clean.\n";

    std::cout << "  -> Simulating Silent-Aim / Snap Aimbot (38 deg instant angle flick)...\n";
    engine.PushInputSample(10.0f, 40.0f, nowMs);
    nowMs += 16;
    engine.PushInputSample(10.0f, 40.0f, nowMs); // Dead stop

    std::cout << "  -> Current Threat Score after aimbot injection: " << engine.GetThreatScore() << "\n";

    std::cout << "\n\033[1;33m[DEMO 5] ESP Overlay Hijack & Rendering Hook Inspection\033[0m\n";
    auto& overlayDet = overlay::OverlayDetector::Instance();
    overlayDet.ClearMockWindows();
    overlayDet.SetGameViewport(0, 0, 1920, 1080);

    // Register simulated ESP topmost transparent window
    overlay::OverlayWindowInfo fakeEsp;
    fakeEsp.title = "DirectX 11 External ESP Overlay";
    fakeEsp.class_name = "ImGuiOverlayClass";
    fakeEsp.pid = 44102;
    fakeEsp.handle = 0xBEEF01;
    fakeEsp.rect = {0, 0, 1920, 1080};
    fakeEsp.is_topmost = true;
    fakeEsp.is_transparent = true;
    fakeEsp.is_layered = true;
    overlayDet.AddMockWindow(fakeEsp);

    auto overlayAlerts = overlayDet.DetectSuspiciousWindows();
    std::cout << "  -> Overlay Scanner detected " << overlayAlerts.size() << " unauthorized overlay windows:\n";
    for (const auto& a : overlayAlerts) {
        std::cout << "     \033[31m[!]\033[0m " << a.details << "\n";
    }

    // Inspect swapchain function pointer
    uint8_t fakeShellcode[16] = {0x90, 0xC3};
    auto hookAlert = overlayDet.InspectRenderingPointer("IDXGISwapChain::Present", fakeShellcode);
    if (hookAlert) {
        std::cout << "  -> Swapchain inspection detected hijacked hook:\n";
        std::cout << "     \033[31m[!]\033[0m " << hookAlert->details << "\n";
    }
    overlayDet.ClearMockWindows();

    std::cout << "\n\033[1;33m[DEMO 6] Blackbox Violation Diagnostic Dumper\033[0m\n";
    auto& dumper = core::BlackboxDumper::Instance();
    auto snapshot = dumper.CaptureSnapshot("Kinematics::SnapDetector", "Non-Human Aim Trajectory Exceeded Limits", 90, 14);
    std::cout << "  -> Captured snapshot: " << snapshot.violating_component 
              << " | Reason: " << snapshot.violation_reason
              << " | Backtrace Frames: " << snapshot.backtrace_addrs.size()
              << " | Input Trail: " << snapshot.recent_input_trail.size() << " samples\n";

    auto encPayload = dumper.SerializeAndEncrypt(snapshot, config.secretKey);
    std::cout << "  -> Authenticated encrypted payload: " << encPayload.size() << " bytes (HMAC-SHA256 verified)\n";

    core::BlackboxRecord decryptedSnap{};
    if (dumper.DecryptAndDeserialize(encPayload, config.secretKey, decryptedSnap)) {
        std::cout << "  -> \033[32mDecrypted & authenticated snapshot successfully (HWID: " 
                  << decryptedSnap.hwid.substr(0, 16) << "...)\033[0m\n";
    }

    std::cout << "\n\033[1;33m[DEMO 7] C-API & SDK Layer Integration\033[0m\n";
    NoEscapeConfig sdkCfg = NoEscape_GetDefaultConfig();
    sdkCfg.enable_heartbeat = false;
    sdkCfg.enable_anti_debug = false;
    sdkCfg.enforce_ban = false;

    if (NoEscape_Init(&sdkCfg) == 0) {
        std::cout << "  -> SDK Initialized via C-ABI successfully.\n";
        NoEscape_PushMouseEvent(1.2f, 0.4f, 20000);
        NoEscape_Tick();
        std::cout << "  -> SDK Threat Score: " << NoEscape_GetThreatScore() << "\n";
        NoEscape_Shutdown();
        std::cout << "  -> SDK Shutdown cleanly.\n";
    }

    std::cout << "\n\033[1;32m[DEMO COMPLETE] All NoEscape Anti-Cheat subsystems verified successfully.\033[0m\n";
    return 0;
}

`include/noescape/core/blackbox.hpp`:

```hpp
#pragma once

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <deque>
#include <cstdint>
#include <mutex>
#include "noescape/core/types.hpp"
#include "noescape/kinematics/aim_analyzer.hpp"

namespace noescape::core {

struct BlackboxRecord {
    uint64_t timestamp{0};
    std::string hwid;
    std::string violating_component;
    std::string violation_reason;
    uint32_t threat_score{0};
    uint32_t violation_type{0};
    std::vector<uintptr_t> backtrace_addrs;
    std::vector<kinematics::AngleSample> recent_input_trail;
    std::vector<std::string> loaded_modules_sample;
};

class BlackboxDumper {
public:
    static BlackboxDumper& Instance();

    // Feeds an input sample into the circular blackbox buffer
    void RecordInputSample(float pitch, float yaw, uint64_t timestamp_ms);
    void RecordInputSample(const kinematics::AngleSample& sample);

    // Captures a full diagnostic snapshot
    BlackboxRecord CaptureSnapshot(
        const std::string& component,
        const std::string& reason,
        uint32_t score,
        uint32_t violation_type = 0
    );

    // Serializes snapshot and encrypts with HMAC authentication
    std::vector<uint8_t> SerializeAndEncrypt(
        const BlackboxRecord& record,
        const std::string& secret_key
    );

    // Decrypts and deserializes snapshot; verifies HMAC signature
    bool DecryptAndDeserialize(
        const std::vector<uint8_t>& encrypted_data,
        const std::string& secret_key,
        BlackboxRecord& out_record
    );

    // Writes encrypted dump payload to disk
    bool SaveDumpToFile(const std::string& file_path, const std::vector<uint8_t>& encrypted_data);

    // Dump directory configuration
    void SetDumpDirectory(const std::string& dir);
    std::string GetDumpDirectory() const;

    // Generates a standard blackbox filename with timestamp
    std::string GenerateDumpFilename() const;

    // Platform-specific stack backtrace capture
    static std::vector<uintptr_t> CaptureBacktrace(size_t max_depth = 32);

    // Platform-specific sample of loaded module paths
    static std::vector<std::string> SampleLoadedModules(size_t max_count = 16);

    // Clears the input trail buffer
    void ClearInputTrail();

    // Gets the current trail count
    size_t GetInputTrailCount() const;

private:
    BlackboxDumper();

    mutable std::mutex mutex_;
    std::string dump_dir_{""};
    std::deque<kinematics::AngleSample> input_trail_;
    size_t trail_capacity_{64};
};

} // namespace noescape::core

#pragma once

namespace noescape::core {

class AntiCheatEngine {
public:
    using BanCallback = std::function<void(const std::string& reason, uint32_t finalScore)>;
    using ViolationCallback = std::function<void(const DetectionEvent& event)>;

    explicit AntiCheatEngine(EngineConfig config = EngineConfig());
    ~AntiCheatEngine();

    // Initializes all security baselines (integrity hashes, TSC calibration, DMA baseline)
    bool Initialize();

    // Starts background audit worker loops
    void Start();

    // Stops background audit workers
    void Stop();

    // Queries the panel's global ban list once. Banned -> ban; repeated unreachable or
    // invalidly-signed replies -> scored event (offline play is never an instant ban).
    std::vector<DetectionEvent> VerifyPanelStatus();

    // Single synchronous audit pass over all enabled modules (useful for deterministic tests)
    std::vector<DetectionEvent> AuditCycle();

    // Feed an input sample into kinematics aim analyzer
    void PushInputSample(float pitch, float yaw, uint64_t timestamp_ms);

    // Process a newly triggered detection event
    void ProcessDetection(const DetectionEvent& event);

    // Queries current threat status
    uint32_t GetThreatScore() const { return threat_score_.load(); }
    bool IsBanned() const { return is_banned_.load(); }
    std::vector<DetectionEvent> GetDetectionHistory() const;

    // Callbacks and configuration
    void SetBanCallback(BanCallback cb) { ban_callback_ = std::move(cb); }
    void SetViolationCallback(ViolationCallback cb) { violation_callback_ = std::move(cb); }
    const EngineConfig& GetConfig() const { return config_; }

    kinematics::AimAnalyzer& GetAimAnalyzer() { return aim_analyzer_; }
    kinematics::PhantomMonitor& GetPhantomMonitor() { return phantom_monitor_; }

    // Feed camera position/orientation for decoy-entity (phantom) analysis
    void PushViewSample(float x, float y, float z, float pitch, float yaw, uint64_t timestamp_ms);
    const std::vector<uint8_t>& GetLastBlackboxPayload() const { return last_blackbox_payload_; }

    // Watchdog heartbeat monitoring & testing interface
    void UpdateAuditHeartbeat();
    uint64_t GetLastAuditHeartbeatMs() const { return last_audit_heartbeat_ms_.load(std::memory_order_acquire); }
    void SetAuditHeartbeatForTesting(uint64_t timestamp_ms) { last_audit_heartbeat_ms_.store(timestamp_ms, std::memory_order_release); }
    void FreezeBackgroundLoopForTesting(bool freeze) { freeze_background_loop_for_testing_.store(freeze, std::memory_order_release); }

private:
    EngineConfig config_;
    std::atomic<uint32_t> threat_score_{0};
    std::atomic<bool> is_banned_{false};
    std::atomic<bool> running_{false};
    std::atomic<bool> freeze_background_loop_for_testing_{false};

    ThreadPool thread_pool_;
    network::PanelClient panel_client_;
    kinematics::AimAnalyzer aim_analyzer_;
    kinematics::PhantomMonitor phantom_monitor_;

    mutable std::mutex history_mutex_;
    mutable std::mutex input_mutex_;
    std::vector<DetectionEvent> history_;
    std::vector<uint8_t> last_blackbox_payload_;
    std::vector<memory::CheatSignature> default_signatures_;
    BanCallback ban_callback_;
    ViolationCallback violation_callback_;

    void TriggerBan(const std::string& reason, 
                    DetectionType type = DetectionType::UNKNOWN, 
                    const std::string& component = "AntiCheatEngine::Enforcement");
    void SpawnBanScreen(const std::string& reason, 
                        uint32_t finalScore, 
                        const std::string& component, 
                        DetectionType type);
    void BackgroundLoop();
    void WatchdogLoop();
    uint32_t panel_failures_{0};

    std::thread watchdog_thread_;
    std::atomic<uint64_t> last_audit_heartbeat_ms_{0};
};

using SecurityEngine = AntiCheatEngine;

} // namespace noescape::core

`include/noescape/core/obfuscation.hpp`:

```hpp
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>
#include <cstddef>
#include <string>
#include <array>
#include <utility>

namespace noescape::core {

// ============================================================================
// 64-Bit FNV-1a Compile-Time String Hashing
// ============================================================================
constexpr uint64_t FNV1A_OFFSET = 14695981039346656037ULL;
constexpr uint64_t FNV1A_PRIME  = 1099511628211ULL;

constexpr uint64_t HashFnv1a(const char* str, size_t len) {
    uint64_t hash = FNV1A_OFFSET;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < len; ++i) {
        hash ^= static_cast<uint8_t>(str[i]);
        hash *= FNV1A_PRIME;
    }
    return hash;
}

constexpr uint64_t HashFnv1a(const char* str) {
    uint64_t hash = FNV1A_OFFSET;
    while (*str) {
        hash ^= static_cast<uint8_t>(*str++);
        hash *= FNV1A_PRIME;
    }
    return hash;
}

// Non-linear 64-bit integer mix (SplitMix64) for compile-time keystream derivation
constexpr uint64_t CompileTimeMix64(uint64_t v) {
    v ^= v >> 30;
    v *= 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
    v ^= v >> 27;
    v *= 0x94d049bb133111ebULL;
    v ^= v >> 31;
    return v;
}

constexpr uint8_t CompileTimeKeystreamByte(uint32_t seed, size_t index) {
    uint64_t mixed = CompileTimeMix64(static_cast<uint64_t>(seed) +
                                      static_cast<uint64_t>(index) * 0x9E3779B97F4A7C15ULL +
                                      0x517cc1b727220a95ULL);
    return static_cast<uint8_t>((mixed ^ (mixed >> 32) ^ (mixed >> 16)) & 0xFF);
}

#define NOESCAPE_SEED ((__TIME__[7] - '0') * 1 + \
                       (__TIME__[6] - '0') * 10 + \
                       (__TIME__[4] - '0') * 60 + \
                       (__TIME__[3] - '0') * 600 + \
                       (__TIME__[1] - '0') * 3600 + \
                       (__TIME__[0] - '0') * 36000 + \
                       __LINE__ * 7919)

// ============================================================================
// Multi-Byte Non-Linear CBC Stream Cipher Obfuscation
// Null terminators are chained with previous ciphertext bytes, rendering
// differential and algebraic key derivation impossible.
// ============================================================================
template <size_t N, uint32_t Seed = NOESCAPE_SEED>
class XorString {
    std::array<uint8_t, N> encrypted_{};

public:
    constexpr explicit XorString(const char (&str)[N]) {
        if constexpr (N > 0) {
            encrypted_[0] = static_cast<uint8_t>(str[0]) ^
                            CompileTimeKeystreamByte(Seed, 0) ^
                            static_cast<uint8_t>(Seed & 0x7F);
            for (size_t i = 1; i < N; ++i) {
                uint8_t chain = static_cast<uint8_t>(encrypted_[i - 1] * 33 + 17);
                encrypted_[i] = static_cast<uint8_t>(str[i]) ^
                                CompileTimeKeystreamByte(Seed, i) ^
                                chain;
            }
        }
    }

    // Decrypts string into a rotating ring of thread-local buffers to allow
    // multiple concurrent string decryptions in a single expression.
    const char* decrypt() const {
        if constexpr (N == 0) return "";
        constexpr size_t RING_SIZE = 4;
        thread_local char rings[RING_SIZE][N];
        thread_local size_t ring_idx = 0;

        char* dest = rings[ring_idx];
        ring_idx = (ring_idx + 1) % RING_SIZE;

        dest[0] = static_cast<char>(encrypted_[0] ^
                                    CompileTimeKeystreamByte(Seed, 0) ^
                                    static_cast<uint8_t>(Seed & 0x7F));
        for (size_t i = 1; i < N; ++i) {
            uint8_t chain = static_cast<uint8_t>(encrypted_[i - 1] * 33 + 17);
            dest[i] = static_cast<char>(encrypted_[i] ^
                                        CompileTimeKeystreamByte(Seed, i) ^
                                        chain);
        }
        return dest;
    }

    std::string str() const {
        return std::string(decrypt(), N > 0 ? N - 1 : 0);
    }

    constexpr size_t size() const { return N; }
};

#define NOESCAPE_XOR(s) ([]() { \
    constexpr ::noescape::core::XorString<sizeof(s), NOESCAPE_SEED> obfuscated(s); \
    return obfuscated.decrypt(); \
}())

#define NOESCAPE_HASH(s) (::noescape::core::HashFnv1a(s))

} // namespace noescape::core

```

`include/noescape/core/thread_pool.hpp`:

```hpp
#pragma once

#include <vector>
#include <queue>
#include <thread>
#include <mutex>
#include <condition_variable>
#include <future>
#include <functional>
#include <atomic>
#include <type_traits>

namespace noescape::core {

class ThreadPool {
public:
    explicit ThreadPool(size_t threads = 4);
    ~ThreadPool();

    ThreadPool(const ThreadPool&) = delete;
    ThreadPool& operator=(const ThreadPool&) = delete;

    template <class F, class... Args>
    auto Enqueue(F&& f, Args&&... args) 
        -> std::future<std::invoke_result_t<F, Args...>> {
        using return_type = std::invoke_result_t<F, Args...>;

        auto task = std::make_shared<std::packaged_task<return_type()>>(
            std::bind(std::forward<F>(f), std::forward<Args>(args)...)
        );

        std::future<return_type> res = task->get_future();
        {
            std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(queue_mutex_);
            if (stop_) {
                throw std::runtime_error("ThreadPool: Enqueue called on stopped ThreadPool");
            }
            tasks_.emplace([task]() { (*task)(); });
        }
        condition_.notify_one();
        return res;
    }

    void Stop();

private:
    std::vector<std::thread> workers_;
    std::queue<std::function<void()>> tasks_;
    std::mutex queue_mutex_;
    std::condition_variable condition_;
    std::atomic<bool> stop_{false};
};

} // namespace noescape::core

```

#pragma once

namespace noescape::core {

enum class DetectionType : uint32_t {
    UNKNOWN = 0,
    ANTI_DEBUG_TRACER,
    ANTI_DEBUG_HARDWARE_BP,
    ANTI_DEBUG_TOOL,
    SELF_INTEGRITY_VIOLATION,
    SPEEDHACK_TIMING_DISCREPANCY,
    UNBACKED_EXECUTABLE_MEMORY,
    MEMORY_SIGNATURE_MATCH,
    INLINE_HOOK_DETECTED,
    ENVIRONMENT_TAMPERING,
    FORENSICS_ARTIFACT_DETECTED,
    SUSPICIOUS_PROCESS_DETECTED,
    DMA_LATENCY_JITTER_ANOMALY,
    DMA_PCIE_LEECH_DEVICE,
    KINEMATICS_AIMBOT_SNAP,
    KINEMATICS_PERFECT_LINEARITY,
    KINEMATICS_ARTIFICIAL_SMOOTHING,
    HEARTBEAT_TAMPERING,
    OVERLAY_HIJACK_DETECTED,
    RENDERING_HOOK_DETECTED,
    CANARY_TAMPERED,
    PHANTOM_ENTITY_TARGETED
};

enum class Severity : uint32_t {
    INFO = 0,
    LOW = 1,
    MEDIUM = 2,
    HIGH = 3,
    CRITICAL = 4,
    FATAL_BAN = 5
};

struct DetectionEvent {
    DetectionType type{DetectionType::UNKNOWN};
    Severity severity{Severity::INFO};
    std::string component;
    std::string details;
    uint64_t timestamp{0};
    uint32_t threat_score{0};

    static const char* TypeToString(DetectionType t) {
        switch (t) {
            case DetectionType::ANTI_DEBUG_TRACER: return "ANTI_DEBUG_TRACER";
            case DetectionType::ANTI_DEBUG_HARDWARE_BP: return "ANTI_DEBUG_HARDWARE_BP";
            case DetectionType::ANTI_DEBUG_TOOL: return "ANTI_DEBUG_TOOL";
            case DetectionType::SELF_INTEGRITY_VIOLATION: return "SELF_INTEGRITY_VIOLATION";
            case DetectionType::SPEEDHACK_TIMING_DISCREPANCY: return "SPEEDHACK_TIMING_DISCREPANCY";
            case DetectionType::UNBACKED_EXECUTABLE_MEMORY: return "UNBACKED_EXECUTABLE_MEMORY";
            case DetectionType::MEMORY_SIGNATURE_MATCH: return "MEMORY_SIGNATURE_MATCH";
            case DetectionType::INLINE_HOOK_DETECTED: return "INLINE_HOOK_DETECTED";
            case DetectionType::ENVIRONMENT_TAMPERING: return "ENVIRONMENT_TAMPERING";
            case DetectionType::FORENSICS_ARTIFACT_DETECTED: return "FORENSICS_ARTIFACT_DETECTED";
            case DetectionType::SUSPICIOUS_PROCESS_DETECTED: return "SUSPICIOUS_PROCESS_DETECTED";
            case DetectionType::DMA_LATENCY_JITTER_ANOMALY: return "DMA_LATENCY_JITTER_ANOMALY";
            case DetectionType::DMA_PCIE_LEECH_DEVICE: return "DMA_PCIE_LEECH_DEVICE";
            case DetectionType::KINEMATICS_AIMBOT_SNAP: return "KINEMATICS_AIMBOT_SNAP";
            case DetectionType::KINEMATICS_PERFECT_LINEARITY: return "KINEMATICS_PERFECT_LINEARITY";
            case DetectionType::KINEMATICS_ARTIFICIAL_SMOOTHING: return "KINEMATICS_ARTIFICIAL_SMOOTHING";
            case DetectionType::HEARTBEAT_TAMPERING: return "HEARTBEAT_TAMPERING";
            case DetectionType::OVERLAY_HIJACK_DETECTED: return "OVERLAY_HIJACK_DETECTED";
            case DetectionType::RENDERING_HOOK_DETECTED: return "RENDERING_HOOK_DETECTED";
            case DetectionType::CANARY_TAMPERED: return "CANARY_TAMPERED";
            case DetectionType::PHANTOM_ENTITY_TARGETED: return "PHANTOM_ENTITY_TARGETED";
            default: return "UNKNOWN";
        }
    }

    static const char* SeverityToString(Severity s) {
        switch (s) {
            case Severity::INFO: return "INFO";
            case Severity::LOW: return "LOW";
            case Severity::MEDIUM: return "MEDIUM";
            case Severity::HIGH: return "HIGH";
            case Severity::CRITICAL: return "CRITICAL";
            case Severity::FATAL_BAN: return "FATAL_BAN";
            default: return "UNKNOWN";
        }
    }
};

    std::string secretKey{"NoEscape_Secret_Key_v2.0"};

namespace noescape::dma {

struct DmaProfile {
    double baseline_mean_cycles{0.0};
    double current_mean_cycles{0.0};
    double jitter_stddev{0.0};
    uint32_t spike_count{0};
    bool anomalous{false};

    std::string vendor_id;
    std::string description;
    bool is_suspicious{false};
};

class DmaDetector {
public:
    static DmaDetector& Instance();

    // Calibrate baseline memory bus latency
    bool Calibrate(size_t probe_size_kb = 4096, size_t iterations = 100);

    // Profile memory bus latency jitter to detect DMA memory bus polling / contention
    DmaProfile ProfileMemoryBusJitter(size_t iterations = 50);

    // Scan PCIe and USB bus for known FPGA / DMA Leech hardware
    std::vector<PciDeviceInfo> ScanHardwareDevices();

    // Check for LeechCore / shadow memory interfaces
    bool CheckLeechCoreArtifacts();

    // Runs a full DMA audit
    std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> PerformAudit();

    // Direct configuration of jitter threshold multiplier
    void SetAnomalyThreshold(double multiplier) { anomaly_multiplier_ = multiplier; }

    DmaDetector();
    ~DmaDetector();

    std::vector<uint8_t> probe_buffer_;
    double baseline_mean_cycles_{0.0};
    double baseline_variance_{0.0};
    double anomaly_multiplier_{3.2};
    bool calibrated_{false};
    std::mutex mutex_;
    uint64_t MeasureBufferTraversal();

`include/noescape/kinematics/aim_analyzer.hpp`:

```hpp
#pragma once

#include <vector>
#include <deque>
#include <optional>
#include <cstdint>
#include "noescape/core/types.hpp"

namespace noescape::kinematics {

struct AngleSample {
    float pitch{0.0f};      // Pitch in degrees [-89, 89]
    float yaw{0.0f};        // Yaw in degrees [-180, 180] or [0, 360]
    uint64_t timestamp_ms{0};
};

struct KinematicMetrics {
    float angular_velocity{0.0f};     // deg/s
    float angular_acceleration{0.0f}; // deg/s^2
    float angular_jerk{0.0f};         // deg/s^3
    float linearity_error{0.0f};      // Orthogonal deviation variance
    float velocity_variance{0.0f};    // Variance of velocity over window
};

class AimAnalyzer {
public:
    AimAnalyzer(size_t history_capacity = 64);

    // Records a new view angle observation
    std::optional<core::DetectionEvent> PushSample(float pitch, float yaw, uint64_t timestamp_ms);
    std::optional<core::DetectionEvent> PushSample(const AngleSample& sample);

    // Clears historical buffer (e.g. on teleport or round reset)
    void Reset();

    // Direct access to computed metrics of the current trajectory window
    KinematicMetrics ComputeCurrentMetrics() const;

    // Threshold setters for customization
    void SetSnapAngleThreshold(float deg) { snap_angle_threshold_ = deg; }
    void SetLinearityThreshold(float err) { linearity_threshold_ = err; }
    void SetMinSamplesForAnalysis(size_t n) { min_samples_ = n; }

private:
    std::deque<AngleSample> history_;
    size_t history_capacity_{64};
    size_t min_samples_{8};

    float snap_angle_threshold_{20.0f}; // Degrees in single tick
    float linearity_threshold_{0.005f}; // Max orthogonal variance for perfect line

    float NormalizeAngleDelta(float delta) const;
    float CalculateAngleDistance(const AngleSample& a, const AngleSample& b) const;

    std::optional<core::DetectionEvent> CheckInstantSnap();
    std::optional<core::DetectionEvent> CheckPerfectLinearity();
    std::optional<core::DetectionEvent> CheckArtificialSmoothing();
};

} // namespace noescape::kinematics

`include/noescape/kinematics/phantom_monitor.hpp`:

```hpp
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>
#include <mutex>
#include <optional>
#include <vector>
#include "noescape/core/types.hpp"

namespace noescape::kinematics {

struct PhantomEntity {
    uint32_t id{0};
    float x{0}, y{0}, z{0};
};

// Decoy-entity tripwire. The game registers invisible, never-rendered, never-networked
// "players". Memory-reading ESP/aimbots that trust entity tables will draw or aim at them.
// Legitimate players have no way to know where a phantom is, so sustained, precise aim at one
// is strong evidence. Works regardless of how the cheat reads memory (kernel, DMA, external).
class PhantomMonitor {
public:
    PhantomMonitor() = default;

    void AddPhantom(uint32_t id, float x, float y, float z);
    void UpdatePhantom(uint32_t id, float x, float y, float z);
    void Clear();
    size_t Count() const;

    // Generates `count` phantoms at random positions around `center` (reproducible via seed).
    void GenerateAround(float cx, float cy, float cz, float radius, size_t count, uint64_t seed);

    void SetToleranceDegrees(float deg) { tolerance_deg_ = deg; }
    void SetRequiredConsecutiveSamples(size_t n) { required_samples_ = n; }

    // Feed camera position and view angles (degrees, game convention: yaw about +Z, pitch up positive).
    std::optional<core::DetectionEvent> PushView(float cam_x, float cam_y, float cam_z,
                                                 float pitch_deg, float yaw_deg, uint64_t timestamp_ms);

private:
    mutable std::mutex mutex_;
    std::vector<PhantomEntity> phantoms_;
    float tolerance_deg_{0.75f};
    size_t required_samples_{12};
    uint32_t tracked_id_{0};
    size_t streak_{0};
};

} // namespace noescape::kinematics

`include/noescape/memory/canary_monitor.hpp`:

```hpp
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>
#include <cstddef>
#include <mutex>
#include <vector>
#include "noescape/core/types.hpp"

namespace noescape::memory {

// Tripwire memory: pages that nothing in the legitimate process ever writes.
// Any write by an external process (WriteProcessMemory / process_vm_writev / ptrace)
// marks the page dirty at the OS level (Windows MEM_WRITE_WATCH, Linux soft-dirty bit)
// and/or alters its random content. Reads cannot be detected from Ring-3.
class CanaryMonitor {
public:
    static CanaryMonitor& Instance();

    // Allocates guard-fenced canary pages filled with random data. Idempotent.
    bool Initialize(size_t page_count = 4);
    void Shutdown();

    // Returns events if the canary was written to since the previous check.
    std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> Check();

    // True if the OS write-tracking mechanism was verified to work at Initialize().
    bool IsWriteTrackingSupported() const { return tracking_supported_; }

    // Address/size of canary region (exposed for tests and phantom-data placement).
    uintptr_t Address() const { return reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(base_); }
    size_t Size() const { return size_; }

private:
    CanaryMonitor() = default;
    ~CanaryMonitor();

    void ResetTracking();
    bool PageWasWritten();

    std::mutex mutex_;
    uint8_t* base_{nullptr};      // first canary byte
    void* alloc_base_{nullptr};   // includes guard pages
    size_t alloc_size_{0};
    size_t size_{0};
    std::vector<uint8_t> expected_;
    bool tracking_supported_{false};
    // Opened once at Initialize(): after PR_SET_DUMPABLE=0 (AntiDebug::HideCurrentThread)
    // /proc/self/* can no longer be opened by an unprivileged process.
    int pagemap_fd_{-1};
    int clear_refs_fd_{-1};
};

} // namespace noescape::memory

```

`include/noescape/memory/cheat_signature.hpp`:

```hpp
#pragma once

#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <cstdint>
#include "noescape/core/types.hpp"
#include "noescape/memory/pattern_scanner.hpp"

namespace noescape::memory {

enum class SignatureCategory : uint32_t {
    GENERIC = 0,
    HOOK_FRAMEWORK,
    INJECTOR_STUB,
    DMA_TOOL,
    DEBUGGER_INTERFACE,
    SHELLCODE_PROLOGUE,
    OVERLAY_HOOK,
    PACKER_PROTECTOR,
    SPEEDHACK_WRAPPER
};

enum class MemoryTargetRule : uint32_t {
    UNBACKED_ONLY = 0,    // Only anonymous / unbacked executable memory
    ALL_EXECUTABLE,       // All executable memory (file-backed and unbacked)
    ANONYMOUS_DATA,       // Anonymous / unbacked readable non-executable memory
    ANY_ANONYMOUS,        // Any anonymous / unbacked memory (executable or data)
    ALL_REGIONS           // All readable memory
};

class CheatSignature : public Pattern {
public:
    CheatSignature() = default;

    CheatSignature(
        std::string_view ida_pattern,
        std::string name,
        SignatureCategory category = SignatureCategory::GENERIC,
        uint32_t threat_score = 100,
        core::Severity severity = core::Severity::FATAL_BAN,
        MemoryTargetRule target_rule = MemoryTargetRule::UNBACKED_ONLY,
        std::string description = ""
    ) : Pattern(ida_pattern, std::move(name)),
        category_(category),
        threat_score_(threat_score),
        severity_(severity),
        target_rule_(target_rule),
        description_(std::move(description)) {}

    CheatSignature(
        const uint8_t* bytes,
        const char* mask,
        std::string name,
        SignatureCategory category = SignatureCategory::GENERIC,
        uint32_t threat_score = 100,
        core::Severity severity = core::Severity::FATAL_BAN,
        MemoryTargetRule target_rule = MemoryTargetRule::UNBACKED_ONLY,
        std::string description = ""
    ) : Pattern(bytes, mask, std::move(name)),
        category_(category),
        threat_score_(threat_score),
        severity_(severity),
        target_rule_(target_rule),
        description_(std::move(description)) {}

    CheatSignature(
        const Pattern& pattern,
        SignatureCategory category = SignatureCategory::GENERIC,
        uint32_t threat_score = 100,
        core::Severity severity = core::Severity::FATAL_BAN,
        MemoryTargetRule target_rule = MemoryTargetRule::UNBACKED_ONLY,
        std::string description = ""
    ) : Pattern(pattern),
        category_(category),
        threat_score_(threat_score),
        severity_(severity),
        target_rule_(target_rule),
        description_(std::move(description)) {}

    SignatureCategory GetCategory() const { return category_; }
    uint32_t GetThreatScore() const { return threat_score_; }
    core::Severity GetSeverity() const { return severity_; }
    MemoryTargetRule GetTargetRule() const { return target_rule_; }
    const std::string& GetDescription() const { return description_; }

    void SetCategory(SignatureCategory cat) { category_ = cat; }
    void SetThreatScore(uint32_t score) { threat_score_ = score; }
    void SetSeverity(core::Severity sev) { severity_ = sev; }
    void SetTargetRule(MemoryTargetRule rule) { target_rule_ = rule; }
    void SetDescription(std::string desc) { description_ = std::move(desc); }

    static const char* CategoryToString(SignatureCategory cat);
    static const char* RuleToString(MemoryTargetRule rule);

    static bool IsTargetMatched(MemoryTargetRule rule, bool isGenuineFile, bool isExecutable, bool isReadable);

private:
    SignatureCategory category_{SignatureCategory::GENERIC};
    uint32_t threat_score_{100};
    core::Severity severity_{core::Severity::FATAL_BAN};
    MemoryTargetRule target_rule_{MemoryTargetRule::UNBACKED_ONLY};
    std::string description_;
};

} // namespace noescape::memory

`include/noescape/memory/memory_scanner.hpp`:

```hpp
#pragma once

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <cstdint>
#include <optional>
#include <map>
#include <mutex>
#include "noescape/core/types.hpp"
#include "noescape/memory/pattern_scanner.hpp"
#include "noescape/memory/cheat_signature.hpp"
#include "noescape/memory/signature_database.hpp"

namespace noescape::memory {

struct MemoryRegion {
    uintptr_t start{0};
    uintptr_t end{0};
    size_t size{0};
    bool readable{false};
    bool writable{false};
    bool executable{false};
    bool is_shared{false};
    bool is_backed_by_file{false};
    uint64_t file_offset{0};
    std::string pathname;
};

class MemoryScanner {
public:
    static MemoryScanner& Instance();

    // Enumerate all active virtual memory regions of the current process
    std::vector<MemoryRegion> QueryMemoryRegions(bool force_refresh = false);

    // Caches memory regions snapshot to eliminate redundant /proc/self/maps reads per cycle
    void RefreshCache();
    void ClearCache();
    bool IsCacheValid() const;

    // Check for unbacked executable regions (e.g. manual mapping, shellcode, RWX allocations)
    std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> DetectUnbackedExecutableRegions();

    // Scans memory regions against cheat signatures with category & target-rule filtering
    std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> ScanSignatures(const std::vector<CheatSignature>& signatures);

    // Legacy overload: scans memory regions against raw byte patterns
    std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> ScanSignatures(const std::vector<Pattern>& patterns);

    // Compares one file-backed executable mapping per call (round-robin) against its
    // on-disk image; detects module stomping / in-memory patching. Linux/ELF only.
    std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> DetectModifiedModules();

    // Detects previously-executable regions that were later made inaccessible
    // (sleep-obfuscation style payloads hiding between audit cycles).
    std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> DetectSleepingPayloads();

    // Runs a full memory audit with custom cheat signatures
    std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> PerformAudit(const std::vector<CheatSignature>& signatures);

    // Legacy overload: runs full memory audit with raw patterns
    std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> PerformAudit(const std::vector<Pattern>& patterns);

    // Runs a full memory audit using the default curated signature database
    std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> PerformAudit();

    // Helpers to retrieve default known cheat signatures
    static std::vector<CheatSignature> GetDefaultCheatSignatures();
    static std::vector<Pattern> GetDefaultCheatPatterns();

private:
    MemoryScanner() = default;

    std::vector<MemoryRegion> QueryMemoryRegionsInternal();

    mutable std::mutex cache_mutex_;
    std::vector<MemoryRegion> cached_regions_;
    bool cache_valid_{false};

    std::mutex state_mutex_;
    std::map<uintptr_t, uintptr_t> seen_exec_regions_; // start -> end
    size_t module_cursor_{0};
};

} // namespace noescape::memory

`include/noescape/memory/pattern_scanner.hpp`:

```hpp
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>
#include <cstddef>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <optional>
#include <string_view>

namespace noescape::memory {

struct PatternByte {
    uint8_t value{0};
    bool is_wildcard{false};
};

class Pattern {
public:
    Pattern() = default;
    explicit Pattern(std::string_view ida_pattern, std::string name = "");
    Pattern(const uint8_t* bytes, const char* mask, std::string name = "");

    virtual ~Pattern() = default;

    bool Empty() const { return bytes_.empty(); }
    size_t Length() const { return bytes_.size(); }
    const std::vector<PatternByte>& GetBytes() const { return bytes_; }
    const std::string& GetName() const { return name_; }

    // First non-wildcard byte and its offset for SIMD quick skipping
    bool HasNonWildcard() const { return has_non_wildcard_; }
    uint8_t FirstByte() const { return first_byte_; }
    size_t FirstByteOffset() const { return first_byte_offset_; }

private:
    std::vector<PatternByte> bytes_;
    std::string name_;
    bool has_non_wildcard_{false};
    uint8_t first_byte_{0};
    size_t first_byte_offset_{0};
};

class CheatSignature;

class PatternScanner {
public:
    // Scans a memory buffer for a single pattern
    static std::optional<uintptr_t> Scan(
        const uint8_t* data, 
        size_t size, 
        const Pattern& pattern,
        uintptr_t base_address = 0
    );

    // Scans a memory buffer for all occurrences of a pattern
    static std::vector<uintptr_t> ScanAll(
        const uint8_t* data, 
        size_t size, 
        const Pattern& pattern,
        uintptr_t base_address = 0,
        size_t max_results = 256
    );

    // Multi-pattern batch scanning in a single pass
    struct MatchResult {
        std::string pattern_name;
        uintptr_t address{0};
    };

    static std::vector<MatchResult> ScanBatch(
        const uint8_t* data, 
        size_t size, 
        const std::vector<Pattern>& patterns,
        uintptr_t base_address = 0,
        size_t max_results = 1024
    );

    static std::vector<MatchResult> ScanBatch(
        const uint8_t* data, 
        size_t size, 
        const std::vector<CheatSignature>& signatures,
        uintptr_t base_address = 0,
        size_t max_results = 1024
    );

    struct SignatureMatchResult {
        const CheatSignature* signature{nullptr};
        std::string signature_name;
        uintptr_t address{0};
    };

    static std::vector<SignatureMatchResult> ScanSignatures(
        const uint8_t* data, 
        size_t size, 
        const std::vector<CheatSignature>& signatures,
        uintptr_t base_address = 0,
        size_t max_results = 1024
    );

    static std::vector<SignatureMatchResult> ScanSignatures(
        const uint8_t* data, 
        size_t size, 
        const std::vector<const CheatSignature*>& signatures,
        uintptr_t base_address = 0,
        size_t max_results = 1024
    );

private:
    // Architecture-specific SIMD implementations
    static const uint8_t* ScanSimd(
        const uint8_t* data, 
        size_t size, 
        const Pattern& pattern
    );

    static const uint8_t* ScanScalar(
        const uint8_t* data, 
        size_t size, 
        const Pattern& pattern
    );

    static bool MatchAt(
        const uint8_t* ptr, 
        const Pattern& pattern
    );
};

} // namespace noescape::memory

#pragma once

namespace noescape::memory {

struct HookInfo {
    std::string function_name;
    uintptr_t function_address{0};
    std::string hook_type;
    uintptr_t target_destination{0};
    std::vector<uint8_t> patched_bytes;
};

    std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> AuditSystemHooks();

    bool RestoreHook(const HookInfo& hook, const uint8_t* original_bytes, size_t length);

`include/noescape/overlay/overlay_detector.hpp`:

```hpp
#pragma once

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <cstdint>
#include <optional>
#include <mutex>
#include "noescape/core/types.hpp"

namespace noescape::overlay {

struct ViewportRect {
    int32_t x{0};
    int32_t y{0};
    int32_t width{0};
    int32_t height{0};

    bool IsZero() const {
        return width <= 0 || height <= 0;
    }

    bool Overlaps(const ViewportRect& other, int tolerance = 20) const {
        if (IsZero() || other.IsZero()) return false;

        // 1. Close boundary match (within tolerance)
        bool matches = (std::abs(x - other.x) <= tolerance) &&
                       (std::abs(y - other.y) <= tolerance) &&
                       (std::abs(width - other.width) <= tolerance) &&
                       (std::abs(height - other.height) <= tolerance);
        if (matches) return true;

        // 2. Enclosing overlay (e.g. fullscreen overlay window completely enclosing the viewport)
        bool covers = (other.x <= (x + tolerance)) &&
                      (other.y <= (y + tolerance)) &&
                      ((other.x + other.width) >= (x + width - tolerance)) &&
                      ((other.y + other.height) >= (y + height - tolerance));
        if (covers) return true;

        // 3. Substantial area intersection (>= 50% of viewport covered by overlay)
        int32_t ix1 = std::max(x, other.x);
        int32_t iy1 = std::max(y, other.y);
        int32_t ix2 = std::min(x + width, other.x + other.width);
        int32_t iy2 = std::min(y + height, other.y + other.height);

        if (ix2 > ix1 && iy2 > iy1) {
            int64_t interArea = static_cast<int64_t>(ix2 - ix1) * static_cast<int64_t>(iy2 - iy1);
            int64_t vpArea = static_cast<int64_t>(width) * static_cast<int64_t>(height);
            if (vpArea > 0 && interArea * 2 >= vpArea) {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }
};

struct OverlayWindowInfo {
    std::string title;
    std::string class_name;
    uint32_t pid{0};
    uintptr_t handle{0};
    ViewportRect rect;
    uint32_t style{0};
    uint32_t ex_style{0};
    bool is_layered{false};
    bool is_transparent{false};
    bool is_topmost{false};
};

class OverlayDetector {
public:
    static OverlayDetector& Instance();

    // Configure game viewport and window
    void SetGameViewport(int32_t x, int32_t y, int32_t width, int32_t height);
    ViewportRect GetGameViewport() const;
    void SetGameWindowHandle(uintptr_t handle);
    uintptr_t GetGameWindowHandle() const;

    // Window enumeration & overlay detection
    std::vector<OverlayWindowInfo> EnumerateTopLevelWindows();
    std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> DetectSuspiciousWindows();

    // Swapchain & rendering hook detection
    std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> AuditSwapchainHooks();
    std::optional<core::DetectionEvent> InspectRenderingPointer(const char* routine_name, void* function_ptr);

    // Dynamic vtable swapchain inspection
    void RegisterSwapchainVTable(void** vtable, size_t entry_count);
    void ClearRegisteredVTables();

    // Full combined audit pass
    std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> PerformAudit();

    // Mock/Simulation support for deterministic cross-platform tests
    void AddMockWindow(const OverlayWindowInfo& window);
    void ClearMockWindows();

    // Helper: checks whether an address is within a mapped module
    bool IsAddressInLoadedModule(uintptr_t addr) const;

private:
    OverlayDetector();

    mutable std::mutex mutex_;
    ViewportRect game_viewport_{0, 0, 1920, 1080};
    uintptr_t game_window_handle_{0};
    std::vector<void**> registered_vtables_;
    std::vector<size_t> registered_vtable_sizes_;
    std::vector<OverlayWindowInfo> mock_windows_;

    bool IsSuspiciousOverlayWindow(const OverlayWindowInfo& win) const;
};

// ============================================================================
// NoEscape Anti-Cheat Engine - Standalone Open Source Edition (v7.0)
// GitHub: https://github.com/KULLANICI_ADIN/NoEscape-AntiCheat
// ============================================================================

#include <windows.h>
#include <tlhelp32.h>
#include <psapi.h>
#include <wininet.h>
#include <wintrust.h>
#include <softpub.h>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <deque>
#include <fstream>
#include <mutex>
#include <sstream>
#include <chrono>
#include <thread>
#include <atomic>
#include <cmath>
#include <optional>
#include <numeric>

#pragma comment(lib, "psapi.lib")
#pragma comment(lib, "advapi32.lib")
#pragma comment(lib, "wininet.lib")
#pragma comment(lib, "wintrust.lib")
#pragma comment(lib, "crypt32.lib")

// ============================================================================
// RAII Handle Wrapper for Safe OS Handle Management
// ============================================================================
class ScopedHandle {
    HANDLE h = nullptr;
public:
    ScopedHandle() = default;
    explicit ScopedHandle(HANDLE handle) : h(handle) {}
    ~ScopedHandle() { reset(); }
    ScopedHandle(const ScopedHandle&) = delete;
    ScopedHandle& operator=(const ScopedHandle&) = delete;
    ScopedHandle(ScopedHandle&& o) noexcept : h(o.h) { o.h = nullptr; }
    ScopedHandle& operator=(ScopedHandle&& o) noexcept {
        if (this != &o) { reset(); h = o.h; o.h = nullptr; }
        return *this;
    }
    void reset(HANDLE n = nullptr) {
        if (h && h != INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) CloseHandle(h);
        h = n;
    }
    HANDLE get() const { return h; }
    bool valid() const { return h != nullptr && h != INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE; }
    explicit operator bool() const { return valid(); }
};

static std::string ToLower(const std::string& s) {
    std::string out = s;
    for (auto& c : out) c = static_cast<char>(tolower(static_cast<unsigned char>(c)));
    return out;
}

static std::string UrlEncode(const std::string& str) {
    std::string strTemp = "";
    size_t length = str.length();
    for (size_t i = 0; i < length; i++) {
        INT ihn = (INT)str[i];
        if (isalnum((unsigned char)str[i]) || str[i] == '-' || str[i] == '_' || str[i] == '.' || str[i] == '~')
            strTemp += str[i];
        else if (str[i] == ' ')
            strTemp += "+";
        else {
            char hex[4]{};
            sprintf_s(hex, sizeof(hex), "%%%02X", ihn);
            strTemp += hex;
        }
    }
    return strTemp;
}

// ============================================================================
// HWID & Network Fingerprint Utilities
// ============================================================================
class HwidUtil {
public:
    static std::string GetMachineHWID() {
        HKEY hKey = nullptr;
        char szHwid[256] = { 0 };
        DWORD dwSize = sizeof(szHwid);
        if (RegOpenKeyExA(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, "SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Cryptography", 0, KEY_READ | KEY_WOW64_64KEY, &hKey) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
            RegQueryValueExA(hKey, "MachineGuid", nullptr, nullptr, (LPBYTE)szHwid, &dwSize);
            RegCloseKey(hKey);
        }
        return strlen(szHwid) == 0 ? "UNKNOWN-HWID-DEVICE" : std::string(szHwid);
    }
};

class NetworkUtil {
public:
    static std::string GetPublicIP() {
        HINTERNET hInternet = InternetOpenA("NoEscapeAntiCheat", INTERNET_OPEN_TYPE_DIRECT, nullptr, nullptr, 0);
        if (!hInternet) return "UNKNOWN_IP";

        HINTERNET hConnect = InternetOpenUrlA(hInternet, "https://api.ipify.org", nullptr, 0, INTERNET_FLAG_RELOAD, 0);
        std::string ip = "UNKNOWN_IP";
        
        if (hConnect) {
            char buffer[128]{};
            DWORD bytesRead = 0;
            if (InternetReadFile(hConnect, buffer, sizeof(buffer) - 1, &bytesRead) && bytesRead > 0) {
                buffer[bytesRead] = '\0';
                ip = buffer;
            }
            InternetCloseHandle(hConnect);
        }
        InternetCloseHandle(hInternet);
        return ip;
    }
};

// ============================================================================
// Panel REST API Client (HWID & IP Banning System)
// ============================================================================
class PanelClient {
    std::string baseUrl;
public:
    explicit PanelClient(std::string url) : baseUrl(std::move(url)) {}

    bool SendBanRequest(const std::string& hwid, const std::string& ip, const std::string& reason) const {
        HINTERNET hInternet = InternetOpenA("NoEscapeAntiCheat", INTERNET_OPEN_TYPE_DIRECT, nullptr, nullptr, 0);
        if (!hInternet) return false;

        std::string encodedReason = UrlEncode(reason);
        std::string fullUrl = baseUrl + "/api/ban-hwid.php?hwid=" + hwid + "&ip=" + ip + "&reason=" + encodedReason;

        HINTERNET hConnect = InternetOpenUrlA(hInternet, fullUrl.c_str(), nullptr, 0, INTERNET_FLAG_RELOAD, 0);
        if (hConnect) {
            InternetCloseHandle(hConnect);
        }
        InternetCloseHandle(hInternet);
        return true;
    }

    bool CheckIfBanned(const std::string& hwid, const std::string& ip, std::string& outDetails) const {
        HINTERNET hInternet = InternetOpenA("NoEscapeAntiCheat", INTERNET_OPEN_TYPE_DIRECT, nullptr, nullptr, 0);
        if (!hInternet) return false;

        std::string fullUrl = baseUrl + "/api/check-hwid.php?hwid=" + hwid + "&ip=" + ip;
        HINTERNET hConnect = InternetOpenUrlA(hInternet, fullUrl.c_str(), nullptr, 0, INTERNET_FLAG_RELOAD, 0);

        bool isBanned = false;
        if (hConnect) {
            char buffer[1024]{};
            DWORD bytesRead = 0;
            if (InternetReadFile(hConnect, buffer, sizeof(buffer) - 1, &bytesRead) && bytesRead > 0) {
                buffer[bytesRead] = '\0';
                std::string response(buffer);
                if (response.find("banned:1") != std::string::npos) {
                    isBanned = true;
                    outDetails = response;
                }
            }
            InternetCloseHandle(hConnect);
        }
        InternetCloseHandle(hInternet);
        return isBanned;
    }
};

// ============================================================================
// Anti-Cheat Configuration Signatures & Thresholds
// ============================================================================
struct Config {
    std::vector<std::string> processSignatures;
    std::vector<std::string> moduleSignatures;
    std::vector<std::string> driverSignatures;

    std::string panelApiUrl = "http://localhost/noescape_panel";

    static Config Default() {
        Config cfg;
        cfg.processSignatures = {
            "vape", "wurst", "meteor", "raven", "astolfo", "liquidbounce", "impact", "aristois",
            "cheatengine", "x64dbg", "ollydbg", "wireshark", "processhacker", "killaura", "aimbot"
        };
        cfg.moduleSignatures = {
            "vape", "wurst", "meteor", "impact", "aristois", "liquidbounce", "killaura", "aimbot", "xray"
        };
        cfg.driverSignatures = { "dbk64.sys", "ksdumper.sys", "winring0x64.sys" };
        return cfg;
    }
};

// ============================================================================
// Privilege Escalation & Scanners (Process, Module, Driver)
// ============================================================================
static bool EnableDebugPrivilege() {
    HANDLE tokenRaw = nullptr;
    if (!OpenProcessToken(GetCurrentProcess(), TOKEN_ADJUST_PRIVILEGES | TOKEN_QUERY, &tokenRaw)) return false;
    ScopedHandle token(tokenRaw);
    LUID luid{};
    if (!LookupPrivilegeValueA(nullptr, "SeDebugPrivilege", &luid)) return false;
    TOKEN_PRIVILEGES tp{};
    tp.PrivilegeCount = 1;
    tp.Privileges[0].Luid = luid;
    tp.Privileges[0].Attributes = SE_PRIVILEGE_ENABLED;
    if (!AdjustTokenPrivileges(token.get(), FALSE, &tp, sizeof(tp), nullptr, nullptr)) return false;
    return true;
}

class ProcessUtil {
public:
    static std::string GetProcessPath(DWORD pid) {
        char path[MAX_PATH]{};
        ScopedHandle hProc(OpenProcess(PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION, FALSE, pid));
        if (hProc) {
            DWORD size = MAX_PATH;
            if (QueryFullProcessImageNameA(hProc.get(), 0, path, &size)) {
                return std::string(path);
            }
        }
        return "";
    }

    static bool IsFileDigitallySigned(const std::string& filePath) {
        if (filePath.empty()) return false;
        int len = MultiByteToWideChar(CP_UTF8, 0, filePath.c_str(), -1, NULL, 0);
        std::wstring wPath(len, 0);
        MultiByteToWideChar(CP_UTF8, 0, filePath.c_str(), -1, &wPath[0], len);

        WINTRUST_FILE_INFO fileInfo{};
        fileInfo.cbStruct = sizeof(fileInfo);
        fileInfo.pcwszFilePath = wPath.c_str();

        WINTRUST_DATA trustData{};
        trustData.cbStruct = sizeof(trustData);
        trustData.dwUIChoice = WTD_UI_NONE;
        trustData.fdwRevocationChecks = WTD_REVOKE_NONE;
        trustData.dwUnionChoice = WTD_CHOICE_FILE;
        trustData.dwStateAction = WTD_STATEACTION_VERIFY;
        trustData.pFile = &fileInfo;

        GUID policyGUID = WINTRUST_ACTION_GENERIC_VERIFY_V2;
        LONG lStatus = WinVerifyTrust(static_cast<HWND>(INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE), &policyGUID, &trustData);

        trustData.dwStateAction = WTD_STATEACTION_CLOSE;
        WinVerifyTrust(static_cast<HWND>(INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE), &policyGUID, &trustData);

        return (lStatus == ERROR_SUCCESS);
    }

    static bool FindSuspiciousProcess(const std::vector<std::string>& sigs, std::string& outMatch, DWORD& outPid) {
        ScopedHandle snap(CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(TH32CS_SNAPPROCESS, 0));
        if (!snap) return false;
        PROCESSENTRY32W pe{}; pe.dwSize = sizeof(pe);
        if (Process32FirstW(snap.get(), &pe)) {
            do {
                char narrow[MAX_PATH]{};
                if (WideCharToMultiByte(CP_UTF8, 0, pe.szExeFile, -1, narrow, MAX_PATH, nullptr, nullptr) <= 0) continue;
                std::string name = ToLower(narrow);

                bool matchesSig = false;
                for (const auto& sig : sigs) {
                    if (!sig.empty() && name.find(ToLower(sig)) != std::string::npos) {
                        matchesSig = true;
                        break;
                    }
                }

                if (matchesSig) {
                    std::string processPath = GetProcessPath(pe.th32ProcessID);
                    std::string lowerPath = ToLower(processPath);

                    if (processPath.empty()) continue;
                    if (lowerPath.find("\\windows\\") != std::string::npos || 
                        lowerPath.find("discord") != std::string::npos || 
                        lowerPath.find("steam") != std::string::npos) {
                        continue;
                    }

                    if (IsFileDigitallySigned(processPath)) continue;

                    outMatch = narrow;
                    outPid = pe.th32ProcessID;
                    return true;
                }
            } while (Process32NextW(snap.get(), &pe));
        }
        return false;
    }
};

// ============================================================================
// Core Standalone Anti-Cheat Engine Controller
// ============================================================================
class AntiCheatEngine {
    Config cfg;
    PanelClient panel;
    std::string hwid;
    std::string publicIp;
public:
    explicit AntiCheatEngine(Config c) : cfg(c), panel(c.panelApiUrl), 
                                         hwid(HwidUtil::GetMachineHWID()), publicIp(NetworkUtil::GetPublicIP()) {}

    [[noreturn]] void BanAndTerminate(const std::string& reason) const {
        panel.SendBanRequest(hwid, publicIp, reason);

        MessageBoxA(nullptr, ("NoEscape Security Violation\n\nReason: " + reason + "\nDevice and IP have been banned.").c_str(), "NoEscape Anti-Cheat - Standalone", MB_OK | MB_ICONERROR | MB_TOPMOST);
        ExitProcess(1);
    }

    void RunLoop() {
        auto lastApiCheck = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - std::chrono::seconds(10);

        while (true) {
            auto now = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
            if (std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::seconds>(now - lastApiCheck).count() >= 10) {
                std::string banDetails;
                if (panel.CheckIfBanned(hwid, publicIp, banDetails)) {
                    BanAndTerminate("Global Ban List Match (HWID/IP)");
                }
                lastApiCheck = now;
            }

            std::string detail; DWORD pid = 0;
            if (ProcessUtil::FindSuspiciousProcess(cfg.processSignatures, detail, pid)) {
                BanAndTerminate("Blacklisted Process Signature: " + detail);
            }

            std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(500));
        }
    }
};

int main() {
    if (HWND hwnd = GetConsoleWindow()) ShowWindow(hwnd, SW_HIDE);
    EnableDebugPrivilege();

    Config cfg = Config::Default();
    AntiCheatEngine engine(cfg);
    engine.RunLoop();

    return 0;
}

`main.cpp`:

```cpp
// ============================================================================
// NoEscape Anti-Cheat Engine - Standalone Open Source Edition (v7.0)
// Next-Generation C++20 Ring-3 Defense System
// ============================================================================

#include "noescape/core/engine.hpp"
#include "noescape/core/logger.hpp"
#include <iostream>
#include <csignal>
#include <atomic>
#include <thread>
#include <chrono>

#if defined(_WIN32)
#include <windows.h>
#endif

static std::atomic<bool> g_running{true};

void SignalHandler(int signum) {
    (void)signum;
    g_running = false;
}

void PrintBanner() {
    std::cout << "\033[1;36m"
              << "====================================================================\n"
              << "     _   _       _____                         \n"
              << "    | \\ | |     |  ___|                        \n"
              << "    |  \\| | ___ | |__   ___  ___ __ _ _ __   ___ \n"
              << "    | . ` |/ _ \\|  __| / __|/ __/ _` | '_ \\ / _ \\\n"
              << "    | |\\  | (_) | |___ \\__ \\ (_| (_| | |_) |  __/\n"
              << "    |_| \\_|\\___/\\____/ |___/\\___\\__,_| .__/ \\___|\n"
              << "                                     | |         \n"
              << "     Anti-Cheat System (v7.0 - C++20)|_|         \n"
              << "====================================================================\n"
              << "\033[0m" << std::endl;
}

int main(int argc, char* argv[]) {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    // Optional: Hide console window in stealth mode
    // if (HWND hwnd = GetConsoleWindow()) ShowWindow(hwnd, SW_HIDE);
#endif

    std::signal(SIGINT, SignalHandler);
    std::signal(SIGTERM, SignalHandler);

    PrintBanner();

    noescape::core::EngineConfig cfg;
    cfg.enforceBanOnViolation = true;
    cfg.banScoreThreshold = 100;
    cfg.panelUrl = "http://127.0.0.1:8080/api/ac";
    cfg.showBanUi = true;

    noescape::core::AntiCheatEngine engine(cfg);

    engine.SetBanCallback([](const std::string& reason, uint32_t finalScore) {
        std::cout << "\n\033[1;41;37m [BAN ENFORCED] " << reason 
                  << " (Final Threat Score: " << finalScore << ") \033[0m\n\n";
    });

    if (!engine.Initialize()) {
        std::cerr << "Failed to initialize AntiCheat engine.\n";
        return 1;
    }

    noescape::core::Logger::Instance().Info("Running initial synchronous security audit...");
    auto initialEvents = engine.AuditCycle();
    noescape::core::Logger::Instance().Info("Initial audit completed with " + 
                                           std::to_string(initialEvents.size()) + " detection events.");

    if (argc > 1 && std::string(argv[1]) == "--once") {
        return engine.IsBanned() ? 1 : 0;
    }

    noescape::core::Logger::Instance().Info("Starting real-time continuous background monitoring. Press Ctrl+C to stop.");
    engine.Start();

    while (g_running && !engine.IsBanned()) {
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(250));
    }

    noescape::core::Logger::Instance().Info("Shutting down NoEscape Anti-Cheat Engine...");
    engine.Stop();

    return 0;
}
