`src/core/blackbox.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "noescape/core/blackbox.hpp"
#include "noescape/network/crypto_util.hpp"
#include "noescape/core/logger.hpp"

#include <fstream>
#include <sstream>
#include <iomanip>
#include <cstring>
#include <ctime>
#include <chrono>
#include <set>

#if defined(_WIN32)
#include <windows.h>
#include <psapi.h>
#else
#include <execinfo.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#endif

namespace noescape::core {

namespace {

void PutU16(std::vector<uint8_t>& buf, uint16_t v) {
    buf.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>(v & 0xFF));
    buf.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>((v >> 8) & 0xFF));
}

void PutU32(std::vector<uint8_t>& buf, uint32_t v) {
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
        buf.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>((v >> (i * 8)) & 0xFF));
    }
}

void PutU64(std::vector<uint8_t>& buf, uint64_t v) {
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
        buf.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>((v >> (i * 8)) & 0xFF));
    }
}

void PutFloat(std::vector<uint8_t>& buf, float f) {
    uint32_t v = 0;
    std::memcpy(&v, &f, sizeof(v));
    PutU32(buf, v);
}

void PutString(std::vector<uint8_t>& buf, const std::string& str) {
    uint16_t len = static_cast<uint16_t>(std::min<size_t>(str.size(), 65535));
    PutU16(buf, len);
    buf.insert(buf.end(), str.data(), str.data() + len);
}

bool GetU16(const uint8_t*& ptr, size_t& remain, uint16_t& v) {
    if (remain < 2) return false;
    v = static_cast<uint16_t>(ptr[0]) | (static_cast<uint16_t>(ptr[1]) << 8);
    ptr += 2; remain -= 2;
    return true;
}

bool GetU32(const uint8_t*& ptr, size_t& remain, uint32_t& v) {
    if (remain < 4) return false;
    v = static_cast<uint32_t>(ptr[0]) |
        (static_cast<uint32_t>(ptr[1]) << 8) |
        (static_cast<uint32_t>(ptr[2]) << 16) |
        (static_cast<uint32_t>(ptr[3]) << 24);
    ptr += 4; remain -= 4;
    return true;
}

bool GetU64(const uint8_t*& ptr, size_t& remain, uint64_t& v) {
    if (remain < 8) return false;
    v = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
        v |= (static_cast<uint64_t>(ptr[i]) << (i * 8));
    }
    ptr += 8; remain -= 8;
    return true;
}

bool GetFloat(const uint8_t*& ptr, size_t& remain, float& f) {
    uint32_t v = 0;
    if (!GetU32(ptr, remain, v)) return false;
    std::memcpy(&f, &v, sizeof(f));
    return true;
}

bool GetString(const uint8_t*& ptr, size_t& remain, std::string& str) {
    uint16_t len = 0;
    if (!GetU16(ptr, remain, len)) return false;
    if (remain < len) return false;
    str.assign(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(ptr), len);
    ptr += len; remain -= len;
    return true;
}

std::vector<uint8_t> HexToBytes(const std::string& hex) {
    std::vector<uint8_t> bytes;
    for (size_t i = 0; i + 1 < hex.size(); i += 2) {
        std::string byteStr = hex.substr(i, 2);
        uint8_t b = static_cast<uint8_t>(std::strtoul(byteStr.c_str(), nullptr, 16));
        bytes.push_back(b);
    }
    return bytes;
}

} // namespace

BlackboxDumper& BlackboxDumper::Instance() {
    static BlackboxDumper* instance = new BlackboxDumper();
    return *instance;
}

BlackboxDumper::BlackboxDumper() : trail_capacity_(64) {}

void BlackboxDumper::RecordInputSample(float pitch, float yaw, uint64_t timestamp_ms) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    if (input_trail_.size() >= trail_capacity_) {
        input_trail_.pop_front();
    }
    input_trail_.push_back({pitch, yaw, timestamp_ms});
}

void BlackboxDumper::RecordInputSample(const kinematics::AngleSample& sample) {
    RecordInputSample(sample.pitch, sample.yaw, sample.timestamp_ms);
}

void BlackboxDumper::ClearInputTrail() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    input_trail_.clear();
}

size_t BlackboxDumper::GetInputTrailCount() const {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    return input_trail_.size();
}

std::vector<uintptr_t> BlackboxDumper::CaptureBacktrace(size_t max_depth) {
    std::vector<uintptr_t> addrs;
    if (max_depth == 0) return addrs;

#if defined(_WIN32)
    std::vector<void*> buffer(max_depth);
    USHORT frames = CaptureStackBackTrace(0, static_cast<DWORD>(max_depth), buffer.data(), nullptr);
    for (USHORT i = 0; i < frames; ++i) {
        addrs.push_back(reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(buffer[i]));
    }
#else
    std::vector<void*> buffer(max_depth);
    int frames = backtrace(buffer.data(), static_cast<int>(max_depth));
    for (int i = 0; i < frames; ++i) {
        addrs.push_back(reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(buffer[i]));
    }
#endif

    return addrs;
}

std::vector<std::string> BlackboxDumper::SampleLoadedModules(size_t max_count) {
    std::vector<std::string> modules;
    std::set<std::string> uniquePaths;

#if defined(_WIN32)
    HMODULE hMods[1024];
    HANDLE hProcess = GetCurrentProcess();
    DWORD cbNeeded = 0;
    if (EnumProcessModules(hProcess, hMods, sizeof(hMods), &cbNeeded)) {
        size_t count = cbNeeded / sizeof(HMODULE);
        for (size_t i = 0; i < count && modules.size() < max_count; ++i) {
            char szModName[MAX_PATH];
            if (GetModuleFileNameExA(hProcess, hMods[i], szModName, sizeof(szModName))) {
                if (uniquePaths.insert(szModName).second) {
                    modules.emplace_back(szModName);
                }
            }
        }
    }
#else
    std::ifstream maps("/proc/self/maps");
    if (maps.is_open()) {
        std::string line;
        while (std::getline(maps, line) && modules.size() < max_count) {
            size_t slashPos = line.find('/');
            if (slashPos != std::string::npos) {
                std::string path = line.substr(slashPos);
                if (uniquePaths.insert(path).second) {
                    modules.push_back(path);
                }
            }
        }
    }
#endif

    return modules;
}

BlackboxRecord BlackboxDumper::CaptureSnapshot(
    const std::string& component,
    const std::string& reason,
    uint32_t score,
    uint32_t violation_type
) {
    BlackboxRecord rec;
    rec.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(std::time(nullptr));
    rec.hwid = network::CryptoUtil::GetMachineHWID();
    rec.violating_component = component;
    rec.violation_reason = reason;
    rec.threat_score = score;
    rec.violation_type = violation_type;

    rec.backtrace_addrs = CaptureBacktrace(32);
    rec.loaded_modules_sample = SampleLoadedModules(16);

    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        rec.recent_input_trail.assign(input_trail_.begin(), input_trail_.end());
    }

    return rec;
}

std::vector<uint8_t> BlackboxDumper::SerializeAndEncrypt(
    const BlackboxRecord& record,
    const std::string& secret_key
) {
    // 1. Serialize plaintext payload
    std::vector<uint8_t> plaintext;
    PutU64(plaintext, record.timestamp);
    PutU32(plaintext, record.threat_score);
    PutU32(plaintext, record.violation_type);
    PutString(plaintext, record.hwid);
    PutString(plaintext, record.violating_component);
    PutString(plaintext, record.violation_reason);

    // Backtrace addresses
    PutU16(plaintext, static_cast<uint16_t>(record.backtrace_addrs.size()));
    for (uintptr_t addr : record.backtrace_addrs) {
        PutU64(plaintext, static_cast<uint64_t>(addr));
    }

    // Recent input trail
    PutU16(plaintext, static_cast<uint16_t>(record.recent_input_trail.size()));
    for (const auto& sample : record.recent_input_trail) {
        PutFloat(plaintext, sample.pitch);
        PutFloat(plaintext, sample.yaw);
        PutU64(plaintext, sample.timestamp_ms);
    }

    // Loaded modules sample
    PutU16(plaintext, static_cast<uint16_t>(record.loaded_modules_sample.size()));
    for (const auto& mod : record.loaded_modules_sample) {
        PutString(plaintext, mod);
    }

    // 2. Generate 16-byte IV
    uint64_t nowNs = static_cast<uint64_t>(std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now().time_since_epoch().count());
    std::string ivSeed = network::CryptoUtil::Sha256(secret_key + ":" + std::to_string(nowNs) + ":" + record.hwid);
    std::vector<uint8_t> iv = HexToBytes(ivSeed.substr(0, 32));
    if (iv.size() < 16) {
        iv.resize(16, 0xA5);
    }

    // 3. Keystream CTR encryption using SHA-256 blocks
    std::string ivStr(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(iv.data()), 16);
    std::string derivedKey = network::CryptoUtil::Sha256(secret_key + ivStr);

    std::vector<uint8_t> ciphertext = plaintext;
    size_t offset = 0;
    uint32_t blockCounter = 0;

    while (offset < ciphertext.size()) {
        std::string blockHashHex = network::CryptoUtil::Sha256(derivedKey + ":" + std::to_string(blockCounter++));
        std::vector<uint8_t> blockKey = HexToBytes(blockHashHex);

        for (size_t b = 0; b < blockKey.size() && offset < ciphertext.size(); ++b, ++offset) {
            ciphertext[offset] ^= blockKey[b];
        }
    }

    // 4. Compute HMAC tag over (IV + ciphertext)
    std::string signPayload = ivStr;
    signPayload.append(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(ciphertext.data()), ciphertext.size());
    std::string hmacTag = network::CryptoUtil::HmacSha256(secret_key, signPayload);

    // 5. Build final packet:
    // [Magic: 4B "NEBB"] [Version: 2B (1)] [IV: 16B] [CipherLen: 4B] [Ciphertext: N] [HMAC Tag: 64B hex]
    std::vector<uint8_t> packet;
    packet.push_back('N');
    packet.push_back('E');
    packet.push_back('B');
    packet.push_back('B');

    PutU16(packet, 1); // version
    packet.insert(packet.end(), iv.begin(), iv.end());
    PutU32(packet, static_cast<uint32_t>(ciphertext.size()));
    packet.insert(packet.end(), ciphertext.begin(), ciphertext.end());
    packet.insert(packet.end(), hmacTag.begin(), hmacTag.end());

    return packet;
}

bool BlackboxDumper::DecryptAndDeserialize(
    const std::vector<uint8_t>& encrypted_data,
    const std::string& secret_key,
    BlackboxRecord& out_record
) {
    // Header checks: 4 (Magic) + 2 (Version) + 16 (IV) + 4 (CipherLen) + 64 (HMAC) = 90 bytes minimum
    constexpr size_t kMinHeader = 4 + 2 + 16 + 4 + 64;
    if (encrypted_data.size() < kMinHeader) {
        return false;
    }

    if (encrypted_data[0] != 'N' || encrypted_data[1] != 'E' ||
        encrypted_data[2] != 'B' || encrypted_data[3] != 'B') {
        return false;
    }

    const uint8_t* ptr = encrypted_data.data() + 4;
    size_t remain = encrypted_data.size() - 4;

    uint16_t version = 0;
    if (!GetU16(ptr, remain, version) || version != 1) {
        return false;
    }

    if (remain < 16 + 4 + 64) return false;
    std::vector<uint8_t> iv(ptr, ptr + 16);
    ptr += 16; remain -= 16;

    uint32_t cipherLen = 0;
    if (!GetU32(ptr, remain, cipherLen)) return false;

    if (remain < cipherLen + 64) return false;
    std::vector<uint8_t> ciphertext(ptr, ptr + cipherLen);
    ptr += cipherLen; remain -= cipherLen;

    std::string providedTag(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(ptr), 64);
    ptr += 64; remain -= 64;

    // Verify HMAC-SHA256 signature
    std::string signPayload(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(iv.data()), 16);
    signPayload.append(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(ciphertext.data()), ciphertext.size());
    std::string expectedTag = network::CryptoUtil::HmacSha256(secret_key, signPayload);

    if (providedTag != expectedTag) {
        Logger::Instance().Error("Blackbox decryption failed: HMAC signature mismatch (Tampered or Wrong Key)");
        return false;
    }

    // Decrypt ciphertext using stream keystream
    std::string ivStr(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(iv.data()), 16);
    std::string derivedKey = network::CryptoUtil::Sha256(secret_key + ivStr);

    std::vector<uint8_t> plaintext = ciphertext;
    size_t offset = 0;
    uint32_t blockCounter = 0;

    while (offset < plaintext.size()) {
        std::string blockHashHex = network::CryptoUtil::Sha256(derivedKey + ":" + std::to_string(blockCounter++));
        std::vector<uint8_t> blockKey = HexToBytes(blockHashHex);

        for (size_t b = 0; b < blockKey.size() && offset < plaintext.size(); ++b, ++offset) {
            plaintext[offset] ^= blockKey[b];
        }
    }

    // Deserialize plaintext fields
    const uint8_t* pPlain = plaintext.data();
    size_t pRemain = plaintext.size();

    if (!GetU64(pPlain, pRemain, out_record.timestamp)) return false;
    if (!GetU32(pPlain, pRemain, out_record.threat_score)) return false;
    if (!GetU32(pPlain, pRemain, out_record.violation_type)) return false;
    if (!GetString(pPlain, pRemain, out_record.hwid)) return false;
    if (!GetString(pPlain, pRemain, out_record.violating_component)) return false;
    if (!GetString(pPlain, pRemain, out_record.violation_reason)) return false;

    // Backtrace
    uint16_t btCount = 0;
    if (!GetU16(pPlain, pRemain, btCount)) return false;
    out_record.backtrace_addrs.clear();
    for (uint16_t i = 0; i < btCount; ++i) {
        uint64_t addr = 0;
        if (!GetU64(pPlain, pRemain, addr)) return false;
        out_record.backtrace_addrs.push_back(static_cast<uintptr_t>(addr));
    }

    // Input trail
    uint16_t trailCount = 0;
    if (!GetU16(pPlain, pRemain, trailCount)) return false;
    out_record.recent_input_trail.clear();
    for (uint16_t i = 0; i < trailCount; ++i) {
        kinematics::AngleSample s;
        if (!GetFloat(pPlain, pRemain, s.pitch)) return false;
        if (!GetFloat(pPlain, pRemain, s.yaw)) return false;
        if (!GetU64(pPlain, pRemain, s.timestamp_ms)) return false;
        out_record.recent_input_trail.push_back(s);
    }

    // Loaded modules
    uint16_t modCount = 0;
    if (!GetU16(pPlain, pRemain, modCount)) return false;
    out_record.loaded_modules_sample.clear();
    for (uint16_t i = 0; i < modCount; ++i) {
        std::string mod;
        if (!GetString(pPlain, pRemain, mod)) return false;
        out_record.loaded_modules_sample.push_back(mod);
    }

    return true;
}

bool BlackboxDumper::SaveDumpToFile(const std::string& file_path, const std::vector<uint8_t>& encrypted_data) {
    std::ofstream ofs(file_path, std::ios::binary);
    if (!ofs.is_open()) return false;
    ofs.write(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(encrypted_data.data()), encrypted_data.size());
    return ofs.good();
}

void BlackboxDumper::SetDumpDirectory(const std::string& dir) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    dump_dir_ = dir;
}

std::string BlackboxDumper::GetDumpDirectory() const {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    return dump_dir_;
}

std::string BlackboxDumper::GenerateDumpFilename() const {
    uint64_t now = static_cast<uint64_t>(std::time(nullptr));
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    std::string base = "noescape_blackbox_" + std::to_string(now) + ".dmp";
    if (dump_dir_.empty()) {
        return base;
    }
    if (dump_dir_.back() == '/' || dump_dir_.back() == '\\') {
        return dump_dir_ + base;
    }
    return dump_dir_ + "/" + base;
}

} // namespace noescape::core

```

`src/core/engine.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "noescape/core/engine.hpp"
#include "noescape/security/anti_debug.hpp"
#include "noescape/security/integrity.hpp"
#include "noescape/security/timing.hpp"
#include "noescape/memory/memory_scanner.hpp"
#include "noescape/memory/unhooker.hpp"
#include "noescape/memory/canary_monitor.hpp"
#include "noescape/forensics/process_analyzer.hpp"
#include "noescape/forensics/artifact_analyzer.hpp"
#include "noescape/dma/dma_detector.hpp"
#include "noescape/overlay/overlay_detector.hpp"
#include "noescape/core/blackbox.hpp"

#include <chrono>
#include <thread>
#include <random>
#include <cstring>
#include <cstdlib>

#if defined(_WIN32)
#include <windows.h>
#else
#include <sys/types.h>
#include <sys/wait.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#include <fcntl.h>
#endif

namespace noescape::core {

namespace {

uint64_t GetSteadyNowMs() {
    return static_cast<uint64_t>(
        std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(
            std::chrono::steady_clock::now().time_since_epoch()
        ).count()
    );
}

} // namespace

AntiCheatEngine::AntiCheatEngine(EngineConfig config)
    : config_(std::move(config)),
      thread_pool_(2),
      panel_client_(config_.panelUrl, config_.secretKey),
      aim_analyzer_(64) {}

AntiCheatEngine::~AntiCheatEngine() {
    Stop();
}

bool AntiCheatEngine::Initialize() {
    Logger::Instance().Info("Initializing NoEscape Anti-Cheat Engine v7.0 (C++20 Unified Ring-3 Architecture)...");

    // Must run before HideCurrentThread(): it opens /proc/self handles that become
    // inaccessible once the process is marked non-dumpable.
    if (config_.enableMemoryScan) {
        memory::CanaryMonitor::Instance().Initialize();
        default_signatures_ = memory::MemoryScanner::GetDefaultCheatSignatures();
    }

    if (config_.enableAntiDebug) {
        security::AntiDebug::HideCurrentThread();
    }

    if (config_.enableIntegrityScan) {
        if (security::IntegrityChecker::Instance().Initialize()) {
            Logger::Instance().Info("Self-integrity baseline initialized (Text hash: " + 
                                   security::IntegrityChecker::Instance().GetBaselineHash().substr(0, 16) + "...)");
        } else {
            Logger::Instance().Warn("Failed to initialize self-integrity baseline");
        }
    }

    if (config_.enableSpeedhackDetection) {
        if (security::TimingIntegrity::Instance().Calibrate(30)) {
            Logger::Instance().Info("Timing TSC baseline calibrated (" + 
                                   std::to_string(static_cast<uint64_t>(security::TimingIntegrity::Instance().GetCyclesPerMs())) + " cycles/ms)");
        }
    }

    if (config_.enableDmaDetection) {
        dma::DmaDetector::Instance().Calibrate(512, 10);
    }

    if (config_.enableBlackboxDump && !config_.blackboxDumpDirectory.empty()) {
        BlackboxDumper::Instance().SetDumpDirectory(config_.blackboxDumpDirectory);
    }

    Logger::Instance().Info("Hardware ID Fingerprint: " + panel_client_.GetHwid());
    return true;
}

std::vector<DetectionEvent> AntiCheatEngine::VerifyPanelStatus() {
    std::vector<DetectionEvent> events;
    auto res = panel_client_.QueryBanStatus();
    using network::BanCheckStatus;

    if (res.status == BanCheckStatus::BANNED) {
        DetectionEvent ev;
        ev.type = DetectionType::HEARTBEAT_TAMPERING;
        ev.severity = Severity::FATAL_BAN;
        ev.component = "PanelClient::GlobalBanList";
        ev.details = res.reason;
        ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(time(nullptr));
        ev.threat_score = 100;
        events.push_back(ev);
        panel_failures_ = 0;
    } else if (res.status == BanCheckStatus::CLEAN) {
        panel_failures_ = 0;
    } else {
        // Unreachable backend or bad signature: a forged reply is far more suspicious than silence
        ++panel_failures_;
        bool forged = (res.status == BanCheckStatus::SIGNATURE_INVALID);
        if (forged || panel_failures_ >= 5) {
            DetectionEvent ev;
            ev.type = DetectionType::HEARTBEAT_TAMPERING;
            ev.severity = forged ? Severity::HIGH : Severity::MEDIUM;
            ev.component = "PanelClient::Verification";
            ev.details = res.reason + " (consecutive failures: " + std::to_string(panel_failures_) + ")";
            ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(time(nullptr));
            ev.threat_score = forged ? 60 : 20;
            events.push_back(ev);
            if (!forged) panel_failures_ = 0; // re-arm: at most one scored event per 5 failed checks
        }
    }

    for (const auto& ev : events) ProcessDetection(ev);
    return events;
}

std::vector<DetectionEvent> AntiCheatEngine::AuditCycle() {
    std::vector<DetectionEvent> cycleEvents;
    if (!running_.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) return cycleEvents;

    // Cache memory maps snapshot for the entire audit cycle to eliminate 25+ redundant file reads
    memory::MemoryScanner::Instance().RefreshCache();
    struct AuditCycleCacheGuard {
        ~AuditCycleCacheGuard() {
            memory::MemoryScanner::Instance().ClearCache();
        }
    } cycleCacheGuard;

    UpdateAuditHeartbeat();

    // 1. Anti-Debug Audit
    if (config_.enableAntiDebug) {
        auto adEvents = security::AntiDebug::PerformAudit(config_.blacklistedProcesses);
        cycleEvents.insert(cycleEvents.end(), adEvents.begin(), adEvents.end());
    }
    if (!running_.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) return cycleEvents;
    UpdateAuditHeartbeat();

    // 2. Self-Integrity Check
    if (config_.enableIntegrityScan) {
        auto integEvent = security::IntegrityChecker::Instance().VerifyIntegrity();
        if (integEvent) {
            cycleEvents.push_back(*integEvent);
        }
    }
    if (!running_.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) return cycleEvents;
    UpdateAuditHeartbeat();

    // 3. Timing / Speedhack Check
    if (config_.enableSpeedhackDetection) {
        auto speedEvent = security::TimingIntegrity::Instance().CheckSpeedhack();
        if (speedEvent) {
            cycleEvents.push_back(*speedEvent);
        }
    }
    if (!running_.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) return cycleEvents;
    UpdateAuditHeartbeat();

    // 4. Memory Scanner (Unbacked executable memory + signature scan)
    if (config_.enableMemoryScan) {
        auto memEvents = memory::MemoryScanner::Instance().PerformAudit(default_signatures_);
        cycleEvents.insert(cycleEvents.end(), memEvents.begin(), memEvents.end());
    }
    if (!running_.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) return cycleEvents;
    UpdateAuditHeartbeat();

    // 4b. Tripwire (canary) memory: detects external memory writes
    if (config_.enableMemoryScan) {
        auto canaryEvents = memory::CanaryMonitor::Instance().Check();
        cycleEvents.insert(cycleEvents.end(), canaryEvents.begin(), canaryEvents.end());
    }
    if (!running_.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) return cycleEvents;
    UpdateAuditHeartbeat();

    // 5. Inline Unhooker Audit
    if (config_.enableUnhooker) {
        auto hookEvents = memory::Unhooker::Instance().AuditSystemHooks();
        cycleEvents.insert(cycleEvents.end(), hookEvents.begin(), hookEvents.end());
    }
    if (!running_.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) return cycleEvents;
    UpdateAuditHeartbeat();

    // 6. Forensics Process & Environment Audit
    if (config_.enableForensics) {
        std::vector<std::string> cmdlineBlacklist = {"--inject", "gdb -p", "frida", "inject.so", "ld_preload="};
        auto procEvents = forensics::ProcessAnalyzer::Instance().AuditProcesses(
            config_.blacklistedProcesses,
            cmdlineBlacklist
        );
        cycleEvents.insert(cycleEvents.end(), procEvents.begin(), procEvents.end());
        if (!running_.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) return cycleEvents;

        std::vector<std::string> driverBlacklist = {"dbk64", "winring0", "ksdumper", "leechcore"};
        auto artEvents = forensics::ArtifactAnalyzer::Instance().PerformAudit(
            config_.blacklistedArtifacts,
            driverBlacklist
        );
        cycleEvents.insert(cycleEvents.end(), artEvents.begin(), artEvents.end());
    }
    if (!running_.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) return cycleEvents;
    UpdateAuditHeartbeat();

    // 7. DMA Hardware & Bus Latency Check
    if (config_.enableDmaDetection) {
        auto dmaEvents = dma::DmaDetector::Instance().PerformAudit();
        cycleEvents.insert(cycleEvents.end(), dmaEvents.begin(), dmaEvents.end());
    }
    if (!running_.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) return cycleEvents;
    UpdateAuditHeartbeat();

    // 8. Overlay & ESP/Wallhack Detector (Rendering hooks & fake window scanner)
    if (config_.enableOverlayDetection) {
        auto overlayEvents = overlay::OverlayDetector::Instance().PerformAudit();
        cycleEvents.insert(cycleEvents.end(), overlayEvents.begin(), overlayEvents.end());
    }
    if (!running_.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) return cycleEvents;
    UpdateAuditHeartbeat();

    // Process all generated events through threat scoring
    for (const auto& ev : cycleEvents) {
        ProcessDetection(ev);
    }

    return cycleEvents;
}

void AntiCheatEngine::PushInputSample(float pitch, float yaw, uint64_t timestamp_ms) {
    if (config_.enableBlackboxDump) {
        BlackboxDumper::Instance().RecordInputSample(pitch, yaw, timestamp_ms);
    }

    if (!config_.enableKinematics) return;

    std::optional<DetectionEvent> ev;
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(input_mutex_);
        ev = aim_analyzer_.PushSample(pitch, yaw, timestamp_ms);
    }
    if (ev) {
        ProcessDetection(*ev);
    }
}

void AntiCheatEngine::PushViewSample(float x, float y, float z, float pitch, float yaw, uint64_t timestamp_ms) {
    if (!config_.enableKinematics) return;
    auto ev = phantom_monitor_.PushView(x, y, z, pitch, yaw, timestamp_ms);
    if (ev) ProcessDetection(*ev);
}

void AntiCheatEngine::ProcessDetection(const DetectionEvent& event) {
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(history_mutex_);
        history_.push_back(event);
    }

    threat_score_.fetch_add(event.threat_score);
    Logger::Instance().SecurityAlert(event);

    if (violation_callback_) {
        violation_callback_(event);
    }

    if (config_.enforceBanOnViolation) {
        if (threat_score_.load() >= config_.banScoreThreshold || event.severity == Severity::FATAL_BAN) {
            TriggerBan(event.details, event.type, event.component);
        }
    }
}

void AntiCheatEngine::TriggerBan(const std::string& reason, DetectionType type, const std::string& component) {
    if (is_banned_.exchange(true)) {
        return; // Already banned
    }

    uint32_t finalScore = threat_score_.load();
    Logger::Instance().Error("CRITICAL SECURITY ENFORCEMENT: Device HWID banned. Reason: " + reason +
                             " (Threat Score: " + std::to_string(finalScore) + ")");

    if (config_.enableBlackboxDump) {
        auto snapshot = BlackboxDumper::Instance().CaptureSnapshot(
            component.empty() ? "AntiCheatEngine::Enforcement" : component,
            reason,
            finalScore,
            static_cast<uint32_t>(type)
        );
        last_blackbox_payload_ = BlackboxDumper::Instance().SerializeAndEncrypt(snapshot, config_.secretKey);
        std::string dumpPath = BlackboxDumper::Instance().GenerateDumpFilename();
        BlackboxDumper::Instance().SaveDumpToFile(dumpPath, last_blackbox_payload_);
        Logger::Instance().Info("Blackbox crash/violation diagnostic dump written to: " + dumpPath);
    }

    // Report ban event to backend panel
    panel_client_.SendBanReport(
        DetectionEvent{
            type,
            Severity::FATAL_BAN,
            component.empty() ? "AntiCheatEngine::Enforcement" : component,
            reason,
            static_cast<uint64_t>(time(nullptr)),
            finalScore
        },
        finalScore
    );

    if (ban_callback_) {
        ban_callback_(reason, finalScore);
    }

    // In test harness, bypass UI and process termination
    const char* testEnv = std::getenv("NOESCAPE_TEST_MODE");
    bool inTestMode = (testEnv && (std::strcmp(testEnv, "1") == 0 || strcasecmp(testEnv, "true") == 0));

    if (!inTestMode && config_.showBanUi) {
        SpawnBanScreen(reason, finalScore, component, type);
    }

    if (!inTestMode && config_.terminateProcessOnBan) {
        Logger::Instance().Error("CRITICAL: Terminating target process due to security ban enforcement.");
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(100));
#if defined(_WIN32)
        ExitProcess(1);
#else
        _exit(1);
#endif
    }
}

void AntiCheatEngine::SpawnBanScreen(const std::string& reason, 
                                     uint32_t finalScore, 
                                     const std::string& component, 
                                     DetectionType type) {
    std::string uiPath = config_.banUiExecutable;
    if (uiPath.empty()) {
        const char* envPath = std::getenv("NOESCAPE_BAN_UI_PATH");
        if (envPath && std::strlen(envPath) > 0) {
            uiPath = envPath;
        }
    }

    std::vector<std::string> candidatePaths;
    if (!uiPath.empty()) candidatePaths.push_back(uiPath);
    candidatePaths.push_back("/home/COSMOS/NoEscapeAC/tools/noescape_ban_ui.py");
    candidatePaths.push_back("/home/COSMOS/NoEscapeAC/build/noescape_ban_ui");

    std::string resolvedPath;
    for (const auto& cp : candidatePaths) {
#if defined(_WIN32)
        DWORD attr = GetFileAttributesA(cp.c_str());
        if (attr != INVALID_FILE_ATTRIBUTES && !(attr & FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY)) {
            resolvedPath = cp;
            break;
        }
#else
        if (access(cp.c_str(), X_OK) == 0 || access(cp.c_str(), R_OK) == 0) {
            resolvedPath = cp;
            break;
        }
#endif
    }

    if (resolvedPath.empty()) {
        Logger::Instance().Warn("Security ban screen executable not found in candidate paths.");
        return;
    }

    std::string hwid = panel_client_.GetHwid();
    std::string scoreStr = std::to_string(finalScore);
    std::string typeStr = DetectionEvent::TypeToString(type);
    std::string compStr = component.empty() ? "AntiCheatEngine::Enforcement" : component;

#if defined(_WIN32)
    std::string cmd = "python.exe \"" + resolvedPath + "\" --hwid \"" + hwid +
                      "\" --score " + scoreStr +
                      " --reason \"" + reason +
                      "\" --component \"" + compStr +
                      "\" --type \"" + typeStr + "\"";
    STARTUPINFOA si{}; si.cb = sizeof(si);
    PROCESS_INFORMATION pi{};
    CreateProcessA(nullptr, cmd.data(), nullptr, nullptr, FALSE, CREATE_NO_WINDOW | DETACHED_PROCESS, nullptr, nullptr, &si, &pi);
    if (pi.hProcess) CloseHandle(pi.hProcess);
    if (pi.hThread) CloseHandle(pi.hThread);
#else
    pid_t pid = fork();
    if (pid == 0) {
        // Child: create independent session detached from terminal / game process
        setsid();
        pid_t gpid = fork();
        if (gpid > 0) {
            _exit(0); // Exit intermediate child so grandchild is adopted by init/systemd
        }
        if (gpid == 0) {
            // Grandchild UI process
            // Unset LD_PRELOAD so python child does not load libnoescape.so
            unsetenv("LD_PRELOAD");
            setenv("NOESCAPE_DISABLE_AUTO_INIT", "1", 1);

            uid_t uid = getuid();
            std::string runUserDir = "/run/user/" + std::to_string(uid);
            std::string waylandSocket = runUserDir + "/wayland-0";

            if (!std::getenv("WAYLAND_DISPLAY") && access(waylandSocket.c_str(), F_OK) == 0) {
                setenv("WAYLAND_DISPLAY", "wayland-0", 1);
            }
            if (!std::getenv("XDG_RUNTIME_DIR") && access(runUserDir.c_str(), F_OK) == 0) {
                setenv("XDG_RUNTIME_DIR", runUserDir.c_str(), 1);
            }
            if (!std::getenv("DISPLAY")) {
                setenv("DISPLAY", ":0", 1);
            }
            setenv("QT_QPA_PLATFORM", "wayland;xcb", 0);

            // Redirect stdout and stderr to a log file instead of discarding
            int logFd = open("/tmp/noescape_ban_ui.log", O_CREAT | O_WRONLY | O_APPEND, 0644);
            if (logFd >= 0) {
                dup2(logFd, STDOUT_FILENO);
                dup2(logFd, STDERR_FILENO);
                close(logFd);
            }
            int devNull = open("/dev/null", O_RDONLY);
            if (devNull >= 0) {
                dup2(devNull, STDIN_FILENO);
                close(devNull);
            }

            execlp("python3", "python3", resolvedPath.c_str(),
                   "--hwid", hwid.c_str(),
                   "--score", scoreStr.c_str(),
                   "--reason", reason.c_str(),
                   "--component", compStr.c_str(),
                   "--type", typeStr.c_str(),
                   static_cast<char*>(nullptr));
            _exit(1);
        }
    } else if (pid > 0) {
        waitpid(pid, nullptr, 0);
    }
#endif
    Logger::Instance().Info("Security ban screen spawned successfully.");
}

std::vector<DetectionEvent> AntiCheatEngine::GetDetectionHistory() const {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(history_mutex_);
    return history_;
}

void AntiCheatEngine::Start() {
    if (running_.exchange(true)) return;

    last_audit_heartbeat_ms_.store(GetSteadyNowMs(), std::memory_order_release);

    thread_pool_.Enqueue([this]() {
        BackgroundLoop();
    });

    watchdog_thread_ = std::thread([this]() {
        WatchdogLoop();
    });
}

void AntiCheatEngine::Stop() {
    if (!running_.exchange(false)) return;
    freeze_background_loop_for_testing_.store(false, std::memory_order_release);
    if (watchdog_thread_.joinable()) {
        watchdog_thread_.join();
    }
    thread_pool_.Stop();
}

void AntiCheatEngine::BackgroundLoop() {
    auto lastHeartbeat = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
    auto lastPanelCheck = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - std::chrono::seconds(60);

    while (running_.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
        while (freeze_background_loop_for_testing_.load(std::memory_order_acquire) &&
               running_.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
            std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(10));
        }
        if (!running_.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) break;

        if (!freeze_background_loop_for_testing_.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
            last_audit_heartbeat_ms_.store(GetSteadyNowMs(), std::memory_order_release);
        }
        AuditCycle();
        if (!freeze_background_loop_for_testing_.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
            last_audit_heartbeat_ms_.store(GetSteadyNowMs(), std::memory_order_release);
        }

        auto now = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
        if (config_.enableHeartbeat && 
            std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::seconds>(now - lastHeartbeat).count() >= 10) {
            panel_client_.SendHeartbeat(threat_score_.load(), is_banned_.load() ? "BANNED" : "HEALTHY");
            lastHeartbeat = now;
        }

        // Randomized interval (300-1200 ms) so audit windows cannot be predicted
        thread_local std::mt19937 rng{std::random_device{}()};
        std::uniform_int_distribution<int> jitter(300, 1200);
        if (config_.verifyPanelBan &&
            std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::seconds>(now - lastPanelCheck).count() >= 60) {
            VerifyPanelStatus();
            lastPanelCheck = now;
        }

        int waitMs = jitter(rng);
        auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + std::chrono::milliseconds(waitMs);
        while (running_.load(std::memory_order_acquire) && std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < deadline) {
            if (freeze_background_loop_for_testing_.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
                break;
            }
            last_audit_heartbeat_ms_.store(GetSteadyNowMs(), std::memory_order_release);
            std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(10));
        }
    }
}

void AntiCheatEngine::UpdateAuditHeartbeat() {
    if (!freeze_background_loop_for_testing_.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
        last_audit_heartbeat_ms_.store(GetSteadyNowMs(), std::memory_order_release);
    }
}

void AntiCheatEngine::WatchdogLoop() {
    while (running_.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(100));
        if (!running_.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) break;
        if (is_banned_.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) break;

        uint64_t now = GetSteadyNowMs();
        uint64_t last = last_audit_heartbeat_ms_.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
        if (last > 0 && now > last && (now - last) > 3000) {
            Logger::Instance().Error("Watchdog detected thread freeze or suspension (> 3000ms heartbeat deadline missed). Flagging tampering.");
            DetectionEvent ev;
            ev.type = DetectionType::HEARTBEAT_TAMPERING;
            ev.severity = Severity::FATAL_BAN;
            ev.component = "AntiCheatEngine::Watchdog";
            ev.details = "Watchdog heartbeat timeout: background audit loop frozen or suspended (> 3000ms)";
            ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(std::time(nullptr));
            ev.threat_score = 100;
            ProcessDetection(ev);
            if (!is_banned_.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
                TriggerBan(ev.details, ev.type, ev.component);
            }
            break;
        }
    }
}

} // namespace noescape::core
```

`src/core/logger.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "noescape/core/logger.hpp"
#include <chrono>
#include <iomanip>
#include <sstream>

namespace noescape::core {

Logger& Logger::Instance() {
    static Logger* instance = new Logger();
    return *instance;
}

void Logger::SetAlertCallback(AlertCallback cb) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    alert_cb_ = std::move(cb);
}

void Logger::SetConsoleOutput(bool enabled) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    console_output_ = enabled;
}

void Logger::PrintFormatted(const char* prefix, const char* colorCode, const std::string& msg) {
    if (!console_output_) return;

    auto now = std::chrono::system_clock::now();
    auto in_time_t = std::chrono::system_clock::to_time_t(now);
    auto ms = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(now.time_since_epoch()) % 1000;

    std::tm bt{};
#if defined(_WIN32)
    localtime_s(&bt, &in_time_t);
#else
    localtime_r(&in_time_t, &bt);
#endif

    std::ostringstream ss;
    ss << "\033[90m[" << std::put_time(&bt, "%H:%M:%S") << '.'
       << std::setfill('0') << std::setw(3) << ms.count() << "]\033[0m "
       << colorCode << "[" << prefix << "]\033[0m " << msg << "\n";

    std::cout << ss.str() << std::flush;
}

void Logger::Info(const std::string& msg) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    PrintFormatted("INFO", "\033[36m", msg);
}

void Logger::Warn(const std::string& msg) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    PrintFormatted("WARN", "\033[33m", msg);
}

void Logger::Error(const std::string& msg) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    PrintFormatted("ERROR", "\033[31m", msg);
}

void Logger::SecurityAlert(const DetectionEvent& event) {
    AlertCallback cb;
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        std::ostringstream ss;
        ss << "\033[1;31m[SECURITY ALERT: " << DetectionEvent::TypeToString(event.type)
           << " | SEVERITY: " << DetectionEvent::SeverityToString(event.severity)
           << " | SCORE: +" << event.threat_score << "]\033[0m "
           << "Component: " << event.component << " - " << event.details;
        PrintFormatted("ALERT", "\033[41;37m", ss.str());
        cb = alert_cb_;
    }

    if (cb) {
        cb(event);
    }
}

} // namespace noescape::core
```

`src/core/thread_pool.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "noescape/core/thread_pool.hpp"

namespace noescape::core {

ThreadPool::ThreadPool(size_t threads) {
    if (threads == 0) threads = 1;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < threads; ++i) {
        workers_.emplace_back([this]() {
            while (true) {
                std::function<void()> task;
                {
                    std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(this->queue_mutex_);
                    this->condition_.wait(lock, [this]() {
                        return this->stop_ || !this->tasks_.empty();
                    });
                    if (this->stop_ && this->tasks_.empty()) {
                        return;
                    }
                    task = std::move(this->tasks_.front());
                    this->tasks_.pop();
                }
                task();
            }
        });
    }
}

ThreadPool::~ThreadPool() {
    Stop();
}

void ThreadPool::Stop() {
    {
        std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(queue_mutex_);
        if (stop_) return;
        stop_ = true;
    }
    condition_.notify_all();
    for (std::thread& worker : workers_) {
        if (worker.joinable()) {
            worker.join();
        }
    }
}

} // namespace noescape::core

```

`src/dma/dma_detector.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "noescape/dma/dma_detector.hpp"
#include "noescape/core/obfuscation.hpp"
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <numeric>
#include <fstream>
#include <sstream>
#include <ctime>
#include <cstring>

#if defined(_WIN32)
#include <windows.h>
#include <setupapi.h>
#else
#include <dirent.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#include <fcntl.h>
#endif

#if defined(__x86_64__) || defined(_M_X64) || defined(__i386__) || defined(_M_IX86)
#include <immintrin.h>
#if defined(_MSC_VER)
#include <intrin.h>
#else
#include <x86intrin.h>
#endif
#endif

namespace noescape::dma {

namespace {

inline uint64_t ReadTsc() {
#if defined(_MSC_VER) || defined(__x86_64__) || defined(__i386__)
    return __rdtsc();
#else
    auto now = std::chrono::steady_clock::now().time_since_epoch();
    return static_cast<uint64_t>(std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::nanoseconds>(now).count());
#endif
}

inline void MemoryBarrier() {
#if defined(__x86_64__) || defined(_M_X64)
    _mm_mfence();
#elif defined(__GNUC__)
    __sync_synchronize();
#endif
}

} // namespace

DmaDetector::DmaDetector() {
    // 4MB cache-aligned probe buffer
    probe_buffer_.resize(4 * 1024 * 1024, 0xAA);
}

DmaDetector::~DmaDetector() = default;

DmaDetector& DmaDetector::Instance() {
    static DmaDetector* instance = new DmaDetector();
    return *instance;
}

uint64_t DmaDetector::MeasureBufferTraversal() {
    if (probe_buffer_.empty()) return 0;

    volatile uint8_t* ptr = probe_buffer_.data();
    size_t size = probe_buffer_.size();
    size_t stride = 64; // Stride by standard cache-line size (64 bytes)
    volatile uint64_t sink = 0;

    // Flush cache lines so traversal hits DRAM bus and evaluates physical PCIe contention
#if defined(__x86_64__) || defined(_M_X64) || defined(__i386__) || defined(_M_IX86)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < size; i += stride) {
        _mm_clflush(const_cast<uint8_t*>(ptr + i));
    }
#endif

    MemoryBarrier();
    uint64_t t0 = ReadTsc();
    MemoryBarrier();

    for (size_t i = 0; i < size; i += stride) {
        sink += ptr[i];
    }

    MemoryBarrier();
    uint64_t t1 = ReadTsc();
    MemoryBarrier();

    (void)sink;
    return (t1 > t0) ? (t1 - t0) : 0;
}

bool DmaDetector::Calibrate(size_t probe_size_kb, size_t iterations) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    if (iterations == 0) iterations = 15;
    probe_buffer_.resize(probe_size_kb * 1024, 0x55);

    std::vector<double> samples;
    samples.reserve(iterations);

    for (size_t i = 0; i < iterations; ++i) {
        uint64_t cycles = MeasureBufferTraversal();
        if (cycles > 0) {
            samples.push_back(static_cast<double>(cycles));
        }
    }

    if (samples.size() < 4) return false;

    double sum = std::accumulate(samples.begin(), samples.end(), 0.0);
    baseline_mean_cycles_ = sum / samples.size();

    double varSum = 0.0;
    for (double s : samples) {
        double diff = s - baseline_mean_cycles_;
        varSum += diff * diff;
    }
    baseline_variance_ = varSum / samples.size();
    calibrated_ = true;

    return true;
}

DmaProfile DmaDetector::ProfileMemoryBusJitter(size_t iterations) {
    DmaProfile profile;
    if (!calibrated_) {
        if (!Calibrate()) return profile;
    }

    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    if (iterations == 0) iterations = 30;
    std::vector<double> samples;
    samples.reserve(iterations);

    for (size_t i = 0; i < iterations; ++i) {
        uint64_t cycles = MeasureBufferTraversal();
        if (cycles > 0) {
            samples.push_back(static_cast<double>(cycles));
        }
    }

    if (samples.empty()) return profile;

    // Reject outliers caused by OS scheduler interrupts
    if (samples.size() >= 5) {
        std::vector<double> sorted = samples;
        std::sort(sorted.begin(), sorted.end());
        double median = sorted[sorted.size() / 2];
        std::vector<double> filtered;
        for (double s : samples) {
            if (s <= median * 3.5) {
                filtered.push_back(s);
            }
        }
        if (filtered.size() >= 5) {
            samples = std::move(filtered);
        }
    }

    double sum = std::accumulate(samples.begin(), samples.end(), 0.0);
    profile.current_mean_cycles = sum / samples.size();
    profile.baseline_mean_cycles = baseline_mean_cycles_;

    double varSum = 0.0;
    for (double s : samples) {
        double diff = s - profile.current_mean_cycles;
        varSum += diff * diff;
    }
    double currentVar = varSum / samples.size();
    profile.jitter_stddev = std::sqrt(currentVar);

    double baselineStdDev = std::sqrt(baseline_variance_ > 0 ? baseline_variance_ : 1.0);

    // Count spikes exceeding threshold (incorporating a minimum relative noise floor to avoid false alarms on idle baselines)
    double minDeviation = std::max(anomaly_multiplier_ * baselineStdDev, baseline_mean_cycles_ * 0.35);
    double spikeCutoff = baseline_mean_cycles_ + minDeviation;
    for (double s : samples) {
        if (s > spikeCutoff) {
            profile.spike_count++;
        }
    }

    // If more than 30% of reads suffer massive bus contention and average latency shifted, or stddev is wildly anomalous
    if ((profile.spike_count > (iterations / 3) && profile.current_mean_cycles > baseline_mean_cycles_ * 1.25) || 
        (profile.jitter_stddev > baselineStdDev * anomaly_multiplier_ && profile.current_mean_cycles > baseline_mean_cycles_ * 1.5)) {
        profile.anomalous = true;
    }

    return profile;
}

std::vector<PciDeviceInfo> DmaDetector::ScanHardwareDevices() {
    std::vector<PciDeviceInfo> detected;

#if defined(_WIN32)
    // Windows: Check known FTDI USB3 FIFO bridges via registry
    HKEY hUsbKey = nullptr;
    if (RegOpenKeyExA(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, "SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Enum\\USB", 0, KEY_READ, &hUsbKey) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
        char subKeyName[256]{};
        DWORD index = 0;
        DWORD nameLen = sizeof(subKeyName);

        while (RegEnumKeyExA(hUsbKey, index++, subKeyName, &nameLen, nullptr, nullptr, nullptr, nullptr) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
            nameLen = sizeof(subKeyName);
            std::string keyUpper = subKeyName;
            for (auto& c : keyUpper) c = static_cast<char>(std::toupper(c));

            // Check for FTDI FT601 (VID_0403&PID_601F) or standard LeechCore bridges
            if (keyUpper.find("VID_0403&PID_601F") != std::string::npos ||
                keyUpper.find("VID_0403&PID_6010") != std::string::npos ||
                keyUpper.find("VID_0403&PID_6014") != std::string::npos) {
                PciDeviceInfo info;
                info.device_id = subKeyName;
                info.vendor_id = "0403";
                info.description = "FTDI SuperSpeed USB3 DMA Bridge (PCIeLeech hardware carrier)";
                info.is_suspicious = true;
                detected.push_back(info);
            }
        }
        RegCloseKey(hUsbKey);
    }

    // Windows: Check PCIe Bus for Xilinx Artix-7 (VEN_10EE) or Altera (VEN_1172) FPGA endpoints
    HKEY hPciKey = nullptr;
    if (RegOpenKeyExA(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, "SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Enum\\PCI", 0, KEY_READ, &hPciKey) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
        char subKeyName[256]{};
        DWORD index = 0;
        DWORD nameLen = sizeof(subKeyName);

        while (RegEnumKeyExA(hPciKey, index++, subKeyName, &nameLen, nullptr, nullptr, nullptr, nullptr) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
            nameLen = sizeof(subKeyName);
            std::string keyUpper = subKeyName;
            for (auto& c : keyUpper) c = static_cast<char>(std::toupper(c));

            if (keyUpper.find("VEN_10EE") != std::string::npos) {
                PciDeviceInfo info;
                info.device_id = subKeyName;
                info.vendor_id = "10EE";
                info.description = "Xilinx FPGA PCIe Endpoint (PCIeLeech DMA Hardware Card)";
                info.is_suspicious = true;
                detected.push_back(info);
            } else if (keyUpper.find("VEN_1172") != std::string::npos) {
                PciDeviceInfo info;
                info.device_id = subKeyName;
                info.vendor_id = "1172";
                info.description = "Altera FPGA PCIe Endpoint (DMA Hardware Carrier)";
                info.is_suspicious = true;
                detected.push_back(info);
            }
        }
        RegCloseKey(hPciKey);
    }

#else
    // Linux: Inspect /sys/bus/pci/devices
    DIR* pciDir = opendir(NOESCAPE_XOR("/sys/bus/pci/devices"));
    if (pciDir) {
        struct dirent* entry = nullptr;
        while ((entry = readdir(pciDir)) != nullptr) {
            if (entry->d_name[0] == '.') continue;

            std::string pciPath = std::string("/sys/bus/pci/devices/") + entry->d_name;
            std::ifstream vendorFile(pciPath + "/vendor");
            std::ifstream deviceFile(pciPath + "/device");

            std::string vid, did;
            if (vendorFile >> vid && deviceFile >> did) {
                // Xilinx Artix-7 / Spartan FPGA default PCIe vendor ID is 0x10ee
                if (vid == "0x10ee") {
                    PciDeviceInfo dev;
                    dev.device_id = did;
                    dev.vendor_id = vid;
                    dev.description = "Xilinx FPGA PCIe Endpoint (Known LeechCore DMA hardware)";
                    dev.is_suspicious = true;
                    detected.push_back(dev);
                }
            }
        }
        closedir(pciDir);
    }

    // Inspect /sys/bus/usb/devices for FTDI FT601 bridge
    DIR* usbDir = opendir(NOESCAPE_XOR("/sys/bus/usb/devices"));
    if (usbDir) {
        struct dirent* entry = nullptr;
        while ((entry = readdir(usbDir)) != nullptr) {
            if (entry->d_name[0] == '.') continue;

            std::string usbPath = std::string("/sys/bus/usb/devices/") + entry->d_name;
            std::ifstream vendorFile(usbPath + "/idVendor");
            std::ifstream prodFile(usbPath + "/idProduct");

            std::string vid, pid;
            if (vendorFile >> vid && prodFile >> pid) {
                // FTDI FT601 USB 3.0 bridge VID: 0403, PID: 601f
                if (vid == "0403" && (pid == "601f" || pid == "6010" || pid == "6014")) {
                    PciDeviceInfo dev;
                    dev.device_id = pid;
                    dev.vendor_id = vid;
                    dev.description = "FTDI SuperSpeed FIFO Bridge (PCIeLeech DMA interface)";
                    dev.is_suspicious = true;
                    detected.push_back(dev);
                }
            }
        }
        closedir(usbDir);
    }
#endif

    return detected;
}

bool DmaDetector::CheckLeechCoreArtifacts() {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    // Check for LeechCore named pipe or driver symlink
    HANDLE hPipe = CreateFileA("\\\\.\\LeechCore", GENERIC_READ, 0, nullptr, OPEN_EXISTING, 0, nullptr);
    if (hPipe != INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) {
        CloseHandle(hPipe);
        return true;
    }
    HANDLE hMem = CreateFileA("\\\\.\\PhysicalMemory", GENERIC_READ, FILE_SHARE_READ, nullptr, OPEN_EXISTING, 0, nullptr);
    if (hMem != INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) {
        CloseHandle(hMem);
        return true;
    }
    return false;
#else
    // Check for /dev/pcileech or /dev/fpgamem or open access to /dev/mem
    std::ifstream dev1(NOESCAPE_XOR("/dev/pcileech"));
    if (dev1.is_open()) return true;

    std::ifstream dev2(NOESCAPE_XOR("/dev/fpgamem"));
    if (dev2.is_open()) return true;

    // Direct /dev/mem handle check
    int fd = open("/dev/mem", O_RDONLY);
    if (fd >= 0) {
        close(fd);
        return true;
    }
    return false;
#endif
}

std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> DmaDetector::PerformAudit() {
    std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> events;

    // 1. Check for LeechCore interface presence
    if (CheckLeechCoreArtifacts()) {
        core::DetectionEvent ev;
        ev.type = core::DetectionType::DMA_PCIE_LEECH_DEVICE;
        ev.severity = core::Severity::FATAL_BAN;
        ev.component = "DmaDetector::LeechCoreDriver";
        ev.details = "Active Direct Memory Access (DMA) LeechCore device handle identified";
        ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(std::time(nullptr));
        ev.threat_score = 100;
        events.push_back(ev);
    }

    // 2. Hardware device scan (PCIe / USB)
    auto devices = ScanHardwareDevices();
    for (const auto& dev : devices) {
        if (dev.is_suspicious) {
            core::DetectionEvent ev;
            ev.type = core::DetectionType::DMA_PCIE_LEECH_DEVICE;
            ev.severity = core::Severity::FATAL_BAN;
            ev.component = "DmaDetector::PciScan";
            ev.details = "DMA Hardware device discovered on bus: " + dev.description +
                         " [VID: " + dev.vendor_id + " PID: " + dev.device_id + "]";
            ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(std::time(nullptr));
            ev.threat_score = 100;
            events.push_back(ev);
        }
    }

    // 3. Memory bus contention / latency jitter profile
    auto profile = ProfileMemoryBusJitter(30);
    if (profile.anomalous) {
        core::DetectionEvent ev;
        ev.type = core::DetectionType::DMA_LATENCY_JITTER_ANOMALY;
        ev.severity = core::Severity::HIGH;
        ev.component = "DmaDetector::LatencyJitter";

        std::ostringstream ss;
        ss << "Memory bus contention anomaly detected: current_mean=" << profile.current_mean_cycles
           << " baseline=" << profile.baseline_mean_cycles
           << " jitter_stddev=" << profile.jitter_stddev
           << " spikes=" << profile.spike_count;
        ev.details = ss.str();
        ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(std::time(nullptr));
        ev.threat_score = 75;
        events.push_back(ev);
    }

    return events;
}

} // namespace noescape::dma
```
