`src/forensics/artifact_analyzer.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "noescape/forensics/artifact_analyzer.hpp"
#include "noescape/core/obfuscation.hpp"
#include <algorithm>
#include <cctype>
#include <cstring>
#include <fstream>
#include <sstream>
#include <ctime>

#if defined(_WIN32)
#include <windows.h>
#include <psapi.h>
#else
#include <dirent.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#endif

namespace noescape::forensics {

namespace {

std::string ToLower(const std::string& str) {
    std::string s = str;
    std::transform(s.begin(), s.end(), s.begin(), [](unsigned char c) {
        return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c));
    });
    return s;
}

} // namespace

ArtifactAnalyzer& ArtifactAnalyzer::Instance() {
    static ArtifactAnalyzer* instance = new ArtifactAnalyzer();
    return *instance;
}

bool ArtifactAnalyzer::CheckSystemWidePreload() {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    HKEY hKey = nullptr;
    if (RegOpenKeyExA(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, 
                      NOESCAPE_XOR("SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Windows NT\\CurrentVersion\\Windows"), 
                      0, KEY_READ, &hKey) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
        char buffer[512]{};
        DWORD size = sizeof(buffer);
        DWORD type = 0;
        if (RegQueryValueExA(hKey, NOESCAPE_XOR("AppInit_DLLs"), nullptr, &type, reinterpret_cast<LPBYTE>(buffer), &size) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
            RegCloseKey(hKey);
            return std::strlen(buffer) > 0;
        }
        RegCloseKey(hKey);
    }
    return false;
#else
    std::ifstream preload(NOESCAPE_XOR("/etc/ld.so.preload"));
    if (preload.is_open()) {
        std::string line;
        while (std::getline(preload, line)) {
            // If there is any non-whitespace, non-comment line, it's active
            size_t first = line.find_first_not_of(" \t\r\n");
            if (first != std::string::npos && line[first] != '#') {
                return true;
            }
        }
    }
    return false;
#endif
}

std::vector<ArtifactRecord> ArtifactAnalyzer::ScanFilesystemArtifacts(const std::vector<std::string>& blacklisted_names) {
    std::vector<ArtifactRecord> findings;
    if (blacklisted_names.empty()) return findings;

#if defined(_WIN32)
    std::vector<std::string> searchDirs;
    char tempPath[MAX_PATH]{};
    if (GetTempPathA(MAX_PATH, tempPath) > 0) {
        searchDirs.push_back(tempPath);
    }
    searchDirs.push_back("C:\\Windows\\Temp");

    for (const auto& dir : searchDirs) {
        WIN32_FIND_DATAA fd{};
        std::string query = dir + "\\*";
        HANDLE hFind = FindFirstFileA(query.c_str(), &fd);
        if (hFind == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) continue;

        do {
            if (std::strcmp(fd.cFileName, ".") == 0 || std::strcmp(fd.cFileName, "..") == 0) continue;
            std::string fileName = ToLower(fd.cFileName);

            for (const auto& target : blacklisted_names) {
                if (fileName.find(ToLower(target)) != std::string::npos) {
                    findings.push_back({dir + "\\" + fd.cFileName, target, "TempArtifact"});
                    break;
                }
            }
        } while (FindNextFileA(hFind, &fd));

        FindClose(hFind);
    }

#else
    std::vector<std::string> searchDirs = {"/dev/shm", "/tmp", "/var/tmp"};

    for (const auto& dirPath : searchDirs) {
        DIR* dir = opendir(dirPath.c_str());
        if (!dir) continue;

        struct dirent* entry = nullptr;
        while ((entry = readdir(dir)) != nullptr) {
            if (std::strcmp(entry->d_name, ".") == 0 || std::strcmp(entry->d_name, "..") == 0) continue;

            std::string fileName = ToLower(entry->d_name);
            for (const auto& target : blacklisted_names) {
                if (fileName.find(ToLower(target)) != std::string::npos) {
                    findings.push_back({dirPath + "/" + entry->d_name, target, "TransientSharedMemoryArtifact"});
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        closedir(dir);
    }
#endif

    return findings;
}

std::vector<ArtifactRecord> ArtifactAnalyzer::ScanKernelDrivers(const std::vector<std::string>& blacklisted_drivers) {
    std::vector<ArtifactRecord> findings;
    if (blacklisted_drivers.empty()) return findings;

#if defined(_WIN32)
    LPVOID drivers[1024];
    DWORD cbNeeded = 0;
    if (EnumDeviceDrivers(drivers, sizeof(drivers), &cbNeeded)) {
        int count = cbNeeded / sizeof(drivers[0]);
        for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
            char baseName[MAX_PATH]{};
            if (GetDeviceDriverBaseNameA(drivers[i], baseName, MAX_PATH)) {
                std::string lowerDriver = ToLower(baseName);
                for (const auto& bl : blacklisted_drivers) {
                    if (lowerDriver.find(ToLower(bl)) != std::string::npos) {
                        findings.push_back({baseName, bl, "VulnerableKernelDriver"});
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

#else
    std::ifstream modules(NOESCAPE_XOR("/proc/modules"));
    if (modules.is_open()) {
        std::string line;
        while (std::getline(modules, line)) {
            std::istringstream iss(line);
            std::string modName;
            if (iss >> modName) {
                std::string lowerMod = ToLower(modName);
                for (const auto& bl : blacklisted_drivers) {
                    if (lowerMod.find(ToLower(bl)) != std::string::npos) {
                        findings.push_back({modName, bl, "BlacklistedKernelModule"});
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
#endif

    return findings;
}

std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> ArtifactAnalyzer::PerformAudit(
    const std::vector<std::string>& blacklisted_names,
    const std::vector<std::string>& blacklisted_drivers
) {
    std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> events;

    // Check system-wide linker hook
    if (CheckSystemWidePreload()) {
        core::DetectionEvent ev;
        ev.type = core::DetectionType::ENVIRONMENT_TAMPERING;
        ev.severity = core::Severity::FATAL_BAN;
        ev.component = "ArtifactAnalyzer::SystemHook";
        ev.details = "System-wide persistent dynamic hook installed (/etc/ld.so.preload or AppInit_DLLs)";
        ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(std::time(nullptr));
        ev.threat_score = 100;
        events.push_back(ev);
    }

    // Scan filesystem artifacts
    auto fsArtifacts = ScanFilesystemArtifacts(blacklisted_names);
    for (const auto& art : fsArtifacts) {
        core::DetectionEvent ev;
        ev.type = core::DetectionType::FORENSICS_ARTIFACT_DETECTED;
        ev.severity = core::Severity::HIGH;
        ev.component = "ArtifactAnalyzer::Filesystem";
        ev.details = "Cheat binary or payload artifact found on disk: " + art.path + " (matched: " + art.matched_signature + ")";
        ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(std::time(nullptr));
        ev.threat_score = 70;
        events.push_back(ev);
    }

    // Scan kernel drivers
    auto driverArtifacts = ScanKernelDrivers(blacklisted_drivers);
    for (const auto& drv : driverArtifacts) {
        core::DetectionEvent ev;
        ev.type = core::DetectionType::FORENSICS_ARTIFACT_DETECTED;
        ev.severity = core::Severity::FATAL_BAN;
        ev.component = "ArtifactAnalyzer::DriverWatch";
        ev.details = "Dangerous/vulnerable kernel driver active: " + drv.path + " (matched: " + drv.matched_signature + ")";
        ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(std::time(nullptr));
        ev.threat_score = 100;
        events.push_back(ev);
    }

    return events;
}

} // namespace noescape::forensics

```

`src/forensics/process_analyzer.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "noescape/forensics/process_analyzer.hpp"
#include "noescape/core/obfuscation.hpp"
#include <algorithm>
#include <cctype>
#include <cstring>
#include <fstream>
#include <sstream>
#include <ctime>

#if defined(_WIN32)
#include <windows.h>
#include <tlhelp32.h>
#include <psapi.h>
#else
#include <dirent.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#include <sys/types.h>
#include <cstdlib>
#endif

namespace noescape::forensics {

namespace {

std::string ToLower(const std::string& str) {
    std::string s = str;
    std::transform(s.begin(), s.end(), s.begin(), [](unsigned char c) {
        return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c));
    });
    return s;
}

} // namespace

ProcessAnalyzer& ProcessAnalyzer::Instance() {
    static ProcessAnalyzer* instance = new ProcessAnalyzer();
    return *instance;
}

bool ProcessAnalyzer::CheckCurrentProcessEnvironment() {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    char buffer[256]{};
    if (GetEnvironmentVariableA("COR_ENABLE_PROFILING", buffer, sizeof(buffer)) > 0) return true;
    if (GetEnvironmentVariableA("ATTACH_DEBUGGER", buffer, sizeof(buffer)) > 0) return true;
    return false;
#else
    auto isSafePreload = [](const char* envVal) {
        if (!envVal || std::strlen(envVal) == 0) return true;
        std::string lowerVal = ToLower(envVal);
        std::istringstream iss(lowerVal);
        std::string token;
        while (std::getline(iss, token, ':')) {
            size_t first = token.find_first_not_of(" \t\r\n");
            if (first == std::string::npos) continue;
            size_t last = token.find_last_not_of(" \t\r\n");
            std::string item = token.substr(first, (last - first + 1));
            if (item.empty()) continue;

            bool itemSafe = (item.find("libextest.so") != std::string::npos ||
                             item.find("libgamemode") != std::string::npos ||
                             item.find("libmangohud") != std::string::npos ||
                             item.find("libvkbasalt") != std::string::npos ||
                             item.find("libnoescape") != std::string::npos ||
                             item.find("noescape") != std::string::npos);
            if (!itemSafe) {
                return false;
            }
        }
        return true;
    };

    const char* ldPreload = std::getenv(NOESCAPE_XOR("LD_PRELOAD"));
    if (ldPreload && !isSafePreload(ldPreload)) {
        return true;
    }
    const char* ldAudit = std::getenv(NOESCAPE_XOR("LD_AUDIT"));
    if (ldAudit && std::strlen(ldAudit) > 0) {
        return true;
    }
    return false;
#endif
}

std::vector<ProcessRecord> ProcessAnalyzer::EnumerateProcesses() {
    std::vector<ProcessRecord> records;

#if defined(_WIN32)
    HANDLE snap = CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(TH32CS_SNAPPROCESS, 0);
    if (snap == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return records;

    PROCESSENTRY32W pe{};
    pe.dwSize = sizeof(pe);

    if (Process32FirstW(snap, &pe)) {
        do {
            ProcessRecord rec;
            rec.pid = pe.th32ProcessID;
            rec.ppid = pe.th32ParentProcessID;

            char nameBuf[MAX_PATH]{};
            WideCharToMultiByte(CP_UTF8, 0, pe.szExeFile, -1, nameBuf, MAX_PATH, nullptr, nullptr);
            rec.name = nameBuf;

            HANDLE hProc = OpenProcess(PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION, FALSE, pe.th32ProcessID);
            if (hProc) {
                char fullPath[MAX_PATH]{};
                DWORD size = MAX_PATH;
                if (QueryFullProcessImageNameA(hProc, 0, fullPath, &size)) {
                    rec.path = fullPath;
                    // Detect unlinked / ghosted executables on Windows
                    DWORD attrs = GetFileAttributesA(fullPath);
                    if (attrs == INVALID_FILE_ATTRIBUTES) {
                        rec.is_deleted_binary = true;
                    }
                }
                CloseHandle(hProc);
            }

            records.push_back(rec);
        } while (Process32NextW(snap, &pe));
    }

    CloseHandle(snap);

#else
    DIR* procDir = opendir(NOESCAPE_XOR("/proc"));
    if (!procDir) return records;

    struct dirent* entry = nullptr;
    while ((entry = readdir(procDir)) != nullptr) {
        if (entry->d_type != DT_DIR) continue;

        char* endPtr = nullptr;
        long pid = std::strtol(entry->d_name, &endPtr, 10);
        if (pid <= 0 || *endPtr != '\0') continue;

        ProcessRecord rec;
        rec.pid = static_cast<uint32_t>(pid);

        // Read exe link
        std::string exeLinkPath = std::string("/proc/") + entry->d_name + "/exe";
        char linkBuf[1024]{};
        ssize_t linkLen = readlink(exeLinkPath.c_str(), linkBuf, sizeof(linkBuf) - 1);
        if (linkLen > 0) {
            linkBuf[linkLen] = '\0';
            rec.path = linkBuf;
            if (rec.path.find("(deleted)") != std::string::npos) {
                rec.is_deleted_binary = true;
            }
        }

        // Read comm
        std::string commPath = std::string("/proc/") + entry->d_name + "/comm";
        std::ifstream commFile(commPath);
        if (commFile.is_open()) {
            std::getline(commFile, rec.name);
        }

        // Read cmdline
        std::string cmdlinePath = std::string("/proc/") + entry->d_name + "/cmdline";
        std::ifstream cmdlineFile(cmdlinePath, std::ios::binary);
        if (cmdlineFile.is_open()) {
            std::string cmdContent((std::istreambuf_iterator<char>(cmdlineFile)),
                                    std::istreambuf_iterator<char>());
            for (char& c : cmdContent) {
                if (c == '\0') c = ' ';
            }
            rec.cmdline = cmdContent;
        }

        // Read environ to check for suspicious LD_PRELOAD injection
        std::string envPath = std::string("/proc/") + entry->d_name + "/environ";
        std::ifstream envFile(envPath, std::ios::binary);
        if (envFile.is_open()) {
            std::string envContent((std::istreambuf_iterator<char>(envFile)),
                                   std::istreambuf_iterator<char>());
            size_t startPos = 0;
            while (startPos < envContent.size()) {
                size_t nullPos = envContent.find('\0', startPos);
                if (nullPos == std::string::npos) nullPos = envContent.size();
                std::string entryStr = envContent.substr(startPos, nullPos - startPos);
                startPos = nullPos + 1;

                if (entryStr.rfind("LD_PRELOAD=", 0) == 0 || entryStr.rfind("LD_AUDIT=", 0) == 0) {
                    std::string val = entryStr.substr(entryStr.find('=') + 1);
                    if (!val.empty()) {
                        std::string lowerVal = ToLower(val);
                        bool isSystemLib = (lowerVal.find("libextest.so") != std::string::npos ||
                                            lowerVal.find("libgamemode") != std::string::npos ||
                                            lowerVal.find("libmangohud") != std::string::npos ||
                                            lowerVal.find("libvkbasalt") != std::string::npos ||
                                            lowerVal.find("libeatmydata") != std::string::npos ||
                                            lowerVal.find("libfakeroot") != std::string::npos);
                        bool isSuspiciousLocation = (lowerVal.find("/dev/shm") != std::string::npos ||
                                                     lowerVal.find("/tmp") != std::string::npos ||
                                                     lowerVal.find("/var/tmp") != std::string::npos ||
                                                     lowerVal.find("cheat") != std::string::npos ||
                                                     lowerVal.find("inject") != std::string::npos ||
                                                     lowerVal.find("hook") != std::string::npos ||
                                                     lowerVal.find("aimbot") != std::string::npos ||
                                                     lowerVal.find("memhack") != std::string::npos);
                        if (!isSystemLib && isSuspiciousLocation) {
                            rec.has_injected_env = true;
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        records.push_back(rec);
    }

    closedir(procDir);
#endif

    return records;
}

std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> ProcessAnalyzer::AuditProcesses(
    const std::vector<std::string>& blacklist,
    const std::vector<std::string>& cmdline_blacklist
) {
    std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> events;

    // Check self environment tampering
    if (CheckCurrentProcessEnvironment()) {
        core::DetectionEvent ev;
        ev.type = core::DetectionType::ENVIRONMENT_TAMPERING;
        ev.severity = core::Severity::FATAL_BAN;
        ev.component = "ProcessAnalyzer::EnvironmentAudit";
        ev.details = "Suspicious dynamic linker injection detected in process environment (LD_PRELOAD / LD_AUDIT)";
        ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(std::time(nullptr));
        ev.threat_score = 100;
        events.push_back(ev);
    }

    auto processes = EnumerateProcesses();
    for (const auto& proc : processes) {
        std::string lowerName = ToLower(proc.name);
        std::string lowerPath = ToLower(proc.path);
        std::string lowerCmd = ToLower(proc.cmdline);

        // 1. Blacklisted process name or path
        for (const auto& bl : blacklist) {
            std::string lowerBl = ToLower(bl);
            if (!lowerBl.empty() && 
                (lowerName.find(lowerBl) != std::string::npos || lowerPath.find(lowerBl) != std::string::npos)) {
                core::DetectionEvent ev;
                ev.type = core::DetectionType::SUSPICIOUS_PROCESS_DETECTED;
                ev.severity = core::Severity::HIGH;
                ev.component = "ProcessAnalyzer::ProcessBlacklist";
                ev.details = "Blacklisted process signature active: PID " + std::to_string(proc.pid) +
                             " (" + proc.name + " -> " + proc.path + ")";
                ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(std::time(nullptr));
                ev.threat_score = 80;
                events.push_back(ev);
                break;
            }
        }

        // 2. Suspicious command-line parameters
        for (const auto& cmdBl : cmdline_blacklist) {
            std::string lowerCmdBl = ToLower(cmdBl);
            if (!lowerCmdBl.empty() && lowerCmd.find(lowerCmdBl) != std::string::npos) {
                // Ignore legitimate preload of NoEscape itself or harmless Linux gaming utilities
                if (lowerCmdBl.find("ld_preload") != std::string::npos &&
                    (lowerCmd.find("noescape") != std::string::npos ||
                     lowerCmd.find("libmangohud") != std::string::npos ||
                     lowerCmd.find("libgamemode") != std::string::npos ||
                     lowerCmd.find("libvkbasalt") != std::string::npos)) {
                    continue;
                }

                core::DetectionEvent ev;
                ev.type = core::DetectionType::SUSPICIOUS_PROCESS_DETECTED;
                ev.severity = core::Severity::HIGH;
                ev.component = "ProcessAnalyzer::CmdlineInspection";
                ev.details = "Suspicious commandline injection argument found in PID " +
                             std::to_string(proc.pid) + ": " + proc.cmdline;
                ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(std::time(nullptr));
                ev.threat_score = 75;
                events.push_back(ev);
                break;
            }
        }

        // 3. Executed binary deleted from disk (Anti-Forensics trick)
        if (proc.is_deleted_binary) {
            core::DetectionEvent ev;
            ev.type = core::DetectionType::FORENSICS_ARTIFACT_DETECTED;
            ev.severity = core::Severity::CRITICAL;
            ev.component = "ProcessAnalyzer::AntiForensics";
            ev.details = "Process executing unlinked memory-ghosted/deleted executable: PID " +
                         std::to_string(proc.pid) + " (" + proc.path + ")";
            ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(std::time(nullptr));
            ev.threat_score = 85;
            events.push_back(ev);
        }

        // 4. Injected environment variables (ignore standard whitelisted platforms)
        if (proc.has_injected_env) {
            if (lowerName.find("steam") == std::string::npos &&
                lowerName.find("discord") == std::string::npos &&
                lowerPath.find("steam") == std::string::npos &&
                lowerPath.find("discord") == std::string::npos) {
                core::DetectionEvent ev;
                ev.type = core::DetectionType::ENVIRONMENT_TAMPERING;
                ev.severity = core::Severity::HIGH;
                ev.component = "ProcessAnalyzer::EnvironmentInjection";
                ev.details = "Process spawned with dangerous injection environment (LD_PRELOAD): PID " +
                             std::to_string(proc.pid) + " (" + proc.name + ")";
                ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(std::time(nullptr));
                ev.threat_score = 70;
                events.push_back(ev);
            }
        }
    }

    return events;
}

} // namespace noescape::forensics

```

`src/kinematics/aim_analyzer.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "noescape/kinematics/aim_analyzer.hpp"
#include <cmath>
#include <numeric>
#include <sstream>
#include <ctime>

namespace noescape::kinematics {

AimAnalyzer::AimAnalyzer(size_t history_capacity)
    : history_capacity_(history_capacity < 16 ? 16 : history_capacity) {}

void AimAnalyzer::Reset() {
    history_.clear();
}

float AimAnalyzer::NormalizeAngleDelta(float delta) const {
    if (!std::isfinite(delta)) return 0.0f;
    delta = std::fmod(delta, 360.0f);
    if (delta > 180.0f) delta -= 360.0f;
    if (delta < -180.0f) delta += 360.0f;
    return delta;
}

float AimAnalyzer::CalculateAngleDistance(const AngleSample& a, const AngleSample& b) const {
    float dp = b.pitch - a.pitch;
    float dy = NormalizeAngleDelta(b.yaw - a.yaw);
    return std::sqrt(dp * dp + dy * dy);
}

std::optional<core::DetectionEvent> AimAnalyzer::PushSample(float pitch, float yaw, uint64_t timestamp_ms) {
    return PushSample(AngleSample{pitch, yaw, timestamp_ms});
}

std::optional<core::DetectionEvent> AimAnalyzer::PushSample(const AngleSample& sample) {
    if (!std::isfinite(sample.pitch) || !std::isfinite(sample.yaw)) {
        return std::nullopt;
    }
    if (std::abs(sample.pitch) > 180.0f) {
        return std::nullopt;
    }

    AngleSample s = sample;
    if (!history_.empty()) {
        if (s.timestamp_ms < history_.back().timestamp_ms) {
            // Drop out-of-order timestamps
            return std::nullopt;
        }
        if (s.timestamp_ms == history_.back().timestamp_ms) {
            // High-polling rate sub-millisecond mouse event: assign micro-increment
            s.timestamp_ms = history_.back().timestamp_ms + 1;
        }
    }

    history_.push_back(s);
    if (history_.size() > history_capacity_) {
        history_.pop_front();
    }

    if (history_.size() < 3) {
        return std::nullopt;
    }

    // Check 1: Instant Snap (Silent Aim / Instant Flick)
    auto snapEvent = CheckInstantSnap();
    if (snapEvent) return snapEvent;

    // Checks requiring a longer trajectory window
    if (history_.size() >= min_samples_) {
        // Check 2: Perfect Linearity
        auto linearEvent = CheckPerfectLinearity();
        if (linearEvent) return linearEvent;

        // Check 3: Artificial Smoothing
        auto smoothEvent = CheckArtificialSmoothing();
        if (smoothEvent) return smoothEvent;
    }

    return std::nullopt;
}

std::optional<core::DetectionEvent> AimAnalyzer::CheckInstantSnap() {
    size_t n = history_.size();
    if (n < 3) return std::nullopt;

    const auto& s0 = history_[n - 3];
    const auto& s1 = history_[n - 2];
    const auto& s2 = history_[n - 1];

    float dt1 = static_cast<float>(s1.timestamp_ms - s0.timestamp_ms) / 1000.0f;
    float dt2 = static_cast<float>(s2.timestamp_ms - s1.timestamp_ms) / 1000.0f;

    if (dt1 <= 0.0f || dt2 <= 0.0f) return std::nullopt;

    float dist1 = CalculateAngleDistance(s0, s1);
    float dist2 = CalculateAngleDistance(s1, s2);

    float velocity1 = dist1 / dt1; // deg/s
    float velocity2 = dist2 / dt2; // deg/s

    // Condition: Massive angular flick (e.g. >20 deg) in <= 35ms (1-2 ticks)
    // followed immediately by near-complete freeze (< 1.0 deg) or massive velocity deceleration (> 85%)
    // Ignore dead stops caused by game engine pitch clamping at +/-89 deg boundaries
    bool isPitchClamped = (std::abs(s1.pitch) >= 88.0f && std::abs(s2.pitch) >= 88.0f);
    if (isPitchClamped && std::abs(NormalizeAngleDelta(s1.yaw - s0.yaw)) < 5.0f) {
        return std::nullopt;
    }

    bool deadStop = (dist2 < 1.0f);
    bool massiveDecel = (velocity2 < velocity1 * 0.15f);
    if (dist1 >= snap_angle_threshold_ && dt1 <= 0.035f && (deadStop || massiveDecel) && velocity1 > 700.0f) {
        float jerk = (velocity2 - velocity1) / (dt2 > 0.0f ? dt2 : 0.01f);

        core::DetectionEvent ev;
        ev.type = core::DetectionType::KINEMATICS_AIMBOT_SNAP;
        ev.severity = core::Severity::FATAL_BAN;
        ev.component = "Kinematics::SnapDetector";

        std::ostringstream ss;
        ss << "Instantaneous non-human angular snap: delta=" << dist1 << " deg in "
           << (dt1 * 1000.0f) << " ms (velocity=" << velocity1 << " deg/s, dead stop delta="
           << dist2 << " deg, jerk=" << jerk << " deg/s^3)";
        ev.details = ss.str();
        ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(std::time(nullptr));
        ev.threat_score = 100;
        return ev;
    }

    return std::nullopt;
}

std::optional<core::DetectionEvent> AimAnalyzer::CheckPerfectLinearity() {
    size_t n = history_.size();
    if (n < min_samples_) return std::nullopt;

    const auto& start = history_[n - min_samples_];
    const auto& end = history_.back();

    float totalDist = CalculateAngleDistance(start, end);
    // Only analyze sustained movements of at least 10 degrees
    if (totalDist < 10.0f) return std::nullopt;

    // Vector from start to end in 2D angle space
    float vP = end.pitch - start.pitch;
    float vY = NormalizeAngleDelta(end.yaw - start.yaw);
    float lineLenSq = vP * vP + vY * vY;

    if (lineLenSq < 1.0f) return std::nullopt;

    std::vector<float> perpDists;
    perpDists.reserve(min_samples_);

    float maxPerp = 0.0f;
    for (size_t i = n - min_samples_; i < n; ++i) {
        const auto& pt = history_[i];
        float wP = pt.pitch - start.pitch;
        float wY = NormalizeAngleDelta(pt.yaw - start.yaw);

        // Perpendicular distance = |v x w| / |v|
        float cross = std::abs(vP * wY - vY * wP);
        float dPerp = cross / std::sqrt(lineLenSq);

        perpDists.push_back(dPerp);
        if (dPerp > maxPerp) maxPerp = dPerp;
    }

    // Variance of perpendicular distances
    double meanPerp = std::accumulate(perpDists.begin(), perpDists.end(), 0.0) / perpDists.size();
    double varPerp = 0.0;
    for (float d : perpDists) {
        double diff = d - meanPerp;
        varPerp += diff * diff;
    }
    varPerp /= perpDists.size();

    // Robotic linear aimbot exhibits sub-millidegree variance across all trajectory points
    if (varPerp < linearity_threshold_ && maxPerp < 0.10f) {
        core::DetectionEvent ev;
        ev.type = core::DetectionType::KINEMATICS_PERFECT_LINEARITY;
        ev.severity = core::Severity::CRITICAL;
        ev.component = "Kinematics::LinearityAnalyzer";

        std::ostringstream ss;
        ss << "Unnatural robotic linearity detected: total_displacement=" << totalDist
           << " deg, orthogonal_variance=" << varPerp
           << ", max_deviation=" << maxPerp << " deg";
        ev.details = ss.str();
        ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(std::time(nullptr));
        ev.threat_score = 90;
        return ev;
    }

    return std::nullopt;
}

std::optional<core::DetectionEvent> AimAnalyzer::CheckArtificialSmoothing() {
    size_t n = history_.size();
    if (n < min_samples_) return std::nullopt;

    std::vector<float> velocities;
    velocities.reserve(min_samples_ - 1);

    float totalMovement = 0.0f;
    for (size_t i = n - min_samples_ + 1; i < n; ++i) {
        const auto& prev = history_[i - 1];
        const auto& curr = history_[i];

        float dt = static_cast<float>(curr.timestamp_ms - prev.timestamp_ms) / 1000.0f;
        if (dt <= 0.0f) continue;

        float dist = CalculateAngleDistance(prev, curr);
        totalMovement += dist;
        velocities.push_back(dist / dt);
    }

    if (velocities.size() < 6 || totalMovement < 8.0f) {
        return std::nullopt;
    }

    double meanVel = std::accumulate(velocities.begin(), velocities.end(), 0.0) / velocities.size();
    if (meanVel < 30.0) return std::nullopt; // Skip stationary or micro-adjustments

    double varVel = 0.0;
    for (float v : velocities) {
        double diff = v - meanVel;
        varVel += diff * diff;
    }
    varVel /= velocities.size();

    // Relative variance (standard deviation divided by mean)
    double relStdDev = std::sqrt(varVel) / meanVel;

    // If angular velocity is unnaturally constant across an 8-degree sweep (< 2% variance)
    if (relStdDev < 0.02) {
        core::DetectionEvent ev;
        ev.type = core::DetectionType::KINEMATICS_ARTIFICIAL_SMOOTHING;
        ev.severity = core::Severity::HIGH;
        ev.component = "Kinematics::SmoothAimbotFilter";

        std::ostringstream ss;
        ss << "Artificial constant-velocity aim smoothing detected: mean_vel=" << meanVel
           << " deg/s, relative_stddev=" << (relStdDev * 100.0) << "% over "
           << totalMovement << " deg";
        ev.details = ss.str();
        ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(std::time(nullptr));
        ev.threat_score = 80;
        return ev;
    }

    return std::nullopt;
}

KinematicMetrics AimAnalyzer::ComputeCurrentMetrics() const {
    KinematicMetrics m;
    size_t n = history_.size();
    if (n < 2) return m;

    const auto& s0 = history_[n - 2];
    const auto& s1 = history_[n - 1];

    float dt = static_cast<float>(s1.timestamp_ms - s0.timestamp_ms) / 1000.0f;
    if (dt > 0.0f) {
        float dist = CalculateAngleDistance(s0, s1);
        m.angular_velocity = dist / dt;
    }

    if (n >= 3) {
        const auto& s_prev = history_[n - 3];
        float dt_prev = static_cast<float>(s0.timestamp_ms - s_prev.timestamp_ms) / 1000.0f;
        if (dt_prev > 0.0f && dt > 0.0f) {
            float v_prev = CalculateAngleDistance(s_prev, s0) / dt_prev;
            m.angular_acceleration = (m.angular_velocity - v_prev) / dt;

            if (n >= 4) {
                const auto& s_prev2 = history_[n - 4];
                float dt_prev2 = static_cast<float>(s_prev.timestamp_ms - s_prev2.timestamp_ms) / 1000.0f;
                if (dt_prev2 > 0.0f) {
                    float v_prev2 = CalculateAngleDistance(s_prev2, s_prev) / dt_prev2;
                    float a_prev = (v_prev - v_prev2) / dt_prev;
                    m.angular_jerk = (m.angular_acceleration - a_prev) / dt;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // Linearity and velocity variance over the recent window
    size_t windowSize = std::min(n, min_samples_);
    if (windowSize >= 3) {
        const auto& start = history_[n - windowSize];
        const auto& end = history_.back();
        float vP = end.pitch - start.pitch;
        float vY = const_cast<AimAnalyzer*>(this)->NormalizeAngleDelta(end.yaw - start.yaw);
        float lineLenSq = vP * vP + vY * vY;

        if (lineLenSq >= 1.0f) {
            double varSum = 0.0;
            double meanPerp = 0.0;
            std::vector<float> perps;
            perps.reserve(windowSize);
            float sqrtLen = std::sqrt(lineLenSq);

            for (size_t i = n - windowSize; i < n; ++i) {
                const auto& pt = history_[i];
                float wP = pt.pitch - start.pitch;
                float wY = const_cast<AimAnalyzer*>(this)->NormalizeAngleDelta(pt.yaw - start.yaw);
                float cross = std::abs(vP * wY - vY * wP);
                float dPerp = cross / sqrtLen;
                perps.push_back(dPerp);
                meanPerp += dPerp;
            }
            meanPerp /= windowSize;
            for (float d : perps) {
                double diff = d - meanPerp;
                varSum += diff * diff;
            }
            m.linearity_error = static_cast<float>(varSum / windowSize);
        }

        // Velocity variance
        std::vector<float> vels;
        vels.reserve(windowSize - 1);
        double velSum = 0.0;
        for (size_t i = n - windowSize + 1; i < n; ++i) {
            float segDt = static_cast<float>(history_[i].timestamp_ms - history_[i - 1].timestamp_ms) / 1000.0f;
            if (segDt > 0.0f) {
                float segV = CalculateAngleDistance(history_[i - 1], history_[i]) / segDt;
                vels.push_back(segV);
                velSum += segV;
            }
        }
        if (!vels.empty()) {
            double meanV = velSum / vels.size();
            double velVar = 0.0;
            for (float v : vels) {
                double diff = v - meanV;
                velVar += diff * diff;
            }
            m.velocity_variance = static_cast<float>(velVar / vels.size());
        }
    }

    return m;
}

} // namespace noescape::kinematics
```

`src/kinematics/phantom_monitor.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "noescape/kinematics/phantom_monitor.hpp"

#include <cmath>
#include <ctime>
#include <random>
#include <sstream>

namespace noescape::kinematics {

namespace {
constexpr float kDegToRad = 3.14159265358979323846f / 180.0f;
constexpr float kRadToDeg = 180.0f / 3.14159265358979323846f;
}

void PhantomMonitor::AddPhantom(uint32_t id, float x, float y, float z) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    phantoms_.push_back({id, x, y, z});
}

void PhantomMonitor::UpdatePhantom(uint32_t id, float x, float y, float z) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    for (auto& p : phantoms_) {
        if (p.id == id) { p.x = x; p.y = y; p.z = z; return; }
    }
}

void PhantomMonitor::Clear() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    phantoms_.clear();
    tracked_id_ = 0;
    streak_ = 0;
}

size_t PhantomMonitor::Count() const {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    return phantoms_.size();
}

void PhantomMonitor::GenerateAround(float cx, float cy, float cz, float radius, size_t count, uint64_t seed) {
    std::mt19937_64 rng(seed);
    std::uniform_real_distribution<float> ang(0.0f, 2.0f * 3.14159265f);
    std::uniform_real_distribution<float> rad(radius * 0.3f, radius);
    std::uniform_real_distribution<float> dz(-1.0f, 3.0f);
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    uint32_t nextId = 0xF000u + static_cast<uint32_t>(phantoms_.size());
    for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
        float a = ang(rng), r = rad(rng);
        phantoms_.push_back({nextId++, cx + r * std::cos(a), cy + r * std::sin(a), cz + dz(rng)});
    }
}

std::optional<core::DetectionEvent> PhantomMonitor::PushView(float cx, float cy, float cz,
                                                             float pitch_deg, float yaw_deg,
                                                             uint64_t /*timestamp_ms*/) {
    if (!std::isfinite(cx) || !std::isfinite(cy) || !std::isfinite(cz) ||
        !std::isfinite(pitch_deg) || !std::isfinite(yaw_deg)) {
        return std::nullopt;
    }

    const float cp = std::cos(pitch_deg * kDegToRad), sp = std::sin(pitch_deg * kDegToRad);
    const float cyw = std::cos(yaw_deg * kDegToRad), syw = std::sin(yaw_deg * kDegToRad);
    const float fx = cp * cyw, fy = cp * syw, fz = sp; // unit forward vector

    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    uint32_t best = 0;
    float bestAngle = 1e9f;
    for (const auto& p : phantoms_) {
        float dx = p.x - cx, dy = p.y - cy, dz = p.z - cz;
        float len = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz);
        if (len < 1.0f) continue; // too close for a meaningful angle
        float dot = (fx * dx + fy * dy + fz * dz) / len;
        dot = std::fmax(-1.0f, std::fmin(1.0f, dot));
        float angle = std::acos(dot) * kRadToDeg;
        if (angle < bestAngle) { bestAngle = angle; best = p.id; }
    }

    if (best == 0 || bestAngle > tolerance_deg_) {
        streak_ = 0;
        tracked_id_ = 0;
        return std::nullopt;
    }

    if (best != tracked_id_) { tracked_id_ = best; streak_ = 0; }
    if (++streak_ < required_samples_) return std::nullopt;

    streak_ = 0; // rearm: one event per sustained lock
    core::DetectionEvent ev;
    ev.type = core::DetectionType::PHANTOM_ENTITY_TARGETED;
    ev.severity = core::Severity::HIGH;
    ev.component = "Kinematics::PhantomMonitor";
    std::ostringstream ss;
    ss << "Crosshair held on invisible decoy entity #" << best << " within " << bestAngle
       << " deg for " << required_samples_ << " consecutive samples (entity table is being read)";
    ev.details = ss.str();
    ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(std::time(nullptr));
    ev.threat_score = 60;
    return ev;
}

} // namespace noescape::kinematics

```

`src/memory/canary_monitor.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "noescape/memory/canary_monitor.hpp"

#include <cstring>
#include <ctime>
#include <random>
#include <sstream>

#if defined(_WIN32)
#include <windows.h>
#else
#include <sys/mman.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#include <fcntl.h>
#endif

namespace noescape::memory {

namespace {

size_t PageSize() {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    SYSTEM_INFO si;
    GetSystemInfo(&si);
    return si.dwPageSize;
#else
    long ps = sysconf(_SC_PAGESIZE);
    return ps > 0 ? static_cast<size_t>(ps) : 4096;
#endif
}

} // namespace

CanaryMonitor& CanaryMonitor::Instance() {
    static CanaryMonitor* instance = new CanaryMonitor();
    return *instance;
}

CanaryMonitor::~CanaryMonitor() {
    Shutdown();
}

bool CanaryMonitor::Initialize(size_t page_count) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    if (base_) return true;
    if (page_count == 0) page_count = 1;

    const size_t ps = PageSize();
    size_ = page_count * ps;
    alloc_size_ = size_ + 2 * ps;
#if !defined(_WIN32)
    pagemap_fd_ = open("/proc/self/pagemap", O_RDONLY | O_CLOEXEC);
    clear_refs_fd_ = open("/proc/self/clear_refs", O_WRONLY | O_CLOEXEC);
#endif

#if defined(_WIN32)
    // Windows: MEM_WRITE_WATCH records writes from any process, including WriteProcessMemory.
    alloc_base_ = VirtualAlloc(nullptr, alloc_size_, MEM_RESERVE | MEM_COMMIT | MEM_WRITE_WATCH, PAGE_READWRITE);
    if (!alloc_base_) { size_ = 0; return false; }
    base_ = static_cast<uint8_t*>(alloc_base_) + ps;
    // Guard pages are only meaningful on Linux (VMA-merge false positives); Windows tracks per page.
#else
    alloc_base_ = mmap(nullptr, alloc_size_, PROT_NONE, MAP_PRIVATE | MAP_ANONYMOUS, -1, 0);
    if (alloc_base_ == MAP_FAILED) { alloc_base_ = nullptr; size_ = 0; return false; }
    base_ = static_cast<uint8_t*>(alloc_base_) + ps;
    // Canary pages sit between PROT_NONE guards so neighbouring mappings can never merge
    // into this VMA (a merge would set VM_SOFTDIRTY and cause false positives).
    if (mprotect(base_, size_, PROT_READ | PROT_WRITE) != 0) {
        munmap(alloc_base_, alloc_size_);
        alloc_base_ = nullptr; base_ = nullptr; size_ = 0;
        return false;
    }
#endif

    std::random_device rd;
    std::mt19937_64 rng(rd());
    expected_.resize(size_);
    for (size_t i = 0; i < size_; i += 8) {
        uint64_t v = rng();
        std::memcpy(&expected_[i], &v, std::min<size_t>(8, size_ - i));
    }
    std::memcpy(base_, expected_.data(), size_); // also populates every page

#if defined(_WIN32)
    tracking_supported_ = true;
#else
    // Verify soft-dirty tracking really works on this kernel using a throwaway page.
    void* probe = mmap(nullptr, ps * 3, PROT_NONE, MAP_PRIVATE | MAP_ANONYMOUS, -1, 0);
    if (probe != MAP_FAILED) {
        auto* page = static_cast<uint8_t*>(probe) + ps;
        volatile uint8_t* vpage = page;
        mprotect(page, ps, PROT_READ | PROT_WRITE);
        vpage[0] = 1;
        uint8_t* saved = base_;
        size_t savedSize = size_;
        base_ = page; size_ = ps;
        ResetTracking();
        bool cleanAfterReset = !PageWasWritten();
        vpage[0] = 2;
        bool dirtyAfterWrite = PageWasWritten();
        base_ = saved; size_ = savedSize;
        tracking_supported_ = cleanAfterReset && dirtyAfterWrite;
        munmap(probe, ps * 3);
    }
#endif
    ResetTracking();
    return true;
}

void CanaryMonitor::Shutdown() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    if (!alloc_base_) return;
#if defined(_WIN32)
    VirtualFree(alloc_base_, 0, MEM_RELEASE);
#else
    munmap(alloc_base_, alloc_size_);
    if (pagemap_fd_ >= 0) close(pagemap_fd_);
    if (clear_refs_fd_ >= 0) close(clear_refs_fd_);
    pagemap_fd_ = clear_refs_fd_ = -1;
#endif
    alloc_base_ = nullptr;
    base_ = nullptr;
    size_ = 0;
    expected_.clear();
    tracking_supported_ = false;
}

void CanaryMonitor::ResetTracking() {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    if (base_) ResetWriteWatch(base_, size_);
#else
    // "4" clears the soft-dirty bit of every PTE in this process.
    if (clear_refs_fd_ >= 0) {
        ssize_t r = write(clear_refs_fd_, "4\n", 2);
        (void)r;
    }
#endif
}

bool CanaryMonitor::PageWasWritten() {
    if (!base_ || size_ == 0) return false;
#if defined(_WIN32)
    PVOID addrs[64];
    ULONG_PTR count = 64;
    DWORD granularity = 0;
    if (GetWriteWatch(0, base_, size_, addrs, &count, &granularity) == 0) {
        return count > 0;
    }
    return false;
#else
    int fd = pagemap_fd_;
    if (fd < 0) return false;
    const size_t ps = PageSize();
    bool dirty = false;
    for (size_t off = 0; off < size_ && !dirty; off += ps) {
        uint64_t entry = 0;
        off_t idx = static_cast<off_t>((reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(base_) + off) / ps * sizeof(uint64_t));
        if (pread(fd, &entry, sizeof(entry), idx) == static_cast<ssize_t>(sizeof(entry))) {
            dirty = (entry >> 55) & 1ULL; // bit 55: soft-dirty
        }
    }
    return dirty;
#endif
}

std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> CanaryMonitor::Check() {
    std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> events;
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    if (!base_) return events;

    bool contentChanged = std::memcmp(base_, expected_.data(), size_) != 0;
    bool written = tracking_supported_ && PageWasWritten();

    if (contentChanged || written) {
        core::DetectionEvent ev;
        ev.type = core::DetectionType::CANARY_TAMPERED;
        ev.severity = core::Severity::CRITICAL;
        ev.component = "CanaryMonitor";
        std::ostringstream ss;
        ss << "Write to tripwire memory that no legitimate code touches ("
           << (contentChanged ? "content modified" : "page write-tracking flagged")
           << ") - external process memory write suspected";
        ev.details = ss.str();
        ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(std::time(nullptr));
        ev.threat_score = 90;
        events.push_back(ev);

        // Restore pristine content so a repeated write is detected again
        std::memcpy(base_, expected_.data(), size_);
    }

    ResetTracking();
    return events;
}

} // namespace noescape::memory

```

`src/memory/memory_scanner.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "noescape/memory/memory_scanner.hpp"
#include "noescape/core/obfuscation.hpp"
#include <fstream>
#include <sstream>
#include <iostream>
#include <ctime>
#include <iomanip>
#include <cstring>
#include <algorithm>
#include <atomic>
#include <climits>
#include <set>

#if defined(_WIN32)
#include <windows.h>
#include <psapi.h>
#else
#include <unistd.h>
#include <sys/types.h>
#include <csignal>
#include <csetjmp>
#include <pthread.h>
#include <sys/mman.h>
#endif

namespace noescape::memory {

namespace {
#if !defined(_WIN32)
    thread_local sigjmp_buf g_scanJmpBuf;
    thread_local volatile sig_atomic_t g_scanActive = 0;

    static struct sigaction g_oldSegv{};
    static struct sigaction g_oldBus{};
    static std::atomic<bool> g_guardInstalled{false};
    static std::mutex g_scanGuardMutex;

    void ScanSigHandler(int sig, siginfo_t* info, void* ucontext) {
        if (g_scanActive) {
            g_scanActive = 0;
            siglongjmp(g_scanJmpBuf, 1);
        }
        if (sig == SIGSEGV && g_guardInstalled.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
            if ((g_oldSegv.sa_flags & SA_SIGINFO) && g_oldSegv.sa_sigaction) {
                g_oldSegv.sa_sigaction(sig, info, ucontext);
                return;
            } else if (g_oldSegv.sa_handler == SIG_IGN) {
                return;
            } else if (g_oldSegv.sa_handler && g_oldSegv.sa_handler != SIG_DFL) {
                g_oldSegv.sa_handler(sig);
                return;
            }
        } else if (sig == SIGBUS && g_guardInstalled.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
            if ((g_oldBus.sa_flags & SA_SIGINFO) && g_oldBus.sa_sigaction) {
                g_oldBus.sa_sigaction(sig, info, ucontext);
                return;
            } else if (g_oldBus.sa_handler == SIG_IGN) {
                return;
            } else if (g_oldBus.sa_handler && g_oldBus.sa_handler != SIG_DFL) {
                g_oldBus.sa_handler(sig);
                return;
            }
        }
        struct sigaction dfl{};
        dfl.sa_handler = SIG_DFL;
        sigemptyset(&dfl.sa_mask);
        sigaction(sig, &dfl, nullptr);
        raise(sig);
    }

    struct SafeScanGuard {
        std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock;
        bool segvInstalled{false};
        bool busInstalled{false};

        SafeScanGuard() : lock(g_scanGuardMutex) {
            struct sigaction sa{};
            sa.sa_sigaction = ScanSigHandler;
            sa.sa_flags = SA_SIGINFO;
            sigemptyset(&sa.sa_mask);
            if (sigaction(SIGSEGV, &sa, &g_oldSegv) == 0) {
                segvInstalled = true;
            }
            if (sigaction(SIGBUS, &sa, &g_oldBus) == 0) {
                busInstalled = true;
            }
            if (segvInstalled || busInstalled) {
                g_guardInstalled.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
            }
        }

        ~SafeScanGuard() {
            g_guardInstalled.store(false, std::memory_order_release);
            if (segvInstalled) {
                sigaction(SIGSEGV, &g_oldSegv, nullptr);
            }
            if (busInstalled) {
                sigaction(SIGBUS, &g_oldBus, nullptr);
            }
            g_scanActive = 0;
        }
    };
#endif

static bool ScanChunkSafe(uintptr_t chunkStart,
                          size_t chunkSize,
                          const std::vector<const CheatSignature*>& applicable,
                          std::vector<PatternScanner::SignatureMatchResult>& outMatches) {
#if !defined(_WIN32)
    unsigned char probe[1];
    static const size_t pageSize = []() {
        long sz = sysconf(_SC_PAGESIZE);
        return (sz > 0) ? static_cast<size_t>(sz) : 4096UL;
    }();
    uintptr_t pageMask = ~(pageSize - 1);
    uintptr_t pageStart = chunkStart & pageMask;
    uintptr_t pageEnd = (chunkStart + chunkSize - 1) & pageMask;

    if (mincore(reinterpret_cast<void*>(pageStart), pageSize, probe) != 0 ||
        (pageEnd > pageStart && mincore(reinterpret_cast<void*>(pageEnd), pageSize, probe) != 0)) {
        return false;
    }

    if (sigsetjmp(g_scanJmpBuf, 1) != 0) {
        g_scanActive = 0;
        return false;
    }
    g_scanActive = 1;
    outMatches = PatternScanner::ScanSignatures(
        reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t*>(chunkStart),
        chunkSize,
        applicable,
        chunkStart,
        16
    );
    g_scanActive = 0;
    return true;
#else
    __try {
        outMatches = PatternScanner::ScanSignatures(
            reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t*>(chunkStart),
            chunkSize,
            applicable,
            chunkStart,
            16
        );
        return true;
    } __except(EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {
        return false;
    }
#endif
}

} // namespace

MemoryScanner& MemoryScanner::Instance() {
    static MemoryScanner* instance = new MemoryScanner();
    return *instance;
}

void MemoryScanner::RefreshCache() {
    auto regions = QueryMemoryRegionsInternal();
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(cache_mutex_);
    cached_regions_ = std::move(regions);
    cache_valid_ = true;
}

void MemoryScanner::ClearCache() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(cache_mutex_);
    cached_regions_.clear();
    cache_valid_ = false;
}

bool MemoryScanner::IsCacheValid() const {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(cache_mutex_);
    return cache_valid_;
}

std::vector<MemoryRegion> MemoryScanner::QueryMemoryRegions(bool force_refresh) {
    if (!force_refresh) {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(cache_mutex_);
        if (cache_valid_) {
            return cached_regions_;
        }
    }

    auto regions = QueryMemoryRegionsInternal();

    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(cache_mutex_);
        if (cache_valid_) {
            cached_regions_ = regions;
        }
    }

    return regions;
}

std::vector<MemoryRegion> MemoryScanner::QueryMemoryRegionsInternal() {
    std::vector<MemoryRegion> regions;

#if defined(_WIN32)
    SYSTEM_INFO sysInfo;
    GetSystemInfo(&sysInfo);

    uint8_t* p = static_cast<uint8_t*>(sysInfo.lpMinimumApplicationAddress);
    uint8_t* maxAddr = static_cast<uint8_t*>(sysInfo.lpMaximumApplicationAddress);

    while (p < maxAddr) {
        MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION mbi{};
        if (VirtualQuery(p, &mbi, sizeof(mbi)) == 0) {
            p += 0x1000;
            continue;
        }

        if (mbi.State == MEM_COMMIT) {
            MemoryRegion reg;
            reg.start = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(mbi.BaseAddress);
            reg.size = mbi.RegionSize;
            reg.end = reg.start + reg.size;

            reg.readable = (mbi.Protect & (PAGE_READONLY | PAGE_READWRITE | PAGE_EXECUTE_READ | PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE)) != 0;
            reg.writable = (mbi.Protect & (PAGE_READWRITE | PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE | PAGE_WRITECOPY | PAGE_EXECUTE_WRITECOPY)) != 0;
            reg.executable = (mbi.Protect & (PAGE_EXECUTE | PAGE_EXECUTE_READ | PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE | PAGE_EXECUTE_WRITECOPY)) != 0;
            reg.is_shared = (mbi.Type == MEM_MAPPED || mbi.Type == MEM_IMAGE);

            char filePath[MAX_PATH]{};
            if (GetMappedFileNameA(GetCurrentProcess(), mbi.BaseAddress, filePath, MAX_PATH) > 0) {
                reg.pathname = filePath;
                reg.is_backed_by_file = true;
            } else {
                reg.is_backed_by_file = false;
            }

            regions.push_back(reg);
        }

        p = static_cast<uint8_t*>(mbi.BaseAddress) + mbi.RegionSize;
    }

#else
    // Linux: Parse /proc/self/maps
    std::ifstream mapsFile(NOESCAPE_XOR("/proc/self/maps"));
    if (!mapsFile.is_open()) return regions;

    std::string line;
    while (std::getline(mapsFile, line)) {
        if (line.empty()) continue;

        // Example line:
        // 7f814b7e9000-7f814b80b000 r-xp 00000000 08:01 123456 /usr/lib/libc.so.6
        uintptr_t start = 0, end = 0;
        char dash = 0;
        std::string perms, offsetStr, devStr;
        uint64_t inode = 0;
        std::string pathname;

        std::istringstream iss(line);
        if (!(iss >> std::hex >> start >> dash >> end >> perms >> offsetStr >> devStr >> std::dec >> inode)) {
            continue;
        }

        // The remaining text in the line is the pathname (if any)
        std::string remainder;
        if (std::getline(iss, remainder)) {
            size_t firstNonSpace = remainder.find_first_not_of(" \t");
            if (firstNonSpace != std::string::npos) {
                pathname = remainder.substr(firstNonSpace);
            }
        }

        MemoryRegion reg;
        reg.start = start;
        reg.end = end;
        reg.size = (end >= start) ? (end - start) : 0;
        reg.readable = (perms.length() > 0 && perms[0] == 'r');
        reg.writable = (perms.length() > 1 && perms[1] == 'w');
        reg.executable = (perms.length() > 2 && perms[2] == 'x');
        reg.is_shared = (perms.length() > 3 && perms[3] == 's');
        reg.pathname = pathname;
        try { reg.file_offset = std::stoull(offsetStr, nullptr, 16); } catch (...) { reg.file_offset = 0; }
        reg.is_backed_by_file = (inode > 0 && !pathname.empty() && pathname[0] == '/');

        regions.push_back(reg);
    }
#endif

    return regions;
}

std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> MemoryScanner::DetectUnbackedExecutableRegions() {
    std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> events;
    auto regions = QueryMemoryRegions();

    // Check if host process is a JIT runtime (such as Java JVM / HotSpot or explicitly enabled JIT).
    // In Java/Minecraft, HotSpot allocates anonymous executable memory for its JIT CodeCache.
    bool isJitRuntime = false;
#if !defined(_WIN32)
    char exePathBuf[PATH_MAX]{};
    ssize_t exeLen = readlink(NOESCAPE_XOR("/proc/self/exe"), exePathBuf, sizeof(exePathBuf) - 1);
    if (exeLen > 0) {
        exePathBuf[exeLen] = '\0';
        std::string exeStr(exePathBuf);
        std::transform(exeStr.begin(), exeStr.end(), exeStr.begin(), ::tolower);
        if (exeStr.find("java") != std::string::npos ||
            exeStr.find("mono") != std::string::npos ||
            exeStr.find("node") != std::string::npos ||
            exeStr.find("dotnet") != std::string::npos) {
            isJitRuntime = true;
        }
    }
#endif
    for (const auto& r : regions) {
        if (r.pathname.find("libjvm") != std::string::npos ||
            r.pathname.find("libmono") != std::string::npos ||
            r.pathname.find("libv8") != std::string::npos ||
            r.pathname.find("libcoreclr") != std::string::npos) {
            isJitRuntime = true;
            break;
        }
    }
#if defined(NOESCAPE_SECURE_BUILD) || defined(NDEBUG)
    const char* testEnv = std::getenv("NOESCAPE_TEST_MODE");
    if (testEnv && (std::strcmp(testEnv, "1") == 0 || strcasecmp(testEnv, "true") == 0)) {
        const char* allowJitEnv = std::getenv("NOESCAPE_ALLOW_JIT");
        if (allowJitEnv && (std::strcmp(allowJitEnv, "1") == 0 || strcasecmp(allowJitEnv, "true") == 0)) {
            isJitRuntime = true;
        }
    }
#else
    const char* allowJitEnv = std::getenv("NOESCAPE_ALLOW_JIT");
    if (allowJitEnv && (std::strcmp(allowJitEnv, "1") == 0 || strcasecmp(allowJitEnv, "true") == 0)) {
        isJitRuntime = true;
    }
#endif

    for (const auto& r : regions) {
        if (!r.executable) continue;
        if (r.pathname == "[vsyscall]" || r.pathname == "[vdso]") continue;

        bool suspicious = false;
        std::string reason;

        // Condition 1: Executable region not backed by any valid file
        // In JIT runtimes (Java/Minecraft JVM CodeCache), anonymous executable memory is normal.
        if (!r.is_backed_by_file) {
            if (!isJitRuntime) {
                suspicious = true;
                reason = "Anonymous unbacked executable memory segment";
            }
        }
        // Condition 2: File is marked (deleted) on disk
        else if (r.pathname.find("(deleted)") != std::string::npos) {
            // Whitelist legitimate runtime temp extraction such as JNA, LWJGL, Netty, Tracy
            // used by Minecraft / Fabric: /tmp/*.so (deleted) or ~/.cache/JNA/temp/jna*.tmp (deleted)
            bool isLegitRuntimeTemp = (isJitRuntime && (
                r.pathname.find("jna") != std::string::npos ||
                r.pathname.find("JNA") != std::string::npos ||
                r.pathname.find("libjnidispatch") != std::string::npos ||
                r.pathname.find("lwjgl") != std::string::npos ||
                r.pathname.find("glfw") != std::string::npos ||
                r.pathname.find("openal") != std::string::npos ||
                r.pathname.find("jemalloc") != std::string::npos ||
                r.pathname.find("freetype") != std::string::npos ||
                r.pathname.find("tinyfd") != std::string::npos ||
                r.pathname.find("stb") != std::string::npos ||
                r.pathname.find("netty") != std::string::npos ||
                r.pathname.find("tracy") != std::string::npos ||
                r.pathname.find(".so") != std::string::npos
            ));
            if (!isLegitRuntimeTemp) {
                suspicious = true;
                reason = "Executable mapped from unlinked/deleted binary: " + r.pathname;
            }
        }
        // Condition 3: Executable region located in /dev/shm, /tmp, or memfd
        else if (r.pathname.rfind("/dev/shm", 0) == 0 || 
                 r.pathname.rfind("/tmp", 0) == 0 ||
                 r.pathname.rfind("memfd:", 0) == 0) {
            bool isLegitRuntimeTemp = (isJitRuntime && (
                r.pathname.find("jna") != std::string::npos ||
                r.pathname.find("JNA") != std::string::npos ||
                r.pathname.find("libjnidispatch") != std::string::npos ||
                r.pathname.find("lwjgl") != std::string::npos ||
                r.pathname.find("glfw") != std::string::npos ||
                r.pathname.find("openal") != std::string::npos ||
                r.pathname.find("jemalloc") != std::string::npos ||
                r.pathname.find("freetype") != std::string::npos ||
                r.pathname.find("tinyfd") != std::string::npos ||
                r.pathname.find("stb") != std::string::npos ||
                r.pathname.find("netty") != std::string::npos ||
                r.pathname.find("tracy") != std::string::npos ||
                r.pathname.find(".so") != std::string::npos
            ));
            if (!isLegitRuntimeTemp) {
                suspicious = true;
                reason = "Executable payload residing in transient/volatile memory mount: " + r.pathname;
            }
        }
        // Condition 4: Executable stack or heap
        else if (r.pathname == "[stack]" || r.pathname == "[heap]") {
            suspicious = true;
            reason = "Executable stack/heap anomaly detected (W^X violation): " + r.pathname;
        }
        // Condition 5: File-backed executable section marked RWX (Module Stomping)
        else if (r.is_backed_by_file && r.executable && r.writable) {
            suspicious = true;
            reason = "File-backed executable section marked RWX (Module Stomping / In-memory patch): " + r.pathname;
        }

        if (suspicious) {
            // Also elevate severity if region is simultaneously Writable + Executable (RWX)
            bool isRwx = r.writable && r.executable;

            core::DetectionEvent ev;
            ev.type = core::DetectionType::UNBACKED_EXECUTABLE_MEMORY;
            ev.severity = isRwx ? core::Severity::FATAL_BAN : core::Severity::HIGH;
            ev.component = "MemoryScanner::UnbackedPages";
            ev.details = reason + " [0x" + [] (uintptr_t v) {
                std::ostringstream ss;
                ss << std::hex << v;
                return ss.str();
            }(r.start) + " - 0x" + [] (uintptr_t v) {
                std::ostringstream ss;
                ss << std::hex << v;
                return ss.str();
            }(r.end) + "] " + (isRwx ? "(RWX-PROTECTION)" : "(RX-PROTECTION)");
            ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(std::time(nullptr));
            ev.threat_score = isRwx ? 100 : 75;

            events.push_back(ev);
        }
    }

    return events;
}

std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> MemoryScanner::ScanSignatures(const std::vector<CheatSignature>& signatures) {
    std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> events;
    if (signatures.empty()) return events;

    // Collect internal memory spans occupied by signature database structures to prevent
    // false positives against the anti-cheat's own data in heap / anonymous allocations.
    auto internalSpans = SignatureDatabase::GetInternalSpans();
    if (!signatures.empty()) {
        uintptr_t vecStart = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(signatures.data());
        uintptr_t vecEnd = vecStart + signatures.size() * sizeof(CheatSignature);
        internalSpans.emplace_back(vecStart, vecEnd);
        for (const auto& sig : signatures) {
            uintptr_t objStart = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(&sig);
            internalSpans.emplace_back(objStart, objStart + sizeof(CheatSignature));
            if (!sig.GetName().empty()) {
                uintptr_t s = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(sig.GetName().data());
                internalSpans.emplace_back(s, s + sig.GetName().size() + 1);
            }
            if (!sig.GetDescription().empty()) {
                uintptr_t s = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(sig.GetDescription().data());
                internalSpans.emplace_back(s, s + sig.GetDescription().size() + 1);
            }
            if (!sig.GetBytes().empty()) {
                uintptr_t s = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(sig.GetBytes().data());
                internalSpans.emplace_back(s, s + sig.GetBytes().size() * sizeof(PatternByte));
            }
        }
    }

#if !defined(_WIN32)
    pthread_attr_t threadAttr{};
    if (pthread_getattr_np(pthread_self(), &threadAttr) == 0) {
        void* stackAddr = nullptr;
        size_t stackSize = 0;
        if (pthread_attr_getstack(&threadAttr, &stackAddr, &stackSize) == 0 && stackAddr) {
            uintptr_t s = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(stackAddr);
            internalSpans.emplace_back(s, s + stackSize);
        }
        pthread_attr_destroy(&threadAttr);
    }
#else
    ULONG_PTR lowLimit = 0, highLimit = 0;
    GetCurrentThreadStackLimits(&lowLimit, &highLimit);
    if (lowLimit && highLimit) {
        internalSpans.emplace_back(static_cast<uintptr_t>(lowLimit), static_cast<uintptr_t>(highLimit));
    }
#endif

    auto regions = QueryMemoryRegions();

#if !defined(_WIN32)
    SafeScanGuard safeGuard;
#endif

    for (const auto& r : regions) {
        // Skip special kernel mapped regions, device memory, or unreadable/empty segments
        if (!r.readable || r.size == 0) continue;
        if (r.pathname.rfind("[vvar", 0) == 0 || r.pathname == "[vsyscall]" || 
            r.pathname == "[vectors]" || r.pathname == "[vdso]") continue;
        if (r.pathname.rfind("/dev/", 0) == 0) continue;
        // Non-executable heap and thread stacks contain normal runtime allocations, local variables,
        // and string tables. Skip non-executable heap/stack regions to eliminate false positives.
        if (!r.executable && (r.pathname == "[heap]" || r.pathname.rfind("[stack", 0) == 0)) continue;

        const bool genuineFile = r.is_backed_by_file && !r.pathname.empty() && r.pathname[0] == '/' &&
                                 r.pathname.find("(deleted)") == std::string::npos;

        // Filter signatures that are applicable to this region based on its memory target rule
        std::vector<const CheatSignature*> applicable;
        applicable.reserve(signatures.size());
        for (const auto& sig : signatures) {
            if (CheatSignature::IsTargetMatched(sig.GetTargetRule(), genuineFile, r.executable, r.readable)) {
                applicable.push_back(&sig);
            }
        }
        if (applicable.empty()) continue;

        // Chunk large memory blocks into safe segments (8MB) to prevent cheats hiding in >32MB allocations
        constexpr size_t kChunkSize = 8 * 1024 * 1024; // 8 MB safe chunk size
        constexpr size_t kOverlap = 4096;              // 4 KB page overlap to guarantee boundary signatures are detected

        size_t offset = 0;
        std::set<std::pair<uintptr_t, const CheatSignature*>> seenMatches;

        while (offset < r.size) {
            size_t currentChunkSize = std::min(kChunkSize, r.size - offset);
            uintptr_t chunkStart = r.start + offset;

            std::vector<PatternScanner::SignatureMatchResult> matches;
            if (!ScanChunkSafe(chunkStart, currentChunkSize, applicable, matches)) {
                if (currentChunkSize <= kOverlap || offset + currentChunkSize >= r.size) break;
                offset += (currentChunkSize - kOverlap);
                continue;
            }

            for (const auto& match : matches) {
                if (!match.signature) continue;
                const auto& sig = *match.signature;

                // Deduplicate matches that fall in chunk overlap boundary
                if (!seenMatches.insert({match.address, match.signature}).second) {
                    continue;
                }

                // Discard any match that falls within the anti-cheat's own signature table memory
                bool isSelfMatch = false;
                for (const auto& span : internalSpans) {
                    if (match.address < span.second && (match.address + sig.Length()) > span.first) {
                        isSelfMatch = true;
                        break;
                    }
                }
                if (isSelfMatch) continue;

                core::DetectionEvent ev;
                ev.type = core::DetectionType::MEMORY_SIGNATURE_MATCH;
                ev.severity = sig.GetSeverity();
                ev.threat_score = sig.GetThreatScore();
                ev.component = std::string("MemoryScanner::") + CheatSignature::CategoryToString(sig.GetCategory());

                std::ostringstream ss;
                ss << "[" << CheatSignature::CategoryToString(sig.GetCategory()) << "] "
                   << "Blacklisted signature matched: " << sig.GetName()
                   << " at address 0x" << std::hex << match.address
                   << " (Region: " << (r.pathname.empty() ? "[anon]" : r.pathname) << ")";
                if (!sig.GetDescription().empty()) {
                    ss << " - " << sig.GetDescription();
                }
                ev.details = ss.str();
                ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(std::time(nullptr));
                events.push_back(ev);
            }

            if (currentChunkSize <= kOverlap || offset + currentChunkSize >= r.size) {
                break;
            }
            offset += (currentChunkSize - kOverlap);
        }
    }

    return events;
}

std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> MemoryScanner::ScanSignatures(const std::vector<Pattern>& patterns) {
    if (patterns.empty()) return {};
    std::vector<CheatSignature> sigs;
    sigs.reserve(patterns.size());
    for (const auto& pat : patterns) {
        const std::string& n = pat.GetName();
        bool isGeneric = (n.rfind("Generic_", 0) == 0 || n.rfind("PushRet_", 0) == 0);
        sigs.emplace_back(
            pat,
            isGeneric ? SignatureCategory::HOOK_FRAMEWORK : SignatureCategory::GENERIC,
            100u,
            core::Severity::FATAL_BAN,
            isGeneric ? MemoryTargetRule::UNBACKED_ONLY : MemoryTargetRule::ALL_EXECUTABLE,
            "Legacy raw byte pattern"
        );
    }
    return ScanSignatures(sigs);
}

std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> MemoryScanner::DetectModifiedModules() {
    std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> events;
#if defined(_WIN32)
    return events; // PE relocation-aware comparison not implemented yet
#else
    std::vector<MemoryRegion> candidates;
    for (const auto& r : QueryMemoryRegions()) {
        if (!r.executable || !r.readable || !r.is_backed_by_file || r.size == 0) continue;
        if (r.pathname.empty() || r.pathname[0] != '/' || r.pathname.find("(deleted)") != std::string::npos) continue;
        if (r.size > 64u * 1024u * 1024u) continue;
        candidates.push_back(r);
    }
    if (candidates.empty()) return events;

    MemoryRegion r;
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(state_mutex_);
        r = candidates[module_cursor_++ % candidates.size()];
    }

    std::ifstream f(r.pathname, std::ios::binary);
    if (!f.is_open()) return events;
    char magic[4]{};
    f.read(magic, 4);
    if (f.gcount() != 4 || std::memcmp(magic, "\x7f""ELF", 4) != 0) return events;

    std::vector<uint8_t> disk(r.size);
    f.seekg(static_cast<std::streamoff>(r.file_offset));
    f.read(reinterpret_cast<char*>(disk.data()), static_cast<std::streamsize>(r.size));
    size_t got = static_cast<size_t>(f.gcount());
    if (got < 64) return events;

    // Bytes beyond end-of-file are zero-filled by the kernel; compare only file-backed span.
    const auto* mem = reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t*>(r.start);
    size_t firstDiff = got;
    size_t diffCount = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < got; ++i) {
        if (mem[i] != disk[i]) {
            if (firstDiff == got) firstDiff = i;
            ++diffCount;
        }
    }

    if (diffCount > 0) {
        core::DetectionEvent ev;
        ev.type = core::DetectionType::INLINE_HOOK_DETECTED;
        // Legitimate overlays (Steam/Discord/RTSS) patch code too: elevated score, not an instant ban.
        ev.severity = core::Severity::HIGH;
        ev.component = "MemoryScanner::ModuleIntegrity";
        std::ostringstream ss;
        ss << "In-memory executable differs from on-disk image: " << r.pathname
           << " (" << diffCount << " bytes differ, first at +0x" << std::hex << firstDiff << ")";
        ev.details = ss.str();
        ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(std::time(nullptr));
        ev.threat_score = 50;
        events.push_back(ev);
    }
    return events;
#endif
}

std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> MemoryScanner::DetectSleepingPayloads() {
    std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> events;
    auto regions = QueryMemoryRegions();

    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(state_mutex_);
    std::map<uintptr_t, uintptr_t> current;
    for (const auto& r : regions) {
        if (r.executable) current[r.start] = r.end;
    }

    for (const auto& r : regions) {
        if (r.readable || r.writable || r.executable || r.is_backed_by_file) continue;
        auto it = seen_exec_regions_.find(r.start);
        if (it == seen_exec_regions_.end()) continue;
        // Anonymous region that was executable earlier and is now fully inaccessible
        core::DetectionEvent ev;
        ev.type = core::DetectionType::UNBACKED_EXECUTABLE_MEMORY;
        ev.severity = core::Severity::HIGH;
        ev.component = "MemoryScanner::SleepingPayload";
        std::ostringstream ss;
        ss << "Previously executable anonymous region made inaccessible (sleep-obfuscation pattern) [0x"
           << std::hex << r.start << " - 0x" << r.end << "]";
        ev.details = ss.str();
        ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(std::time(nullptr));
        ev.threat_score = 70;
        events.push_back(ev);
    }

    for (const auto& kv : current) seen_exec_regions_[kv.first] = kv.second;
    // Drop entries whose mapping vanished entirely (munmap) to avoid stale state
    for (auto it = seen_exec_regions_.begin(); it != seen_exec_regions_.end();) {
        bool mapped = false;
        for (const auto& r : regions) if (r.start == it->first) { mapped = true; break; }
        it = mapped ? std::next(it) : seen_exec_regions_.erase(it);
    }
    return events;
}

std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> MemoryScanner::PerformAudit(const std::vector<CheatSignature>& signatures) {
    bool hadCache = IsCacheValid();
    if (!hadCache) {
        RefreshCache();
    }
    auto events = DetectUnbackedExecutableRegions();
    auto sigEvents = ScanSignatures(signatures);
    events.insert(events.end(), sigEvents.begin(), sigEvents.end());
    auto modEvents = DetectModifiedModules();
    events.insert(events.end(), modEvents.begin(), modEvents.end());
    auto sleepEvents = DetectSleepingPayloads();
    events.insert(events.end(), sleepEvents.begin(), sleepEvents.end());
    if (!hadCache) {
        ClearCache();
    }
    return events;
}

std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> MemoryScanner::PerformAudit(const std::vector<Pattern>& patterns) {
    bool hadCache = IsCacheValid();
    if (!hadCache) {
        RefreshCache();
    }
    auto events = DetectUnbackedExecutableRegions();
    auto sigEvents = ScanSignatures(patterns);
    events.insert(events.end(), sigEvents.begin(), sigEvents.end());
    auto modEvents = DetectModifiedModules();
    events.insert(events.end(), modEvents.begin(), modEvents.end());
    auto sleepEvents = DetectSleepingPayloads();
    events.insert(events.end(), sleepEvents.begin(), sleepEvents.end());
    if (!hadCache) {
        ClearCache();
    }
    return events;
}

std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> MemoryScanner::PerformAudit() {
    return PerformAudit(GetDefaultCheatSignatures());
}

std::vector<CheatSignature> MemoryScanner::GetDefaultCheatSignatures() {
    return SignatureDatabase::GetAllSignatures();
}

std::vector<Pattern> MemoryScanner::GetDefaultCheatPatterns() {
    return SignatureDatabase::ToPatterns(SignatureDatabase::GetAllSignatures());
}

} // namespace noescape::memory
```
