`src/memory/pattern_scanner.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "noescape/memory/pattern_scanner.hpp"
#include "noescape/memory/cheat_signature.hpp"
#include <sstream>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cstring>
#include <bit>

#if defined(__x86_64__) || defined(_M_X64) || defined(__i386__) || defined(_M_IX86)
#include <immintrin.h>
#define NOESCAPE_X86 1
#endif

namespace noescape::memory {

Pattern::Pattern(std::string_view ida_pattern, std::string name) 
    : name_(std::move(name)) {
    std::string token;
    std::istringstream stream{std::string(ida_pattern)};

    while (stream >> token) {
        if (token == "?" || token == "??") {
            bytes_.push_back({0, true});
        } else {
            auto val = static_cast<uint8_t>(std::strtoul(token.c_str(), nullptr, 16));
            bytes_.push_back({val, false});
        }
    }

    // Determine first non-wildcard byte
    has_non_wildcard_ = false;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < bytes_.size(); ++i) {
        if (!bytes_[i].is_wildcard) {
            first_byte_ = bytes_[i].value;
            first_byte_offset_ = i;
            has_non_wildcard_ = true;
            break;
        }
    }
}

Pattern::Pattern(const uint8_t* bytes, const char* mask, std::string name)
    : name_(std::move(name)) {
    if (!bytes || !mask) return;
    size_t len = std::strlen(mask);
    bytes_.reserve(len);

    for (size_t i = 0; i < len; ++i) {
        bool wildcard = (mask[i] == '?');
        bytes_.push_back({bytes[i], wildcard});
    }

    has_non_wildcard_ = false;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < bytes_.size(); ++i) {
        if (!bytes_[i].is_wildcard) {
            first_byte_ = bytes_[i].value;
            first_byte_offset_ = i;
            has_non_wildcard_ = true;
            break;
        }
    }
}

inline bool PatternScanner::MatchAt(const uint8_t* ptr, const Pattern& pattern) {
    const auto& bytes = pattern.GetBytes();
    const size_t len = bytes.size();
    for (size_t i = 0; i < len; ++i) {
        if (!bytes[i].is_wildcard && ptr[i] != bytes[i].value) {
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

const uint8_t* PatternScanner::ScanScalar(
    const uint8_t* data, 
    size_t size, 
    const Pattern& pattern
) {
    if (size < pattern.Length()) return nullptr;

    const size_t max_scan = size - pattern.Length();
    if (!pattern.HasNonWildcard()) {
        // Pattern contains exclusively wildcards; matches immediately at offset 0
        return data;
    }

    const uint8_t first = pattern.FirstByte();
    const size_t first_offset = pattern.FirstByteOffset();

    for (size_t i = 0; i <= max_scan; ++i) {
        if (data[i + first_offset] == first && MatchAt(data + i, pattern)) {
            return data + i;
        }
    }
    return nullptr;
}

const uint8_t* PatternScanner::ScanSimd(
    const uint8_t* data, 
    size_t size, 
    const Pattern& pattern
) {
    if (size < pattern.Length()) return nullptr;
    if (!pattern.HasNonWildcard()) {
        return data;
    }

#if defined(NOESCAPE_X86)
    const size_t pattern_len = pattern.Length();
    const size_t max_scan = size - pattern_len;
    const uint8_t first = pattern.FirstByte();
    const size_t first_offset = pattern.FirstByteOffset();

#if defined(__AVX2__)
    const __m256i match_vec256 = _mm256_set1_epi8(static_cast<char>(first));
    size_t i = 0;

    // Fast-path AVX2 32-byte chunks
    while (i + first_offset + 32 <= size && i <= max_scan) {
        __m256i block = _mm256_loadu_si256(reinterpret_cast<const __m256i*>(data + i + first_offset));
        __m256i cmp = _mm256_cmpeq_epi8(block, match_vec256);
        uint32_t mask = static_cast<uint32_t>(_mm256_movemask_epi8(cmp));

        while (mask != 0) {
            int bit = std::countr_zero(mask);
            size_t match_cand = i + bit;
            if (match_cand <= max_scan && MatchAt(data + match_cand, pattern)) {
                return data + match_cand;
            }
            mask &= mask - 1; // Clear lowest set bit
        }
        i += 32;
    }

    // Process leftover with SSE2 or scalar
    if (i <= max_scan) {
        auto res = ScanScalar(data + i, size - i, pattern);
        if (res) return res;
    }
    return nullptr;

#elif defined(__SSE2__)
    const __m128i match_vec128 = _mm_set1_epi8(static_cast<char>(first));
    size_t i = 0;

    // Fast-path SSE2 16-byte chunks
    while (i + first_offset + 16 <= size && i <= max_scan) {
        __m128i block = _mm_loadu_si128(reinterpret_cast<const __m128i*>(data + i + first_offset));
        __m128i cmp = _mm_cmpeq_epi8(block, match_vec128);
        uint32_t mask = static_cast<uint32_t>(_mm_movemask_epi8(cmp));

        while (mask != 0) {
            int bit = std::countr_zero(mask);
            size_t match_cand = i + bit;
            if (match_cand <= max_scan && MatchAt(data + match_cand, pattern)) {
                return data + match_cand;
            }
            mask &= mask - 1;
        }
        i += 16;
    }

    if (i <= max_scan) {
        auto res = ScanScalar(data + i, size - i, pattern);
        if (res) return res;
    }
    return nullptr;

#else
    return ScanScalar(data, size, pattern);
#endif

#else
    return ScanScalar(data, size, pattern);
#endif
}

std::optional<uintptr_t> PatternScanner::Scan(
    const uint8_t* data, 
    size_t size, 
    const Pattern& pattern,
    uintptr_t base_address
) {
    if (!data || size == 0 || pattern.Empty()) return std::nullopt;

    const uint8_t* match = ScanSimd(data, size, pattern);
    if (match) {
        return base_address + static_cast<uintptr_t>(match - data);
    }
    return std::nullopt;
}

std::vector<uintptr_t> PatternScanner::ScanAll(
    const uint8_t* data, 
    size_t size, 
    const Pattern& pattern,
    uintptr_t base_address,
    size_t max_results
) {
    std::vector<uintptr_t> results;
    if (!data || size == 0 || pattern.Empty()) return results;

    const uint8_t* current = data;
    size_t remaining = size;

    while (remaining >= pattern.Length() && results.size() < max_results) {
        const uint8_t* match = ScanSimd(current, remaining, pattern);
        if (!match) break;

        results.push_back(base_address + static_cast<uintptr_t>(match - data));
        size_t consumed = (match - current) + 1;
        current += consumed;
        remaining -= consumed;
    }

    return results;
}

std::vector<PatternScanner::MatchResult> PatternScanner::ScanBatch(
    const uint8_t* data,
    size_t size,
    const std::vector<Pattern>& patterns,
    uintptr_t base_address,
    size_t max_results
) {
    std::vector<MatchResult> batch_results;
    if (!data || size == 0 || patterns.empty()) return batch_results;

    for (const auto& pat : patterns) {
        if (batch_results.size() >= max_results) break;
        auto matches = ScanAll(data, size, pat, base_address, max_results - batch_results.size());
        for (auto addr : matches) {
            batch_results.push_back({pat.GetName(), addr});
        }
    }

    return batch_results;
}

std::vector<PatternScanner::MatchResult> PatternScanner::ScanBatch(
    const uint8_t* data,
    size_t size,
    const std::vector<CheatSignature>& signatures,
    uintptr_t base_address,
    size_t max_results
) {
    std::vector<MatchResult> batch_results;
    if (!data || size == 0 || signatures.empty()) return batch_results;

    for (const auto& sig : signatures) {
        if (batch_results.size() >= max_results) break;
        auto matches = ScanAll(data, size, sig, base_address, max_results - batch_results.size());
        for (auto addr : matches) {
            batch_results.push_back({sig.GetName(), addr});
        }
    }

    return batch_results;
}

std::vector<PatternScanner::SignatureMatchResult> PatternScanner::ScanSignatures(
    const uint8_t* data,
    size_t size,
    const std::vector<CheatSignature>& signatures,
    uintptr_t base_address,
    size_t max_results
) {
    std::vector<SignatureMatchResult> results;
    if (!data || size == 0 || signatures.empty()) return results;

    for (const auto& sig : signatures) {
        if (results.size() >= max_results) break;
        auto matches = ScanAll(data, size, sig, base_address, max_results - results.size());
        for (auto addr : matches) {
            results.push_back({&sig, sig.GetName(), addr});
        }
    }

    return results;
}

std::vector<PatternScanner::SignatureMatchResult> PatternScanner::ScanSignatures(
    const uint8_t* data,
    size_t size,
    const std::vector<const CheatSignature*>& signatures,
    uintptr_t base_address,
    size_t max_results
) {
    std::vector<SignatureMatchResult> results;
    if (!data || size == 0 || signatures.empty()) return results;

    for (const auto* sig : signatures) {
        if (!sig) continue;
        if (results.size() >= max_results) break;
        auto matches = ScanAll(data, size, *sig, base_address, max_results - results.size());
        for (auto addr : matches) {
            results.push_back({sig, sig->GetName(), addr});
        }
    }

    return results;
}

} // namespace noescape::memory
```

`src/memory/signature_database.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "noescape/memory/signature_database.hpp"
#include <mutex>
#include <algorithm>

namespace noescape::memory {

const char* CheatSignature::CategoryToString(SignatureCategory cat) {
    switch (cat) {
        case SignatureCategory::HOOK_FRAMEWORK: return "HOOK_FRAMEWORK";
        case SignatureCategory::INJECTOR_STUB: return "INJECTOR_STUB";
        case SignatureCategory::DMA_TOOL: return "DMA_TOOL";
        case SignatureCategory::DEBUGGER_INTERFACE: return "DEBUGGER_INTERFACE";
        case SignatureCategory::SHELLCODE_PROLOGUE: return "SHELLCODE_PROLOGUE";
        case SignatureCategory::OVERLAY_HOOK: return "OVERLAY_HOOK";
        case SignatureCategory::PACKER_PROTECTOR: return "PACKER_PROTECTOR";
        case SignatureCategory::SPEEDHACK_WRAPPER: return "SPEEDHACK_WRAPPER";
        case SignatureCategory::GENERIC:
        default: return "GENERIC";
    }
}

const char* CheatSignature::RuleToString(MemoryTargetRule rule) {
    switch (rule) {
        case MemoryTargetRule::UNBACKED_ONLY: return "UNBACKED_ONLY";
        case MemoryTargetRule::ALL_EXECUTABLE: return "ALL_EXECUTABLE";
        case MemoryTargetRule::ANONYMOUS_DATA: return "ANONYMOUS_DATA";
        case MemoryTargetRule::ANY_ANONYMOUS: return "ANY_ANONYMOUS";
        case MemoryTargetRule::ALL_REGIONS: return "ALL_REGIONS";
        default: return "UNKNOWN";
    }
}

bool CheatSignature::IsTargetMatched(MemoryTargetRule rule, bool isGenuineFile, bool isExecutable, bool isReadable) {
    if (!isReadable) return false;
    switch (rule) {
        case MemoryTargetRule::UNBACKED_ONLY:
            return !isGenuineFile && isExecutable;
        case MemoryTargetRule::ALL_EXECUTABLE:
            return isExecutable;
        case MemoryTargetRule::ANONYMOUS_DATA:
            return !isGenuineFile && !isExecutable;
        case MemoryTargetRule::ANY_ANONYMOUS:
            return !isGenuineFile;
        case MemoryTargetRule::ALL_REGIONS:
            return true;
        default:
            return !isGenuineFile && isExecutable;
    }
}

std::vector<CheatSignature> SignatureDatabase::GetAllSignatures() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_dbMutex);
    if (!g_initialized) {
        g_signatures = CreateDefaultSignatures();
        g_initialized = true;
    }
    return g_signatures;
}

std::vector<CheatSignature> SignatureDatabase::GetByCategory(SignatureCategory category) {
    auto all = GetAllSignatures();
    std::vector<CheatSignature> filtered;
    for (const auto& sig : all) {
        if (sig.GetCategory() == category) {
            filtered.push_back(sig);
        }
    }
    return filtered;
}

std::vector<CheatSignature> SignatureDatabase::GetByTargetRule(MemoryTargetRule rule) {
    auto all = GetAllSignatures();
    std::vector<CheatSignature> filtered;
    for (const auto& sig : all) {
        if (sig.GetTargetRule() == rule) {
            filtered.push_back(sig);
        }
    }
    return filtered;
}

std::optional<CheatSignature> SignatureDatabase::FindByName(std::string_view name) {
    auto all = GetAllSignatures();
    for (const auto& sig : all) {
        if (sig.GetName() == name) {
            return sig;
        }
    }
    return std::nullopt;
}

void SignatureDatabase::RegisterCustomSignature(CheatSignature signature) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_dbMutex);
    if (!g_initialized) {
        g_signatures = CreateDefaultSignatures();
        g_initialized = true;
    }
    // Update existing if matching name, otherwise push
    auto it = std::find_if(g_signatures.begin(), g_signatures.end(), [&](const CheatSignature& s) {
        return s.GetName() == signature.GetName();
    });
    if (it != g_signatures.end()) {
        *it = std::move(signature);
    } else {
        g_signatures.push_back(std::move(signature));
    }
}

void SignatureDatabase::Reset() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_dbMutex);
    g_signatures = CreateDefaultSignatures();
    g_initialized = true;
}

size_t SignatureDatabase::Count() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_dbMutex);
    if (!g_initialized) {
        g_signatures = CreateDefaultSignatures();
        g_initialized = true;
    }
    return g_signatures.size();
}

std::vector<Pattern> SignatureDatabase::ToPatterns(const std::vector<CheatSignature>& signatures) {
    std::vector<Pattern> patterns;
    patterns.reserve(signatures.size());
    for (const auto& sig : signatures) {
        patterns.push_back(sig);
    }
    return patterns;
}

std::vector<std::pair<uintptr_t, uintptr_t>> SignatureDatabase::GetInternalSpans() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_dbMutex);
    std::vector<std::pair<uintptr_t, uintptr_t>> spans;
    if (!g_initialized) {
        g_signatures = CreateDefaultSignatures();
        g_initialized = true;
    }
    if (!g_signatures.empty()) {
        uintptr_t vecStart = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(g_signatures.data());
        uintptr_t vecEnd = vecStart + g_signatures.size() * sizeof(CheatSignature);
        spans.emplace_back(vecStart, vecEnd);
    }
    for (const auto& sig : g_signatures) {
        uintptr_t objStart = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(&sig);
        spans.emplace_back(objStart, objStart + sizeof(CheatSignature));
        if (!sig.GetName().empty()) {
            uintptr_t s = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(sig.GetName().data());
            spans.emplace_back(s, s + sig.GetName().size() + 1);
        }
        if (!sig.GetDescription().empty()) {
            uintptr_t s = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(sig.GetDescription().data());
            spans.emplace_back(s, s + sig.GetDescription().size() + 1);
        }
        if (!sig.GetBytes().empty()) {
            uintptr_t s = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(sig.GetBytes().data());
            spans.emplace_back(s, s + sig.GetBytes().size() * sizeof(PatternByte));
        }
    }
    return spans;
}

} // namespace noescape::memory

```

`src/memory/unhooker.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "noescape/memory/unhooker.hpp"
#include "noescape/memory/memory_scanner.hpp"
#include <cstring>
#include <ctime>
#include <fstream>
#include <sstream>
#include <iomanip>

#if defined(_WIN32)
#include <windows.h>
#else
#include <dlfcn.h>
#include <elf.h>
#include <sys/mman.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#endif

namespace noescape::memory {

Unhooker& Unhooker::Instance() {
    static Unhooker* instance = new Unhooker();
    return *instance;
}

namespace {

bool IsReadableAddress(uintptr_t target, size_t len = sizeof(uintptr_t)) {
    if (target < 0x10000) return false;
#if defined(_WIN32)
    MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION mbi{};
    if (VirtualQuery(reinterpret_cast<LPCVOID>(target), &mbi, sizeof(mbi)) != 0) {
        if (mbi.State == MEM_COMMIT && !(mbi.Protect & PAGE_NOACCESS) && !(mbi.Protect & PAGE_GUARD)) {
            return (mbi.Protect & (PAGE_READONLY | PAGE_READWRITE | PAGE_EXECUTE_READ | PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE)) != 0;
        }
    }
    return false;
#else
    auto regions = MemoryScanner::Instance().QueryMemoryRegions();
    for (const auto& r : regions) {
        if (r.readable && target >= r.start && (target + len) <= r.end) {
            return true;
        }
    }
    return false;
#endif
}

} // namespace

std::optional<HookInfo> Unhooker::InspectFunction(const char* name, void* function_ptr) {
    if (!function_ptr) return std::nullopt;

    uintptr_t addr = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(function_ptr);
    if (addr < 0x10000 || !IsReadableAddress(addr, 16)) {
        return std::nullopt;
    }

    const auto* code = static_cast<const uint8_t*>(function_ptr);

    // Pattern 1: E9 xx xx xx xx -> JMP rel32
    if (code[0] == 0xE9) {
        int32_t relOffset = 0;
        std::memcpy(&relOffset, code + 1, sizeof(relOffset));
        uintptr_t dest = addr + 5 + relOffset;

        HookInfo info;
        info.function_name = name ? name : "Unknown";
        info.function_address = addr;
        info.hook_type = "JMP_REL32 (0xE9)";
        info.target_destination = dest;
        info.patched_bytes.assign(code, code + 5);
        return info;
    }

    // Pattern 2: EB xx -> JMP rel8 (short jump to hook stub)
    if (code[0] == 0xEB) {
        int8_t rel = static_cast<int8_t>(code[1]);
        uintptr_t dest = addr + 2 + rel;

        HookInfo info;
        info.function_name = name ? name : "Unknown";
        info.function_address = addr;
        info.hook_type = "JMP_REL8 (0xEB)";
        info.target_destination = dest;
        info.patched_bytes.assign(code, code + 2);
        return info;
    }

    // Pattern 3: FF 25 xx xx xx xx -> JMP [RIP + disp32] (x64 indirect jump)
    if (code[0] == 0xFF && code[1] == 0x25) {
        int32_t disp = 0;
        std::memcpy(&disp, code + 2, sizeof(disp));
        uintptr_t targetPtr = addr + 6 + disp;
        uintptr_t dest = 0;
        // Verify address mapping safely before dereferencing to prevent SIGSEGV
        if (IsReadableAddress(targetPtr, sizeof(dest))) {
            std::memcpy(&dest, reinterpret_cast<const void*>(targetPtr), sizeof(dest));
        }

        HookInfo info;
        info.function_name = name ? name : "Unknown";
        info.function_address = addr;
        info.hook_type = "JMP_RIP_DISP32 (0xFF 0x25)";
        info.target_destination = dest;
        info.patched_bytes.assign(code, code + 6);
        return info;
    }

    // Pattern 4: 48 B8 [imm64] FF E0 -> MOV RAX, imm64; JMP RAX
    if (code[0] == 0x48 && code[1] == 0xB8 && code[10] == 0xFF && code[11] == 0xE0) {
        uint64_t dest = 0;
        std::memcpy(&dest, code + 2, sizeof(dest));

        HookInfo info;
        info.function_name = name ? name : "Unknown";
        info.function_address = addr;
        info.hook_type = "MOV_RAX_JMP_RAX (0x48 0xB8 ... 0xFF 0xE0)";
        info.target_destination = static_cast<uintptr_t>(dest);
        info.patched_bytes.assign(code, code + 12);
        return info;
    }

    // Pattern 5: 49 BA [imm64] 41 FF E2 -> MOV R10, imm64; JMP R10 (MinHook / Detours 64-bit jump)
    if (code[0] == 0x49 && code[1] == 0xBA && code[10] == 0x41 && code[11] == 0xFF && code[12] == 0xE2) {
        uint64_t dest = 0;
        std::memcpy(&dest, code + 2, sizeof(dest));

        HookInfo info;
        info.function_name = name ? name : "Unknown";
        info.function_address = addr;
        info.hook_type = "MOV_R10_JMP_R10 (0x49 0xBA ... 0x41 0xFF 0xE2)";
        info.target_destination = static_cast<uintptr_t>(dest);
        info.patched_bytes.assign(code, code + 13);
        return info;
    }

    // Pattern 6: 49 BB [imm64] 41 FF E3 -> MOV R11, imm64; JMP R11
    if (code[0] == 0x49 && code[1] == 0xBB && code[10] == 0x41 && code[11] == 0xFF && code[12] == 0xE3) {
        uint64_t dest = 0;
        std::memcpy(&dest, code + 2, sizeof(dest));

        HookInfo info;
        info.function_name = name ? name : "Unknown";
        info.function_address = addr;
        info.hook_type = "MOV_R11_JMP_R11 (0x49 0xBB ... 0x41 0xFF 0xE3)";
        info.target_destination = static_cast<uintptr_t>(dest);
        info.patched_bytes.assign(code, code + 13);
        return info;
    }

    // Pattern 7: 68 [imm32_low] C7 44 24 04 [imm32_high] C3 -> Push-Ret x64 Jump
    if (code[0] == 0x68 && code[5] == 0xC7 && code[6] == 0x44 && code[7] == 0x24 && code[8] == 0x04 && code[13] == 0xC3) {
        uint32_t low = 0, high = 0;
        std::memcpy(&low, code + 1, sizeof(low));
        std::memcpy(&high, code + 9, sizeof(high));
        uint64_t dest = (static_cast<uint64_t>(high) << 32) | low;

        HookInfo info;
        info.function_name = name ? name : "Unknown";
        info.function_address = addr;
        info.hook_type = "PUSH_RET_X64 (0x68 ... 0xC3)";
        info.target_destination = static_cast<uintptr_t>(dest);
        info.patched_bytes.assign(code, code + 14);
        return info;
    }

    // Pattern 8: CC -> Software Breakpoint (INT 3)
    if (code[0] == 0xCC) {
        HookInfo info;
        info.function_name = name ? name : "Unknown";
        info.function_address = addr;
        info.hook_type = "SOFTWARE_BREAKPOINT (0xCC)";
        info.target_destination = 0;
        info.patched_bytes.assign(code, code + 1);
        return info;
    }

    return std::nullopt;
}

std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> Unhooker::AuditSystemHooks() {
    std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> events;

    std::vector<std::pair<const char*, void*>> apisToCheck;

#if defined(_WIN32)
    HMODULE hNtdll = GetModuleHandleA("ntdll.dll");
    if (hNtdll) {
        apisToCheck.emplace_back("NtQueryInformationProcess", GetProcAddress(hNtdll, "NtQueryInformationProcess"));
        apisToCheck.emplace_back("NtSetInformationThread", GetProcAddress(hNtdll, "NtSetInformationThread"));
        apisToCheck.emplace_back("NtProtectVirtualMemory", GetProcAddress(hNtdll, "NtProtectVirtualMemory"));
    }
    HMODULE hKernel32 = GetModuleHandleA("kernel32.dll");
    if (hKernel32) {
        apisToCheck.emplace_back("CreateRemoteThread", GetProcAddress(hKernel32, "CreateRemoteThread"));
        apisToCheck.emplace_back("VirtualProtect", GetProcAddress(hKernel32, "VirtualProtect"));
    }
#else
    apisToCheck.emplace_back("read", dlsym(RTLD_DEFAULT, "read"));
    apisToCheck.emplace_back("write", dlsym(RTLD_DEFAULT, "write"));
    apisToCheck.emplace_back("open", dlsym(RTLD_DEFAULT, "open"));
    apisToCheck.emplace_back("mprotect", dlsym(RTLD_DEFAULT, "mprotect"));
    apisToCheck.emplace_back("ptrace", dlsym(RTLD_DEFAULT, "ptrace"));
#endif

    for (const auto& [apiName, pfn] : apisToCheck) {
        if (!pfn) continue;

        auto hook = InspectFunction(apiName, pfn);
        if (hook) {
            bool restored = AutoRestoreHook(*hook);

            core::DetectionEvent ev;
            ev.type = core::DetectionType::INLINE_HOOK_DETECTED;
            ev.severity = core::Severity::CRITICAL;
            ev.component = "Unhooker::InlineInterceptor";

            std::ostringstream ss;
            ss << "Inline hook detected on routine '" << hook->function_name 
               << "' [0x" << std::hex << hook->function_address << "] "
               << "Hook Type: " << hook->hook_type 
               << " -> Target: 0x" << hook->target_destination;
            if (restored) {
                ss << " [RESTORATION SUCCESS: Function prologue neutralized and restored to clean state]";
            }
            ev.details = ss.str();
            ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(std::time(nullptr));
            ev.threat_score = 90;
            events.push_back(ev);
        }
    }

    return events;
}

bool Unhooker::RestoreHook(const HookInfo& hook, const uint8_t* original_bytes, size_t length) {
    if (hook.function_address == 0 || !original_bytes || length == 0) return false;

    void* targetAddr = reinterpret_cast<void*>(hook.function_address);

#if defined(_WIN32)
    DWORD oldProtect = 0;
    if (!VirtualProtect(targetAddr, length, PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE, &oldProtect)) {
        return false;
    }
    std::memcpy(targetAddr, original_bytes, length);
    VirtualProtect(targetAddr, length, oldProtect, &oldProtect);
    FlushInstructionCache(GetCurrentProcess(), targetAddr, length);
    return true;
#else
    long pageSize = sysconf(_SC_PAGESIZE);
    if (pageSize <= 0) pageSize = 4096;

    uintptr_t pageStart = hook.function_address & ~(pageSize - 1);
    size_t mappingLen = (hook.function_address + length - pageStart + pageSize - 1) & ~(pageSize - 1);

    int originalProt = PROT_READ | PROT_EXEC;
    for (const auto& reg : MemoryScanner::Instance().QueryMemoryRegions()) {
        if (hook.function_address >= reg.start && hook.function_address < reg.end) {
            originalProt = 0;
            if (reg.readable) originalProt |= PROT_READ;
            if (reg.writable) originalProt |= PROT_WRITE;
            if (reg.executable) originalProt |= PROT_EXEC;
            break;
        }
    }

    if (mprotect(reinterpret_cast<void*>(pageStart), mappingLen, PROT_READ | PROT_WRITE | PROT_EXEC) != 0) {
        return false;
    }

    std::memcpy(targetAddr, original_bytes, length);

    mprotect(reinterpret_cast<void*>(pageStart), mappingLen, originalProt);
    __builtin___clear_cache(static_cast<char*>(targetAddr), static_cast<char*>(targetAddr) + length);
    return true;
#endif
}

bool Unhooker::AutoRestoreHook(const HookInfo& hook) {
    if (hook.function_address == 0) return false;

#if defined(_WIN32)
    // PE file offsets differ from RVAs (section alignment); restoring from a naive
    // offset would corrupt code, so automatic restoration is disabled until a
    // section-aware mapping is implemented.
    return false;
#else
    Dl_info dlinfo{};
    if (!dladdr(reinterpret_cast<void*>(hook.function_address), &dlinfo) || !dlinfo.dli_fname || !dlinfo.dli_fbase) {
        return false;
    }

    std::ifstream diskFile(dlinfo.dli_fname, std::ios::binary);
    if (!diskFile.is_open()) return false;

    Elf64_Ehdr eh{};
    diskFile.read(reinterpret_cast<char*>(&eh), sizeof(eh));
    if (diskFile.gcount() != static_cast<std::streamsize>(sizeof(eh)) ||
        std::memcmp(eh.e_ident, ELFMAG, SELFMAG) != 0 || eh.e_ident[EI_CLASS] != ELFCLASS64) {
        return false;
    }

    // Translate virtual address -> file offset through PT_LOAD program headers
    const uint64_t vaddr = hook.function_address - reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(dlinfo.dli_fbase);
    for (uint16_t i = 0; i < eh.e_phnum; ++i) {
        Elf64_Phdr ph{};
        diskFile.seekg(static_cast<std::streamoff>(eh.e_phoff + static_cast<uint64_t>(i) * eh.e_phentsize));
        diskFile.read(reinterpret_cast<char*>(&ph), sizeof(ph));
        if (diskFile.gcount() != static_cast<std::streamsize>(sizeof(ph))) return false;
        if (ph.p_type != PT_LOAD || vaddr < ph.p_vaddr || vaddr + 16 > ph.p_vaddr + ph.p_filesz) continue;

        uint8_t originalBytes[16]{};
        diskFile.seekg(static_cast<std::streamoff>(ph.p_offset + (vaddr - ph.p_vaddr)));
        diskFile.read(reinterpret_cast<char*>(originalBytes), sizeof(originalBytes));
        if (diskFile.gcount() != static_cast<std::streamsize>(sizeof(originalBytes))) return false;
        return RestoreHook(hook, originalBytes, sizeof(originalBytes));
    }
    return false;
#endif
}

} // namespace noescape::memory

```

`src/network/crypto_util.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "noescape/network/crypto_util.hpp"
#include "noescape/core/obfuscation.hpp"
#include <cstring>
#include <sstream>
#include <iomanip>
#include <fstream>
#include <array>

#if defined(_WIN32)
#include <windows.h>
#else
#include <unistd.h>
#include <sys/types.h>
#endif

namespace noescape::network {

namespace {

// ============================================================================
// Standard SHA-256 Implementation (FIPS 180-4)
// ============================================================================

constexpr uint32_t K[64] = {
    0x428a2f98, 0x71374491, 0xb5c0fbcf, 0xe9b5dba5,
    0x3956c25b, 0x59f111f1, 0x923f82a4, 0xab1c5ed5,
    0xd807aa98, 0x12835b01, 0x243185be, 0x550c7dc3,
    0x72be5d74, 0x80deb1fe, 0x9bdc06a7, 0xc19bf174,
    0xe49b69c1, 0xefbe4786, 0x0fc19dc6, 0x240ca1cc,
    0x2de92c6f, 0x4a7484aa, 0x5cb0a9dc, 0x76f988da,
    0x983e5152, 0xa831c66d, 0xb00327c8, 0xbf597fc7,
    0xc6e00bf3, 0xd5a79147, 0x06ca6351, 0x14292967,
    0x27b70a85, 0x2e1b2138, 0x4d2c6dfc, 0x53380d13,
    0x650a7354, 0x766a0abb, 0x81c2c92e, 0x92722c85,
    0xa2bfe8a1, 0xa81a664b, 0xc24b8b70, 0xc76c51a3,
    0xd192e819, 0xd6990624, 0xf40e3585, 0x106aa070,
    0x19a4c116, 0x1e376c08, 0x2748774c, 0x34b0bcb5,
    0x391c0cb3, 0x4ed8aa4a, 0x5b9cca4f, 0x682e6ff3,
    0x748f82ee, 0x78a5636f, 0x84c87814, 0x8cc70208,
    0x90befffa, 0xa4506ceb, 0xbef9a3f7, 0xc67178f2
};

inline uint32_t ROR(uint32_t x, uint32_t n) {
    return (x >> n) | (x << (32 - n));
}

inline uint32_t Ch(uint32_t x, uint32_t y, uint32_t z) {
    return (x & y) ^ (~x & z);
}

inline uint32_t Maj(uint32_t x, uint32_t y, uint32_t z) {
    return (x & y) ^ (x & z) ^ (y & z);
}

inline uint32_t Sigma0(uint32_t x) {
    return ROR(x, 2) ^ ROR(x, 13) ^ ROR(x, 22);
}

inline uint32_t Sigma1(uint32_t x) {
    return ROR(x, 6) ^ ROR(x, 11) ^ ROR(x, 25);
}

inline uint32_t Gamma0(uint32_t x) {
    return ROR(x, 7) ^ ROR(x, 18) ^ (x >> 3);
}

inline uint32_t Gamma1(uint32_t x) {
    return ROR(x, 17) ^ ROR(x, 19) ^ (x >> 10);
}

struct Sha256Context {
    uint32_t state[8]{
        0x6a09e667, 0xbb67ae85, 0x3c6ef372, 0xa54ff53a,
        0x510e527f, 0x9b05688c, 0x1f83d9ab, 0x5be0cd19
    };
    uint64_t count{0};
    uint8_t buffer[64]{};

    void Transform(const uint8_t chunk[64]) {
        uint32_t w[64];
        for (int i = 0; i < 16; ++i) {
            w[i] = (static_cast<uint32_t>(chunk[i * 4]) << 24) |
                   (static_cast<uint32_t>(chunk[i * 4 + 1]) << 16) |
                   (static_cast<uint32_t>(chunk[i * 4 + 2]) << 8) |
                   (static_cast<uint32_t>(chunk[i * 4 + 3]));
        }
        for (int i = 16; i < 64; ++i) {
            w[i] = Gamma1(w[i - 2]) + w[i - 7] + Gamma0(w[i - 15]) + w[i - 16];
        }

        uint32_t a = state[0];
        uint32_t b = state[1];
        uint32_t c = state[2];
        uint32_t d = state[3];
        uint32_t e = state[4];
        uint32_t f = state[5];
        uint32_t g = state[6];
        uint32_t h = state[7];

        for (int i = 0; i < 64; ++i) {
            uint32_t t1 = h + Sigma1(e) + Ch(e, f, g) + K[i] + w[i];
            uint32_t t2 = Sigma0(a) + Maj(a, b, c);
            h = g;
            g = f;
            f = e;
            e = d + t1;
            d = c;
            c = b;
            b = a;
            a = t1 + t2;
        }

        state[0] += a;
        state[1] += b;
        state[2] += c;
        state[3] += d;
        state[4] += e;
        state[5] += f;
        state[6] += g;
        state[7] += h;
    }

    void Update(const uint8_t* data, size_t len) {
        size_t bufferIndex = count % 64;
        count += len;
        size_t partLen = 64 - bufferIndex;
        size_t i = 0;

        if (len >= partLen) {
            std::memcpy(&buffer[bufferIndex], data, partLen);
            Transform(buffer);
            for (i = partLen; i + 63 < len; i += 64) {
                Transform(&data[i]);
            }
            bufferIndex = 0;
        }
        std::memcpy(&buffer[bufferIndex], &data[i], len - i);
    }

    void Finalize(uint8_t digest[32]) {
        uint8_t finalCount[8];
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
            finalCount[i] = static_cast<uint8_t>((count * 8) >> ((7 - i) * 8));
        }

        size_t bufferIndex = count % 64;
        size_t padLen = (bufferIndex < 56) ? (56 - bufferIndex) : (120 - bufferIndex);
        uint8_t padding[64]{0x80};
        Update(padding, padLen);
        Update(finalCount, 8);

        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
            digest[i * 4]     = static_cast<uint8_t>(state[i] >> 24);
            digest[i * 4 + 1] = static_cast<uint8_t>(state[i] >> 16);
            digest[i * 4 + 2] = static_cast<uint8_t>(state[i] >> 8);
            digest[i * 4 + 3] = static_cast<uint8_t>(state[i]);
        }
    }
};

} // namespace

std::string CryptoUtil::ToHex(const uint8_t* data, size_t length) {
    std::ostringstream ss;
    ss << std::hex << std::setfill('0');
    for (size_t i = 0; i < length; ++i) {
        ss << std::setw(2) << static_cast<int>(data[i]);
    }
    return ss.str();
}

std::string CryptoUtil::Sha256(const uint8_t* data, size_t length) {
    Sha256Context ctx;
    ctx.Update(data, length);
    uint8_t digest[32];
    ctx.Finalize(digest);
    return ToHex(digest, 32);
}

std::string CryptoUtil::Sha256(const std::string& input) {
    return Sha256(reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t*>(input.data()), input.size());
}

std::string CryptoUtil::HmacSha256(const std::string& key, const std::string& data) {
    uint8_t keyPad[64]{};
    if (key.length() > 64) {
        Sha256Context keyCtx;
        keyCtx.Update(reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t*>(key.data()), key.length());
        keyCtx.Finalize(keyPad);
    } else {
        std::memcpy(keyPad, key.data(), key.length());
    }

    uint8_t iPad[64];
    uint8_t oPad[64];
    for (int i = 0; i < 64; ++i) {
        iPad[i] = keyPad[i] ^ 0x36;
        oPad[i] = keyPad[i] ^ 0x5c;
    }

    Sha256Context inner;
    inner.Update(iPad, 64);
    inner.Update(reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t*>(data.data()), data.length());
    uint8_t innerHash[32];
    inner.Finalize(innerHash);

    Sha256Context outer;
    outer.Update(oPad, 64);
    outer.Update(innerHash, 32);
    uint8_t finalDigest[32];
    outer.Finalize(finalDigest);

    return ToHex(finalDigest, 32);
}

std::string CryptoUtil::GetMachineHWID() {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    HKEY hKey = nullptr;
    char szHwid[256] = {0};
    DWORD dwSize = sizeof(szHwid);
    if (RegOpenKeyExA(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, "SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Cryptography", 0, KEY_READ | KEY_WOW64_64KEY, &hKey) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
        RegQueryValueExA(hKey, "MachineGuid", nullptr, nullptr, reinterpret_cast<LPBYTE>(szHwid), &dwSize);
        RegCloseKey(hKey);
    }
    if (strlen(szHwid) > 0) {
        return Sha256(szHwid);
    }
    return Sha256("WIN32-FALLBACK-HWID-DEVICE");
#else
    // Linux HWID: Read /etc/machine-id or fallback to /var/lib/dbus/machine-id
    std::ifstream mid(NOESCAPE_XOR("/etc/machine-id"));
    std::string id;
    if (mid.is_open() && std::getline(mid, id) && !id.empty()) {
        return Sha256(id);
    }
    std::ifstream dbusMid(NOESCAPE_XOR("/var/lib/dbus/machine-id"));
    if (dbusMid.is_open() && std::getline(dbusMid, id) && !id.empty()) {
        return Sha256(id);
    }
    // Fallback: hostname
    char hostname[256]{};
    gethostname(hostname, sizeof(hostname) - 1);
    return Sha256(std::string("LINUX-HWID-") + hostname);
#endif
}

} // namespace noescape::network
```
