`src/network/panel_client.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "noescape/network/panel_client.hpp"
#include "noescape/network/crypto_util.hpp"
#include <sstream>
#include <ctime>
#include <cstring>

#if defined(_WIN32)
#include <winsock2.h>
#include <ws2tcpip.h>
#pragma comment(lib, "ws2_32.lib")
using socket_t = SOCKET;
#define IS_VALID_SOCKET(s) ((s) != INVALID_SOCKET)
#define CLOSE_SOCKET(s) closesocket(s)
#else
#include <sys/types.h>
#include <sys/socket.h>
#include <netinet/in.h>
#include <netdb.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#include <poll.h>
#include <fcntl.h>
using socket_t = int;
#define IS_VALID_SOCKET(s) ((s) >= 0)
#define CLOSE_SOCKET(s) close(s)
#endif

namespace noescape::network {

namespace {

void EnsureNetworkInit() {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    static bool initialized = false;
    if (!initialized) {
        WSADATA wsa;
        WSAStartup(MAKEWORD(2, 2), &wsa);
        initialized = true;
    }
#endif
}

bool ConnectWithTimeout(socket_t sock, const struct sockaddr* addr, socklen_t addrlen, uint32_t timeout_ms) {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    u_long mode = 1;
    ioctlsocket(sock, FIONBIO, &mode);
    int res = connect(sock, addr, static_cast<int>(addrlen));
    if (res == 0) {
        mode = 0;
        ioctlsocket(sock, FIONBIO, &mode);
        return true;
    }
    if (WSAGetLastError() != WSAEWOULDBLOCK) {
        return false;
    }
    fd_set writeSet, errSet;
    FD_ZERO(&writeSet);
    FD_ZERO(&errSet);
    FD_SET(sock, &writeSet);
    FD_SET(sock, &errSet);
    timeval tv{};
    tv.tv_sec = timeout_ms / 1000;
    tv.tv_usec = (timeout_ms % 1000) * 1000;
    if (select(0, nullptr, &writeSet, &errSet, &tv) > 0 && FD_ISSET(sock, &writeSet) && !FD_ISSET(sock, &errSet)) {
        mode = 0;
        ioctlsocket(sock, FIONBIO, &mode);
        return true;
    }
    return false;
#else
    int flags = fcntl(sock, F_GETFL, 0);
    fcntl(sock, F_SETFL, flags | O_NONBLOCK);
    int res = connect(sock, addr, addrlen);
    if (res == 0) {
        fcntl(sock, F_SETFL, flags);
        return true;
    }
    if (errno != EINPROGRESS) {
        return false;
    }
    struct pollfd pfd{};
    pfd.fd = sock;
    pfd.events = POLLOUT;
    int pollRes = poll(&pfd, 1, static_cast<int>(timeout_ms));
    if (pollRes > 0 && (pfd.revents & POLLOUT) && !(pfd.revents & (POLLERR | POLLHUP))) {
        int err = 0;
        socklen_t len = sizeof(err);
        if (getsockopt(sock, SOL_SOCKET, SO_ERROR, &err, &len) == 0 && err == 0) {
            fcntl(sock, F_SETFL, flags);
            return true;
        }
    }
    return false;
#endif
}

std::string JsonEscape(const std::string& in) {
    std::string out;
    out.reserve(in.size() + 8);
    for (unsigned char c : in) {
        switch (c) {
            case '"':  out += "\\\""; break;
            case '\\': out += "\\\\"; break;
            case '\n': out += "\\n"; break;
            case '\r': out += "\\r"; break;
            case '\t': out += "\\t"; break;
            default:
                if (c < 0x20) { char b[8]; snprintf(b, sizeof(b), "\\u%04x", c); out += b; }
                else out += static_cast<char>(c);
        }
    }
    return out;
}

} // namespace

PanelClient::PanelClient(std::string base_url, std::string secret_key)
    : base_url_(std::move(base_url)),
      secret_key_(std::move(secret_key)),
      hwid_(CryptoUtil::GetMachineHWID()) {
    EnsureNetworkInit();
}

PanelClient::~PanelClient() = default;

PanelClient::ParsedUrl PanelClient::ParseUrl(const std::string& url) {
    ParsedUrl res;
    res.port = 80;
    res.path = "/";

    std::string s = url;
    size_t protoPos = s.find("://");
    if (protoPos != std::string::npos) {
        s = s.substr(protoPos + 3);
    }

    size_t slashPos = s.find('/');
    std::string hostPort;
    if (slashPos != std::string::npos) {
        hostPort = s.substr(0, slashPos);
        res.path = s.substr(slashPos);
    } else {
        hostPort = s;
    }

    size_t colonPos = hostPort.find(':');
    if (colonPos != std::string::npos) {
        res.host = hostPort.substr(0, colonPos);
        res.port = static_cast<uint16_t>(std::atoi(hostPort.substr(colonPos + 1).c_str()));
    } else {
        res.host = hostPort;
    }

    return res;
}

std::string PanelClient::HttpPost(const ParsedUrl& target, const std::string& body, uint32_t timeout_ms) {
    EnsureNetworkInit();

    struct addrinfo hints{}, *servinfo = nullptr;
    hints.ai_family = AF_INET;
    hints.ai_socktype = SOCK_STREAM;

    std::string portStr = std::to_string(target.port);
    if (getaddrinfo(target.host.c_str(), portStr.c_str(), &hints, &servinfo) != 0 || !servinfo) {
        return "";
    }

    socket_t sock = socket(servinfo->ai_family, servinfo->ai_socktype, servinfo->ai_protocol);
    if (!IS_VALID_SOCKET(sock)) {
        freeaddrinfo(servinfo);
        return "";
    }

#if defined(_WIN32)
    DWORD tv = timeout_ms;
    setsockopt(sock, SOL_SOCKET, SO_RCVTIMEO, reinterpret_cast<const char*>(&tv), sizeof(tv));
    setsockopt(sock, SOL_SOCKET, SO_SNDTIMEO, reinterpret_cast<const char*>(&tv), sizeof(tv));
#else
    struct timeval tv{};
    tv.tv_sec = timeout_ms / 1000;
    tv.tv_usec = (timeout_ms % 1000) * 1000;
    setsockopt(sock, SOL_SOCKET, SO_RCVTIMEO, &tv, sizeof(tv));
    setsockopt(sock, SOL_SOCKET, SO_SNDTIMEO, &tv, sizeof(tv));
#endif

    if (!ConnectWithTimeout(sock, servinfo->ai_addr, static_cast<socklen_t>(servinfo->ai_addrlen), timeout_ms)) {
        CLOSE_SOCKET(sock);
        freeaddrinfo(servinfo);
        return "";
    }
    freeaddrinfo(servinfo);

    std::ostringstream req;
    req << "POST " << target.path << " HTTP/1.1\r\n"
        << "Host: " << target.host << "\r\n"
        << "Content-Type: application/json\r\n"
        << "Content-Length: " << body.length() << "\r\n"
        << "Connection: close\r\n\r\n"
        << body;

    std::string reqStr = req.str();
    if (send(sock, reqStr.c_str(), static_cast<int>(reqStr.length()), 0) <= 0) {
        CLOSE_SOCKET(sock);
        return "";
    }

    std::string response;
    char buf[1024];
    int bytes = 0;
    while ((bytes = recv(sock, buf, sizeof(buf) - 1, 0)) > 0) {
        buf[bytes] = '\0';
        response.append(buf, bytes);
    }

    CLOSE_SOCKET(sock);
    return response;
}

std::string PanelClient::HttpGet(const ParsedUrl& target, uint32_t timeout_ms) {
    EnsureNetworkInit();

    struct addrinfo hints{}, *servinfo = nullptr;
    hints.ai_family = AF_INET;
    hints.ai_socktype = SOCK_STREAM;

    std::string portStr = std::to_string(target.port);
    if (getaddrinfo(target.host.c_str(), portStr.c_str(), &hints, &servinfo) != 0 || !servinfo) {
        return "";
    }

    socket_t sock = socket(servinfo->ai_family, servinfo->ai_socktype, servinfo->ai_protocol);
    if (!IS_VALID_SOCKET(sock)) {
        freeaddrinfo(servinfo);
        return "";
    }

#if defined(_WIN32)
    DWORD tv = timeout_ms;
    setsockopt(sock, SOL_SOCKET, SO_RCVTIMEO, reinterpret_cast<const char*>(&tv), sizeof(tv));
    setsockopt(sock, SOL_SOCKET, SO_SNDTIMEO, reinterpret_cast<const char*>(&tv), sizeof(tv));
#else
    struct timeval tv{};
    tv.tv_sec = timeout_ms / 1000;
    tv.tv_usec = (timeout_ms % 1000) * 1000;
    setsockopt(sock, SOL_SOCKET, SO_RCVTIMEO, &tv, sizeof(tv));
    setsockopt(sock, SOL_SOCKET, SO_SNDTIMEO, &tv, sizeof(tv));
#endif

    if (!ConnectWithTimeout(sock, servinfo->ai_addr, static_cast<socklen_t>(servinfo->ai_addrlen), timeout_ms)) {
        CLOSE_SOCKET(sock);
        freeaddrinfo(servinfo);
        return "";
    }
    freeaddrinfo(servinfo);

    std::ostringstream req;
    req << "GET " << target.path << " HTTP/1.1\r\n"
        << "Host: " << target.host << "\r\n"
        << "Connection: close\r\n\r\n";

    std::string reqStr = req.str();
    if (send(sock, reqStr.c_str(), static_cast<int>(reqStr.length()), 0) <= 0) {
        CLOSE_SOCKET(sock);
        return "";
    }

    std::string response;
    char buf[1024];
    int bytes = 0;
    while ((bytes = recv(sock, buf, sizeof(buf) - 1, 0)) > 0) {
        buf[bytes] = '\0';
        response.append(buf, bytes);
    }

    CLOSE_SOCKET(sock);
    return response;
}

HeartbeatPacket PanelClient::ConstructHeartbeat(uint32_t current_threat_score, const std::string& status) {
    HeartbeatPacket pkt;
    pkt.hwid = hwid_;
    pkt.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(std::time(nullptr));
    pkt.threat_score = current_threat_score;
    pkt.events_count = 0;
    pkt.status = status;

    std::ostringstream payload;
    payload << pkt.hwid << ":" << pkt.timestamp << ":" << pkt.threat_score << ":" << pkt.status;
    pkt.hmac_signature = CryptoUtil::HmacSha256(secret_key_, payload.str());

    return pkt;
}

bool PanelClient::SendHeartbeat(uint32_t current_threat_score, const std::string& status) {
    auto pkt = ConstructHeartbeat(current_threat_score, status);

    std::ostringstream json;
    json << "{"
         << "\"hwid\":\"" << pkt.hwid << "\","
         << "\"timestamp\":" << pkt.timestamp << ","
         << "\"threat_score\":" << pkt.threat_score << ","
         << "\"status\":\"" << pkt.status << "\","
         << "\"signature\":\"" << pkt.hmac_signature << "\""
         << "}";

    ParsedUrl parsed = ParseUrl(base_url_ + "/heartbeat");
    std::string resp = HttpPost(parsed, json.str(), 1500);
    return resp.rfind("HTTP/1.1 2", 0) == 0 || resp.rfind("HTTP/1.0 2", 0) == 0;
}

bool PanelClient::SendBanReport(const core::DetectionEvent& event, uint32_t current_threat_score) {
    uint64_t now = static_cast<uint64_t>(std::time(nullptr));
    std::ostringstream payload;
    payload << hwid_ << ":" << now << ":" << core::DetectionEvent::TypeToString(event.type)
            << ":" << event.details;
    std::string sig = CryptoUtil::HmacSha256(secret_key_, payload.str());

    std::ostringstream json;
    json << "{"
         << "\"hwid\":\"" << hwid_ << "\","
         << "\"timestamp\":" << now << ","
         << "\"threat_score\":" << current_threat_score << ","
         << "\"type\":\"" << core::DetectionEvent::TypeToString(event.type) << "\","
         << "\"severity\":\"" << core::DetectionEvent::SeverityToString(event.severity) << "\","
         << "\"component\":\"" << JsonEscape(event.component) << "\","
         << "\"details\":\"" << JsonEscape(event.details) << "\","
         << "\"signature\":\"" << sig << "\""
         << "}";

    ParsedUrl parsed = ParseUrl(base_url_ + "/alert");
    std::string resp = HttpPost(parsed, json.str(), 2000);
    return resp.rfind("HTTP/1.1 2", 0) == 0 || resp.rfind("HTTP/1.0 2", 0) == 0;
}

BanCheckResult PanelClient::QueryBanStatus() {
    BanCheckResult result;
    uint64_t now = static_cast<uint64_t>(std::time(nullptr));
    std::string path = base_url_ + "/check?hwid=" + hwid_ + "&nonce=" + std::to_string(now);
    ParsedUrl parsed = ParseUrl(path);
    std::string resp = HttpGet(parsed, 1500);

    if (resp.empty()) {
        result.status = BanCheckStatus::NETWORK_ERROR;
        result.reason = "Backend server unreachable or timeout";
        return result;
    }

    // Signature is mandatory and bound to hwid + status + our nonce (anti-replay, anti-strip)
    bool isBanned = (resp.find("\"banned\":true") != std::string::npos);
    bool isClean = (resp.find("\"banned\":false") != std::string::npos);
    size_t sigPos = resp.find("\"signature\":\"");
    size_t sigEnd = (sigPos == std::string::npos) ? std::string::npos : resp.find('"', sigPos + 13);
    if (sigEnd == std::string::npos || isBanned == isClean) {
        result.status = BanCheckStatus::SIGNATURE_INVALID;
        result.reason = "Missing signature or ambiguous panel response";
        return result;
    }
    std::string serverSig = resp.substr(sigPos + 13, sigEnd - (sigPos + 13));
    std::string expectedSig = CryptoUtil::HmacSha256(
        secret_key_, hwid_ + ":" + (isBanned ? "1" : "0") + ":" + std::to_string(now));
    if (serverSig != expectedSig) {
        result.status = BanCheckStatus::SIGNATURE_INVALID;
        result.reason = "Panel response HMAC signature validation failed (Tampered or MitM)";
        return result;
    }

    if (resp.find("\"banned\":true") != std::string::npos ||
        resp.find("banned:1") != std::string::npos) {
        result.status = BanCheckStatus::BANNED;
        result.reason = "Global Hardware ID Blacklist Match";
        return result;
    }

    if (resp.find("\"banned\":false") != std::string::npos ||
        resp.find("banned:0") != std::string::npos) {
        result.status = BanCheckStatus::CLEAN;
        result.reason = "Hardware ID is clean";
        return result;
    }

    // Response arrived but did not match expected JSON schema
    result.status = BanCheckStatus::NETWORK_ERROR;
    result.reason = "Malformed response payload from backend";
    return result;
}

bool PanelClient::CheckBanStatus(bool& out_is_banned, std::string& out_reason) {
    auto res = QueryBanStatus();
    if (res.status == BanCheckStatus::BANNED) {
        out_is_banned = true;
        out_reason = res.reason;
        return true;
    } else if (res.status == BanCheckStatus::CLEAN) {
        out_is_banned = false;
        out_reason = res.reason;
        return true;
    } else {
        out_is_banned = false;
        out_reason = res.reason;
        return false;
    }
}

} // namespace noescape::network

```

`src/overlay/overlay_detector.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "noescape/overlay/overlay_detector.hpp"
#include "noescape/memory/memory_scanner.hpp"
#include "noescape/memory/unhooker.hpp"
#include "noescape/core/logger.hpp"

#include <algorithm>
#include <sstream>
#include <iomanip>
#include <fstream>
#include <filesystem>
#include <ctime>
#include <cstring>

#if defined(_WIN32)
#include <windows.h>
#else
#include <dlfcn.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#include <dirent.h>
#endif

namespace noescape::overlay {

namespace {

std::string ToLower(std::string_view str) {
    std::string s(str);
    std::transform(s.begin(), s.end(), s.begin(), [](unsigned char c) {
        return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c));
    });
    return s;
}

std::string PtrToHex(uintptr_t addr) {
    std::ostringstream ss;
    ss << "0x" << std::hex << addr;
    return ss.str();
}

} // namespace

OverlayDetector& OverlayDetector::Instance() {
    static OverlayDetector* instance = new OverlayDetector();
    return *instance;
}

OverlayDetector::OverlayDetector() {
    game_viewport_ = {0, 0, 1920, 1080};
}

void OverlayDetector::SetGameViewport(int32_t x, int32_t y, int32_t width, int32_t height) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    game_viewport_ = {x, y, width, height};
}

ViewportRect OverlayDetector::GetGameViewport() const {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    return game_viewport_;
}

void OverlayDetector::SetGameWindowHandle(uintptr_t handle) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    game_window_handle_ = handle;
}

uintptr_t OverlayDetector::GetGameWindowHandle() const {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    return game_window_handle_;
}

void OverlayDetector::AddMockWindow(const OverlayWindowInfo& window) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    mock_windows_.push_back(window);
}

void OverlayDetector::ClearMockWindows() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    mock_windows_.clear();
}

void OverlayDetector::RegisterSwapchainVTable(void** vtable, size_t entry_count) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    if (vtable && entry_count > 0) {
        registered_vtables_.push_back(vtable);
        registered_vtable_sizes_.push_back(entry_count);
    }
}

void OverlayDetector::ClearRegisteredVTables() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    registered_vtables_.clear();
    registered_vtable_sizes_.clear();
}

std::vector<OverlayWindowInfo> OverlayDetector::EnumerateTopLevelWindows() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    if (!mock_windows_.empty()) {
        return mock_windows_;
    }
    std::vector<OverlayWindowInfo> windows;

#if defined(_WIN32)
    struct EnumData {
        DWORD currentPid;
        HWND gameHwnd;
        std::vector<OverlayWindowInfo>* list;
    };

    DWORD curPid = GetCurrentProcessId();
    EnumData data{curPid, reinterpret_cast<HWND>(game_window_handle_), &windows};

    EnumWindows([](HWND hwnd, LPARAM lParam) -> BOOL {
        auto* d = reinterpret_cast<EnumData*>(lParam);
        if (!IsWindowVisible(hwnd)) return TRUE;
        if (d->gameHwnd && hwnd == d->gameHwnd) return TRUE;

        DWORD pid = 0;
        GetWindowThreadProcessId(hwnd, &pid);
        if (pid == d->currentPid) return TRUE;

        LONG_PTR exStyle = GetWindowLongPtrA(hwnd, GWL_EXSTYLE);
        LONG_PTR style = GetWindowLongPtrA(hwnd, GWL_STYLE);

        RECT rc{};
        GetWindowRect(hwnd, &rc);

        char title[256] = {0};
        GetWindowTextA(hwnd, title, sizeof(title));

        char className[256] = {0};
        GetClassNameA(hwnd, className, sizeof(className));

        OverlayWindowInfo info;
        info.title = title;
        info.class_name = className;
        info.pid = static_cast<uint32_t>(pid);
        info.handle = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(hwnd);
        info.rect = {rc.left, rc.top, rc.right - rc.left, rc.bottom - rc.top};
        info.style = static_cast<uint32_t>(style);
        info.ex_style = static_cast<uint32_t>(exStyle);
        info.is_topmost = (exStyle & WS_EX_TOPMOST) != 0;
        info.is_transparent = (exStyle & WS_EX_TRANSPARENT) != 0;
        info.is_layered = (exStyle & WS_EX_LAYERED) != 0;

        d->list->push_back(info);
        return TRUE;
    }, reinterpret_cast<LPARAM>(&data));
#else
    // 1. Dynamic X11 Window Inspection (detects transparent, override-redirect, and unmapped draw windows)
    void* libX11 = dlopen("libX11.so.6", RTLD_LAZY);
    if (!libX11) {
        libX11 = dlopen("libX11.so", RTLD_LAZY);
    }

    if (libX11) {
        typedef void* (*XOpenDisplayFn)(const char*);
        typedef int (*XCloseDisplayFn)(void*);
        typedef unsigned long (*XDefaultRootWindowFn)(void*);
        typedef int (*XQueryTreeFn)(void*, unsigned long, unsigned long*, unsigned long*, unsigned long**, unsigned int*);
        typedef int (*XGetWindowAttributesFn)(void*, unsigned long, void*);
        typedef int (*XFreeFn)(void*);
        typedef int (*XFetchNameFn)(void*, unsigned long, char**);

        auto pXOpenDisplay = reinterpret_cast<XOpenDisplayFn>(dlsym(libX11, "XOpenDisplay"));
        auto pXCloseDisplay = reinterpret_cast<XCloseDisplayFn>(dlsym(libX11, "XCloseDisplay"));
        auto pXDefaultRootWindow = reinterpret_cast<XDefaultRootWindowFn>(dlsym(libX11, "XDefaultRootWindow"));
        auto pXQueryTree = reinterpret_cast<XQueryTreeFn>(dlsym(libX11, "XQueryTree"));
        auto pXGetWindowAttributes = reinterpret_cast<XGetWindowAttributesFn>(dlsym(libX11, "XGetWindowAttributes"));
        auto pXFree = reinterpret_cast<XFreeFn>(dlsym(libX11, "XFree"));
        auto pXFetchName = reinterpret_cast<XFetchNameFn>(dlsym(libX11, "XFetchName"));

        if (pXOpenDisplay && pXCloseDisplay && pXDefaultRootWindow && pXQueryTree && pXGetWindowAttributes && pXFree) {
            void* display = pXOpenDisplay(nullptr);
            if (display) {
                unsigned long root = pXDefaultRootWindow(display);
                unsigned long rootRet = 0, parentRet = 0;
                unsigned long* children = nullptr;
                unsigned int numChildren = 0;

                if (pXQueryTree(display, root, &rootRet, &parentRet, &children, &numChildren) && children) {
                    struct XWindowAttributesRaw {
                        int x, y, width, height, border_width, depth;
                        void* visual;
                        unsigned long root;
                        int c_class;
                        int bit_gravity, win_gravity, backing_store;
                        unsigned long backing_planes, backing_pixel;
                        int save_under;
                        void* colormap;
                        int map_installed;
                        int map_state; // 0=IsUnmapped, 1=IsUnviewable, 2=IsViewable
                        long all_event_masks, your_event_mask, do_not_propagate_mask;
                        int override_redirect;
                        void* screen;
                    };

                    for (unsigned int i = 0; i < numChildren; ++i) {
                        XWindowAttributesRaw attrs{};
                        if (pXGetWindowAttributes(display, children[i], &attrs)) {
                            bool isOverride = (attrs.override_redirect != 0);
                            bool is32Bit = (attrs.depth == 32);
                            bool isUnmapped = (attrs.map_state == 0);

                            ViewportRect winRect{attrs.x, attrs.y, attrs.width, attrs.height};

                            if (is32Bit && (isOverride || isUnmapped || attrs.map_state == 2) && game_viewport_.Overlaps(winRect)) {
                                char* winName = nullptr;
                                if (pXFetchName) pXFetchName(display, children[i], &winName);
                                std::string titleStr = winName ? winName : "X11Window";
                                if (winName && pXFree) pXFree(winName);

                                OverlayWindowInfo info;
                                info.title = titleStr;
                                info.class_name = isOverride ? "X11OverrideRedirect" : (isUnmapped ? "X11UnmappedDraw" : "X11ARGBWindow");
                                info.handle = static_cast<uintptr_t>(children[i]);
                                info.rect = winRect;
                                info.is_topmost = isOverride;
                                info.is_transparent = true;
                                info.is_layered = true;
                                windows.push_back(info);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    pXFree(children);
                }
                pXCloseDisplay(display);
            }
        }
        dlclose(libX11);
    }

    // 2. On Linux: scan /proc for active processes that exhibit overlay characteristics
    DIR* procDir = opendir("/proc");
    if (procDir) {
        struct dirent* entry = nullptr;
        pid_t myPid = getpid();
        while ((entry = readdir(procDir)) != nullptr) {
            if (entry->d_type != DT_DIR && entry->d_type != DT_UNKNOWN) continue;
            std::string name = entry->d_name;
            if (name.empty() || !std::isdigit(name[0])) continue;

            pid_t pid = std::atoi(name.c_str());
            if (pid <= 0 || pid == myPid) continue;

            std::string cmdPath = "/proc/" + name + "/cmdline";
            std::ifstream cmdFile(cmdPath);
            if (!cmdFile.is_open()) continue;

            std::string cmd;
            std::string line;
            while (std::getline(cmdFile, line, '\0')) {
                if (!cmd.empty()) cmd += " ";
                cmd += line;
            }

            std::string lowerCmd = ToLower(cmd);

            // Whitelist verified system compositors and legitimate gaming utilities
            static const std::vector<std::string> whitelistedTools = {
                "picom", "compton", "kwin", "mutter", "sway", "waybar",
                "wf-shell", "mangohud", "vkbasalt", "obs-vkcapture", "obs",
                "steam", "discord", "gamescope", "xwayland", "gnome-shell",
                "plasmashell", "xfwm4", "marco", "compiz"
            };

            bool isWhitelisted = false;
            for (const auto& w : whitelistedTools) {
                if (lowerCmd.find(w) != std::string::npos) {
                    isWhitelisted = true;
                    break;
                }
            }
            if (isWhitelisted) continue;

            static const std::vector<std::string> overlayTools = {
                "gl-inject", "pyglass", "esp_overlay", "liboverlay",
                "gameoverlay", "cheat_overlay", "d3d_overlay", "aimbot_overlay"
            };

            for (const auto& tool : overlayTools) {
                if (lowerCmd.find(tool) != std::string::npos) {
                    OverlayWindowInfo info;
                    info.title = tool + " (Active Overlay Process)";
                    info.class_name = "LinuxOverlayProcess";
                    info.pid = static_cast<uint32_t>(pid);
                    info.handle = static_cast<uintptr_t>(pid);
                    info.rect = game_viewport_;
                    info.is_topmost = true;
                    info.is_transparent = true;
                    info.is_layered = true;
                    windows.push_back(info);
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
        closedir(procDir);
    }
#endif

    return windows;
}

bool OverlayDetector::IsSuspiciousOverlayWindow(const OverlayWindowInfo& win) const {
    std::string lowerTitle = ToLower(win.title);
    std::string lowerClass = ToLower(win.class_name);

    // Whitelist desktop compositors, official overlays (Steam, Discord, GeForce)
    static const std::vector<std::string> whitelistedTitles = {
        "picom", "compton", "kwin", "mutter", "sway", "waybar",
        "wf-shell", "mangohud", "steam", "steam overlay", "gameoverlayui",
        "discord overlay", "obs", "nvidia share", "geforce experience"
    };

    for (const auto& white : whitelistedTitles) {
        if (lowerTitle.find(white) != std::string::npos || lowerClass.find(white) != std::string::npos) {
            return false;
        }
    }

    // Check 1: Explicit topmost + transparent + layered window matching, enclosing, or intersecting viewport
    if (win.is_layered && win.is_transparent && win.is_topmost) {
        if (game_viewport_.Overlaps(win.rect)) {
            return true;
        }
    }

    // Check 2: Topmost transparent window with suspicious class or window title keywords
    static const std::vector<std::string> suspiciousKeywords = {
        "overlay", "esp", "wallhack", "imgui", "dear imgui",
        "external", "aimbot", "chams", "d3d overlay", "transparent draw",
        "crosshair", "radar", "glow"
    };

    for (const auto& kw : suspiciousKeywords) {
        if (lowerTitle.find(kw) != std::string::npos || lowerClass.find(kw) != std::string::npos) {
            if (win.is_topmost || win.is_layered || win.is_transparent) {
                return true;
            }
        }
    }

    return false;
}

std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> OverlayDetector::DetectSuspiciousWindows() {
    std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> events;
    auto windows = EnumerateTopLevelWindows();

    for (const auto& win : windows) {
        if (IsSuspiciousOverlayWindow(win)) {
            core::DetectionEvent ev;
            ev.type = core::DetectionType::OVERLAY_HIJACK_DETECTED;
            ev.severity = core::Severity::HIGH;
            ev.threat_score = 75;
            ev.component = "OverlayDetector::WindowScanner";
            ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(std::time(nullptr));

            std::ostringstream ss;
            ss << "Suspicious transparent overlay window detected: '" << win.title 
               << "' (Class: '" << win.class_name << "', PID: " << win.pid 
               << ", Rect: [" << win.rect.x << "," << win.rect.y << " " 
               << win.rect.width << "x" << win.rect.height << "]"
               << ", Layered=" << win.is_layered 
               << ", Transparent=" << win.is_transparent 
               << ", Topmost=" << win.is_topmost << ")";
            ev.details = ss.str();

            events.push_back(ev);
        }
    }

    return events;
}

bool OverlayDetector::IsAddressInLoadedModule(uintptr_t addr) const {
    if (addr < 0x10000) return false;

#if defined(_WIN32)
    MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION mbi{};
    if (VirtualQuery(reinterpret_cast<LPCVOID>(addr), &mbi, sizeof(mbi)) != 0) {
        if (mbi.State == MEM_COMMIT && mbi.Type == MEM_IMAGE) {
            DWORD execMask = PAGE_EXECUTE | PAGE_EXECUTE_READ | PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE | PAGE_EXECUTE_WRITECOPY;
            if (mbi.Protect & execMask) {
                return true;
            }
        }
    }
    return false;
#else
    auto regions = memory::MemoryScanner::Instance().QueryMemoryRegions();
    for (const auto& reg : regions) {
        if (addr >= reg.start && addr < reg.end) {
            // Must be backed by an image file, not heap/stack, and marked executable
            if (!reg.is_backed_by_file || reg.pathname.empty() || !reg.executable) {
                return false;
            }
            if (reg.pathname == "[heap]" || reg.pathname == "[stack]" || reg.pathname == "[vdso]") {
                return false;
            }
            std::string lowerPath = ToLower(reg.pathname);
            if (lowerPath.find("inject") != std::string::npos ||
                lowerPath.find("hook.so") != std::string::npos ||
                lowerPath.find("libcheat") != std::string::npos ||
                lowerPath.find("(deleted)") != std::string::npos) {
                return false;
            }
            return true;
        }
    }
    return false;
#endif
}

std::optional<core::DetectionEvent> OverlayDetector::InspectRenderingPointer(const char* routine_name, void* function_ptr) {
    if (!function_ptr) return std::nullopt;

    uintptr_t addr = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(function_ptr);
    if (addr < 0x10000) return std::nullopt;

    // 1. Check if the function pointer itself resides in a legitimate executable module image
    if (!IsAddressInLoadedModule(addr)) {
        core::DetectionEvent ev;
        ev.type = core::DetectionType::RENDERING_HOOK_DETECTED;
        ev.severity = core::Severity::CRITICAL;
        ev.threat_score = 100;
        ev.component = "OverlayDetector::SwapchainValidator";
        ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(std::time(nullptr));

        std::ostringstream ss;
        ss << "Swapchain function pointer '" << (routine_name ? routine_name : "VTableEntry")
           << "' [" << PtrToHex(addr) << "] points outside legitimate loaded module address space (Unbacked Trampoline)";
        ev.details = ss.str();
        return ev;
    }

    // 2. Check for inline hooks (JMP rel32, MOV RAX, imm; JMP RAX, INT3, etc.)
    auto hook = memory::Unhooker::Instance().InspectFunction(routine_name, function_ptr);
    if (hook) {
        core::DetectionEvent ev;
        ev.type = core::DetectionType::RENDERING_HOOK_DETECTED;
        ev.severity = core::Severity::CRITICAL;
        ev.threat_score = 95;
        ev.component = "OverlayDetector::RenderingIntegrity";
        ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(std::time(nullptr));

        std::ostringstream ss;
        ss << "Inline hook detected on rendering swapchain routine '" << routine_name 
           << "' [" << PtrToHex(addr) << "] Type: " << hook->hook_type 
           << " -> Diverted Target: " << PtrToHex(hook->target_destination);
        ev.details = ss.str();
        return ev;
    }

    return std::nullopt;
}

std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> OverlayDetector::AuditSwapchainHooks() {
    std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> events;

    // 1. Audit system graphics swapchain APIs
    std::vector<std::pair<const char*, void*>> graphicsApis;

#if defined(_WIN32)
    HMODULE hOpengl = GetModuleHandleA("opengl32.dll");
    if (hOpengl) {
        graphicsApis.emplace_back("wglSwapBuffers", GetProcAddress(hOpengl, "wglSwapBuffers"));
    }
    HMODULE hDxgi = GetModuleHandleA("dxgi.dll");
    if (hDxgi) {
        graphicsApis.emplace_back("CreateDXGIFactory", GetProcAddress(hDxgi, "CreateDXGIFactory"));
        graphicsApis.emplace_back("CreateDXGIFactory1", GetProcAddress(hDxgi, "CreateDXGIFactory1"));
        graphicsApis.emplace_back("CreateDXGIFactory2", GetProcAddress(hDxgi, "CreateDXGIFactory2"));
    }
    HMODULE hD3d11 = GetModuleHandleA("d3d11.dll");
    if (hD3d11) {
        graphicsApis.emplace_back("D3D11CreateDeviceAndSwapChain", GetProcAddress(hD3d11, "D3D11CreateDeviceAndSwapChain"));
    }
    HMODULE hD3d9 = GetModuleHandleA("d3d9.dll");
    if (hD3d9) {
        graphicsApis.emplace_back("Direct3DCreate9", GetProcAddress(hD3d9, "Direct3DCreate9"));
    }
    HMODULE hVulkan = GetModuleHandleA("vulkan-1.dll");
    if (hVulkan) {
        graphicsApis.emplace_back("vkQueuePresentKHR", GetProcAddress(hVulkan, "vkQueuePresentKHR"));
    }
#else
    void* pGlx = dlsym(RTLD_DEFAULT, "glXSwapBuffers");
    if (pGlx) graphicsApis.emplace_back("glXSwapBuffers", pGlx);

    void* pEgl = dlsym(RTLD_DEFAULT, "eglSwapBuffers");
    if (pEgl) graphicsApis.emplace_back("eglSwapBuffers", pEgl);

    void* pVk = dlsym(RTLD_DEFAULT, "vkQueuePresentKHR");
    if (pVk) graphicsApis.emplace_back("vkQueuePresentKHR", pVk);
#endif

    for (const auto& [name, ptr] : graphicsApis) {
        auto ev = InspectRenderingPointer(name, ptr);
        if (ev) {
            events.push_back(*ev);
        }
    }

    // 2. Audit dynamically registered vtables (e.g. game IDXGISwapChain or Vulkan dispatch table)
    std::vector<void**> vtablesCopy;
    std::vector<size_t> sizesCopy;
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        vtablesCopy = registered_vtables_;
        sizesCopy = registered_vtable_sizes_;
    }

    for (size_t i = 0; i < vtablesCopy.size(); ++i) {
        void** vtable = vtablesCopy[i];
        size_t count = sizesCopy[i];
        if (!vtable) continue;

        for (size_t idx = 0; idx < count; ++idx) {
            void* entryPtr = vtable[idx];
            if (!entryPtr) continue;

            std::string entryName = "VTable[" + std::to_string(i) + "][" + std::to_string(idx) + "]";
            auto ev = InspectRenderingPointer(entryName.c_str(), entryPtr);
            if (ev) {
                events.push_back(*ev);
            }
        }
    }

    return events;
}

std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> OverlayDetector::PerformAudit() {
    std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> events;

    auto winEvents = DetectSuspiciousWindows();
    events.insert(events.end(), winEvents.begin(), winEvents.end());

    auto hookEvents = AuditSwapchainHooks();
    events.insert(events.end(), hookEvents.begin(), hookEvents.end());

    return events;
}

} // namespace noescape::overlay

```

`src/sdk/auto_init.cpp`:

```cpp
#if defined(NOESCAPE_EXPORTS) || defined(NOESCAPE_BUILD_SHARED)

#include "noescape_sdk.h"

// ============================================================================
// Windows Platform Auto-Initialization (DllMain / Loader Lock Safe)
// ============================================================================
#if defined(_WIN32)

#ifndef WIN32_LEAN_AND_MEAN
#define WIN32_LEAN_AND_MEAN
#endif
#ifndef NOMINMAX
#define NOMINMAX
#endif

#include <windows.h>
#include <process.h>
#include <atomic>
#include <chrono>
#include <thread>
#include <cstring>
#include <cstdlib>

namespace {

HANDLE g_winWorkerThread = NULL;
std::atomic<bool> g_winShutdownRequested{false};
std::atomic<bool> g_winInitInProgress{false};
std::atomic<bool> g_winInitFinished{false};

DWORD WINAPI WindowsAutoInitWorker(LPVOID lpParam) {
    (void)lpParam;
    g_winInitInProgress.store(true, std::memory_order_release);

    // Yield/sleep briefly to let OS Loader Lock release completely
    for (int i = 0; i < 10 && !g_winShutdownRequested.load(std::memory_order_acquire); ++i) {
        Sleep(1);
    }

    if (g_winShutdownRequested.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
        g_winInitFinished.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
        return 0;
    }

    // Fast check: allow runtime opt-out (in secure production builds, require NOESCAPE_TEST_MODE)
#if defined(NOESCAPE_SECURE_BUILD) || defined(NDEBUG)
    char testModeBuf[16]{};
    DWORD testLen = GetEnvironmentVariableA("NOESCAPE_TEST_MODE", testModeBuf, sizeof(testModeBuf));
    bool allowDevOptOut = (testLen > 0 && (_stricmp(testModeBuf, "1") == 0 || _stricmp(testModeBuf, "true") == 0));
#else
    bool allowDevOptOut = true;
#endif

    if (allowDevOptOut) {
        char envBuf[256]{};
        DWORD len = GetEnvironmentVariableA("NOESCAPE_DISABLE_AUTO_INIT", envBuf, sizeof(envBuf));
        if (len > 0 && (_stricmp(envBuf, "1") == 0 || _stricmp(envBuf, "true") == 0)) {
            g_winInitFinished.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
            return 0;
        }

        len = GetEnvironmentVariableA("NOESCAPE_AUTO_START", envBuf, sizeof(envBuf));
        if (len > 0 && (_stricmp(envBuf, "0") == 0 || _stricmp(envBuf, "false") == 0)) {
            g_winInitFinished.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
            return 0;
        }
    }

    NoEscapeConfig cfg = NoEscape_GetDefaultConfig();

    // Stack buffers for environment overrides (NoEscape_Init copies into std::string immediately)
    char urlBuf[256]{};
    len = GetEnvironmentVariableA("NOESCAPE_PANEL_URL", urlBuf, sizeof(urlBuf));
    if (len > 0) {
        cfg.panel_url = urlBuf;
    }

    char keyBuf[256]{};
    len = GetEnvironmentVariableA("NOESCAPE_SECRET_KEY", keyBuf, sizeof(keyBuf));
    if (len > 0) {
        cfg.secret_key = keyBuf;
    }

    char dumpBuf[MAX_PATH]{};
    len = GetEnvironmentVariableA("NOESCAPE_DUMP_DIR", dumpBuf, sizeof(dumpBuf));
    if (len > 0) {
        cfg.blackbox_dump_dir = dumpBuf;
    }

    char threshBuf[32]{};
    len = GetEnvironmentVariableA("NOESCAPE_BAN_THRESHOLD", threshBuf, sizeof(threshBuf));
    if (len > 0) {
        int v = std::atoi(threshBuf);
        if (v > 0) cfg.ban_threshold = static_cast<uint32_t>(v);
    }

    char enforceBuf[16]{};
    len = GetEnvironmentVariableA("NOESCAPE_ENFORCE_BAN", enforceBuf, sizeof(enforceBuf));
    if (len > 0) {
        if (_stricmp(enforceBuf, "0") == 0 || _stricmp(enforceBuf, "false") == 0) {
            cfg.enforce_ban = false;
        } else if (_stricmp(enforceBuf, "1") == 0 || _stricmp(enforceBuf, "true") == 0) {
            cfg.enforce_ban = true;
        }
    }

    char antiDebugBuf[16]{};
    len = GetEnvironmentVariableA("NOESCAPE_ENABLE_ANTI_DEBUG", antiDebugBuf, sizeof(antiDebugBuf));
    if (len > 0) {
        if (_stricmp(antiDebugBuf, "0") == 0 || _stricmp(antiDebugBuf, "false") == 0) {
            cfg.enable_anti_debug = false;
        } else if (_stricmp(antiDebugBuf, "1") == 0 || _stricmp(antiDebugBuf, "true") == 0) {
            cfg.enable_anti_debug = true;
        }
    }

    char heartbeatBuf[16]{};
    len = GetEnvironmentVariableA("NOESCAPE_ENABLE_HEARTBEAT", heartbeatBuf, sizeof(heartbeatBuf));
    if (len > 0) {
        if (_stricmp(heartbeatBuf, "1") == 0 || _stricmp(heartbeatBuf, "true") == 0) {
            cfg.enable_heartbeat = true;
        }
    }

    char termBuf[16]{};
    len = GetEnvironmentVariableA("NOESCAPE_TERMINATE_ON_BAN", termBuf, sizeof(termBuf));
    if (len > 0) {
        if (_stricmp(termBuf, "0") == 0 || _stricmp(termBuf, "false") == 0) {
            cfg.terminate_on_ban = false;
        } else if (_stricmp(termBuf, "1") == 0 || _stricmp(termBuf, "true") == 0) {
            cfg.terminate_on_ban = true;
        }
    } else {
        cfg.terminate_on_ban = true;
    }

    char banUiBuf[16]{};
    len = GetEnvironmentVariableA("NOESCAPE_SHOW_BAN_UI", banUiBuf, sizeof(banUiBuf));
    if (len > 0) {
        if (_stricmp(banUiBuf, "0") == 0 || _stricmp(banUiBuf, "false") == 0) {
            cfg.show_ban_ui = false;
        } else if (_stricmp(banUiBuf, "1") == 0 || _stricmp(banUiBuf, "true") == 0) {
            cfg.show_ban_ui = true;
        }
    } else {
        cfg.show_ban_ui = true;
    }

    char uiPathBuf[MAX_PATH]{};
    len = GetEnvironmentVariableA("NOESCAPE_BAN_UI_PATH", uiPathBuf, sizeof(uiPathBuf));
    if (len > 0) {
        cfg.ban_ui_path = uiPathBuf;
    }

    if (!g_winShutdownRequested.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
        if (NoEscape_Init(&cfg) == 0) {
            if (g_winShutdownRequested.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
                NoEscape_Shutdown();
            } else {
                NoEscape_Start();
            }
        }
    }

    g_winInitFinished.store(true, std::memory_order_release);

    // Keep thread alive until process shutdown or explicit FreeLibrary unload
    while (!g_winShutdownRequested.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
        Sleep(20);
    }
    return 0;
}

} // namespace

extern "C" BOOL WINAPI DllMain(HINSTANCE hinstDLL, DWORD fdwReason, LPVOID lpReserved) {
    switch (fdwReason) {
    case DLL_PROCESS_ATTACH:
        DisableThreadLibraryCalls(hinstDLL);

        // Fast opt-out check to avoid thread creation if explicitly disabled
        {
#if defined(NOESCAPE_SECURE_BUILD) || defined(NDEBUG)
            char testModeBuf[16]{};
            DWORD testLen = GetEnvironmentVariableA("NOESCAPE_TEST_MODE", testModeBuf, sizeof(testModeBuf));
            bool allowDevOptOut = (testLen > 0 && (_stricmp(testModeBuf, "1") == 0 || _stricmp(testModeBuf, "true") == 0));
#else
            bool allowDevOptOut = true;
#endif
            if (allowDevOptOut) {
                char envBuf[32]{};
                DWORD len = GetEnvironmentVariableA("NOESCAPE_DISABLE_AUTO_INIT", envBuf, sizeof(envBuf));
                if (len > 0 && len < sizeof(envBuf) && (_stricmp(envBuf, "1") == 0 || _stricmp(envBuf, "true") == 0)) {
                    break;
                }
                len = GetEnvironmentVariableA("NOESCAPE_AUTO_START", envBuf, sizeof(envBuf));
                if (len > 0 && len < sizeof(envBuf) && (_stricmp(envBuf, "0") == 0 || _stricmp(envBuf, "false") == 0)) {
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        // Classic Windows Loader Lock Rule:
        // Spawning a background worker thread via CreateThread is safe.
        // Never wait for or perform blocking operations inside DllMain.
        g_winWorkerThread = CreateThread(
            nullptr,
            0,
            WindowsAutoInitWorker,
            nullptr,
            0,
            nullptr
        );
        break;

    case DLL_PROCESS_DETACH:
        g_winShutdownRequested.store(true, std::memory_order_release);

        if (lpReserved != nullptr) {
            // Process termination (ExitProcess):
            // Windows has already forcefully terminated all other threads.
            // Calling NoEscape_Shutdown() or waiting on/joining threads causes an unrecoverable
            // loader lock deadlock because the worker threads are already dead.
            if (g_winWorkerThread != NULL) {
                CloseHandle(g_winWorkerThread);
                g_winWorkerThread = NULL;
            }
        } else {
            // Explicit unload via FreeLibrary while process continues:
            // Other threads are still running normally, so full graceful shutdown is performed.
            NoEscape_Shutdown();
            if (g_winWorkerThread != NULL) {
                WaitForSingleObject(g_winWorkerThread, 3000);
                CloseHandle(g_winWorkerThread);
                g_winWorkerThread = NULL;
            }
        }
        break;

    case DLL_THREAD_ATTACH:
    case DLL_THREAD_DETACH:
        break;
    }
    return TRUE;
}

// ============================================================================
// Linux Platform Auto-Initialization (ELF Constructor & Destructor)
// ============================================================================
#else

#include <pthread.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#include <cstring>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <strings.h>
#include <chrono>
#include <thread>
#include <atomic>

namespace {

pthread_t g_linuxWorkerThread{};
std::atomic<bool> g_linuxThreadSpawned{false};
std::atomic<bool> g_linuxShutdownRequested{false};
std::atomic<bool> g_linuxInitInProgress{false};
std::atomic<bool> g_linuxInitFinished{false};

void* LinuxAutoInitWorker(void* arg) {
    (void)arg;
    g_linuxInitInProgress.store(true, std::memory_order_release);

    // Yield/sleep briefly to let dynamic linker complete relocations and host process main begin
    for (int i = 0; i < 10 && !g_linuxShutdownRequested.load(std::memory_order_acquire); ++i) {
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(1));
    }

    if (g_linuxShutdownRequested.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
        g_linuxInitFinished.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
        return nullptr;
    }

#if defined(NOESCAPE_SECURE_BUILD) || defined(NDEBUG)
    const char* testEnv = std::getenv("NOESCAPE_TEST_MODE");
    bool allowDevOptOut = (testEnv && (std::strcmp(testEnv, "1") == 0 || strcasecmp(testEnv, "true") == 0));
#else
    bool allowDevOptOut = true;
#endif

    if (allowDevOptOut) {
        const char* disableEnv = std::getenv("NOESCAPE_DISABLE_AUTO_INIT");
        if (disableEnv && (std::strcmp(disableEnv, "1") == 0 || strcasecmp(disableEnv, "true") == 0)) {
            g_linuxInitFinished.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
            return nullptr;
        }

        const char* autoStartEnv = std::getenv("NOESCAPE_AUTO_START");
        if (autoStartEnv && (std::strcmp(autoStartEnv, "0") == 0 || strcasecmp(autoStartEnv, "false") == 0)) {
            g_linuxInitFinished.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
            return nullptr;
        }
    }

    NoEscapeConfig cfg = NoEscape_GetDefaultConfig();

    // Support runtime environment variable overrides for headless / preloaded deployments
    const char* panelUrlEnv = std::getenv("NOESCAPE_PANEL_URL");
    if (panelUrlEnv && std::strlen(panelUrlEnv) > 0) {
        cfg.panel_url = panelUrlEnv;
    }

    const char* secretKeyEnv = std::getenv("NOESCAPE_SECRET_KEY");
    if (secretKeyEnv && std::strlen(secretKeyEnv) > 0) {
        cfg.secret_key = secretKeyEnv;
    }

    const char* dumpDirEnv = std::getenv("NOESCAPE_DUMP_DIR");
    if (dumpDirEnv && std::strlen(dumpDirEnv) > 0) {
        cfg.blackbox_dump_dir = dumpDirEnv;
    }

    const char* banThreshEnv = std::getenv("NOESCAPE_BAN_THRESHOLD");
    if (banThreshEnv) {
        char* endp = nullptr;
        unsigned long v = std::strtoul(banThreshEnv, &endp, 10);
        if (endp && *endp == '\0' && v > 0) {
            cfg.ban_threshold = static_cast<uint32_t>(v);
        }
    }

    const char* enforceBanEnv = std::getenv("NOESCAPE_ENFORCE_BAN");
    if (enforceBanEnv) {
        if (std::strcmp(enforceBanEnv, "0") == 0 || strcasecmp(enforceBanEnv, "false") == 0) {
            cfg.enforce_ban = false;
        } else if (std::strcmp(enforceBanEnv, "1") == 0 || strcasecmp(enforceBanEnv, "true") == 0) {
            cfg.enforce_ban = true;
        }
    }

    const char* antiDebugEnv = std::getenv("NOESCAPE_ENABLE_ANTI_DEBUG");
    if (antiDebugEnv) {
        if (std::strcmp(antiDebugEnv, "0") == 0 || strcasecmp(antiDebugEnv, "false") == 0) {
            cfg.enable_anti_debug = false;
        } else if (std::strcmp(antiDebugEnv, "1") == 0 || strcasecmp(antiDebugEnv, "true") == 0) {
            cfg.enable_anti_debug = true;
        }
    }

    const char* heartbeatEnv = std::getenv("NOESCAPE_ENABLE_HEARTBEAT");
    if (heartbeatEnv) {
        if (std::strcmp(heartbeatEnv, "1") == 0 || strcasecmp(heartbeatEnv, "true") == 0) {
            cfg.enable_heartbeat = true;
        }
    }

    const char* termEnv = std::getenv("NOESCAPE_TERMINATE_ON_BAN");
    if (termEnv) {
        if (std::strcmp(termEnv, "0") == 0 || strcasecmp(termEnv, "false") == 0) {
            cfg.terminate_on_ban = false;
        } else if (std::strcmp(termEnv, "1") == 0 || strcasecmp(termEnv, "true") == 0) {
            cfg.terminate_on_ban = true;
        }
    } else {
        cfg.terminate_on_ban = true;
    }

    const char* banUiEnv = std::getenv("NOESCAPE_SHOW_BAN_UI");
    if (banUiEnv) {
        if (std::strcmp(banUiEnv, "0") == 0 || strcasecmp(banUiEnv, "false") == 0) {
            cfg.show_ban_ui = false;
        } else if (std::strcmp(banUiEnv, "1") == 0 || strcasecmp(banUiEnv, "true") == 0) {
            cfg.show_ban_ui = true;
        }
    } else {
        cfg.show_ban_ui = true;
    }

    const char* banUiPathEnv = std::getenv("NOESCAPE_BAN_UI_PATH");
    if (banUiPathEnv && std::strlen(banUiPathEnv) > 0) {
        cfg.ban_ui_path = banUiPathEnv;
    }

    if (!g_linuxShutdownRequested.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
        if (NoEscape_Init(&cfg) == 0) {
            if (!g_linuxShutdownRequested.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
                NoEscape_Start();
            }
        }
    }

    g_linuxInitFinished.store(true, std::memory_order_release);

    // Keep worker thread alive until shutdown requested, preventing premature unmapping
    // of its thread stack while background engine audits are running.
    while (!g_linuxShutdownRequested.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(20));
    }

    return nullptr;
}

std::atomic<bool> g_linuxDestructorRan{false};

void NoEscape_AutoInit_Destructor();

__attribute__((constructor))
void NoEscape_AutoInit_Constructor() {
    // Fast path: avoid thread creation if explicitly disabled
#if defined(NOESCAPE_SECURE_BUILD) || defined(NDEBUG)
    const char* testEnv = std::getenv("NOESCAPE_TEST_MODE");
    bool allowDevOptOut = (testEnv && (std::strcmp(testEnv, "1") == 0 || strcasecmp(testEnv, "true") == 0));
#else
    bool allowDevOptOut = true;
#endif

    if (allowDevOptOut) {
        const char* disableEnv = std::getenv("NOESCAPE_DISABLE_AUTO_INIT");
        if (disableEnv && (std::strcmp(disableEnv, "1") == 0 || strcasecmp(disableEnv, "true") == 0)) {
            return;
        }
        const char* autoStartEnv = std::getenv("NOESCAPE_AUTO_START");
        if (autoStartEnv && (std::strcmp(autoStartEnv, "0") == 0 || strcasecmp(autoStartEnv, "false") == 0)) {
            return;
        }
    }

    // Register atexit handler so clean shutdown occurs before C++ runtime / thread caches are torn down
    std::atexit(NoEscape_AutoInit_Destructor);

    g_linuxThreadSpawned.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
    if (pthread_create(&g_linuxWorkerThread, nullptr, LinuxAutoInitWorker, nullptr) != 0) {
        g_linuxThreadSpawned.store(false, std::memory_order_release);
    }
}

__attribute__((destructor))
void NoEscape_AutoInit_Destructor() {
    if (g_linuxDestructorRan.exchange(true, std::memory_order_acq_rel)) {
        return;
    }
    g_linuxShutdownRequested.store(true, std::memory_order_release);

    // If init thread was spawned and has not finished initialization yet, wait briefly for it
    if (g_linuxThreadSpawned.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
        int waitCount = 0;
        while (!g_linuxInitFinished.load(std::memory_order_acquire) && waitCount++ < 200) {
            std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(5));
        }
    }

    NoEscape_Shutdown();

    // Cleanly join the auto-init worker thread before dynamic linker unloads the library
    if (g_linuxThreadSpawned.exchange(false, std::memory_order_acq_rel)) {
        pthread_join(g_linuxWorkerThread, nullptr);
    }
}

} // namespace

#endif

#endif // NOESCAPE_EXPORTS || NOESCAPE_BUILD_SHARED
```

`src/sdk/noescape_sdk.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "noescape_sdk.h"
#include "noescape/core/engine.hpp"
#include "noescape/overlay/overlay_detector.hpp"
#include "noescape/core/logger.hpp"

#include <memory>
#include <mutex>
#include <atomic>
#include <string>

namespace {

std::mutex g_lifecycle_mutex;
std::unique_ptr<noescape::core::AntiCheatEngine> g_engine;
std::atomic<noescape::core::AntiCheatEngine*> g_active_engine{nullptr};
NoEscapeViolationCallback g_callback = nullptr;

// Dedicated high-frequency input mutex to guarantee zero-stutter mouse processing
std::mutex g_input_mutex;
float g_accum_pitch = 0.0f;
float g_accum_yaw = 0.0f;

} // namespace

extern "C" {

NoEscapeConfig NoEscape_GetDefaultConfig(void) {
    NoEscapeConfig cfg{};
    cfg.panel_url = "http://127.0.0.1:8080/api/ac";
    cfg.secret_key = "NoEscape_Secret_Key_v2.0";
    cfg.blackbox_dump_dir = nullptr;
    cfg.ban_threshold = 100;
    cfg.enforce_ban = true;
    cfg.enable_anti_debug = true;
    cfg.enable_integrity_scan = true;
    cfg.enable_speedhack_detection = true;
    cfg.enable_memory_scan = true;
    cfg.enable_unhooker = true;
    cfg.enable_forensics = true;
    cfg.enable_dma_detection = true;
    cfg.enable_kinematics = true;
    cfg.enable_overlay_detection = true;
    cfg.enable_blackbox_dump = true;
    cfg.enable_heartbeat = false;
    cfg.terminate_on_ban = false;
    cfg.show_ban_ui = true;
    cfg.ban_ui_path = nullptr;
    return cfg;
}

int NoEscape_Init(const NoEscapeConfig* config) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_lifecycle_mutex);

    if (g_engine) {
        g_active_engine.store(nullptr, std::memory_order_release);
        g_engine->Stop();
        g_engine.reset();
    }

    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> inLock(g_input_mutex);
        g_accum_pitch = 0.0f;
        g_accum_yaw = 0.0f;
    }

    noescape::core::EngineConfig engineCfg;
    if (config) {
        if (config->panel_url) engineCfg.panelUrl = config->panel_url;
        if (config->secret_key) engineCfg.secretKey = config->secret_key;
        if (config->blackbox_dump_dir) engineCfg.blackboxDumpDirectory = config->blackbox_dump_dir;
        engineCfg.banScoreThreshold = config->ban_threshold;
        engineCfg.enforceBanOnViolation = config->enforce_ban;
        engineCfg.enableAntiDebug = config->enable_anti_debug;
        engineCfg.enableIntegrityScan = config->enable_integrity_scan;
        engineCfg.enableSpeedhackDetection = config->enable_speedhack_detection;
        engineCfg.enableMemoryScan = config->enable_memory_scan;
        engineCfg.enableUnhooker = config->enable_unhooker;
        engineCfg.enableForensics = config->enable_forensics;
        engineCfg.enableDmaDetection = config->enable_dma_detection;
        engineCfg.enableKinematics = config->enable_kinematics;
        engineCfg.enableOverlayDetection = config->enable_overlay_detection;
        engineCfg.enableBlackboxDump = config->enable_blackbox_dump;
        engineCfg.enableHeartbeat = config->enable_heartbeat;
        engineCfg.terminateProcessOnBan = config->terminate_on_ban;
        engineCfg.showBanUi = config->show_ban_ui;
        if (config->ban_ui_path) engineCfg.banUiExecutable = config->ban_ui_path;
    } else {
        engineCfg.enableHeartbeat = false;
    }

    g_engine = std::make_unique<noescape::core::AntiCheatEngine>(engineCfg);

    // Wire violation callback to notify the registered C callback for every detection event
    g_engine->SetViolationCallback([](const noescape::core::DetectionEvent& ev) {
        if (g_callback) {
            g_callback(static_cast<int>(ev.type), 
                       static_cast<int>(ev.threat_score), 
                       ev.details.c_str());
        }
    });

    if (!g_engine->Initialize()) {
        g_engine.reset();
        g_active_engine.store(nullptr, std::memory_order_release);
        return -1;
    }

    g_active_engine.store(g_engine.get(), std::memory_order_release);
    return 0;
}

void NoEscape_Shutdown(void) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_lifecycle_mutex);
    g_active_engine.store(nullptr, std::memory_order_release);

    if (g_engine) {
        g_engine->Stop();
        g_engine.reset();
    }

    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> inLock(g_input_mutex);
    g_accum_pitch = 0.0f;
    g_accum_yaw = 0.0f;
}

void NoEscape_Start(void) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_lifecycle_mutex);
    if (g_engine) {
        g_engine->Start();
    }
}

void NoEscape_Stop(void) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_lifecycle_mutex);
    if (g_engine) {
        g_engine->Stop();
    }
}

void NoEscape_Tick(void) {
    auto* engine = g_active_engine.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    if (!engine) return;

    // AuditCycle() runs synchronous module checks and dispatches any detected events
    // through ProcessDetection(), which automatically notifies the registered ViolationCallback.
    engine->AuditCycle();
}

void NoEscape_PushMouseEvent(float delta_yaw, float delta_pitch, uint64_t timestamp_ms) {
    auto* engine = g_active_engine.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    if (!engine) return;

    float p = 0.0f, y = 0.0f;
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_input_mutex);
        g_accum_pitch += delta_pitch;
        if (g_accum_pitch > 89.0f) g_accum_pitch = 89.0f;
        if (g_accum_pitch < -89.0f) g_accum_pitch = -89.0f;

        g_accum_yaw += delta_yaw;
        while (g_accum_yaw > 180.0f) g_accum_yaw -= 360.0f;
        while (g_accum_yaw < -180.0f) g_accum_yaw += 360.0f;
        p = g_accum_pitch;
        y = g_accum_yaw;
    }

    // Pass sample into the engine's internal synchronized input queue without holding SDK mutex
    engine->PushInputSample(p, y, timestamp_ms);
}

void NoEscape_ResetInput(void) {
    auto* engine = g_active_engine.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_input_mutex);
        g_accum_pitch = 0.0f;
        g_accum_yaw = 0.0f;
    }
    if (engine) {
        engine->GetAimAnalyzer().Reset();
    }
}

uint32_t NoEscape_GetThreatScore(void) {
    auto* engine = g_active_engine.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    if (!engine) return 0;
    return engine->GetThreatScore();
}

bool NoEscape_IsBanned(void) {
    auto* engine = g_active_engine.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    if (!engine) return false;
    return engine->IsBanned();
}

void NoEscape_SetViolationCallback(NoEscapeViolationCallback callback) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_lifecycle_mutex);
    g_callback = callback;
}

void NoEscape_AddPhantom(uint32_t id, float x, float y, float z) {
    auto* engine = g_active_engine.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    if (engine) engine->GetPhantomMonitor().AddPhantom(id, x, y, z);
}

void NoEscape_UpdatePhantom(uint32_t id, float x, float y, float z) {
    auto* engine = g_active_engine.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    if (engine) engine->GetPhantomMonitor().UpdatePhantom(id, x, y, z);
}

void NoEscape_PushView(float x, float y, float z, float pitch, float yaw, uint64_t timestamp_ms) {
    auto* engine = g_active_engine.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    if (engine) engine->PushViewSample(x, y, z, pitch, yaw, timestamp_ms);
}

void NoEscape_SetViewport(int x, int y, int width, int height) {
    noescape::overlay::OverlayDetector::Instance().SetGameViewport(x, y, width, height);
}

} // extern "C"
```

`src/security/anti_debug_linux.cpp`:

```cpp
#if !defined(_WIN32)

#include "noescape/security/anti_debug.hpp"
#include "noescape/core/obfuscation.hpp"
#include <sys/ptrace.h>
#include <sys/prctl.h>
#include <sys/syscall.h>
#include <linux/perf_event.h>
#include <linux/hw_breakpoint.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#include <fcntl.h>
#include <dirent.h>
#include <fstream>
#include <sstream>
#include <cstring>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cctype>
#include <climits>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cerrno>

namespace noescape::security {

namespace {

std::string ToLower(const std::string& str) {
    std::string s = str;
    std::transform(s.begin(), s.end(), s.begin(), [](unsigned char c) {
        return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c));
    });
    return s;
}

static void __attribute__((noinline, used)) HardwareBpProbeTarget() {}

static bool CheckThreadHwBp(pid_t tid) {
    int fds[4];
    int count = 0;
    bool detected = false;

    for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
        struct perf_event_attr pe;
        std::memset(&pe, 0, sizeof(pe));
        pe.type = PERF_TYPE_BREAKPOINT;
        pe.size = sizeof(pe);
        pe.bp_type = HW_BREAKPOINT_X;
        pe.bp_addr = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(&HardwareBpProbeTarget);
        pe.bp_len = sizeof(long);
        pe.disabled = 1;
        pe.exclude_kernel = 1;
        pe.exclude_hv = 1;

        int fd = static_cast<int>(syscall(__NR_perf_event_open, &pe, tid, -1, -1, 0));
        if (fd >= 0) {
            fds[count++] = fd;
        } else {
            if (errno == ENOSPC || errno == EBUSY) {
                // Hardware breakpoint debug registers (DR0-DR3) exhausted prematurely
                detected = true;
            }
            break;
        }
    }

    for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
        close(fds[i]);
    }

    return detected;
}

} // namespace

bool AntiDebug::IsDebuggerAttached() {
    int tracerPid = 0;
    if (CheckTracerPid(tracerPid) && tracerPid > 0) {
        return true;
    }
    if (CheckWchanWait()) {
        return true;
    }
    return false;
}

bool AntiDebug::CheckTracerPid(int& outPid) {
    outPid = 0;
    std::ifstream statusFile(NOESCAPE_XOR("/proc/self/status"));
    if (!statusFile.is_open()) return false;

    std::string line;
    std::string targetPrefix = NOESCAPE_XOR("TracerPid:");
    while (std::getline(statusFile, line)) {
        if (line.compare(0, targetPrefix.length(), targetPrefix) == 0) {
            std::istringstream iss(line.substr(targetPrefix.length()));
            iss >> outPid;
            return outPid != 0;
        }
    }
    return false;
}

bool AntiDebug::CheckWchanWait() {
    std::ifstream wchanFile(NOESCAPE_XOR("/proc/self/wchan"));
    if (!wchanFile.is_open()) return false;

    std::string wchan;
    if (std::getline(wchanFile, wchan)) {
        std::string lower = ToLower(wchan);
        if (lower.find("ptrace") != std::string::npos ||
            lower.find("sys_ptrace") != std::string::npos) {
            return true;
        }
    }
    return false;
}

bool AntiDebug::CheckHardwareBreakpoints() {
    // 1. Check calling thread
    if (CheckThreadHwBp(0)) {
        return true;
    }

    // 2. Check all other threads in /proc/self/task/
    pid_t selfTid = static_cast<pid_t>(syscall(SYS_gettid));
    DIR* taskDir = opendir(NOESCAPE_XOR("/proc/self/task"));
    if (taskDir) {
        struct dirent* entry = nullptr;
        while ((entry = readdir(taskDir)) != nullptr) {
            if (entry->d_type != DT_DIR && entry->d_type != DT_UNKNOWN) continue;
            std::string tidStr = entry->d_name;
            if (tidStr.empty() || !std::isdigit(static_cast<unsigned char>(tidStr[0]))) continue;

            pid_t tid = static_cast<pid_t>(std::strtol(tidStr.c_str(), nullptr, 10));
            if (tid > 0 && tid != selfTid && CheckThreadHwBp(tid)) {
                closedir(taskDir);
                return true;
            }
        }
        closedir(taskDir);
    }

    return false;
}

bool AntiDebug::HideCurrentThread() {
    // PR_SET_DUMPABLE=0 disables ptrace attaching by non-root users and suppresses core dumps.
    // However, on Linux setting PR_SET_DUMPABLE=0 changes ownership of /proc/self/* to root,
    // which prevents JIT runtimes (such as Java JVM / HotSpot) from accessing /proc/self/maps
    // and causes fatal JVM aborts. For JVM environments, only restrict ptrace via PR_SET_PTRACER.
    bool isJvmRuntime = false;
    char exeBuf[PATH_MAX]{};
    ssize_t len = readlink("/proc/self/exe", exeBuf, sizeof(exeBuf) - 1);
    if (len > 0) {
        exeBuf[len] = '\0';
        std::string exeStr(exeBuf);
        std::transform(exeStr.begin(), exeStr.end(), exeStr.begin(), ::tolower);
        if (exeStr.find("java") != std::string::npos ||
            exeStr.find("mono") != std::string::npos ||
            exeStr.find("node") != std::string::npos ||
            exeStr.find("dotnet") != std::string::npos) {
            isJvmRuntime = true;
        }
    }

    int res1 = 0;
    if (!isJvmRuntime) {
        res1 = prctl(PR_SET_DUMPABLE, 0, 0, 0, 0);
    }
#ifdef PR_SET_PTRACER
    int res2 = prctl(PR_SET_PTRACER, 0, 0, 0, 0);
    return (res1 == 0 && res2 == 0);
#else
    return (res1 == 0);
#endif
}

bool AntiDebug::CheckKnownDebuggerProcesses(const std::vector<std::string>& blacklist, std::string& detectedName) {
    DIR* procDir = opendir("/proc");
    if (!procDir) return false;

    struct dirent* entry = nullptr;
    while ((entry = readdir(procDir)) != nullptr) {
        if (entry->d_type != DT_DIR && entry->d_type != DT_UNKNOWN) continue;

        // Check if directory name is numeric (PID)
        char* endPtr = nullptr;
        long pid = strtol(entry->d_name, &endPtr, 10);
        if (pid <= 0 || *endPtr != '\0') continue;

        // Ignore our own PID
        if (pid == getpid()) continue;

        std::string commPath = std::string("/proc/") + entry->d_name + "/comm";
        std::ifstream commFile(commPath);
        if (!commFile.is_open()) continue;

        std::string comm;
        if (std::getline(commFile, comm)) {
            std::string lowerComm = ToLower(comm);
            for (const auto& target : blacklist) {
                if (lowerComm.find(ToLower(target)) != std::string::npos) {
                    detectedName = comm;
                    closedir(procDir);
                    return true;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    closedir(procDir);
    return false;
}

std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> AntiDebug::PerformAudit(const std::vector<std::string>& blacklist) {
    std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> events;

    int tracerPid = 0;
    if (CheckTracerPid(tracerPid) && tracerPid > 0) {
        core::DetectionEvent ev;
        ev.type = core::DetectionType::ANTI_DEBUG_TRACER;
        ev.severity = core::Severity::FATAL_BAN;
        ev.component = "AntiDebug::LinuxTracer";
        ev.details = "Active TracerPid detected attached to process: PID " + std::to_string(tracerPid);
        ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(time(nullptr));
        ev.threat_score = 100;
        events.push_back(ev);
    }

    if (CheckWchanWait()) {
        core::DetectionEvent ev;
        ev.type = core::DetectionType::ANTI_DEBUG_TRACER;
        ev.severity = core::Severity::CRITICAL;
        ev.component = "AntiDebug::LinuxWchan";
        ev.details = "Process is waiting in ptrace_stop state via kernel wchan";
        ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(time(nullptr));
        ev.threat_score = 80;
        events.push_back(ev);
    }

    if (CheckHardwareBreakpoints()) {
        core::DetectionEvent ev;
        ev.type = core::DetectionType::ANTI_DEBUG_HARDWARE_BP;
        ev.severity = core::Severity::CRITICAL;
        ev.component = "AntiDebug::HardwareRegisters";
        ev.details = "Hardware breakpoint detected in CPU debug registers (DR0-DR3)";
        ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(time(nullptr));
        ev.threat_score = 90;
        events.push_back(ev);
    }

    std::string detectedTool;
    if (CheckKnownDebuggerProcesses(blacklist, detectedTool)) {
        core::DetectionEvent ev;
        ev.type = core::DetectionType::ANTI_DEBUG_TOOL;
        ev.severity = core::Severity::HIGH;
        ev.component = "AntiDebug::ProcessScanner";
        ev.details = "Debugger or analysis tool process running in background: " + detectedTool;
        ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(time(nullptr));
        ev.threat_score = 60;
        events.push_back(ev);
    }

    return events;
}

} // namespace noescape::security

#endif
```

`src/security/anti_debug_windows.cpp`:

```cpp
#if defined(_WIN32)

#include "noescape/security/anti_debug.hpp"
#include "noescape/core/obfuscation.hpp"
#include <windows.h>
#include <tlhelp32.h>
#include <winternl.h>
#include <intrin.h>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cctype>

#pragma comment(lib, "ntdll.lib")

namespace noescape::security {

namespace {

std::string ToLower(const std::string& str) {
    std::string s = str;
    std::transform(s.begin(), s.end(), s.begin(), [](unsigned char c) {
        return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(c));
    });
    return s;
}

typedef NTSTATUS(NTAPI* pfnNtSetInformationThread)(
    HANDLE ThreadHandle,
    ULONG ThreadInformationClass,
    PVOID ThreadInformation,
    ULONG ThreadInformationLength
);

typedef NTSTATUS(NTAPI* pfnNtQueryInformationProcess)(
    HANDLE ProcessHandle,
    ULONG ProcessInformationClass,
    PVOID ProcessInformation,
    ULONG ProcessInformationLength,
    PULONG ReturnLength
);

} // namespace

bool AntiDebug::IsDebuggerAttached() {
    // 1. Direct Win32 API Check
    if (IsDebuggerPresent()) return true;

    // 2. Remote Debugger Attached Check
    BOOL isRemotePresent = FALSE;
    if (CheckRemoteDebuggerPresent(GetCurrentProcess(), &isRemotePresent) && isRemotePresent) {
        return true;
    }

    // 3. PEB BeingDebugged & NtGlobalFlag verification
#if defined(_M_X64) || defined(__x86_64__)
    auto peb = reinterpret_cast<PPEB>(__readgsqword(0x60));
#else
    auto peb = reinterpret_cast<PPEB>(__readfsdword(0x30));
#endif
    if (peb) {
        if (peb->BeingDebugged) return true;

        // NtGlobalFlag check (offset 0xBC on x64, 0x68 on x86)
        // Flags: FLG_HEAP_ENABLE_TAIL_CHECK (0x10) | FLG_HEAP_ENABLE_FREE_CHECK (0x20) | FLG_HEAP_VALIDATE_PARAMETERS (0x40)
#if defined(_M_X64) || defined(__x86_64__)
        DWORD ntGlobalFlag = *reinterpret_cast<PDWORD>(reinterpret_cast<PBYTE>(peb) + 0xBC);
#else
        DWORD ntGlobalFlag = *reinterpret_cast<PDWORD>(reinterpret_cast<PBYTE>(peb) + 0x68);
#endif
        if (ntGlobalFlag & 0x70) return true;
    }

    // 4. NtQueryInformationProcess (ProcessDebugPort = 7)
    HMODULE hNtdll = GetModuleHandleA(NOESCAPE_XOR("ntdll.dll"));
    if (hNtdll) {
        auto pNtQuery = reinterpret_cast<pfnNtQueryInformationProcess>(
            GetProcAddress(hNtdll, NOESCAPE_XOR("NtQueryInformationProcess"))
        );
        if (pNtQuery) {
            DWORD_PTR debugPort = 0;
            NTSTATUS status = pNtQuery(GetCurrentProcess(), 7, &debugPort, sizeof(debugPort), nullptr);
            if (status == 0 && debugPort != 0) return true;
        }
    }

    return false;
}

bool AntiDebug::CheckHardwareBreakpoints() {
    DWORD currentPid = GetCurrentProcessId();
    HANDLE snap = CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(TH32CS_SNAPTHREAD, 0);
    if (snap == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) {
        CONTEXT ctx{};
        ctx.ContextFlags = CONTEXT_DEBUG_REGISTERS;
        if (GetThreadContext(GetCurrentThread(), &ctx)) {
            return (ctx.Dr0 || ctx.Dr1 || ctx.Dr2 || ctx.Dr3 || (ctx.Dr7 & 0x000000FF));
        }
        return false;
    }

    THREADENTRY32 te{};
    te.dwSize = sizeof(te);
    bool foundBreakpoint = false;

    if (Thread32First(snap, &te)) {
        do {
            if (te.th32OwnerProcessID == currentPid) {
                HANDLE hThread = OpenThread(THREAD_QUERY_INFORMATION | THREAD_GET_CONTEXT, FALSE, te.th32ThreadID);
                if (hThread) {
                    CONTEXT ctx{};
                    ctx.ContextFlags = CONTEXT_DEBUG_REGISTERS;
                    if (GetThreadContext(hThread, &ctx)) {
                        if (ctx.Dr0 || ctx.Dr1 || ctx.Dr2 || ctx.Dr3 || (ctx.Dr7 & 0x000000FF)) {
                            foundBreakpoint = true;
                            CloseHandle(hThread);
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                    CloseHandle(hThread);
                }
            }
        } while (Thread32Next(snap, &te));
    }

    CloseHandle(snap);
    return foundBreakpoint;
}

bool AntiDebug::CheckTracerPid(int& outPid) {
    outPid = 0;
    return false;
}

bool AntiDebug::CheckWchanWait() {
    return false;
}

bool AntiDebug::HideCurrentThread() {
    HMODULE hNtdll = GetModuleHandleA(NOESCAPE_XOR("ntdll.dll"));
    if (!hNtdll) return false;

    auto pNtSetInfo = reinterpret_cast<pfnNtSetInformationThread>(
        GetProcAddress(hNtdll, NOESCAPE_XOR("NtSetInformationThread"))
    );
    if (!pNtSetInfo) return false;

    // ThreadHideFromDebugger = 0x11
    NTSTATUS status = pNtSetInfo(GetCurrentThread(), 0x11, nullptr, 0);
    return status == 0;
}

bool AntiDebug::CheckKnownDebuggerProcesses(const std::vector<std::string>& blacklist, std::string& detectedName) {
    HANDLE snap = CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(TH32CS_SNAPPROCESS, 0);
    if (snap == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return false;

    PROCESSENTRY32W pe{};
    pe.dwSize = sizeof(pe);

    if (Process32FirstW(snap, &pe)) {
        do {
            char nameBuf[MAX_PATH]{};
            WideCharToMultiByte(CP_UTF8, 0, pe.szExeFile, -1, nameBuf, MAX_PATH, nullptr, nullptr);
            std::string exeName = ToLower(nameBuf);

            for (const auto& target : blacklist) {
                if (exeName.find(ToLower(target)) != std::string::npos) {
                    detectedName = nameBuf;
                    CloseHandle(snap);
                    return true;
                }
            }
        } while (Process32NextW(snap, &pe));
    }

    CloseHandle(snap);
    return false;
}

std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> AntiDebug::PerformAudit(const std::vector<std::string>& blacklist) {
    std::vector<core::DetectionEvent> events;

    if (IsDebuggerAttached()) {
        core::DetectionEvent ev;
        ev.type = core::DetectionType::ANTI_DEBUG_TRACER;
        ev.severity = core::Severity::FATAL_BAN;
        ev.component = "AntiDebug::WindowsDebugger";
        ev.details = "Active debugger detected via PEB / RemoteDebugger / ProcessDebugPort";
        ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(GetTickCount64());
        ev.threat_score = 100;
        events.push_back(ev);
    }

    if (CheckHardwareBreakpoints()) {
        core::DetectionEvent ev;
        ev.type = core::DetectionType::ANTI_DEBUG_HARDWARE_BP;
        ev.severity = core::Severity::CRITICAL;
        ev.component = "AntiDebug::HardwareRegisters";
        ev.details = "Hardware breakpoint detected in CPU debug registers (DR0-DR7)";
        ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(GetTickCount64());
        ev.threat_score = 90;
        events.push_back(ev);
    }

    std::string detectedTool;
    if (CheckKnownDebuggerProcesses(blacklist, detectedTool)) {
        core::DetectionEvent ev;
        ev.type = core::DetectionType::ANTI_DEBUG_TOOL;
        ev.severity = core::Severity::HIGH;
        ev.component = "AntiDebug::ProcessScanner";
        ev.details = "Known debugger / memory dumper running: " + detectedTool;
        ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(GetTickCount64());
        ev.threat_score = 70;
        events.push_back(ev);
    }

    return events;
}

} // namespace noescape::security

#endif
```
