`src/security/integrity.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "noescape/security/integrity.hpp"
#include "noescape/network/crypto_util.hpp"
#include "noescape/core/obfuscation.hpp"

#if defined(_WIN32)
#include <windows.h>
#else
#include <link.h>
#include <dlfcn.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#include <fstream>
#include <sstream>
#endif

namespace noescape::security {

IntegrityChecker& IntegrityChecker::Instance() {
    static IntegrityChecker* instance = new IntegrityChecker();
    return *instance;
}

bool IntegrityChecker::LocateTextSection(uintptr_t& outStart, size_t& outSize) {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    HMODULE hModule = nullptr;
    // Resolve the exact module (DLL or EXE) hosting this anti-cheat instance
    if (!GetModuleHandleExA(GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_FROM_ADDRESS | 
                            GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_UNCHANGED_REFCOUNT,
                            reinterpret_cast<LPCSTR>(&IntegrityChecker::Instance), 
                            &hModule) || !hModule) {
        hModule = GetModuleHandleA(nullptr);
    }
    if (!hModule) return false;

    auto base = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(hModule);
    auto dosHeader = reinterpret_cast<PIMAGE_DOS_HEADER>(base);
    if (dosHeader->e_magic != IMAGE_DOS_SIGNATURE) return false;

    auto ntHeaders = reinterpret_cast<PIMAGE_NT_HEADERS>(base + dosHeader->e_lfanew);
    if (ntHeaders->Signature != IMAGE_NT_SIGNATURE) return false;

    auto section = IMAGE_FIRST_SECTION(ntHeaders);
    for (WORD i = 0; i < ntHeaders->FileHeader.NumberOfSections; ++i, ++section) {
        if (std::memcmp(section->Name, ".text", 5) == 0 || 
            (section->Characteristics & IMAGE_SCN_MEM_EXECUTE)) {
            outStart = base + section->VirtualAddress;
            outSize = section->Misc.VirtualSize;
            return true;
        }
    }
    return false;
#else
    // Determine the base address of the module hosting IntegrityChecker (so or exe)
    Dl_info dlinfo{};
    void* targetBase = nullptr;
    if (dladdr(reinterpret_cast<void*>(&IntegrityChecker::Instance), &dlinfo) && dlinfo.dli_fbase) {
        targetBase = dlinfo.dli_fbase;
    }

    struct CallbackData {
        void* targetBase{nullptr};
        uintptr_t start{0};
        size_t size{0};
        bool found{false};
    };

    CallbackData cbData;
    cbData.targetBase = targetBase;

    auto callback = [](struct dl_phdr_info* info, size_t, void* data) -> int {
        auto pData = static_cast<CallbackData*>(data);
        bool isTarget = false;

        if (pData->targetBase) {
            isTarget = (reinterpret_cast<void*>(info->dlpi_addr) == pData->targetBase);
        } else {
            // Main binary fallback
            isTarget = (info->dlpi_name == nullptr || info->dlpi_name[0] == '\0');
        }

        if (isTarget) {
            for (int i = 0; i < info->dlpi_phnum; ++i) {
                if (info->dlpi_phdr[i].p_type == PT_LOAD && 
                    (info->dlpi_phdr[i].p_flags & PF_X)) {
                    pData->start = info->dlpi_addr + info->dlpi_phdr[i].p_vaddr;
                    pData->size = info->dlpi_phdr[i].p_memsz;
                    pData->found = true;
                    return 1;
                }
            }
        }
        return 0;
    };

    dl_iterate_phdr(callback, &cbData);
    if (cbData.found && cbData.size > 0) {
        outStart = cbData.start;
        outSize = cbData.size;
        return true;
    }

    // Fallback: Read /proc/self/maps for primary r-xp mapping
    std::ifstream maps(NOESCAPE_XOR("/proc/self/maps"));
    if (maps.is_open()) {
        std::string line;
        while (std::getline(maps, line)) {
            if (line.find(" r-xp ") != std::string::npos || line.find(" r--p ") != std::string::npos) {
                uintptr_t s = 0, e = 0;
                char dash = 0;
                std::istringstream iss(line);
                if (iss >> std::hex >> s >> dash >> e) {
                    if (line.find("r-xp") != std::string::npos) {
                        outStart = s;
                        outSize = e - s;
                        return true;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    return false;
#endif
}

bool IntegrityChecker::Initialize() {
    if (initialized_) return true;

    if (!LocateTextSection(text_start_, text_size_)) {
        return false;
    }

    baseline_hash_ = ComputeCurrentTextHash();
    if (baseline_hash_.empty()) {
        return false;
    }

    initialized_ = true;
    return true;
}

std::string IntegrityChecker::ComputeCurrentTextHash() const {
    if (text_start_ == 0 || text_size_ == 0) return "";
    return network::CryptoUtil::Sha256(reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t*>(text_start_), text_size_);
}

std::optional<core::DetectionEvent> IntegrityChecker::VerifyIntegrity() {
    if (!initialized_) {
        if (!Initialize()) return std::nullopt;
    }

    std::string currentHash = ComputeCurrentTextHash();
    if (currentHash != baseline_hash_) {
        core::DetectionEvent ev;
        ev.type = core::DetectionType::SELF_INTEGRITY_VIOLATION;
        ev.severity = core::Severity::FATAL_BAN;
        ev.component = "IntegrityChecker::TextSection";
        ev.details = "Executable text segment hash mismatch. Expected: " + 
                     baseline_hash_.substr(0, 16) + "... Actual: " + currentHash.substr(0, 16) + "...";
        ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(time(nullptr));
        ev.threat_score = 100;
        return ev;
    }

    return std::nullopt;
}

} // namespace noescape::security

```

`src/security/timing.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "noescape/security/timing.hpp"
#include <thread>
#include <cmath>

#if defined(_MSC_VER)
#include <intrin.h>
#elif defined(__x86_64__) || defined(__i386__)
#include <x86intrin.h>
#endif

namespace noescape::security {

TimingIntegrity& TimingIntegrity::Instance() {
    static TimingIntegrity* instance = new TimingIntegrity();
    return *instance;
}

uint64_t TimingIntegrity::ReadHardwareTsc() const {
#if defined(_MSC_VER) || defined(__x86_64__) || defined(__i386__)
    return __rdtsc();
#else
    // Fallback on ARM/other: use high-resolution hardware steady clock
    auto now = std::chrono::steady_clock::now().time_since_epoch();
    return static_cast<uint64_t>(std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::nanoseconds>(now).count());
#endif
}

bool TimingIntegrity::Calibrate(uint32_t calibrationMs) {
    auto startOs = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
    uint64_t startTsc = ReadHardwareTsc();

    std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(calibrationMs));

    auto endOs = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
    uint64_t endTsc = ReadHardwareTsc();

    double elapsedMs = std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(endOs - startOs).count();
    if (elapsedMs <= 0.0 || endTsc <= startTsc) {
        return false;
    }

    cycles_per_ms_ = static_cast<double>(endTsc - startTsc) / elapsedMs;
    last_tsc_ = endTsc;
    last_os_time_ = endOs;
    calibrated_ = true;
    consecutive_anomalies_ = 0;
    return true;
}

std::optional<core::DetectionEvent> TimingIntegrity::CheckSpeedhack() {
    if (!calibrated_) {
        if (!Calibrate()) return std::nullopt;
    }

    auto nowOs = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
    uint64_t nowTsc = ReadHardwareTsc();

    double elapsedMs = std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(nowOs - last_os_time_).count();
    // Only verify if at least 15ms have elapsed to minimize quantization error
    if (elapsedMs < 15.0) {
        return std::nullopt;
    }

    // Check for negative delta / backwards time travel
    if (nowTsc < last_tsc_) {
        core::DetectionEvent ev;
        ev.type = core::DetectionType::SPEEDHACK_TIMING_DISCREPANCY;
        ev.severity = core::Severity::FATAL_BAN;
        ev.component = "TimingIntegrity::TSC";
        ev.details = "Hardware TSC jumped backwards (Anti-Tick / Rewind exploit detected)";
        ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(time(nullptr));
        ev.threat_score = 100;
        return ev;
    }

    uint64_t deltaTsc = nowTsc - last_tsc_;
    double expectedCycles = cycles_per_ms_ * elapsedMs;
    double ratio = static_cast<double>(deltaTsc) / (expectedCycles > 0 ? expectedCycles : 1.0);

    last_tsc_ = nowTsc;
    last_os_time_ = nowOs;

    // A ratio significantly < 0.60 means OS time is ticking much faster than CPU cycles (Speedhack acceleration)
    // A ratio significantly > 1.60 means OS time was dilated / slowed down (Bullet-time or speedhack slowdown)
    if (ratio < 0.60 || ratio > 1.60) {
        consecutive_anomalies_++;
        // Require 2 consecutive anomalies to eliminate scheduling hiccups / context switch spikes
        if (consecutive_anomalies_ >= 2) {
            core::DetectionEvent ev;
            ev.type = core::DetectionType::SPEEDHACK_TIMING_DISCREPANCY;
            ev.severity = core::Severity::CRITICAL;
            ev.component = "TimingIntegrity::ClockRatio";
            ev.details = "Clock frequency deviation: ratio=" + std::to_string(ratio) +
                         " (elapsed_ms=" + std::to_string(elapsedMs) + ")";
            ev.timestamp = static_cast<uint64_t>(time(nullptr));
            ev.threat_score = 75;
            return ev;
        }
    } else {
        if (consecutive_anomalies_ > 0) consecutive_anomalies_--;
    }

    return std::nullopt;
}

} // namespace noescape::security

```

```cpp
TEST(Remediation, ObfuscationCbcKeystreamNonLinearity) {
    // 1. Verify decryption correctness of multiple diverse strings
    const char* str1 = NOESCAPE_XOR("kernel32.dll");
    const char* str2 = NOESCAPE_XOR("SeDebugPrivilege");
    const char* str3 = NOESCAPE_XOR("/proc/self/task");

    ASSERT_EQ(std::string(str1), "kernel32.dll");
    ASSERT_EQ(std::string(str2), "SeDebugPrivilege");
    ASSERT_EQ(std::string(str3), "/proc/self/task");

    // 2. Verify constexpr object creation and size
    constexpr core::XorString<13, 0x12345678> obfuscated("kernel32.dll");
    ASSERT_EQ(obfuscated.size(), 13);
    ASSERT_EQ(obfuscated.str(), "kernel32.dll");
}

TEST(Remediation, NetworkBanCheckResultStatuses) {
    network::PanelClient client("http://127.0.0.1:54321", "TestSecretKey");

    // Attempting query against nonexistent port should result in NETWORK_ERROR (fail-closed/unverified)
    auto res = client.QueryBanStatus();
    ASSERT_EQ(static_cast<int>(res.status), static_cast<int>(network::BanCheckStatus::NETWORK_ERROR));
    ASSERT_FALSE(res.reason.empty());

    bool isBanned = false;
    std::string reason;
    bool checkOk = client.CheckBanStatus(isBanned, reason);
    // When network fails, CheckBanStatus returns false (not blindly reporting clean status)
    ASSERT_FALSE(checkOk);
    ASSERT_FALSE(isBanned);
}

TEST(Remediation, OverlayCompositorWhitelist) {
    auto& detector = overlay::OverlayDetector::Instance();
    detector.ClearMockWindows();
    detector.SetGameViewport(0, 0, 1920, 1080);

    // 1. Linux Picom Compositor window
    overlay::OverlayWindowInfo picomWin;
    picomWin.title = "picom compositor";
    picomWin.class_name = "picom";
    picomWin.pid = 1001;
    picomWin.handle = 0x5001;
    picomWin.rect = {0, 0, 1920, 1080};
    picomWin.is_topmost = true;
    picomWin.is_transparent = true;
    picomWin.is_layered = true;
    detector.AddMockWindow(picomWin);

    // 2. Steam Overlay window
    overlay::OverlayWindowInfo steamWin;
    steamWin.title = "GameOverlayUI";
    steamWin.class_name = "SteamOverlay";
    steamWin.pid = 1002;
    steamWin.handle = 0x5002;
    steamWin.rect = {0, 0, 1920, 1080};
    steamWin.is_topmost = true;
    steamWin.is_transparent = true;
    steamWin.is_layered = true;
    detector.AddMockWindow(steamWin);

    // 3. MangoHud FPS HUD
    overlay::OverlayWindowInfo mangoWin;
    mangoWin.title = "MangoHud overlay";
    mangoWin.class_name = "MangoHud";
    mangoWin.pid = 1003;
    mangoWin.handle = 0x5003;
    mangoWin.rect = {0, 0, 500, 200};
    mangoWin.is_topmost = true;
    mangoWin.is_transparent = true;
    mangoWin.is_layered = true;
    detector.AddMockWindow(mangoWin);

    auto alerts = detector.DetectSuspiciousWindows();
    // All whitelisted desktop & legitimate gaming tools must yield 0 detections
    ASSERT_EQ(alerts.size(), 0);

    // 4. Rogue Unwhitelisted ESP overlay
    overlay::OverlayWindowInfo cheatWin;
    cheatWin.title = "UnknownESP";
    cheatWin.class_name = "ImGuiTransparentOverlay";
    cheatWin.pid = 9999;
    cheatWin.handle = 0x9999;
    cheatWin.rect = {0, 0, 1920, 1080};
    cheatWin.is_topmost = true;
    cheatWin.is_transparent = true;
    cheatWin.is_layered = true;
    detector.AddMockWindow(cheatWin);

    alerts = detector.DetectSuspiciousWindows();
    ASSERT_EQ(alerts.size(), 1);
    ASSERT_EQ(alerts[0].type, core::DetectionType::OVERLAY_HIJACK_DETECTED);

    detector.ClearMockWindows();
}

TEST(Remediation, KinematicsPitchBoundaryGrace) {
    kinematics::AimAnalyzer analyzer(64);
    uint64_t t = 1000;

    // Looking straight up at ceiling boundary: pitch = 89.0 deg
    analyzer.PushSample(89.0f, 10.0f, t);
    t += 16;
    analyzer.PushSample(89.0f, 10.0f, t);
    t += 16;

    // Player continues swiping up rapidly: pitch clamped at 89.0 deg, slight yaw
    analyzer.PushSample(89.0f, 11.0f, t);
    t += 16;
    auto ev = analyzer.PushSample(89.0f, 11.0f, t);

    // Boundary saturation must not be falsely treated as an unnatural aimbot dead stop
    ASSERT_FALSE(ev.has_value());
}

TEST(Remediation, SdkZeroStutterConcurrentInput) {
    NoEscapeConfig cfg = NoEscape_GetDefaultConfig();
    cfg.enable_heartbeat = false;
    cfg.enable_dma_detection = false; // Disable heavy HW loops for this concurrency test
    ASSERT_EQ(NoEscape_Init(&cfg), 0);

    std::atomic<bool> keepRunning{true};
    std::atomic<size_t> mousePushCount{0};

    // Thread 1: High frequency mouse input thread (1000Hz simulation)
    std::thread inputThread([&]() {
        uint64_t ts = 1000;
        while (keepRunning.load()) {
            NoEscape_PushMouseEvent(0.5f, 0.2f, ts);
            ts += 1;
            mousePushCount.fetch_add(1);
            std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::microseconds(500));
        }
    });

    // Thread 2: Main game thread running NoEscape_Tick()
    for (int i = 0; i < 20; ++i) {
        NoEscape_Tick();
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(5));
    }

    keepRunning.store(false);
    inputThread.join();

    // Verify hundreds of mouse events were processed concurrently without stutter or deadlock
    ASSERT_GT(mousePushCount.load(), 50);

    NoEscape_Shutdown();
}

TEST(Remediation, DmaCacheFlushAndOutlierRejection) {
    auto& dma = dma::DmaDetector::Instance();
    // Calibrate with small probe buffer for fast deterministic test execution
    bool calibOk = dma.Calibrate(256, 15);
    ASSERT_TRUE(calibOk);

    auto profile = dma.ProfileMemoryBusJitter(15);
    ASSERT_GT(profile.baseline_mean_cycles, 0.0);
    ASSERT_GT(profile.current_mean_cycles, 0.0);
    // On clean machine with outlier filtering, anomalous bus congestion should be false
    ASSERT_FALSE(profile.anomalous);
}

TEST(Remediation, MemoryModuleStompingDetection) {
    auto& scanner = memory::MemoryScanner::Instance();
    auto regions = scanner.QueryMemoryRegions();
    ASSERT_FALSE(regions.empty());

    // In a clean running process, standard libraries should not have RWX permissions
    bool hasUnintendedRwx = false;
    for (const auto& r : regions) {
        if (r.is_backed_by_file && r.executable && r.writable) {
            hasUnintendedRwx = true;
            break;
        }
    }
    // Clean process has no RWX module stomping
    ASSERT_FALSE(hasUnintendedRwx);
}

TEST(Remediation, UnhookerAutoRestoreAPI) {
    auto& unhooker = memory::Unhooker::Instance();

    // In position-independent binaries, &puts points to the local PLT stub (FF 25 ...),
    // so dlsym must be used to inspect the actual runtime libc implementation.
#if defined(_WIN32)
    void* pfn = reinterpret_cast<void*>(GetProcAddress(GetModuleHandleA("ntdll.dll"), "NtClose"));
#else
    void* pfn = dlsym(RTLD_DEFAULT, "puts");
#endif
    if (pfn) {
        auto hook = unhooker.InspectFunction("puts", pfn);
        ASSERT_FALSE(hook.has_value());
    }

    // Test AutoRestoreHook handles mock invalid or unhooked addresses safely
    memory::HookInfo mockHook;
    mockHook.function_name = "test_stub";
    mockHook.function_address = 0;
    ASSERT_FALSE(unhooker.AutoRestoreHook(mockHook));
}

TEST(Remediation, AntiDebugMultiThreadInspection) {
    // CheckHardwareBreakpoints checks DR0-DR7 across all threads in the process
    bool bpDetected = security::AntiDebug::CheckHardwareBreakpoints();
    // Clean environment has no active hardware breakpoints
    ASSERT_FALSE(bpDetected);
}

#if !defined(_WIN32)
#include <sys/mman.h>
#include <unistd.h>
TEST(Phase1, CleanProcessHasNoModifiedModules) {
    auto& sc = memory::MemoryScanner::Instance();
    size_t n = sc.QueryMemoryRegions().size();
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n + 2; ++i) {
        ASSERT_EQ(sc.DetectModifiedModules().size(), 0);
    }

TEST(Phase1, SleepingPayloadDetected) {
    auto& sc = memory::MemoryScanner::Instance();
    size_t len = 4096;
    void* p = mmap(nullptr, len, PROT_READ | PROT_WRITE | PROT_EXEC, MAP_PRIVATE | MAP_ANONYMOUS, -1, 0);
    ASSERT_TRUE(p != MAP_FAILED);
    sc.DetectSleepingPayloads();                 // observe while executable
    mprotect(p, len, PROT_NONE);                 // payload goes to sleep
    auto ev = sc.DetectSleepingPayloads();
    munmap(p, len);
    bool found = false;
    for (const auto& e : ev) if (e.component == "MemoryScanner::SleepingPayload") found = true;
    ASSERT_TRUE(found);
}

TEST(Phase1, ModuleStompingDetected) {
    auto& sc = memory::MemoryScanner::Instance();
    // Patch one byte inside a real file-backed executable mapping (private copy-on-write)
    void* fn = dlsym(RTLD_DEFAULT, "strlen");
    ASSERT_TRUE(fn != nullptr);
    auto* b = static_cast<uint8_t*>(fn);
    long ps = sysconf(_SC_PAGESIZE);
    void* page = reinterpret_cast<void*>(reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(fn) & ~(static_cast<uintptr_t>(ps) - 1));
    ASSERT_EQ(mprotect(page, static_cast<size_t>(ps), PROT_READ | PROT_WRITE | PROT_EXEC), 0);
    uint8_t orig = b[8];
    b[8] = static_cast<uint8_t>(orig ^ 0xFF);
    bool found = false;
    size_t n = sc.QueryMemoryRegions().size();
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n + 2 && !found; ++i)
        for (const auto& e : sc.DetectModifiedModules()) if (e.component == "MemoryScanner::ModuleIntegrity") found = true;
    b[8] = orig;
    mprotect(page, static_cast<size_t>(ps), PROT_READ | PROT_EXEC);
    ASSERT_TRUE(found);
}
#endif

#if !defined(_WIN32)
#include <sys/uio.h>
#include "noescape/memory/canary_monitor.hpp"
TEST(Phase2, CanaryCleanAndNoFalsePositiveUnderChurn) {
    auto& c = memory::CanaryMonitor::Instance();
    ASSERT_TRUE(c.Initialize(4));
    ASSERT_TRUE(c.IsWriteTrackingSupported());
    ASSERT_EQ(c.Check().size(), 0);
    // Heavy allocator churn around the canary must not trigger it
    for (int i = 0; i < 200; ++i) {
        void* m = mmap(nullptr, 65536, PROT_READ | PROT_WRITE, MAP_PRIVATE | MAP_ANONYMOUS, -1, 0);
        static_cast<volatile char*>(m)[0] = 1;
        if (i % 2) munmap(m, 65536);
    }
    std::vector<int> v(100000, 7);
    ASSERT_EQ(c.Check().size(), 0);
    ASSERT_EQ(c.Check().size(), 0);
}

TEST(Phase2, CanaryDetectsExternalStyleWriteSameValue) {
    auto& c = memory::CanaryMonitor::Instance();
    ASSERT_TRUE(c.Initialize(4));
    c.Check();
    // Emulate WriteProcessMemory: process_vm_writev writing the *same* byte value,
    // so only OS write tracking (not content comparison) can notice it.
    uint8_t same = *reinterpret_cast<uint8_t*>(c.Address() + 100);
    struct iovec local{&same, 1};
    struct iovec remote{reinterpret_cast<void*>(c.Address() + 100), 1};
    ssize_t n = process_vm_writev(getpid(), &local, 1, &remote, 1, 0);
    ASSERT_EQ(n, 1);
    ASSERT_EQ(c.Check().size(), 1);
    ASSERT_EQ(c.Check().size(), 0); // re-armed
}

TEST(Phase2, CanaryDetectsContentChange) {
    auto& c = memory::CanaryMonitor::Instance();
    ASSERT_TRUE(c.Initialize(4));
    c.Check();
    uint8_t v = *reinterpret_cast<uint8_t*>(c.Address() + 7) ^ 0xFF;
    struct iovec local{&v, 1};
    struct iovec remote{reinterpret_cast<void*>(c.Address() + 7), 1};
    ASSERT_EQ(process_vm_writev(getpid(), &local, 1, &remote, 1, 0), 1);
    auto ev = c.Check();
    ASSERT_EQ(ev.size(), 1);
    ASSERT_EQ(ev[0].type, core::DetectionType::CANARY_TAMPERED);
}
#endif

#include "noescape/kinematics/phantom_monitor.hpp"
TEST(Phase4, PhantomLockDetectedAndNormalAimIgnored) {
    kinematics::PhantomMonitor pm;
    pm.AddPhantom(1, 10.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f);   // straight ahead along +X
    pm.AddPhantom(2, 0.0f, 20.0f, 0.0f);

    // Looking at a random other direction: never fires, even for many samples
    for (int i = 0; i < 100; ++i) ASSERT_FALSE(pm.PushView(0, 0, 0, 10.0f, 135.0f, i).has_value());

    // Short pass over the phantom (human sweeping crosshair): below required streak
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) ASSERT_FALSE(pm.PushView(0, 0, 0, 0.0f, 0.2f, i).has_value());
    ASSERT_FALSE(pm.PushView(0, 0, 0, 30.0f, 45.0f, 99).has_value()); // leaves -> streak reset

    // Sustained lock on phantom 1 -> event on the 12th sample
    std::optional<core::DetectionEvent> ev;
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; ++i) ev = pm.PushView(0, 0, 0, 0.0f, 0.3f, 200 + i);
    ASSERT_TRUE(ev.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(ev->type, core::DetectionType::PHANTOM_ENTITY_TARGETED);
}

    b.GenerateAround(0, 0, 0, 50.0f, 8, 1234);
    ASSERT_EQ(a.Count(), 8);
    ASSERT_EQ(b.Count(), 8);
    ASSERT_FALSE(a.PushView(NAN, 0, 0, 0, 0, 1).has_value());
}

#include <cstdlib>
TEST(Phase3, SignedPanelProtocolAgainstReferenceServer) {
    if (std::system("python3 --version >/dev/null 2>&1") != 0) return; // environment lacks python
    const char* secret = "IntegrationSecret";
    network::PanelClient probe("http://127.0.0.1:18431/api/ac", secret);
    std::string hwid = probe.GetHwid();

    // Server 1: this device is clean
    std::string cmd = std::string("python3 ") + NOESCAPE_SOURCE_DIR + "/tools/reference_server.py 18431 " + secret + " >/dev/null 2>&1 &";
    ASSERT_EQ(std::system(cmd.c_str()), 0);
    std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(700));

    network::PanelClient good("http://127.0.0.1:18431/api/ac", secret);
    ASSERT_EQ(static_cast<int>(good.QueryBanStatus().status), static_cast<int>(network::BanCheckStatus::CLEAN));
    ASSERT_TRUE(good.SendHeartbeat(10, "HEALTHY"));

    // Wrong secret: server rejects our signed heartbeat; we reject the server's signature
    network::PanelClient wrong("http://127.0.0.1:18431/api/ac", "WrongSecret");
    ASSERT_FALSE(wrong.SendHeartbeat(10, "HEALTHY"));
    ASSERT_EQ(static_cast<int>(wrong.QueryBanStatus().status), static_cast<int>(network::BanCheckStatus::SIGNATURE_INVALID));

    // Ban report with quotes/backslashes in details must still verify (JSON escaping + HMAC)
    core::DetectionEvent ev;
    ev.type = core::DetectionType::INLINE_HOOK_DETECTED;
    ev.component = "Test";
    ev.details = "path \"C:\\x\" line\nbreak";
    ASSERT_TRUE(good.SendBanReport(ev, 100));
    std::system("pkill -f 'reference_server.py 18431' >/dev/null 2>&1");
    std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(300));

    // Server 2: this device is globally banned
    cmd = std::string("python3 ") + NOESCAPE_SOURCE_DIR + "/tools/reference_server.py 18431 " + secret + " " + hwid + " >/dev/null 2>&1 &";
    ASSERT_EQ(std::system(cmd.c_str()), 0);
    std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(700));
    ASSERT_EQ(static_cast<int>(good.QueryBanStatus().status), static_cast<int>(network::BanCheckStatus::BANNED));
    std::system("pkill -f 'reference_server.py 18431' >/dev/null 2>&1");

    // Unreachable server: never reported clean
    std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(300));
    ASSERT_EQ(static_cast<int>(good.QueryBanStatus().status), static_cast<int>(network::BanCheckStatus::NETWORK_ERROR));
}

#if !defined(_WIN32)
#include <linux/perf_event.h>
#include <linux/hw_breakpoint.h>
#include <sys/syscall.h>

TEST(Hardening, HardwareBreakpointDetectionWithPerfEvent) {
    // 1. Verify clean process reports zero hardware breakpoints
    ASSERT_FALSE(security::AntiDebug::CheckHardwareBreakpoints());

    // 2. Simulate hardware debug register occupation using perf_event_open
    static void (*probeFunc)() = []() {};
    struct perf_event_attr pe{};
    pe.type = PERF_TYPE_BREAKPOINT;
    pe.size = sizeof(pe);
    pe.bp_type = HW_BREAKPOINT_X;
    pe.bp_addr = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(probeFunc);
    pe.bp_len = sizeof(long);
    pe.disabled = 1;
    pe.exclude_kernel = 1;
    pe.exclude_hv = 1;

    int simFd = static_cast<int>(syscall(__NR_perf_event_open, &pe, 0, -1, -1, 0));
    if (simFd >= 0) {
        // DR slot occupied -> CheckHardwareBreakpoints detects it
        bool detected = security::AntiDebug::CheckHardwareBreakpoints();
        close(simFd);
        ASSERT_TRUE(detected);

        // Released DR slot -> clean again
        ASSERT_FALSE(security::AntiDebug::CheckHardwareBreakpoints());
    }
}
#endif

TEST(Hardening, MemoryScannerCacheSnapshot) {
    auto& scanner = memory::MemoryScanner::Instance();
    ASSERT_FALSE(scanner.IsCacheValid());

    // Un-cached query
    auto r1 = scanner.QueryMemoryRegions();
    ASSERT_FALSE(r1.empty());
    ASSERT_FALSE(scanner.IsCacheValid());

    // Populate and query cached snapshot
    scanner.RefreshCache();
    ASSERT_TRUE(scanner.IsCacheValid());

    auto r2 = scanner.QueryMemoryRegions();
    ASSERT_EQ(r1.size(), r2.size());
    ASSERT_EQ(r1[0].start, r2[0].start);

    // Clear cache
    scanner.ClearCache();
    ASSERT_FALSE(scanner.IsCacheValid());
}

TEST(Hardening, LargeMemoryChunkingScanAbove32MB) {
    auto& scanner = memory::MemoryScanner::Instance();
    // Allocate 36 MB of anonymous executable memory (>32MB threshold previously skipped)
    size_t allocSize = 36 * 1024 * 1024;
#if !defined(_WIN32)
    uint8_t* mem = static_cast<uint8_t*>(mmap(nullptr, allocSize, PROT_READ | PROT_WRITE | PROT_EXEC, MAP_PRIVATE | MAP_ANONYMOUS, -1, 0));
    ASSERT_TRUE(mem != MAP_FAILED);

    // Inject a known signature pattern into chunk 4 (offset 33 MB > 32 MB)
    // MinHook x64 Trampoline: 49 BA [8 bytes target] 41 FF E2
    size_t targetOffset = 33 * 1024 * 1024 + 1024;
    mem[targetOffset] = 0x49; mem[targetOffset + 1] = 0xBA;
    mem[targetOffset + 2] = 0x78; mem[targetOffset + 3] = 0x56;
    mem[targetOffset + 4] = 0x34; mem[targetOffset + 5] = 0x12;
    mem[targetOffset + 6] = 0x00; mem[targetOffset + 7] = 0x00;
    mem[targetOffset + 8] = 0x00; mem[targetOffset + 9] = 0x00;
    mem[targetOffset + 10] = 0x41; mem[targetOffset + 11] = 0xFF; mem[targetOffset + 12] = 0xE2;

    auto events = scanner.ScanSignatures(memory::MemoryScanner::GetDefaultCheatSignatures());
    bool foundInLargeBlock = false;
    for (const auto& ev : events) {
        if (ev.details.find("MinHook_X64_R10_Trampoline") != std::string::npos &&
            ev.details.find("0x" + [] (uintptr_t v) {
                std::ostringstream ss; ss << std::hex << v; return ss.str();
            }(reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(mem + targetOffset))) != std::string::npos) {
            foundInLargeBlock = true;
            break;
        }
    }

    munmap(mem, allocSize);
    ASSERT_TRUE(foundInLargeBlock);
#endif
}

TEST(Hardening, WatchdogThreadLifecycleClean) {
    core::EngineConfig cfg;
    cfg.enableHeartbeat = false;
    cfg.terminateProcessOnBan = false;
    cfg.showBanUi = false;

    core::AntiCheatEngine engine(cfg);
    ASSERT_TRUE(engine.Initialize());
    engine.Start();
    // Running under normal conditions maintains active heartbeat without false positives
    std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(300));
    ASSERT_FALSE(engine.IsBanned());
    ASSERT_EQ(engine.GetThreatScore(), 0);
    engine.Stop();
}

TEST(Hardening, WatchdogDetectsThreadFreezeTampering) {
    core::EngineConfig cfg;
    cfg.enableHeartbeat = false;
    cfg.terminateProcessOnBan = false;
    cfg.showBanUi = false;
    cfg.enforceBanOnViolation = true;

    core::AntiCheatEngine engine(cfg);
    ASSERT_TRUE(engine.Initialize());
    engine.Start();

    // Verify engine starts cleanly
    ASSERT_FALSE(engine.IsBanned());
    ASSERT_TRUE(engine.GetLastAuditHeartbeatMs() > 0);

    // Simulate an attacker suspending/freezing the background audit thread (> 3000ms deadline missed)
    engine.FreezeBackgroundLoopForTesting(true);
    uint64_t simulatedStallTime = engine.GetLastAuditHeartbeatMs() - 4000;
    engine.SetAuditHeartbeatForTesting(simulatedStallTime);

    // Watchdog thread checks every 100ms; wait 250ms for watchdog enforcement to trigger
    std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(250));

    // Watchdog must detect thread freeze, increment threat score to >= 100, and ban
    ASSERT_TRUE(engine.IsBanned());
    ASSERT_TRUE(engine.GetThreatScore() >= 100);

    auto history = engine.GetDetectionHistory();
    bool foundWatchdogDetection = false;
    for (const auto& ev : history) {
        if (ev.type == core::DetectionType::HEARTBEAT_TAMPERING &&
            ev.component == "AntiCheatEngine::Watchdog") {
            foundWatchdogDetection = true;
            break;
        }
    }
    ASSERT_TRUE(foundWatchdogDetection);

    engine.FreezeBackgroundLoopForTesting(false);
    engine.Stop();
}

TEST(Hardening, SecureEnvironmentOverrides) {
    // Verify that NOESCAPE_TEST_MODE is set in test environment
    const char* testMode = std::getenv("NOESCAPE_TEST_MODE");
    ASSERT_TRUE(testMode != nullptr);
    ASSERT_EQ(std::string(testMode), "1");

    // In test mode, opt-out checking operates safely without breaking test harnesses
    NoEscapeConfig cfg = NoEscape_GetDefaultConfig();
    cfg.enable_heartbeat = false;
    cfg.enable_anti_debug = false;
    ASSERT_EQ(NoEscape_Init(&cfg), 0);
    ASSERT_FALSE(NoEscape_IsBanned());
    NoEscape_Shutdown();
}
```

`tests/test_blackbox.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "tests/test_framework.hpp"

#include "noescape/core/engine.hpp"
#include <filesystem>
#include <fstream>

using namespace noescape::core;

TEST(Blackbox, BacktraceCapture) {
    auto frames = BlackboxDumper::CaptureBacktrace(16);
    ASSERT_GT(frames.size(), 0);
}

TEST(Blackbox, InputTrailCircularBuffer) {
    auto& dumper = BlackboxDumper::Instance();
    dumper.ClearInputTrail();
    ASSERT_EQ(dumper.GetInputTrailCount(), 0);

    for (int i = 0; i < 70; ++i) {
        dumper.RecordInputSample(static_cast<float>(i), static_cast<float>(i * 2), 1000 + i * 16);
    }

    // Capacity is 64; 70 pushes should cap at 64
    ASSERT_EQ(dumper.GetInputTrailCount(), 64);

    dumper.ClearInputTrail();

TEST(Blackbox, SnapshotCaptureAndSerializationRoundTrip) {
    auto& dumper = BlackboxDumper::Instance();
    dumper.ClearInputTrail();

    // Fill in a few input samples
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        dumper.RecordInputSample(1.5f * i, 2.5f * i, 10000 + i * 16);
    }

    std::string key = "TestSuperSecretKey123!@#";
    auto snap = dumper.CaptureSnapshot("Kinematics::AimbotDetector", "Instant 90-degree Snap Detected", 85, 14);

    ASSERT_EQ(snap.violating_component, "Kinematics::AimbotDetector");
    ASSERT_EQ(snap.violation_reason, "Instant 90-degree Snap Detected");
    ASSERT_EQ(snap.threat_score, 85);
    ASSERT_EQ(snap.violation_type, 14);
    ASSERT_FALSE(snap.hwid.empty());
    ASSERT_EQ(snap.recent_input_trail.size(), 5);
    ASSERT_GT(snap.backtrace_addrs.size(), 0);

    // Serialize and encrypt
    auto encrypted = dumper.SerializeAndEncrypt(snap, key);
    ASSERT_GT(encrypted.size(), 90);
    // Magic check
    ASSERT_EQ(encrypted[0], 'N');
    ASSERT_EQ(encrypted[1], 'E');
    ASSERT_EQ(encrypted[2], 'B');
    ASSERT_EQ(encrypted[3], 'B');

    // Decrypt and deserialize
    BlackboxRecord decrypted{};
    bool success = dumper.DecryptAndDeserialize(encrypted, key, decrypted);
    ASSERT_TRUE(success);

    ASSERT_EQ(decrypted.timestamp, snap.timestamp);
    ASSERT_EQ(decrypted.threat_score, snap.threat_score);
    ASSERT_EQ(decrypted.violation_type, snap.violation_type);
    ASSERT_EQ(decrypted.hwid, snap.hwid);
    ASSERT_EQ(decrypted.violating_component, snap.violating_component);
    ASSERT_EQ(decrypted.violation_reason, snap.violation_reason);
    ASSERT_EQ(decrypted.backtrace_addrs.size(), snap.backtrace_addrs.size());
    ASSERT_EQ(decrypted.recent_input_trail.size(), snap.recent_input_trail.size());

    for (size_t i = 0; i < snap.recent_input_trail.size(); ++i) {
        ASSERT_NEAR(decrypted.recent_input_trail[i].pitch, snap.recent_input_trail[i].pitch, 0.0001f);
        ASSERT_NEAR(decrypted.recent_input_trail[i].yaw, snap.recent_input_trail[i].yaw, 0.0001f);
        ASSERT_EQ(decrypted.recent_input_trail[i].timestamp_ms, snap.recent_input_trail[i].timestamp_ms);
    }
}

TEST(Blackbox, TamperResistanceCiphertextMutation) {
    auto& dumper = BlackboxDumper::Instance();
    std::string key = "TamperResistanceKey_v1";

    auto snap = dumper.CaptureSnapshot("Unhooker::Integrity", "Kernel inline hook detected", 95, 8);
    auto encrypted = dumper.SerializeAndEncrypt(snap, key);

    // Mutate a byte in the ciphertext body
    size_t targetByte = 30; // Within IV/ciphertext region
    ASSERT_LT(targetByte, encrypted.size());
    encrypted[targetByte] ^= 0x55;

    BlackboxRecord outRecord{};
    bool success = dumper.DecryptAndDeserialize(encrypted, key, outRecord);
    // HMAC check MUST fail on any single-bit mutation
    ASSERT_FALSE(success);
}

TEST(Blackbox, TamperResistanceWrongKey) {
    auto& dumper = BlackboxDumper::Instance();
    std::string correctKey = "CorrectKey999";
    std::string wrongKey = "AttackerForgedKey";

    auto snap = dumper.CaptureSnapshot("ProcessAnalyzer", "CheatEngine detected", 100, 11);
    auto encrypted = dumper.SerializeAndEncrypt(snap, correctKey);

    BlackboxRecord outRecord{};
    bool success = dumper.DecryptAndDeserialize(encrypted, wrongKey, outRecord);
    ASSERT_FALSE(success);
}

TEST(Blackbox, FileDumpSaving) {
    auto& dumper = BlackboxDumper::Instance();
    std::string key = "DumpFileTestKey";
    auto snap = dumper.CaptureSnapshot("OverlayDetector", "Fake Window Detected", 75, 18);
    auto encrypted = dumper.SerializeAndEncrypt(snap, key);

    std::string testPath = "/tmp/test_blackbox_output.dmp";
    ASSERT_TRUE(dumper.SaveDumpToFile(testPath, encrypted));

    ASSERT_TRUE(std::filesystem::exists(testPath));
    ASSERT_GT(std::filesystem::file_size(testPath), 90);

    // Read back and decrypt
    std::ifstream ifs(testPath, std::ios::binary);
    std::vector<uint8_t> readBytes((std::istreambuf_iterator<char>(ifs)), std::istreambuf_iterator<char>());
    ifs.close();

    BlackboxRecord readRecord{};
    ASSERT_TRUE(dumper.DecryptAndDeserialize(readBytes, key, readRecord));
    ASSERT_EQ(readRecord.violating_component, "OverlayDetector");

    std::filesystem::remove(testPath);
}

TEST(Blackbox, TriggerBanDiagnosticCaptureIntegrity) {
    EngineConfig cfg;
    cfg.enableHeartbeat = false;
    cfg.banScoreThreshold = 50;
    cfg.enforceBanOnViolation = true;
    cfg.enableBlackboxDump = true;
    cfg.blackboxDumpDirectory = "/tmp";
    cfg.secretKey = "BlackboxIntegrityKey!@#";

    AntiCheatEngine engine(cfg);
    ASSERT_TRUE(engine.Initialize());

    // Trigger a specific detection
    DetectionEvent ev;
    ev.type = DetectionType::OVERLAY_HIJACK_DETECTED;
    ev.severity = Severity::FATAL_BAN;
    ev.threat_score = 100;
    ev.component = "OverlayDetector::WindowScanner";
    ev.details = "ESP Overlay detected";
    engine.ProcessDetection(ev);

    ASSERT_TRUE(engine.IsBanned());
    const auto& payload = engine.GetLastBlackboxPayload();
    ASSERT_GT(payload.size(), 90);

    BlackboxRecord rec{};
    ASSERT_TRUE(BlackboxDumper::Instance().DecryptAndDeserialize(payload, cfg.secretKey, rec));
    ASSERT_EQ(rec.violating_component, "OverlayDetector::WindowScanner");
    ASSERT_EQ(rec.violation_reason, "ESP Overlay detected");
    ASSERT_EQ(rec.violation_type, static_cast<uint32_t>(DetectionType::OVERLAY_HIJACK_DETECTED));
    ASSERT_EQ(rec.threat_score, 100);
}
```

`tests/test_crypto.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "tests/test_framework.hpp"
#include "noescape/network/crypto_util.hpp"

using namespace noescape::network;

TEST(Crypto, Sha256StandardVectors) {
    // NIST Standard Test Vectors
    std::string hEmpty = CryptoUtil::Sha256("");
    ASSERT_EQ(hEmpty, "e3b0c44298fc1c149afbf4c8996fb92427ae41e4649b934ca495991b7852b855");

    std::string hAbc = CryptoUtil::Sha256("abc");
    ASSERT_EQ(hAbc, "ba7816bf8f01cfea414140de5dae2223b00361a396177a9cb410ff61f20015ad");

    std::string longInput = "abcdbcdecdefdefgefghfghighijhijkiklklmlmnmnonopnopq";
    std::string hLong = CryptoUtil::Sha256(longInput);
    ASSERT_EQ(hLong, "4aed8ec5b3cbe72badd60bce25faf91b6928fc2cc0dd1c190a218c1a952151dc");
}

TEST(Crypto, HmacSha256StandardVectors) {
    // RFC 4231 / Standard HMAC Vector
    std::string key = "key";
    std::string data = "The quick brown fox jumps over the lazy dog";
    std::string expected = "f7bc83f430538424b13298e6aa6fb143ef4d59a14946175997479dbc2d1a3cd8";

    std::string actual = CryptoUtil::HmacSha256(key, data);
    ASSERT_EQ(actual, expected);

    // Empty Key
    std::string emptyKeyHmac = CryptoUtil::HmacSha256("", "data");
    ASSERT_EQ(emptyKeyHmac.length(), 64);
    ASSERT_NE(emptyKeyHmac, actual);

    // Long Key (>64 bytes, verifies key hashing path)
    std::string longKey(100, 'K');
    std::string longKeyHmac = CryptoUtil::HmacSha256(longKey, data);
    ASSERT_EQ(longKeyHmac.length(), 64);
}

TEST(Crypto, MachineHwidGeneration) {
    std::string hwid1 = CryptoUtil::GetMachineHWID();
    std::string hwid2 = CryptoUtil::GetMachineHWID();

    // HWID should be a deterministic 64-character SHA-256 hex digest
    ASSERT_EQ(hwid1.length(), 64);
    ASSERT_EQ(hwid1, hwid2);
}
```

`tests/test_engine.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "tests/test_framework.hpp"
#include "noescape/core/engine.hpp"

using namespace noescape::core;

TEST(Engine, InitializationAndThreatScoring) {
    EngineConfig cfg;
    cfg.enableHeartbeat = false;
    cfg.banScoreThreshold = 100;
    cfg.blackboxDumpDirectory = "/tmp";

    AntiCheatEngine engine(cfg);
    ASSERT_TRUE(engine.Initialize());
    ASSERT_EQ(engine.GetThreatScore(), 0);
    ASSERT_FALSE(engine.IsBanned());

    bool banCalled = false;
    std::string banReason;
    engine.SetBanCallback([&](const std::string& reason, uint32_t finalScore) {
        banCalled = true;
        banReason = reason;
        (void)finalScore;
    });

    // Fire detection 1 (Threat score 40)
    DetectionEvent ev1;
    ev1.type = DetectionType::SUSPICIOUS_PROCESS_DETECTED;
    ev1.severity = Severity::MEDIUM;
    ev1.threat_score = 40;
    ev1.details = "Suspicious process";
    engine.ProcessDetection(ev1);

    ASSERT_EQ(engine.GetThreatScore(), 40);
    ASSERT_FALSE(engine.IsBanned());
    ASSERT_FALSE(banCalled);

    // Fire detection 2 (Threat score 65 -> total 105 >= 100 threshold)
    DetectionEvent ev2;
    ev2.type = DetectionType::UNBACKED_EXECUTABLE_MEMORY;
    ev2.severity = Severity::HIGH;
    ev2.threat_score = 65;
    ev2.details = "Unbacked memory detected";
    engine.ProcessDetection(ev2);

    ASSERT_EQ(engine.GetThreatScore(), 105);
    ASSERT_TRUE(engine.IsBanned());
    ASSERT_TRUE(banCalled);
    ASSERT_EQ(banReason, "Unbacked memory detected");
}

TEST(Engine, FatalBanEnforcement) {
    EngineConfig cfg;
    cfg.enableHeartbeat = false;
    cfg.banScoreThreshold = 500;
    cfg.blackboxDumpDirectory = "/tmp";

    AntiCheatEngine engine(cfg);
    engine.Initialize();

    bool banCalled = false;
    engine.SetBanCallback([&](const std::string&, uint32_t) {
        banCalled = true;
    });

    // FATAL_BAN severity triggers immediate ban regardless of current threat score
    DetectionEvent ev;
    ev.type = DetectionType::ANTI_DEBUG_TRACER;
    ev.severity = Severity::FATAL_BAN;
    ev.threat_score = 100;
    ev.details = "Kernel debugger attached";
    engine.ProcessDetection(ev);

    ASSERT_TRUE(engine.IsBanned());
    ASSERT_TRUE(banCalled);
}

TEST(Engine, BanScreenConfigAndInvocation) {
    EngineConfig cfg;
    cfg.enableHeartbeat = false;
    cfg.banScoreThreshold = 100;
    cfg.showBanUi = true;
    cfg.terminateProcessOnBan = false;
    cfg.banUiExecutable = "/home/COSMOS/NoEscapeAC/tools/noescape_ban_ui.py";

    AntiCheatEngine engine(cfg);
    ASSERT_TRUE(engine.Initialize());
    ASSERT_TRUE(cfg.showBanUi);
    ASSERT_FALSE(cfg.terminateProcessOnBan);
    ASSERT_EQ(cfg.banUiExecutable, "/home/COSMOS/NoEscapeAC/tools/noescape_ban_ui.py");

    bool banFired = false;
    engine.SetBanCallback([&](const std::string&, uint32_t) {
        banFired = true;
    });

    DetectionEvent ev;
    ev.type = DetectionType::UNBACKED_EXECUTABLE_MEMORY;
    ev.severity = Severity::FATAL_BAN;
    ev.threat_score = 100;
    ev.details = "Simulated injection attack";
    engine.ProcessDetection(ev);

    ASSERT_TRUE(engine.IsBanned());
    ASSERT_TRUE(banFired);
}
```

`tests/test_forensics.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "tests/test_framework.hpp"
#include "noescape/forensics/process_analyzer.hpp"
#include "noescape/forensics/artifact_analyzer.hpp"

#if !defined(_WIN32)
#include <unistd.h>
#endif

using namespace noescape::forensics;
using namespace noescape::core;

TEST(Forensics, ProcessEnumeration) {
    auto procs = ProcessAnalyzer::Instance().EnumerateProcesses();
    ASSERT_FALSE(procs.empty());

#if !defined(_WIN32)
    uint32_t myPid = static_cast<uint32_t>(getpid());
    bool foundSelf = false;
    for (const auto& p : procs) {
        if (p.pid == myPid) {
            foundSelf = true;
            break;
        }
    }
    ASSERT_TRUE(foundSelf);
#endif
}

TEST(Forensics, ProcessBlacklistAudit) {
    std::vector<std::string> blacklist = {"non_existent_cheat_daemon_xyz", "vape", "cheatengine"};
    std::vector<std::string> cmdlineBlacklist = {"--inject-cheat-payload-mock"};

    auto events = ProcessAnalyzer::Instance().AuditProcesses(blacklist, cmdlineBlacklist);
    // On clean testing system, blacklisted cheat processes should not be running
    for (const auto& ev : events) {
        if (ev.type == DetectionType::SUSPICIOUS_PROCESS_DETECTED) {
            ASSERT_NE(ev.details.find("non_existent_cheat_daemon_xyz"), std::string::npos);
        }
        // Verify that benign system processes with libextest.so are NOT flagged as environment tampering
        ASSERT_NE(ev.type, DetectionType::ENVIRONMENT_TAMPERING);
    }
}

TEST(Forensics, ArtifactAnalyzer) {
    std::vector<std::string> fakeArtifacts = {"impossible_cheat_file_xyz123.bin"};
    auto fsArtifacts = ArtifactAnalyzer::Instance().ScanFilesystemArtifacts(fakeArtifacts);
    ASSERT_TRUE(fsArtifacts.empty());

    std::vector<std::string> fakeDrivers = {"impossible_driver_xyz.sys"};
    auto drvArtifacts = ArtifactAnalyzer::Instance().ScanKernelDrivers(fakeDrivers);
    ASSERT_TRUE(drvArtifacts.empty());
}
```

`tests/test_framework.hpp`:

```hpp
#pragma once

#include <iostream>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <functional>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <cmath>

namespace noescape::test {

class TestRunner {
public:
    using TestCase = std::function<void()>;

    static TestRunner& Instance() {
        static TestRunner instance;
        return instance;
    }

    void RegisterTest(std::string suite, std::string name, TestCase test) {
        tests_.push_back({std::move(suite), std::move(name), std::move(test)});
    }

    int RunAll() {
        int passed = 0;
        int failed = 0;

        std::cout << "\033[1;35m====================================================\n"
                  << "        NoEscape Anti-Cheat C++20 Test Suite         \n"
                  << "====================================================\033[0m\n\n";

        for (const auto& t : tests_) {
            std::cout << "\033[1;34m[ RUN      ]\033[0m " << t.suite << "." << t.name << " ... " << std::flush;
            try {
                t.func();
                std::cout << "\033[1;32mPASSED\033[0m\n";
                passed++;
            } catch (const std::exception& ex) {
                std::cout << "\033[1;31mFAILED\033[0m\n";
                std::cout << "  \033[31mError: " << ex.what() << "\033[0m\n";
                failed++;
            } catch (...) {
                std::cout << "\033[1;31mFAILED (Unknown exception)\033[0m\n";
                failed++;
            }
        }

        std::cout << "\n\033[1;35m====================================================\033[0m\n";
        std::cout << "Total Tests: " << tests_.size() << " | \033[32mPassed: " << passed 
                  << "\033[0m | \033[" << (failed > 0 ? "31" : "32") << "mFailed: " << failed << "\033[0m\n";

        return failed == 0 ? 0 : 1;
    }

private:
    struct Entry {
        std::string suite;
        std::string name;
        TestCase func;
    };
    std::vector<Entry> tests_;
};

struct TestRegistrar {
    TestRegistrar(std::string suite, std::string name, TestRunner::TestCase test) {
        TestRunner::Instance().RegisterTest(std::move(suite), std::move(name), std::move(test));
    }
};

#define TEST(suite, name) \
    void test_##suite##_##name(); \
    static ::noescape::test::TestRegistrar reg_##suite##_##name(#suite, #name, test_##suite##_##name); \
    void test_##suite##_##name()

#define ASSERT_TRUE(cond) \
    do { \
        if (!(cond)) { \
            throw std::runtime_error("Assertion failed: " #cond " at " __FILE__ ":" + std::to_string(__LINE__)); \
        } \
    } while (0)

#define ASSERT_FALSE(cond) \
    do { \
        if (cond) { \
            throw std::runtime_error("Assertion failed: NOT " #cond " at " __FILE__ ":" + std::to_string(__LINE__)); \
        } \
    } while (0)

#define ASSERT_EQ(a, b) \
    do { \
        if (!((a) == (b))) { \
            throw std::runtime_error("Assertion failed: " #a " == " #b " at " __FILE__ ":" + std::to_string(__LINE__)); \
        } \
    } while (0)

#define ASSERT_NE(a, b) \
    do { \
        if ((a) == (b)) { \
            throw std::runtime_error("Assertion failed: " #a " != " #b " at " __FILE__ ":" + std::to_string(__LINE__)); \
        } \
    } while (0)

#define ASSERT_GT(a, b) \
    do { \
        if (!((a) > (b))) { \
            throw std::runtime_error("Assertion failed: " #a " > " #b " at " __FILE__ ":" + std::to_string(__LINE__)); \
        } \
    } while (0)

#define ASSERT_LT(a, b) \
    do { \
        if (!((a) < (b))) { \
            throw std::runtime_error("Assertion failed: " #a " < " #b " at " __FILE__ ":" + std::to_string(__LINE__)); \
        } \
    } while (0)

#define ASSERT_NEAR(a, b, eps) \
    do { \
        if (std::abs((a) - (b)) > (eps)) { \
            throw std::runtime_error("Assertion failed: |" #a " - " #b "| <= " #eps " at " __FILE__ ":" + std::to_string(__LINE__)); \
        } \
    } while (0)

} // namespace noescape::test

```

`tests/test_kinematics.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "tests/test_framework.hpp"
#include "noescape/kinematics/aim_analyzer.hpp"
#include <cmath>

using namespace noescape::kinematics;
using namespace noescape::core;

TEST(Kinematics, NormalHumanMovement) {
    AimAnalyzer analyzer(64);
    uint64_t t = 1000;

    // Simulate natural human movement: curve with tremor and acceleration/deceleration
    float p = 0.0f;
    float y = 0.0f;

    for (int i = 0; i < 20; ++i) {
        float progress = static_cast<float>(i) / 20.0f;
        // Human bell curve velocity
        float vel = std::sin(progress * 3.14159f) * 1.5f;
        // Biological micro-tremor
        float tremor = (i % 2 == 0 ? 0.08f : -0.07f);

        p += vel * 0.4f + tremor;
        y += vel * 0.8f + (tremor * 1.2f);
        t += 16; // 60 FPS tick

        auto ev = analyzer.PushSample(p, y, t);
        ASSERT_FALSE(ev.has_value());
    }
}

TEST(Kinematics, InstantSnapAimbot) {
    AimAnalyzer analyzer(64);
    uint64_t t = 1000;

    // Steady crosshair at target A
    analyzer.PushSample(0.0f, 0.0f, t);
    t += 16;
    analyzer.PushSample(0.1f, 0.1f, t);
    t += 16;

    // Aimbot silent snap: 45 degree flick in a single 16ms frame!
    analyzer.PushSample(15.0f, 42.0f, t);
    t += 16;

    // Followed by instant lock / dead stop (delta = 0.0)
    auto ev = analyzer.PushSample(15.0f, 42.0f, t);
    ASSERT_TRUE(ev.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(ev->type, DetectionType::KINEMATICS_AIMBOT_SNAP);
}

TEST(Kinematics, RoboticPerfectLinearity) {
    AimAnalyzer analyzer(64);
    analyzer.SetLinearityThreshold(0.005f);
    analyzer.SetMinSamplesForAnalysis(8);

    uint64_t t = 1000;
    // Pure mathematical line with zero perpendicular noise (y = 2.0 * p)
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; ++i) {
        float p = static_cast<float>(i) * 1.5f;      // 0 to 16.5 deg
        float y = p * 2.0f;                          // Exactly on line
        t += 16;

        auto ev = analyzer.PushSample(p, y, t);
        if (i >= 8 && ev.has_value()) {
            ASSERT_EQ(ev->type, DetectionType::KINEMATICS_PERFECT_LINEARITY);
            return;
        }
    }
}

TEST(Kinematics, ArtificialSmoothingConstantVelocity) {
    AimAnalyzer analyzer(64);
    analyzer.SetMinSamplesForAnalysis(8);

    uint64_t t = 1000;
    // Exact constant velocity: exactly 1.5 degrees per 16ms tick without any variance
    float p = 0.0f;
    float y = 0.0f;

    for (int i = 0; i < 15; ++i) {
        p += 1.0f;
        y += 1.0f;
        t += 16;

        auto ev = analyzer.PushSample(p, y, t);
        if (i >= 8 && ev.has_value()) {
            ASSERT_EQ(ev->type, DetectionType::KINEMATICS_ARTIFICIAL_SMOOTHING);
            return;
        }
    }
}

TEST(Kinematics, NonFiniteAndMalformedInputs) {
    AimAnalyzer analyzer(64);
    uint64_t t = 1000;

    // NAN values should be safely rejected without crashing or infinite loop
    auto evNan1 = analyzer.PushSample(NAN, 10.0f, t);
    ASSERT_FALSE(evNan1.has_value());

    auto evNan2 = analyzer.PushSample(10.0f, NAN, t);
    ASSERT_FALSE(evNan2.has_value());

    // INFINITY values should be rejected and never cause an infinite loop in NormalizeAngleDelta
    auto evInf = analyzer.PushSample(INFINITY, -INFINITY, t);
    ASSERT_FALSE(evInf.has_value());

    // Extreme corrupt pitch (> 180 deg)
    auto evCorrupt = analyzer.PushSample(9999.0f, 0.0f, t);
    ASSERT_FALSE(evCorrupt.has_value());
}

TEST(Kinematics, ComputeCurrentMetricsFull) {
    AimAnalyzer analyzer(64);
    uint64_t t = 1000;

    // Push 6 accelerating points
    for (int i = 0; i < 6; ++i) {
        float angle = static_cast<float>(i * i) * 1.5f;
        analyzer.PushSample(angle, angle * 1.2f, t);
        t += 20;
    }

    auto metrics = analyzer.ComputeCurrentMetrics();
    ASSERT_GT(metrics.angular_velocity, 0.0f);
    ASSERT_GT(metrics.angular_acceleration, 0.0f);
    ASSERT_TRUE(std::isfinite(metrics.angular_jerk));
    ASSERT_TRUE(std::isfinite(metrics.linearity_error));
    ASSERT_TRUE(std::isfinite(metrics.velocity_variance));
}

TEST(Kinematics, SubMillisecondMousePolling) {
    AimAnalyzer analyzer(64);
    uint64_t t = 1000;

    // Multiple samples arriving at the same timestamp (high polling rate mouse)
    auto ev1 = analyzer.PushSample(1.0f, 2.0f, t);
    auto ev2 = analyzer.PushSample(1.2f, 2.4f, t); // Same timestamp_ms
    auto ev3 = analyzer.PushSample(1.4f, 2.8f, t); // Same timestamp_ms

    ASSERT_FALSE(ev1.has_value());
    ASSERT_FALSE(ev2.has_value());
    ASSERT_FALSE(ev3.has_value());
}
```

`tests/test_memory.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "tests/test_framework.hpp"
#include "noescape/memory/memory_scanner.hpp"
#include "noescape/memory/unhooker.hpp"
#include <vector>
#if defined(_WIN32)
#include <windows.h>
#else
#include <sys/mman.h>
#endif

using namespace noescape::memory;
using namespace noescape::core;

TEST(Memory, QueryMemoryRegions) {
    auto regions = MemoryScanner::Instance().QueryMemoryRegions();
    ASSERT_FALSE(regions.empty());

    bool foundExecutable = false;
    for (const auto& r : regions) {
        ASSERT_GT(r.size, 0);
        ASSERT_GT(r.end, r.start);
        if (r.executable) {
            foundExecutable = true;
        }
    }
    ASSERT_TRUE(foundExecutable);
}

TEST(Memory, UnhookerInspection) {
    // 1. Clean function prologue: push rbp; mov rbp, rsp; sub rsp, 20h
    uint8_t cleanFunc[16] = {0x55, 0x48, 0x89, 0xE5, 0x48, 0x83, 0xEC, 0x20};
    auto cleanRes = Unhooker::Instance().InspectFunction("CleanFunc", cleanFunc);
    ASSERT_FALSE(cleanRes.has_value());

    // 2. Hooked function with JMP rel32 (0xE9 0x10 0x00 0x00 0x00)
    uint8_t jmpHook[16] = {0xE9, 0x10, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x90, 0x90, 0x90};
    auto jmpRes = Unhooker::Instance().InspectFunction("HookedJmp", jmpHook);
    ASSERT_TRUE(jmpRes.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(jmpRes->hook_type, "JMP_REL32 (0xE9)");

    // 3. Software Breakpoint (0xCC)
    uint8_t bpFunc[16] = {0xCC, 0x48, 0x89, 0xE5};
    auto bpRes = Unhooker::Instance().InspectFunction("BpFunc", bpFunc);
    ASSERT_TRUE(bpRes.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(bpRes->hook_type, "SOFTWARE_BREAKPOINT (0xCC)");

    // 4. MOV RAX, imm64; JMP RAX (48 B8 ... FF E0)
    uint8_t movJmpFunc[16] = {
        0x48, 0xB8, 0x11, 0x22, 0x33, 0x44, 0x55, 0x66, 0x77, 0x88, 0xFF, 0xE0
    };
    auto movJmpRes = Unhooker::Instance().InspectFunction("MovJmpFunc", movJmpFunc);
    ASSERT_TRUE(movJmpRes.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(movJmpRes->hook_type, "MOV_RAX_JMP_RAX (0x48 0xB8 ... 0xFF 0xE0)");
    ASSERT_EQ(movJmpRes->target_destination, 0x8877665544332211ULL);

    // 5. Short jump JMP rel8 (0xEB 0x10)
    uint8_t shortJmpFunc[16] = {0xEB, 0x10, 0x90, 0x90};
    auto shortJmpRes = Unhooker::Instance().InspectFunction("ShortJmpFunc", shortJmpFunc);
    ASSERT_TRUE(shortJmpRes.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(shortJmpRes->hook_type, "JMP_REL8 (0xEB)");

    // 6. MinHook 64-bit jump (MOV R10, imm64; JMP R10)
    uint8_t minHookFunc[16] = {
        0x49, 0xBA, 0x11, 0x22, 0x33, 0x44, 0x55, 0x66, 0x77, 0x88, 0x41, 0xFF, 0xE2
    };
    auto minHookRes = Unhooker::Instance().InspectFunction("MinHookFunc", minHookFunc);
    ASSERT_TRUE(minHookRes.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(minHookRes->hook_type, "MOV_R10_JMP_R10 (0x49 0xBA ... 0x41 0xFF 0xE2)");
    ASSERT_EQ(minHookRes->target_destination, 0x8877665544332211ULL);

    // 7. Push-Ret x64 Jump (68 [low32] C7 44 24 04 [high32] C3)
    uint8_t pushRetFunc[16] = {
        0x68, 0x11, 0x22, 0x33, 0x44, 0xC7, 0x44, 0x24, 0x04, 0x55, 0x66, 0x77, 0x88, 0xC3
    };
    auto pushRetRes = Unhooker::Instance().InspectFunction("PushRetFunc", pushRetFunc);
    ASSERT_TRUE(pushRetRes.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(pushRetRes->hook_type, "PUSH_RET_X64 (0x68 ... 0xC3)");
    ASSERT_EQ(pushRetRes->target_destination, 0x8877665544332211ULL);

    // 8. Live system hooks audit on current process (clean process should not trigger critical hooks)
    auto systemEvents = Unhooker::Instance().AuditSystemHooks();
    (void)systemEvents; // Verify execution does not crash
}

TEST(Memory, UnhookerRestoration) {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    uint8_t* mockPage = static_cast<uint8_t*>(VirtualAlloc(nullptr, 4096, MEM_COMMIT | MEM_RESERVE, PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE));
    ASSERT_TRUE(mockPage != nullptr);
#else
    uint8_t* mockPage = static_cast<uint8_t*>(mmap(nullptr, 4096, PROT_READ | PROT_WRITE | PROT_EXEC, MAP_PRIVATE | MAP_ANONYMOUS, -1, 0));
    ASSERT_TRUE(mockPage != MAP_FAILED);
#endif

    // Setup initial hooked code: JMP rel32
    mockPage[0] = 0xE9; mockPage[1] = 0x20; mockPage[2] = 0x00; mockPage[3] = 0x00; mockPage[4] = 0x00;
    mockPage[5] = 0x90; mockPage[6] = 0x90; mockPage[7] = 0x90;

    uint8_t originalBytes[5] = {0x55, 0x48, 0x89, 0xE5, 0x48};

    HookInfo info;
    info.function_name = "MockFunc";
    info.function_address = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(mockPage);
    info.hook_type = "JMP_REL32 (0xE9)";

    bool restored = Unhooker::Instance().RestoreHook(info, originalBytes, sizeof(originalBytes));
    ASSERT_TRUE(restored);

    // Verify mockPage first 5 bytes now match original clean bytes
    for (size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(originalBytes); ++i) {
        ASSERT_EQ(mockPage[i], originalBytes[i]);
    }

#if defined(_WIN32)
    VirtualFree(mockPage, 0, MEM_RELEASE);
#else
    munmap(mockPage, 4096);
#endif
}
```

`tests/test_obfuscation.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "tests/test_framework.hpp"
#include "noescape/core/obfuscation.hpp"

using namespace noescape::core;

TEST(Obfuscation, CompileTimeFnv1a) {
    constexpr uint64_t hash1 = NOESCAPE_HASH("ntdll.dll");
    constexpr uint64_t hash2 = NOESCAPE_HASH("kernel32.dll");
    constexpr uint64_t hashEmpty = NOESCAPE_HASH("");

    ASSERT_NE(hash1, 0ULL);
    ASSERT_NE(hash2, 0ULL);
    ASSERT_NE(hash1, hash2);
    ASSERT_EQ(hashEmpty, FNV1A_OFFSET);

    // Verify consistency
    uint64_t runtimeHash = HashFnv1a("ntdll.dll");
    ASSERT_EQ(hash1, runtimeHash);
}

TEST(Obfuscation, CompileTimeXorStrings) {
    const char* str1 = NOESCAPE_XOR("/proc/self/status");
    ASSERT_EQ(std::string(str1), "/proc/self/status");

    const char* str2 = NOESCAPE_XOR("SeDebugPrivilege");
    ASSERT_EQ(std::string(str2), "SeDebugPrivilege");

    const char* str3 = NOESCAPE_XOR("dbk64.sys");
    ASSERT_EQ(std::string(str3), "dbk64.sys");
}
```

`tests/test_overlay.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "tests/test_framework.hpp"
#include "noescape/overlay/overlay_detector.hpp"
#include <cstring>

#if !defined(_WIN32)
#include <sys/mman.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#endif

using namespace noescape;
using namespace noescape::overlay;

TEST(Overlay, ViewportGeometry) {
    ViewportRect r1{0, 0, 1920, 1080};
    ViewportRect r2{5, 5, 1915, 1075};
    ViewportRect r3{200, 200, 800, 600};

    ASSERT_TRUE(r1.Overlaps(r2, 10));
    ASSERT_FALSE(r1.Overlaps(r3, 10));
}

TEST(Overlay, WindowDetectionLayeredTopmost) {
    auto& detector = OverlayDetector::Instance();
    detector.ClearMockWindows();
    detector.SetGameViewport(0, 0, 1920, 1080);

    // 1. Normal game child or background window (clean)
    OverlayWindowInfo cleanWin;
    cleanWin.title = "Game Chat Window";
    cleanWin.class_name = "GameUI";
    cleanWin.pid = 12345;
    cleanWin.handle = 0x1000;
    cleanWin.rect = {100, 100, 400, 300};
    cleanWin.is_layered = false;
    cleanWin.is_transparent = false;
    cleanWin.is_topmost = false;
    detector.AddMockWindow(cleanWin);

    auto alertsClean = detector.DetectSuspiciousWindows();
    ASSERT_EQ(alertsClean.size(), 0);

    // 2. Suspicious ESP overlay: Topmost + Layered + Transparent matching game viewport
    OverlayWindowInfo cheatOverlay;
    cheatOverlay.title = "Overlay Window";
    cheatOverlay.class_name = "TransparentOverlay";
    cheatOverlay.pid = 99999;
    cheatOverlay.handle = 0x2000;
    cheatOverlay.rect = {0, 0, 1920, 1080};
    cheatOverlay.is_layered = true;
    cheatOverlay.is_transparent = true;
    cheatOverlay.is_topmost = true;
    detector.AddMockWindow(cheatOverlay);

    auto alertsOverlay = detector.DetectSuspiciousWindows();
    ASSERT_EQ(alertsOverlay.size(), 1);
    ASSERT_EQ(alertsOverlay[0].type, core::DetectionType::OVERLAY_HIJACK_DETECTED);
    ASSERT_EQ(alertsOverlay[0].severity, core::Severity::HIGH);

    // 3. Suspicious Window with ImGui / ESP title
    OverlayWindowInfo imguiOverlay;
    imguiOverlay.title = "Dear ImGui ESP Overlay v1.2";
    imguiOverlay.class_name = "ImGuiWindow";
    imguiOverlay.pid = 88888;
    imguiOverlay.handle = 0x3000;
    imguiOverlay.rect = {200, 200, 500, 400};
    imguiOverlay.is_layered = false;
    imguiOverlay.is_transparent = false;
    imguiOverlay.is_topmost = true;
    detector.AddMockWindow(imguiOverlay);

    auto alertsAll = detector.DetectSuspiciousWindows();
    ASSERT_EQ(alertsAll.size(), 2);

    detector.ClearMockWindows();
}

static void LegitimateSwapchainStub() {
    volatile int x = 42;
    (void)x;
}

TEST(Overlay, SwapchainPointerInspectionValidRoutine) {
    auto& detector = OverlayDetector::Instance();

    // Valid routine residing in text section of loaded binary
    void* validFn = reinterpret_cast<void*>(&LegitimateSwapchainStub);
    auto ev = detector.InspectRenderingPointer("LegitimateSwapchainStub", validFn);
    ASSERT_FALSE(ev.has_value());
}

TEST(Overlay, SwapchainPointerInspectionUnbackedAddress) {
    auto& detector = OverlayDetector::Instance();

    // Allocate memory on heap / anonymous memory (not backed by executable file module)
    uint8_t dummyCode[16] = {0x90, 0x90, 0xC3};
    void* fakeSwapchainFn = reinterpret_cast<void*>(dummyCode);

    auto ev = detector.InspectRenderingPointer("FakeIDXGISwapChain_Present", fakeSwapchainFn);
    ASSERT_TRUE(ev.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(ev->type, core::DetectionType::RENDERING_HOOK_DETECTED);
    ASSERT_EQ(ev->severity, core::Severity::CRITICAL);
}

TEST(Overlay, SwapchainInlineHookDetection) {
    auto& detector = OverlayDetector::Instance();

#if !defined(_WIN32)
    // Create an executable page with an inline hook stub (e.g. 0xE9 JMP rel32)
    long pageSize = sysconf(_SC_PAGESIZE);
    if (pageSize <= 0) pageSize = 4096;

    void* page = mmap(nullptr, pageSize, PROT_READ | PROT_WRITE | PROT_EXEC, MAP_PRIVATE | MAP_ANONYMOUS, -1, 0);
    ASSERT_TRUE(page != MAP_FAILED);

    uint8_t* code = static_cast<uint8_t*>(page);
    code[0] = 0xE9; // JMP rel32
    int32_t rel = 0x1234;
    std::memcpy(code + 1, &rel, sizeof(rel));

    auto ev = detector.InspectRenderingPointer("Hooked_Present", page);
    ASSERT_TRUE(ev.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(ev->type, core::DetectionType::RENDERING_HOOK_DETECTED);

    munmap(page, pageSize);
#endif
}

TEST(Overlay, DynamicSwapchainVTableInspection) {
    auto& detector = OverlayDetector::Instance();
    detector.ClearRegisteredVTables();

    void* dummyVtable[4];
    dummyVtable[0] = reinterpret_cast<void*>(&LegitimateSwapchainStub);
    dummyVtable[1] = reinterpret_cast<void*>(&LegitimateSwapchainStub);
    // Vtable entry 2: hooked/unbacked address
    uint8_t fakeTrampoline[8] = {0x90, 0xC3};
    dummyVtable[2] = reinterpret_cast<void*>(fakeTrampoline);
    dummyVtable[3] = nullptr;

    detector.RegisterSwapchainVTable(dummyVtable, 4);

    auto events = detector.AuditSwapchainHooks();
    ASSERT_GT(events.size(), 0);
    ASSERT_EQ(events[0].type, core::DetectionType::RENDERING_HOOK_DETECTED);

    detector.ClearRegisteredVTables();
}

TEST(Overlay, ViewportContainmentAndMultiMonitor) {
    auto& detector = OverlayDetector::Instance();
    detector.ClearMockWindows();
    detector.SetGameViewport(0, 0, 1920, 1080);

    // 1. Multi-monitor fullscreen overlay completely enclosing game viewport
    OverlayWindowInfo enclosingOverlay;
    enclosingOverlay.title = "Multi-Monitor Transparent Canvas";
    enclosingOverlay.class_name = "MultiMonOverlay";
    enclosingOverlay.pid = 91919;
    enclosingOverlay.handle = 0x5000;
    enclosingOverlay.rect = {-1920, 0, 3840, 1080}; // Spans left monitor across main monitor
    enclosingOverlay.is_layered = true;
    enclosingOverlay.is_transparent = true;
    enclosingOverlay.is_topmost = true;
    detector.AddMockWindow(enclosingOverlay);

    // 2. Non-overlapping window placed entirely on the secondary screen
    OverlayWindowInfo otherMonitorWin;
    otherMonitorWin.title = "Secondary Screen Utility Window";
    otherMonitorWin.class_name = "CleanApp";
    otherMonitorWin.pid = 92929;
    otherMonitorWin.handle = 0x6000;
    otherMonitorWin.rect = {-1920, 0, 1920, 1080};
    otherMonitorWin.is_layered = true;
    otherMonitorWin.is_transparent = true;
    otherMonitorWin.is_topmost = true;
    detector.AddMockWindow(otherMonitorWin);

    auto alerts = detector.DetectSuspiciousWindows();
    // Only the enclosing overlay covering the game viewport should trigger
    ASSERT_EQ(alerts.size(), 1);
    ASSERT_EQ(alerts[0].type, core::DetectionType::OVERLAY_HIJACK_DETECTED);

    detector.ClearMockWindows();
}

TEST(Overlay, SwapchainPointerInspectionInvalidAddressSafe) {
    auto& detector = OverlayDetector::Instance();

    // Passing an address below 0x10000: safely handled without crash
    void* lowAddr = reinterpret_cast<void*>(0x1000);
    auto evLow = detector.InspectRenderingPointer("LowAddrRoutine", lowAddr);
    ASSERT_FALSE(evLow.has_value());

    // Passing unbacked / unmapped address: safely detects unbacked trampoline without SIGSEGV
    void* unbacked = reinterpret_cast<void*>(0x7FFFF0001000ULL);
    auto evUnbacked = detector.InspectRenderingPointer("UnbackedRoutine", unbacked);
    ASSERT_TRUE(evUnbacked.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(evUnbacked->type, core::DetectionType::RENDERING_HOOK_DETECTED);
}

TEST(Overlay, SwapchainPointerInspectionDataSectionNonExecutable) {
    auto& detector = OverlayDetector::Instance();

    // Static buffer residing in non-executable data section
    static const char s_dummyData[] = "NonExecutableDataSectionBuffer";
    void* dataPtr = const_cast<void*>(static_cast<const void*>(s_dummyData));

    auto ev = detector.InspectRenderingPointer("DataSectionVTableEntry", dataPtr);
    ASSERT_TRUE(ev.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(ev->type, core::DetectionType::RENDERING_HOOK_DETECTED);
}
```

`tests/test_sdk.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "tests/test_framework.hpp"
#include "noescape_sdk.h"
#include "noescape/core/types.hpp"
#include <string>
#include <thread>

static int g_last_violation_type = -1;
static int g_last_violation_score = 0;
static std::string g_last_violation_details;

static void SdkViolationCallback(int type, int score, const char* details) {
    g_last_violation_type = type;
    g_last_violation_score = score;
    if (details) g_last_violation_details = details;
}

TEST(SDK, DefaultConfiguration) {
    NoEscapeConfig cfg = NoEscape_GetDefaultConfig();
    ASSERT_EQ(cfg.ban_threshold, 100);
    ASSERT_TRUE(cfg.enforce_ban);
    ASSERT_TRUE(cfg.enable_anti_debug);
    ASSERT_TRUE(cfg.enable_overlay_detection);
    ASSERT_TRUE(cfg.enable_blackbox_dump);
    ASSERT_FALSE(cfg.enable_heartbeat);
}

TEST(SDK, LifecycleAndIdempotency) {
    NoEscapeConfig cfg = NoEscape_GetDefaultConfig();
    cfg.enable_heartbeat = false;
    cfg.enable_anti_debug = false; // Keep quiet in test harness

    // Normal Init
    int rc = NoEscape_Init(&cfg);
    ASSERT_EQ(rc, 0);
    ASSERT_EQ(NoEscape_GetThreatScore(), 0);
    ASSERT_FALSE(NoEscape_IsBanned());

    // Idempotent re-initialization
    rc = NoEscape_Init(&cfg);
    ASSERT_EQ(rc, 0);

    // Shutdown
    NoEscape_Shutdown();
    ASSERT_EQ(NoEscape_GetThreatScore(), 0);
    ASSERT_FALSE(NoEscape_IsBanned());

    // Multiple shutdown calls are safe
    NoEscape_Shutdown();
}

TEST(SDK, PushMouseEventAndAimAnalysis) {
    NoEscapeConfig cfg = NoEscape_GetDefaultConfig();
    cfg.enable_heartbeat = false;
    cfg.enable_anti_debug = false;
    cfg.enforce_ban = false;

    ASSERT_EQ(NoEscape_Init(&cfg), 0);

    uint64_t timestamp = 1000;
    // Push smooth human-like mouse movement (constant small deltas)
    for (int i = 0; i < 15; ++i) {
        NoEscape_PushMouseEvent(0.5f, 0.3f, timestamp);
        timestamp += 16;
    }
    ASSERT_EQ(NoEscape_GetThreatScore(), 0);

    // Push instant snap aimbot: 45 degree flick in a single 16ms tick
    NoEscape_PushMouseEvent(45.0f, 15.0f, timestamp);
    timestamp += 16;
    // Followed by instant lock / dead stop (motion delta = 0.0)
    NoEscape_PushMouseEvent(0.0f, 0.0f, timestamp);

    // Threat score should increase due to snap detection
    ASSERT_GT(NoEscape_GetThreatScore(), 0);

    NoEscape_Shutdown();
}

TEST(SDK, ViolationCallbackInvocation) {
    g_last_violation_type = -1;
    g_last_violation_score = 0;
    g_last_violation_details.clear();

    NoEscape_SetViolationCallback(SdkViolationCallback);

    NoEscapeConfig cfg = NoEscape_GetDefaultConfig();
    cfg.enable_heartbeat = false;
    cfg.enable_anti_debug = false;
    cfg.ban_threshold = 50;
    cfg.enforce_ban = true;

    ASSERT_EQ(NoEscape_Init(&cfg), 0);

    // Trigger instant snap aimbot to fire a violation
    uint64_t t = 5000;
    for (int i = 0; i < 10; ++i) {
        NoEscape_PushMouseEvent(0.2f, 0.1f, t);
        t += 16;
    }
    NoEscape_PushMouseEvent(50.0f, 20.0f, t);
    t += 16;
    NoEscape_PushMouseEvent(0.0f, 0.0f, t);

    ASSERT_GT(NoEscape_GetThreatScore(), 0);
    // Explicitly verify the callback was dispatched with genuine detection type and score
    ASSERT_EQ(g_last_violation_type, static_cast<int>(noescape::core::DetectionType::KINEMATICS_AIMBOT_SNAP));
    ASSERT_GT(g_last_violation_score, 0);
    ASSERT_FALSE(g_last_violation_details.empty());

    NoEscape_Shutdown();
}

TEST(SDK, CppWrapperClientAPI) {
    NoEscapeConfig cfg = NoEscape_GetDefaultConfig();
    cfg.enable_heartbeat = false;
    cfg.enable_anti_debug = false;

    ASSERT_TRUE(noescape::sdk::Client::Init(&cfg));
    ASSERT_EQ(noescape::sdk::Client::GetThreatScore(), 0);
    ASSERT_FALSE(noescape::sdk::Client::IsBanned());

    noescape::sdk::Client::SetViewport(0, 0, 2560, 1440);
    noescape::sdk::Client::PushMouseEvent(1.0f, 2.0f, 100);
    noescape::sdk::Client::ResetInput();
    noescape::sdk::Client::Tick();

    noescape::sdk::Client::Shutdown();
}

TEST(SDK, ConcurrentMousePushing) {
    NoEscapeConfig cfg = NoEscape_GetDefaultConfig();
    cfg.enable_heartbeat = false;
    cfg.enable_anti_debug = false;
    cfg.enforce_ban = false;

    ASSERT_EQ(NoEscape_Init(&cfg), 0);

    std::vector<std::thread> threads;
    constexpr int kThreads = 4;
    constexpr int kIterations = 100;

    for (int t = 0; t < kThreads; ++t) {
        threads.emplace_back([t]() {
            uint64_t ts = 10000 + t * 5000;
            for (int i = 0; i < kIterations; ++i) {
                NoEscape_PushMouseEvent(0.05f, 0.02f, ts + i * 2);
            }
        });
    }

    for (auto& th : threads) {
        th.join();
    }

    NoEscape_Tick();
    ASSERT_FALSE(NoEscape_IsBanned());

    NoEscape_Shutdown();
}

TEST(SDK, ResetInputLifecycle) {
    NoEscapeConfig cfg = NoEscape_GetDefaultConfig();
    cfg.enable_heartbeat = false;
    cfg.enable_anti_debug = false;

    ASSERT_EQ(NoEscape_Init(&cfg), 0);
    NoEscape_PushMouseEvent(10.0f, 5.0f, 1000);
    NoEscape_ResetInput();
    ASSERT_EQ(NoEscape_GetThreatScore(), 0);
    NoEscape_Shutdown();
}
```
