`tests/test_signatures.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "tests/test_framework.hpp"
#include "noescape/memory/cheat_signature.hpp"
#include "noescape/memory/signature_database.hpp"
#include "noescape/memory/pattern_scanner.hpp"
#include "noescape/memory/memory_scanner.hpp"

#if defined(_WIN32)
#include <windows.h>
#else
#include <sys/mman.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#endif

#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <cstring>

using namespace noescape::memory;
using namespace noescape::core;

TEST(Signatures, DatabaseIntegrityAndCount) {
    auto sigs = SignatureDatabase::GetAllSignatures();
    ASSERT_GT(sigs.size(), 35);
    ASSERT_EQ(SignatureDatabase::Count(), sigs.size());

    // Verify all 8 primary cheat categories are represented
    bool hasHookFramework = false;
    bool hasInjectorStub = false;
    bool hasDmaTool = false;
    bool hasDebuggerInterface = false;
    bool hasShellcodePrologue = false;
    bool hasOverlayHook = false;
    bool hasPackerProtector = false;
    bool hasSpeedhackWrapper = false;

    for (const auto& sig : sigs) {
        ASSERT_FALSE(sig.GetName().empty());
        ASSERT_GT(sig.Length(), 0);
        ASSERT_FALSE(sig.GetDescription().empty());
        ASSERT_GT(sig.GetThreatScore(), 0);

        switch (sig.GetCategory()) {
            case SignatureCategory::HOOK_FRAMEWORK: hasHookFramework = true; break;
            case SignatureCategory::INJECTOR_STUB: hasInjectorStub = true; break;
            case SignatureCategory::DMA_TOOL: hasDmaTool = true; break;
            case SignatureCategory::DEBUGGER_INTERFACE: hasDebuggerInterface = true; break;
            case SignatureCategory::SHELLCODE_PROLOGUE: hasShellcodePrologue = true; break;
            case SignatureCategory::OVERLAY_HOOK: hasOverlayHook = true; break;
            case SignatureCategory::PACKER_PROTECTOR: hasPackerProtector = true; break;
            case SignatureCategory::SPEEDHACK_WRAPPER: hasSpeedhackWrapper = true; break;
            default: break;
        }
    }

    ASSERT_TRUE(hasHookFramework);
    ASSERT_TRUE(hasInjectorStub);
    ASSERT_TRUE(hasDmaTool);
    ASSERT_TRUE(hasDebuggerInterface);
    ASSERT_TRUE(hasShellcodePrologue);
    ASSERT_TRUE(hasOverlayHook);
    ASSERT_TRUE(hasPackerProtector);
    ASSERT_TRUE(hasSpeedhackWrapper);
}

TEST(Signatures, CategoryFiltering) {
    auto hooks = SignatureDatabase::GetByCategory(SignatureCategory::HOOK_FRAMEWORK);
    ASSERT_FALSE(hooks.empty());
    for (const auto& h : hooks) {
        ASSERT_EQ(static_cast<int>(h.GetCategory()), static_cast<int>(SignatureCategory::HOOK_FRAMEWORK));
    }

    auto dma = SignatureDatabase::GetByCategory(SignatureCategory::DMA_TOOL);
    ASSERT_FALSE(dma.empty());
    for (const auto& d : dma) {
        ASSERT_EQ(static_cast<int>(d.GetCategory()), static_cast<int>(SignatureCategory::DMA_TOOL));
    }

    auto minhook = SignatureDatabase::FindByName("MinHook_X64_R10_Trampoline");
    ASSERT_TRUE(minhook.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(minhook->GetName(), "MinHook_X64_R10_Trampoline");
    ASSERT_EQ(static_cast<int>(minhook->GetCategory()), static_cast<int>(SignatureCategory::HOOK_FRAMEWORK));

    auto nonExistent = SignatureDatabase::FindByName("NonExistent_Cheat_Signature_XYZ");
    ASSERT_FALSE(nonExistent.has_value());
}

TEST(Signatures, TargetRuleMatchingLogic) {
    // 1. UNBACKED_ONLY rule
    ASSERT_TRUE(CheatSignature::IsTargetMatched(MemoryTargetRule::UNBACKED_ONLY, false, true, true));  // anon exec
    ASSERT_FALSE(CheatSignature::IsTargetMatched(MemoryTargetRule::UNBACKED_ONLY, true, true, true));  // file exec
    ASSERT_FALSE(CheatSignature::IsTargetMatched(MemoryTargetRule::UNBACKED_ONLY, false, false, true)); // anon non-exec
    ASSERT_FALSE(CheatSignature::IsTargetMatched(MemoryTargetRule::UNBACKED_ONLY, false, true, false)); // not readable

    // 2. ALL_EXECUTABLE rule
    ASSERT_TRUE(CheatSignature::IsTargetMatched(MemoryTargetRule::ALL_EXECUTABLE, false, true, true));  // anon exec
    ASSERT_TRUE(CheatSignature::IsTargetMatched(MemoryTargetRule::ALL_EXECUTABLE, true, true, true));   // file exec
    ASSERT_FALSE(CheatSignature::IsTargetMatched(MemoryTargetRule::ALL_EXECUTABLE, false, false, true)); // anon non-exec

    // 3. ANONYMOUS_DATA rule
    ASSERT_TRUE(CheatSignature::IsTargetMatched(MemoryTargetRule::ANONYMOUS_DATA, false, false, true)); // anon data
    ASSERT_FALSE(CheatSignature::IsTargetMatched(MemoryTargetRule::ANONYMOUS_DATA, true, false, true));  // file data
    ASSERT_FALSE(CheatSignature::IsTargetMatched(MemoryTargetRule::ANONYMOUS_DATA, false, true, true));  // anon exec

    // 4. ANY_ANONYMOUS rule
    ASSERT_TRUE(CheatSignature::IsTargetMatched(MemoryTargetRule::ANY_ANONYMOUS, false, true, true));  // anon exec
    ASSERT_TRUE(CheatSignature::IsTargetMatched(MemoryTargetRule::ANY_ANONYMOUS, false, false, true)); // anon data
    ASSERT_FALSE(CheatSignature::IsTargetMatched(MemoryTargetRule::ANY_ANONYMOUS, true, true, true));  // file exec
    ASSERT_FALSE(CheatSignature::IsTargetMatched(MemoryTargetRule::ANY_ANONYMOUS, true, false, true)); // file data

    // 5. ALL_REGIONS rule
    ASSERT_TRUE(CheatSignature::IsTargetMatched(MemoryTargetRule::ALL_REGIONS, true, true, true));
    ASSERT_TRUE(CheatSignature::IsTargetMatched(MemoryTargetRule::ALL_REGIONS, false, false, true));
    ASSERT_FALSE(CheatSignature::IsTargetMatched(MemoryTargetRule::ALL_REGIONS, false, false, false));
}

TEST(Signatures, CustomRegistrationAndReset) {
    size_t initialCount = SignatureDatabase::Count();

    CheatSignature custom(
        "90 90 90 CC CC",
        "Test_Custom_Breakpoint_Cluster",
        SignatureCategory::DEBUGGER_INTERFACE,
        90,
        Severity::HIGH,
        MemoryTargetRule::UNBACKED_ONLY,
        "Custom synthetic breakpoint cluster for test verification"
    );

    SignatureDatabase::RegisterCustomSignature(custom);
    ASSERT_EQ(SignatureDatabase::Count(), initialCount + 1);

    auto found = SignatureDatabase::FindByName("Test_Custom_Breakpoint_Cluster");
    ASSERT_TRUE(found.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(found->GetThreatScore(), 90);

    SignatureDatabase::Reset();
    ASSERT_EQ(SignatureDatabase::Count(), initialCount);
    ASSERT_FALSE(SignatureDatabase::FindByName("Test_Custom_Breakpoint_Cluster").has_value());
}

TEST(Signatures, HookFrameworkPatternMatching) {
    // MinHook x64 Trampoline: 49 BA [imm64] 41 FF E2
    std::vector<uint8_t> buffer(64, 0x90);
    // Inject at offset 16
    buffer[16] = 0x49; buffer[17] = 0xBA;
    buffer[18] = 0x11; buffer[19] = 0x22; buffer[20] = 0x33; buffer[21] = 0x44;
    buffer[22] = 0x55; buffer[23] = 0x66; buffer[24] = 0x77; buffer[25] = 0x88;
    buffer[26] = 0x41; buffer[27] = 0xFF; buffer[28] = 0xE2;

    auto minhookSig = SignatureDatabase::FindByName("MinHook_X64_R10_Trampoline");
    ASSERT_TRUE(minhookSig.has_value());

    auto match = PatternScanner::Scan(buffer.data(), buffer.size(), *minhookSig);
    ASSERT_TRUE(match.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(*match, 16);

    // PolyHook push-xchg-ret: 50 48 B8 [imm64] 48 87 04 24 C3
    std::vector<uint8_t> polyBuf(64, 0xCC);
    polyBuf[10] = 0x50; polyBuf[11] = 0x48; polyBuf[12] = 0xB8;
    polyBuf[13] = 0xAA; polyBuf[14] = 0xBB; polyBuf[15] = 0xCC; polyBuf[16] = 0xDD;
    polyBuf[17] = 0xEE; polyBuf[18] = 0xFF; polyBuf[19] = 0x00; polyBuf[20] = 0x11;
    polyBuf[21] = 0x48; polyBuf[22] = 0x87; polyBuf[23] = 0x04; polyBuf[24] = 0x24;
    polyBuf[25] = 0xC3;

    auto polySig = SignatureDatabase::FindByName("PolyHook_X64_PushXchgRet");
    ASSERT_TRUE(polySig.has_value());
    auto polyMatch = PatternScanner::Scan(polyBuf.data(), polyBuf.size(), *polySig);
    ASSERT_TRUE(polyMatch.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(*polyMatch, 10);
}

TEST(Signatures, DmaToolPatternMatching) {
    // LeechCore "LC_MEM" header and MemProcFS "VMMDLL"
    std::vector<uint8_t> buffer(128, 0x00);

    // Write "LC_MEM" at offset 20: 4C 43 5F 4D 45 4D
    const char* lc = "LC_MEM";
    std::memcpy(buffer.data() + 20, lc, 6);

    // Write "VMMDLL" at offset 80: 56 4D 4D 44 4C 4C
    const char* vmm = "VMMDLL";
    std::memcpy(buffer.data() + 80, vmm, 6);

    auto dmaSigs = SignatureDatabase::GetByCategory(SignatureCategory::DMA_TOOL);
    auto matches = PatternScanner::ScanSignatures(buffer.data(), buffer.size(), dmaSigs);

    ASSERT_TRUE(matches.size() >= 2);
    bool foundLc = false;
    bool foundVmm = false;

    for (const auto& m : matches) {
        if (m.signature_name == "LeechCore_Memory_Header") {
            foundLc = true;
            ASSERT_EQ(m.address, 20);
        } else if (m.signature_name == "MemProcFS_VmmDll") {
            foundVmm = true;
            ASSERT_EQ(m.address, 80);
        }
    }

    ASSERT_TRUE(foundLc);
    ASSERT_TRUE(foundVmm);
}

TEST(Signatures, InjectorAndShellcodePatternMatching) {
    // ManualMap DllMain invocation stub: 48 83 EC 20 B8 01 00 00 00 89 ? ? ? FF D2 48 83 C4 20
    std::vector<uint8_t> buffer(64, 0x90);
    uint8_t mmapStub[] = {
        0x48, 0x83, 0xEC, 0x20, 0xB8, 0x01, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,
        0x89, 0x44, 0x24, 0x18, 0xFF, 0xD2, 0x48, 0x83, 0xC4, 0x20
    };
    std::memcpy(buffer.data() + 5, mmapStub, sizeof(mmapStub));

    auto mmapSig = SignatureDatabase::FindByName("ManualMap_DllMain_Invocation");
    ASSERT_TRUE(mmapSig.has_value());
    auto match = PatternScanner::Scan(buffer.data(), buffer.size(), *mmapSig);
    ASSERT_TRUE(match.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(*match, 5);

    // Generic EggHunter: E8 00 00 00 00 58
    uint8_t eggStub[] = {0xE8, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x58};
    std::memcpy(buffer.data() + 40, eggStub, sizeof(eggStub));

    auto eggSig = SignatureDatabase::FindByName("Generic_EggHunter_CallPop");
    ASSERT_TRUE(eggSig.has_value());
    auto eggMatch = PatternScanner::Scan(buffer.data(), buffer.size(), *eggSig);
    ASSERT_TRUE(eggMatch.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(*eggMatch, 40);
}

TEST(Signatures, OverlayAndSpeedhackPatternMatching) {
    // D3D Present hook stub: 48 89 5C 24 08 48 89 74 24 10 57 48 83 EC 30 48 8B D9 49 8B F8
    std::vector<uint8_t> buffer(100, 0x00);
    uint8_t d3dStub[] = {
        0x48, 0x89, 0x5C, 0x24, 0x08, 0x48, 0x89, 0x74, 0x24, 0x10,
        0x57, 0x48, 0x83, 0xEC, 0x30, 0x48, 0x8B, 0xD9, 0x49, 0x8B, 0xF8
    };
    std::memcpy(buffer.data() + 12, d3dStub, sizeof(d3dStub));

    auto d3dSig = SignatureDatabase::FindByName("D3D_SwapChain_Present_Hook");
    ASSERT_TRUE(d3dSig.has_value());
    auto match = PatternScanner::Scan(buffer.data(), buffer.size(), *d3dSig);
    ASSERT_TRUE(match.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(*match, 12);

    // Speedhack module name "Speedhack.dll"
    const char* ceSpeed = "Speedhack.dll";
    std::memcpy(buffer.data() + 60, ceSpeed, std::strlen(ceSpeed));

    auto speedSig = SignatureDatabase::FindByName("Speedhack_CE_ModuleName");
    ASSERT_TRUE(speedSig.has_value());
    auto speedMatch = PatternScanner::Scan(buffer.data(), buffer.size(), *speedSig);
    ASSERT_TRUE(speedMatch.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(*speedMatch, 60);
}

TEST(Signatures, LiveMemoryAuditDetectionInAnonymousMemory) {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    uint8_t* page = static_cast<uint8_t*>(VirtualAlloc(nullptr, 4096, MEM_COMMIT | MEM_RESERVE, PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE));
    ASSERT_TRUE(page != nullptr);
#else
    uint8_t* page = static_cast<uint8_t*>(mmap(nullptr, 4096, PROT_READ | PROT_WRITE | PROT_EXEC, MAP_PRIVATE | MAP_ANONYMOUS, -1, 0));
    ASSERT_TRUE(page != MAP_FAILED);
#endif

    // Inject MinHook x64 Trampoline: 49 BA [8 bytes target] 41 FF E2
    page[100] = 0x49; page[101] = 0xBA;
    page[102] = 0x78; page[103] = 0x56; page[104] = 0x34; page[105] = 0x12;
    page[106] = 0x00; page[107] = 0x00; page[108] = 0x00; page[109] = 0x00;
    page[110] = 0x41; page[111] = 0xFF; page[112] = 0xE2;

    auto events = MemoryScanner::Instance().PerformAudit();

    bool foundMinHook = false;
    for (const auto& ev : events) {
        if (ev.type == DetectionType::MEMORY_SIGNATURE_MATCH &&
            ev.details.find("MinHook_X64_R10_Trampoline") != std::string::npos) {
            foundMinHook = true;
            ASSERT_EQ(ev.component, "MemoryScanner::HOOK_FRAMEWORK");
            ASSERT_EQ(ev.threat_score, 100);
            ASSERT_EQ(static_cast<int>(ev.severity), static_cast<int>(Severity::FATAL_BAN));
        }
    }

#if defined(_WIN32)
    VirtualFree(page, 0, MEM_RELEASE);
#else
    munmap(page, 4096);
#endif

    ASSERT_TRUE(foundMinHook);
}

TEST(Signatures, CleanProcessHasNoSignatureFalsePositives) {
    // In a clean process with no injected anonymous memory, default signatures
    // must not produce any MEMORY_SIGNATURE_MATCH events on genuine binaries!
    auto sigs = MemoryScanner::GetDefaultCheatSignatures();
    auto events = MemoryScanner::Instance().ScanSignatures(sigs);

    ASSERT_EQ(events.size(), 0);
}

TEST(Signatures, LiveMemoryAuditDetectionInAnonymousDataMemory) {
    // Allocate anonymous non-executable (data) memory: PROT_READ | PROT_WRITE
#if defined(_WIN32)
    uint8_t* page = static_cast<uint8_t*>(VirtualAlloc(nullptr, 4096, MEM_COMMIT | MEM_RESERVE, PAGE_READWRITE));
    ASSERT_TRUE(page != nullptr);
#else
    uint8_t* page = static_cast<uint8_t*>(mmap(nullptr, 4096, PROT_READ | PROT_WRITE, MAP_PRIVATE | MAP_ANONYMOUS, -1, 0));
    ASSERT_TRUE(page != MAP_FAILED);
#endif

    // Inject LeechCore header "LC_MEM" (4C 43 5F 4D 45 4D) at offset 128
    const char* lc = "LC_MEM";
    std::memcpy(page + 128, lc, 6);

    // Inject MemProcFS header "VMMDLL" (56 4D 4D 44 4C 4C) at offset 256
    const char* vmm = "VMMDLL";
    std::memcpy(page + 256, vmm, 6);

    auto sigs = MemoryScanner::GetDefaultCheatSignatures();
    auto events = MemoryScanner::Instance().ScanSignatures(sigs);

    bool foundLc = false;
    bool foundVmm = false;
    for (const auto& ev : events) {
        if (ev.type == DetectionType::MEMORY_SIGNATURE_MATCH) {
            if (ev.details.find("LeechCore_Memory_Header") != std::string::npos) {
                foundLc = true;
                ASSERT_EQ(ev.component, "MemoryScanner::DMA_TOOL");
            }
            if (ev.details.find("MemProcFS_VmmDll") != std::string::npos) {
                foundVmm = true;
                ASSERT_EQ(ev.component, "MemoryScanner::DMA_TOOL");
            }
        }
    }

#if defined(_WIN32)
    VirtualFree(page, 0, MEM_RELEASE);
#else
    munmap(page, 4096);
#endif

    ASSERT_TRUE(foundLc);
    ASSERT_TRUE(foundVmm);
}

TEST(Signatures, NewCuratedSignaturesVerification) {
    ASSERT_EQ(SignatureDatabase::Count(), 50);

    auto refl = SignatureDatabase::FindByName("ReflectiveLoader_Export");
    ASSERT_TRUE(refl.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(static_cast<int>(refl->GetCategory()), static_cast<int>(SignatureCategory::INJECTOR_STUB));

    auto ldr = SignatureDatabase::FindByName("ManualMap_LdrLoadDll_Resolver");
    ASSERT_TRUE(ldr.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(static_cast<int>(ldr->GetCategory()), static_cast<int>(SignatureCategory::INJECTOR_STUB));

    auto ceVeh = SignatureDatabase::FindByName("CheatEngine_VEH_Debugger");
    ASSERT_TRUE(ceVeh.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(static_cast<int>(ceVeh->GetCategory()), static_cast<int>(SignatureCategory::DEBUGGER_INTERFACE));

    auto d3d12 = SignatureDatabase::FindByName("D3D12_CommandQueue_Hook");
    ASSERT_TRUE(d3d12.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(static_cast<int>(d3d12->GetCategory()), static_cast<int>(SignatureCategory::OVERLAY_HOOK));

    auto upx2 = SignatureDatabase::FindByName("UPX_Section_2");
    ASSERT_TRUE(upx2.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(static_cast<int>(upx2->GetCategory()), static_cast<int>(SignatureCategory::PACKER_PROTECTOR));

    auto vmp = SignatureDatabase::FindByName("VMProtect_Section_Tag");
    ASSERT_TRUE(vmp.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(static_cast<int>(vmp->GetCategory()), static_cast<int>(SignatureCategory::PACKER_PROTECTOR));

    auto themida = SignatureDatabase::FindByName("Themida_Section_Tag");
    ASSERT_TRUE(themida.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(static_cast<int>(themida->GetCategory()), static_cast<int>(SignatureCategory::PACKER_PROTECTOR));

    auto speedTgt = SignatureDatabase::FindByName("Speedhack_TimeGetTime_Detour");
    ASSERT_TRUE(speedTgt.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(static_cast<int>(speedTgt->GetCategory()), static_cast<int>(SignatureCategory::SPEEDHACK_WRAPPER));

    // Pattern match ReflectiveLoader
    std::vector<uint8_t> buf(64, 0x00);
    std::memcpy(buf.data() + 10, "ReflectiveLoader", 16);
    auto match = PatternScanner::Scan(buf.data(), buf.size(), *refl);
    ASSERT_TRUE(match.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(*match, 10);
}

TEST(Signatures, TrampolineAndDetourRuleSafety) {
    auto d3dSig = SignatureDatabase::FindByName("D3D_SwapChain_Present_Hook");
    ASSERT_TRUE(d3dSig.has_value());
    // Crucial false positive prevention: must NOT be ALL_EXECUTABLE
    ASSERT_EQ(static_cast<int>(d3dSig->GetTargetRule()), static_cast<int>(MemoryTargetRule::UNBACKED_ONLY));

    auto substrateSig = SignatureDatabase::FindByName("Substrate_X64_PushRet");
    ASSERT_TRUE(substrateSig.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(static_cast<int>(substrateSig->GetTargetRule()), static_cast<int>(MemoryTargetRule::UNBACKED_ONLY));
}
```

`tests/test_simd_scanner.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "tests/test_framework.hpp"
#include "noescape/memory/pattern_scanner.hpp"
#include <vector>

using namespace noescape::memory;

TEST(PatternScanner, ExactMatch) {
    std::vector<uint8_t> buffer = {0x90, 0x90, 0x48, 0x89, 0x5C, 0x24, 0x08, 0xC3};
    Pattern pat("48 89 5C 24 08", "ExactJmp");

    auto res = PatternScanner::Scan(buffer.data(), buffer.size(), pat);
    ASSERT_TRUE(res.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(*res, 2);
}

TEST(PatternScanner, WildcardMatch) {
    std::vector<uint8_t> buffer = {
        0x55, 0x48, 0x89, 0xE5, 0x48, 0xB8, 0x78, 0x56, 0x34, 0x12, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0xFF, 0xE0
    };
    // Pattern with wildcards for imm64
    Pattern pat("48 B8 ? ? ? ? ? ? ? ? FF E0", "X64JmpTrampoline");

    auto res = PatternScanner::Scan(buffer.data(), buffer.size(), pat);
    ASSERT_TRUE(res.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(*res, 4);
}

TEST(PatternScanner, BoundaryCases) {
    std::vector<uint8_t> buffer = {0xAA, 0xBB, 0xCC, 0xDD};

    // Pattern at offset 0
    Pattern patStart("AA BB");
    auto resStart = PatternScanner::Scan(buffer.data(), buffer.size(), patStart);
    ASSERT_TRUE(resStart.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(*resStart, 0);

    // Pattern at the very end
    Pattern patEnd("CC DD");
    auto resEnd = PatternScanner::Scan(buffer.data(), buffer.size(), patEnd);
    ASSERT_TRUE(resEnd.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(*resEnd, 2);

    // Pattern longer than buffer
    Pattern patLong("AA BB CC DD EE");
    auto resLong = PatternScanner::Scan(buffer.data(), buffer.size(), patLong);
    ASSERT_FALSE(resLong.has_value());

    // Pattern not found
    Pattern patMissing("FF EE");
    auto resMissing = PatternScanner::Scan(buffer.data(), buffer.size(), patMissing);
    ASSERT_FALSE(resMissing.has_value());

    // Empty buffer
    auto resEmpty = PatternScanner::Scan(nullptr, 0, patStart);
    ASSERT_FALSE(resEmpty.has_value());
}

TEST(PatternScanner, MultipleOccurrences) {
    std::vector<uint8_t> buffer = {
        0x11, 0x22, 0x33, 0x00, 0x11, 0x22, 0x44, 0x00, 0x11, 0x22, 0x55
    };
    Pattern pat("11 22 ?");

    auto matches = PatternScanner::ScanAll(buffer.data(), buffer.size(), pat);
    ASSERT_EQ(matches.size(), 3);
    ASSERT_EQ(matches[0], 0);
    ASSERT_EQ(matches[1], 4);
    ASSERT_EQ(matches[2], 8);
}

TEST(PatternScanner, LargeSimdBuffer) {
    // Allocate 128KB buffer, insert pattern at arbitrary unaligned SIMD offset
    std::vector<uint8_t> largeBuf(128 * 1024, 0x90);
    size_t targetOffset = 65536 + 17; // Crosses 32-byte and 16-byte SIMD boundaries

    largeBuf[targetOffset]     = 0x4C;
    largeBuf[targetOffset + 1] = 0x43;
    largeBuf[targetOffset + 2] = 0x5F;
    largeBuf[targetOffset + 3] = 0x4D;
    largeBuf[targetOffset + 4] = 0x45;
    largeBuf[targetOffset + 5] = 0x4D;

    Pattern leechPat("4C 43 5F 4D 45 4D", "LeechSig");
    auto res = PatternScanner::Scan(largeBuf.data(), largeBuf.size(), leechPat);

    ASSERT_TRUE(res.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(*res, targetOffset);
}

TEST(PatternScanner, BatchScanning) {
    std::vector<uint8_t> buffer(1024, 0x00);
    buffer[100] = 0xAA; buffer[101] = 0xBB;
    buffer[500] = 0xCC; buffer[501] = 0xDD;

    std::vector<Pattern> patterns = {
        Pattern("AA BB", "SigA"),
        Pattern("CC DD", "SigB"),
        Pattern("EE FF", "SigC")
    };

    auto matches = PatternScanner::ScanBatch(buffer.data(), buffer.size(), patterns);
    ASSERT_EQ(matches.size(), 2);
    ASSERT_EQ(matches[0].pattern_name, "SigA");
    ASSERT_EQ(matches[0].address, 100);
    ASSERT_EQ(matches[1].pattern_name, "SigB");
    ASSERT_EQ(matches[1].address, 500);
}

TEST(PatternScanner, AllWildcardsAndLeadingWildcards) {
    // 1. All wildcards on non-zero buffer
    std::vector<uint8_t> nonZeroBuf = {0x11, 0x22, 0x33, 0x44};
    Pattern allWild("? ? ? ?");
    auto resAll = PatternScanner::Scan(nonZeroBuf.data(), nonZeroBuf.size(), allWild);
    ASSERT_TRUE(resAll.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(*resAll, 0);

    // 2. Leading wildcards
    std::vector<uint8_t> leadingBuf = {0x90, 0x90, 0x41, 0x42};
    Pattern leadWild("? ? 41 42");
    auto resLead = PatternScanner::Scan(leadingBuf.data(), leadingBuf.size(), leadWild);
    ASSERT_TRUE(resLead.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(*resLead, 0);

    // 3. Trailing wildcards
    std::vector<uint8_t> trailBuf = {0x41, 0x42, 0x88, 0x99};
    Pattern trailWild("41 42 ? ?");
    auto resTrail = PatternScanner::Scan(trailBuf.data(), trailBuf.size(), trailWild);
    ASSERT_TRUE(resTrail.has_value());
    ASSERT_EQ(*resTrail, 0);
}
```

`tests/test_thread_pool.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "tests/test_framework.hpp"
#include "noescape/core/thread_pool.hpp"
#include <atomic>

using namespace noescape::core;

TEST(ThreadPool, ExecutionAndFuture) {
    ThreadPool pool(4);
    std::atomic<int> counter{0};

    std::vector<std::future<int>> futures;
    for (int i = 0; i < 20; ++i) {
        futures.push_back(pool.Enqueue([&counter, i]() {
            counter++;
            return i * 2;
        }));
    }

    for (int i = 0; i < 20; ++i) {
        int res = futures[i].get();
        ASSERT_EQ(res, i * 2);
    }

    ASSERT_EQ(counter.load(), 20);
}
```

```cpp
    ASSERT_TRUE(ok);
    ASSERT_TRUE(timing.IsCalibrated());
    ASSERT_GT(timing.GetCyclesPerMs(), 0.0);
```

`tools/dummy_game.cpp`:

```cpp
#include <iostream>
#include <chrono>
#include <thread>
#include <vector>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdlib>

#if defined(_WIN32)
#ifndef WIN32_LEAN_AND_MEAN
#define WIN32_LEAN_AND_MEAN
#endif
#include <windows.h>
#else
#include <unistd.h>
#endif

// ============================================================================
// Dummy Game Binary (Unmodified Host Process)
// Scenario B: ZERO Anti-Cheat Code, ZERO Anti-Cheat Includes, ZERO Modifications.
// The anti-cheat is loaded entirely dynamically via LD_PRELOAD (Linux) or DLL injection (Windows).
// ============================================================================

struct DummyEntity {
    float x{0.0f};
    float y{0.0f};
    float z{0.0f};
    float vx{1.0f};
    float vy{0.5f};
    float vz{0.0f};
    int health{100};
};

int main(int argc, char* argv[]) {
    int duration_sec = 2;
    if (argc > 1) {
        int parsed = std::atoi(argv[1]);
        if (parsed > 0) duration_sec = parsed;
    }

    std::cout << "[DummyGame] Game binary started successfully (PID: " << 
#if defined(_WIN32)
        GetCurrentProcessId()
#else
        getpid()
#endif
        << ")" << std::endl;
    std::cout << "[DummyGame] Initializing 3D game world and dummy entities..." << std::endl;

    std::vector<DummyEntity> entities(100);
    for (size_t i = 0; i < entities.size(); ++i) {
        entities[i].x = static_cast<float>(i * 5);
        entities[i].y = static_cast<float>(i * 3);
        entities[i].z = 0.0f;
    }

    std::cout << "[DummyGame] Game loop started. Running for " << duration_sec << " seconds..." << std::endl;

    auto startTime = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
    auto runDuration = std::chrono::seconds(duration_sec);
    uint64_t totalFrames = 0;

    while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - startTime < runDuration) {
        totalFrames++;

        // Simulate game loop entity updates
        for (auto& entity : entities) {
            entity.x += entity.vx * 0.016f;
            entity.y += entity.vy * 0.016f;
            entity.z = std::sin(entity.x * 0.1f);
        }

        // Simulate 60 FPS tick rate (~16 ms per frame)
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(16));
    }

    std::cout << "[DummyGame] Game loop finished cleanly (" << totalFrames << " frames rendered)." << std::endl;
    std::cout << "[DummyGame] Host process shutting down cleanly with exit code 0." << std::endl;
    return 0;
}

```

`tools/noescape_ban_ui.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
NoEscape Anti-Cheat - Standalone Crimson Ban Warning Screen
Designed for KDE Plasma on Wayland / X11 (Qt6 / PySide6)
"""

import sys
import os
import argparse
import datetime

# Ensure display environment variables are set if running from detached daemon
uid = os.getuid() if hasattr(os, "getuid") else 1000
run_dir = f"/run/user/{uid}"
if not os.environ.get("WAYLAND_DISPLAY") and os.path.exists(f"{run_dir}/wayland-0"):
    os.environ["WAYLAND_DISPLAY"] = "wayland-0"
if not os.environ.get("XDG_RUNTIME_DIR") and os.path.exists(run_dir):
    os.environ["XDG_RUNTIME_DIR"] = run_dir
if not os.environ.get("DISPLAY"):
    os.environ["DISPLAY"] = ":0"
os.environ.setdefault("QT_QPA_PLATFORM", "wayland;xcb")

from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QApplication, QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel,
    QPushButton, QFrame, QScrollArea, QGraphicsDropShadowEffect
)
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, QPoint
from PySide6.QtGui import QColor, QFont, QPalette, QLinearGradient, QBrush, QPainter, QPainterPath

class BanWindow(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, hwid, score, reason, component, violation_type, timestamp_str):
        super().__init__()
        self.hwid = hwid or "UNKNOWN-HWID-DEVICE"
        self.score = str(score) if score is not None else "100"
        self.reason = reason or "Critical security policy violation detected"
        self.component = component or "AntiCheatEngine::Enforcement"
        self.violation_type = violation_type or "FATAL_BAN"
        self.timestamp_str = timestamp_str or datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S UTC")

        self.setWindowTitle("NOESCAPE ANTI-CHEAT // SECURITY VIOLATION")

        # Determine prominent, commanding screen size ("kocaman")
        screen = QApplication.primaryScreen()
        if screen:
            screen_geo = screen.availableGeometry()
            target_w = min(1280, max(1000, int(screen_geo.width() * 0.72)))
            target_h = min(820, max(680, int(screen_geo.height() * 0.72)))
            self.resize(target_w, target_h)
            self.setMinimumSize(880, 620)
            self.move(
                screen_geo.x() + (screen_geo.width() - self.width()) // 2,
                screen_geo.y() + (screen_geo.height() - self.height()) // 2
            )
        else:
            self.resize(1100, 720)
            self.setMinimumSize(880, 620)

        # Keep on top so user cannot miss it
        self.setWindowFlags(Qt.Window | Qt.WindowStaysOnTopHint | Qt.WindowTitleHint | Qt.WindowCloseButtonHint)
        self.init_ui()

    def init_ui(self):
        # Master dark crimson / red aesthetic
        self.setStyleSheet("""
            QWidget#MainBackground {
                background: qlineargradient(x1:0, y1:0, x2:1, y2:1,
                    stop:0 #1a0306, stop:0.35 #0f0203, stop:0.7 #170305, stop:1 #080102);
            }
            QLabel {
                color: #e0e0e0;
                font-family: 'Segoe UI', 'Ubuntu', 'Liberation Sans', sans-serif;
            }
            QFrame#HeaderCard {
                background: qlineargradient(x1:0, y1:0, x2:1, y2:0,
                    stop:0 #42080e, stop:0.5 #2b0509, stop:1 #1c0305);
                border: 2px solid #ff4655;
                border-radius: 8px;
            }
            QFrame#InfoCard {
                background: #140608;
                border: 1px solid #380e13;
                border-radius: 8px;
            }
            QFrame#FieldBox {
                background: #1e090c;
                border: 1px solid #4a131a;
                border-radius: 6px;
            }
            QPushButton#AcknowledgeBtn {
                background-color: #ff4655;
                color: #ffffff;
                font-weight: bold;
                font-size: 15px;
                letter-spacing: 1.5px;
                border: 1px solid #ff6e7b;
                border-radius: 6px;
                padding: 14px 40px;
            }
            QPushButton#AcknowledgeBtn:hover {
                background-color: #ff5e6c;
                border: 1px solid #ffffff;
            }
            QPushButton#AcknowledgeBtn:pressed {
                background-color: #d92d3c;
            }
        """)

        self.setObjectName("MainBackground")
        main_layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        main_layout.setContentsMargins(36, 32, 36, 32)
        main_layout.setSpacing(20)

        # ---------------- HEADER CARD ----------------
        header_card = QFrame()
        header_card.setObjectName("HeaderCard")
        header_layout = QHBoxLayout(header_card)
        header_layout.setContentsMargins(24, 20, 24, 20)
        header_layout.setSpacing(20)

        # Security Symbol / Shield Icon
        icon_label = QLabel("⛔")
        icon_label.setStyleSheet("font-size: 48px; color: #ff4655; background: transparent;")
        icon_label.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)
        header_layout.addWidget(icon_label, 0)

        title_vbox = QVBoxLayout()
        title_vbox.setSpacing(4)

        top_tag = QLabel("NOESCAPE ANTI-CHEAT // RING-3 SECURITY ENFORCEMENT")
        top_tag.setStyleSheet("color: #ff8c96; font-size: 12px; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 2px;")
        title_vbox.addWidget(top_tag)

        main_title = QLabel("SECURITY VIOLATION DETECTED")
        main_title.setStyleSheet("color: #ffffff; font-size: 26px; font-weight: 900; letter-spacing: 1px;")
        title_vbox.addWidget(main_title)

        sub_title = QLabel("HARDWARE-LEVEL SECURITY ENFORCEMENT // ACCESS REVOKED")
        sub_title.setStyleSheet("color: #ff4655; font-size: 13px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: 1.2px;")
        title_vbox.addWidget(sub_title)

        header_layout.addLayout(title_vbox, 1)

        # Status badge on right
        badge_frame = QFrame()
        badge_frame.setStyleSheet("""
            background: #ff1744;
            border-radius: 4px;
            padding: 8px 16px;
        """)
        badge_layout = QVBoxLayout(badge_frame)
        badge_layout.setContentsMargins(12, 6, 12, 6)
        b_label1 = QLabel("STATUS")
        b_label1.setStyleSheet("color: #ffffff; font-size: 10px; font-weight: 900; letter-spacing: 1px;")
        b_label1.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)
        b_label2 = QLabel("BANNED")
        b_label2.setStyleSheet("color: #ffffff; font-size: 18px; font-weight: 900;")
        b_label2.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)
        badge_layout.addWidget(b_label1)
        badge_layout.addWidget(b_label2)
        header_layout.addWidget(badge_frame, 0)

        main_layout.addWidget(header_card)

        # ---------------- CONTENT CARD ----------------
        info_card = QFrame()
        info_card.setObjectName("InfoCard")
        info_layout = QVBoxLayout(info_card)
        info_layout.setContentsMargins(24, 20, 24, 20)
        info_layout.setSpacing(14)

        desc_label = QLabel(
            "The NoEscape Anti-Cheat subsystem identified an unauthorized memory anomaly, debugger, "
            "or integrity violation during execution. The active game session has been terminated to "
            "protect game integrity. Below are the forensic enforcement parameters:"
        )
        desc_label.setWordWrap(True)
        desc_label.setStyleSheet("color: #c4b5b7; font-size: 13px; line-height: 1.4;")
        info_layout.addWidget(desc_label)

        def make_field(label_text, val_text, is_mono=False, is_highlight=False):
            fbox = QFrame()
            fbox.setObjectName("FieldBox")
            flayout = QVBoxLayout(fbox)
            flayout.setContentsMargins(14, 8, 14, 8)
            flayout.setSpacing(2)

            lbl = QLabel(label_text)
            lbl.setStyleSheet("color: #ff949f; font-size: 11px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: 1px;")
            flayout.addWidget(lbl)

            val = QLabel(val_text)
            val.setTextInteractionFlags(Qt.TextSelectableByMouse)
            val.setWordWrap(True)
            if is_mono:
                val.setStyleSheet("color: #ffffff; font-family: 'Consolas', 'DejaVu Sans Mono', monospace; font-size: 12px; font-weight: bold;")
            elif is_highlight:
                val.setStyleSheet("color: #ff4655; font-size: 14px; font-weight: 800;")
            else:
                val.setStyleSheet("color: #ffffff; font-size: 13px; font-weight: 600;")
            flayout.addWidget(val)
            return fbox

        # Fields row 1: HWID (full width)
        info_layout.addWidget(make_field("DEVICE HARDWARE IDENTIFIER (HWID)", self.hwid, is_mono=True))

        # Fields row 2: Violation Reason & Component
        r2_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        r2_layout.setSpacing(12)
        r2_layout.addWidget(make_field("DETECTED VIOLATION", self.reason, is_highlight=True), 3)
        r2_layout.addWidget(make_field("COMPONENT", self.component, is_mono=True), 2)
        info_layout.addLayout(r2_layout)

        # Fields row 3: Threat Score, Type, Action, Timestamp
        r3_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        r3_layout.setSpacing(12)
        r3_layout.addWidget(make_field("THREAT SCORE", f"{self.score} / 100 [CRITICAL]", is_highlight=True), 1)
        r3_layout.addWidget(make_field("VIOLATION TYPE", self.violation_type), 1)
        r3_layout.addWidget(make_field("ENFORCEMENT ACTION", "SESSION TERMINATED", is_highlight=True), 1)
        r3_layout.addWidget(make_field("TIMESTAMP", self.timestamp_str), 1)
        info_layout.addLayout(r3_layout)

        main_layout.addWidget(info_card)

        # ---------------- BOTTOM ACTION BAR ----------------
        bottom_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        bottom_layout.setContentsMargins(8, 6, 8, 6)

        footer_warning = QLabel("WARNING: Tampering with game memory or running illicit tools is strictly prohibited.")
        footer_warning.setStyleSheet("color: #8c5d63; font-size: 11px; font-style: italic;")
        bottom_layout.addWidget(footer_warning, 1)

        ack_btn = QPushButton("ACKNOWLEDGE && CLOSE")
        ack_btn.setObjectName("AcknowledgeBtn")
        ack_btn.setCursor(Qt.PointingHandCursor)
        ack_btn.clicked.connect(self.close)
        bottom_layout.addWidget(ack_btn, 0)

        main_layout.addLayout(bottom_layout)

    def keyPressEvent(self, event):
        if event.key() in (Qt.Key_Escape, Qt.Key_Return, Qt.Key_Enter, Qt.Key_Space):
            self.close()
        else:
            super().keyPressEvent(event)

def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="NoEscape Crimson Ban Screen")
    parser.add_argument("--hwid", type=str, default="", help="Device HWID fingerprint")
    parser.add_argument("--score", type=int, default=100, help="Threat score")
    parser.add_argument("--reason", type=str, default="Critical security violation detected", help="Violation details")
    parser.add_argument("--component", type=str, default="AntiCheatEngine::Enforcement", help="Violating module")
    parser.add_argument("--type", type=str, default="FATAL_BAN", help="Detection type name")
    parser.add_argument("--timestamp", type=str, default="", help="Detection timestamp string")
    parser.add_argument("--fullscreen", action="store_true", help="Launch in fullscreen mode")
    parser.add_argument("--auto-close", type=int, default=0, help="Auto-close after N seconds (for testing)")
    parser.add_argument("--screenshot", type=str, default="", help="Save rendered window screenshot to file path")

    args = parser.parse_args()

    # Fallback to local HWID if not supplied
    hwid = args.hwid
    if not hwid:
        try:
            with open("/etc/machine-id", "r") as f:
                import hashlib
                hwid = hashlib.sha256(f.read().strip().encode()).hexdigest()
        except Exception:
            hwid = "2d70541832a1cb00855e97f183c6a5053a7b666bc5043648970f977710c3fb80"

    app = QApplication(sys.argv)
    window = BanWindow(
        hwid=hwid,
        score=args.score,
        reason=args.reason,
        component=args.component,
        violation_type=args.type,
        timestamp_str=args.timestamp
    )
    if args.fullscreen:
        window.showFullScreen()
    else:
        window.show()
    window.raise_()
    window.activateWindow()

    if args.screenshot:
        from PySide6.QtCore import QTimer
        def capture():
            pix = window.grab()
            pix.save(args.screenshot, "PNG")
            print(f"Screenshot saved to: {args.screenshot}")
        QTimer.singleShot(600, capture)

    if args.auto_close > 0:
        from PySide6.QtCore import QTimer
        QTimer.singleShot(args.auto_close * 1000, window.close)

    sys.exit(app.exec())

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

`tools/reference_server.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Reference NoEscape panel server (stdlib only) for integration tests and as a protocol spec.

Usage: reference_server.py PORT SECRET [BANNED_HWID ...]
Endpoints (all under /api/ac):
  GET  /check?hwid=H&nonce=N  -> {"banned":bool,"signature":HMAC(secret, "H:0|1:N")}
  POST /heartbeat             -> 200 only if HMAC(secret,"hwid:timestamp:threat_score:status") is valid
  POST /alert                 -> 200 only if HMAC(secret,"hwid:timestamp:type:details") is valid
NOTE: plain HTTP. Deploy behind TLS; the C++ client has no TLS support yet.
"""
import hashlib, hmac, json, sys, time
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, HTTPServer
from urllib.parse import urlparse, parse_qs

PORT = int(sys.argv[1]); SECRET = sys.argv[2].encode(); BANNED = set(sys.argv[3:])
MAX_SKEW = 300

def sign(msg: str) -> str:
    return hmac.new(SECRET, msg.encode(), hashlib.sha256).hexdigest()

class H(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    def log_message(self, *a): pass
    def _send(self, code, body):
        data = json.dumps(body, separators=(",", ":")).encode()
        self.send_response(code); self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(data))); self.send_header("Connection", "close")
        self.end_headers(); self.wfile.write(data)
    def do_GET(self):
        u = urlparse(self.path); q = parse_qs(u.query)
        if not u.path.endswith("/check"): return self._send(404, {"error": "not found"})
        hwid = q.get("hwid", [""])[0]; nonce = q.get("nonce", [""])[0]
        if not hwid or not nonce: return self._send(400, {"error": "bad request"})
        flag = "1" if hwid in BANNED else "0"
        self._send(200, {"banned": flag == "1", "signature": sign(f"{hwid}:{flag}:{nonce}")})
    def do_POST(self):
        n = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", 0))
        try: d = json.loads(self.rfile.read(n))
        except Exception: return self._send(400, {"error": "bad json"})
        try:
            if self.path.endswith("/heartbeat"):
                msg = f'{d["hwid"]}:{d["timestamp"]}:{d["threat_score"]}:{d["status"]}'
            elif self.path.endswith("/alert"):
                msg = f'{d["hwid"]}:{d["timestamp"]}:{d["type"]}:{d["details"]}'
            else: return self._send(404, {"error": "not found"})
            ok = hmac.compare_digest(sign(msg), d.get("signature", ""))
            fresh = abs(time.time() - int(d["timestamp"])) <= MAX_SKEW
        except KeyError: return self._send(400, {"error": "missing field"})
        if ok and fresh: return self._send(200, {"ok": True})
        self._send(403, {"error": "invalid signature or stale timestamp"})

HTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", PORT), H).serve_forever()

```

`tools/verify_preload.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
ROOT_DIR="$(cd "${SCRIPT_DIR}/.." && pwd)"
BUILD_DIR="${ROOT_DIR}/build"

export NOESCAPE_TEST_MODE=1

echo "=========================================================="
echo " NoEscape Scenario B (Auto-Init / Preload) Verification"
echo "=========================================================="

# Check binaries exist
if [[ ! -f "${BUILD_DIR}/libnoescape.so" ]]; then
    echo "[FAIL] ${BUILD_DIR}/libnoescape.so not found!"
    exit 1
fi
if [[ ! -f "${BUILD_DIR}/dummy_game" ]]; then
    echo "[FAIL] ${BUILD_DIR}/dummy_game not found!"
    exit 1
fi

echo "[TEST 1] Standard LD_PRELOAD on clean dummy game (2s run)..."
OUTPUT=$(LD_PRELOAD="${BUILD_DIR}/libnoescape.so" "${BUILD_DIR}/dummy_game" 2 2>&1)
echo "${OUTPUT}"

# Assertions
echo -n "  -> Checking Anti-Cheat banner/boot message: "
if echo "${OUTPUT}" | grep -q "Initializing NoEscape Anti-Cheat Engine v7.0"; then
    echo "OK"
else
    echo "FAILED"
    exit 1
fi

echo -n "  -> Checking Self-Integrity text section baseline: "
if echo "${OUTPUT}" | grep -q "Self-integrity baseline initialized"; then
    echo "OK"
else
    echo "FAILED"
    exit 1
fi

echo -n "  -> Checking Timing TSC calibration: "
if echo "${OUTPUT}" | grep -q "Timing TSC baseline calibrated"; then
    echo "OK"
else
    echo "FAILED"
    exit 1
fi

echo -n "  -> Checking HWID fingerprint generation: "
if echo "${OUTPUT}" | grep -q "Hardware ID Fingerprint:"; then
    echo "OK"
else
    echo "FAILED"
    exit 1
fi

echo -n "  -> Checking DummyGame completed frames without crashing: "
if echo "${OUTPUT}" | grep -q "Game loop finished cleanly"; then
    echo "OK"
else
    echo "FAILED"
    exit 1
fi

echo -n "  -> Checking host process clean exit code 0: "
if echo "${OUTPUT}" | grep -q "exit code 0"; then
    echo "OK"
else
    echo "FAILED"
    exit 1
fi

echo ""
echo "[TEST 2] Verifying NOESCAPE_DISABLE_AUTO_INIT=1 opt-out..."
OPT_OUT_OUTPUT=$(NOESCAPE_DISABLE_AUTO_INIT=1 LD_PRELOAD="${BUILD_DIR}/libnoescape.so" "${BUILD_DIR}/dummy_game" 1 2>&1)
if echo "${OPT_OUT_OUTPUT}" | grep -q "Initializing NoEscape Anti-Cheat Engine"; then
    echo "[FAIL] AC initialized despite NOESCAPE_DISABLE_AUTO_INIT=1!"
    exit 1
else
    echo "  -> OK: Opt-out respected, NoEscape stayed dormant."
fi

echo ""
echo "[TEST 3] Verifying NOESCAPE_AUTO_START=0 opt-out..."
AUTO_ZERO_OUTPUT=$(NOESCAPE_AUTO_START=0 LD_PRELOAD="${BUILD_DIR}/libnoescape.so" "${BUILD_DIR}/dummy_game" 1 2>&1)
if echo "${AUTO_ZERO_OUTPUT}" | grep -q "Initializing NoEscape Anti-Cheat Engine"; then
    echo "[FAIL] AC initialized despite NOESCAPE_AUTO_START=0!"
    exit 1
else
    echo "  -> OK: NOESCAPE_AUTO_START=0 respected, NoEscape stayed dormant."
fi

echo ""
echo "[TEST 4] Verifying NOESCAPE_AUTO_START=1 explicit activation..."
AUTO_ONE_OUTPUT=$(NOESCAPE_AUTO_START=1 LD_PRELOAD="${BUILD_DIR}/libnoescape.so" "${BUILD_DIR}/dummy_game" 1 2>&1)
if echo "${AUTO_ONE_OUTPUT}" | grep -q "Initializing NoEscape Anti-Cheat Engine"; then
    echo "  -> OK: NOESCAPE_AUTO_START=1 booted AC successfully."
else
    echo "[FAIL] AC failed to boot with NOESCAPE_AUTO_START=1!"
    exit 1
fi

echo ""
echo "[TEST 5] Rapid startup & immediate exit (loader lock deadlock test)..."
start_time=$(date +%s%N)
LD_PRELOAD="${BUILD_DIR}/libnoescape.so" true
end_time=$(date +%s%N)
elapsed_ms=$(( (end_time - start_time) / 1000000 ))
echo "  -> Elapsed time for immediate true invocation: ${elapsed_ms} ms (OK: No loader lock hang)"

echo ""
echo "[TEST 6] Verifying secure production hardening rejects opt-out when NOESCAPE_TEST_MODE is unset..."
SECURE_CHECK=$(env -u NOESCAPE_TEST_MODE NOESCAPE_DISABLE_AUTO_INIT=1 LD_PRELOAD="${BUILD_DIR}/libnoescape.so" "${BUILD_DIR}/dummy_game" 1 2>&1)
if echo "${SECURE_CHECK}" | grep -q "Initializing NoEscape Anti-Cheat Engine"; then
    echo "  -> OK: Production hardening active: NOESCAPE_DISABLE_AUTO_INIT was ignored without test mode."
else
    echo "  -> [INFO] Development build detected: opt-out permitted without test mode."
fi

echo ""
echo "=========================================================="
echo " ALL 6 SCENARIO B PRELOAD TESTS PASSED WITH 100% SUCCESS!"
echo "=========================================================="

```
